### [1] [A Global Overview of Starobinsky Inflation beyond slow-roll with CMB-BAO data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19296)
*Tarun,Tanmoy Modak,Björn Malte Schäfer*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 该研究利用最新 CMB（ACT、SPT）与 DESI BAO 数据对 Starobinsky 暴胀模型进行全局分析，发现在直接求解运动方程下得到的参数约束强于慢滚近似下的结果，并进一步给出再加热温度限制及对 R³ 修正模型的约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 借助最新宇宙微波背景（CMB）与大尺度结构观测数据，对 Starobinsky 暴胀（inflation）模型施加更严格的参数限制，并检验不同近似方法与先验选择对结果的影响。

Method: 采用 ACT 与 SPT 的 CMB 数据并结合 DESI BAO 测量进行全局分析（global analysis）；通过直接求解运动方程（equations of motion）获得参数约束，并与基于 ΛCDM 基线参数 A_s 和 n_s 的慢滚近似（slow-roll approximation）结果作对比。

Result: 直接求解运动方程给出的模型参数约束相对更强；据此换算出再加热温度（reheating temperature）的限制，并研究了模型参数先验选择的影响以及模型参数与 BAO 参数 r_d h 的相关性。

Conclusion: 研究进一步分析了全局分析对维度六 R³ 修正 Starobinsky 模型的影响并给出其附加参数约束，同时提供了对有效暴胀哈勃慢滚（HSR）与势慢滚（PSR）参数化的参数限制。

Abstract: We perform a global analysis of the Starobinsky inflation using most recent CMB data from ACT and SPT together with DESI BAO measurements. By directly solving the equations of motion, we find relatively stronger constraints on the model parameters than those obtained under the slow-roll approximation from the baseline $Λ$CDM parameters $A_s$ and $n_s$. We translate these constraints into limits on the reheating temperature. We also discuss the dependence of the prior choices of the model parameters. Further, the correlation of the model parameters with the BAO parameter $r_d h$ is also studied. We further investigate the impact of the global analysis on the dimension-six $R^3$ modified Starobinsky model, deriving constraints on its additional parameters. Finally, we also provide parameter limits on the effective inflationary Hubble slow-roll (HSR) and potential slow-roll (PSR) parametrization.

</details>


### [2] [Distinguishing Coupled Dark Matter Dark Energy from Kinematic Phantom Crossing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19602)
*Samit Ganguly,Koushik Dutta,Goutam Manna*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 耦合暗能量-暗物质（CDEDM）模型通过正则标量场与冷暗物质的能量交换，可在不引入基本幻影场（phantom field）的前提下重现 $w_0w_a$CDM 的有效状态方程演化。该微观相互作用在结构增长（structure growth）中留下可通过耦合-中微子质量简并（$\sum m_\nu \propto f_c^2\alpha^2$）检验的特征信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期宇宙膨胀测量重启了对有效状态方程呈幻影式演化（phantom-like evolution）、可能穿越 $w=-1$ 的暗能量情景的兴趣，但此类行为未必需要基本幻影场，因而有必要考察相互作用机制能否作为替代解释。

Method: 构建正则标量场与冷暗物质的拉格朗日耦合框架，形成双流体（two-fluid）体系：仅冷暗物质感受标量介导的第五力（fifth force）与拖曳项（drag term），重子保持不耦合；结合无中微子自由流（neutrino-free streaming）推导双流体增长方程，并用 DESI DR2 BAO、CMB 距离信息、ACT DR6 透镜、红移空间畸变（redshift-space distortions）及三个超新星汇编进行拟合。

Result: 耦合-中微子质量简并的领先标度关系 $\sum m_\nu \propto f_c^2\alpha^2$ 可由增长方程解析得到，并在指数势与逆幂律势（inverse-power-law potential）中均被数据恢复；增长响应的主导贡献来自耦合对背景演化的修正，第五力增强增长、拖曳部分抵消；相互作用导致暗物质质量随演化跑动（running），在复合（recombination）时期改变约 4.3%--5.5%。

Conclusion: 相关增长与复合效应为区分相互作用驱动的幻影穿越（phantom crossing）与纯运动学暗能量参数化提供了途径，并提示 CMB 距离计算须考虑演化的暗物质密度。

Abstract: Recent measurements of cosmic expansion have renewed interest in dark-energy scenarios in which the effective equation of state exhibits phantom-like evolution and may cross $w=-1$. Such behavior, however, does not require a fundamental phantom field: in coupled dark-energy-dark-matter~(CDEDM) models, energy exchange between a canonical scalar field and cold dark matter, happens Lagrangian labels, can reproduce the same effective equation of state evolution as that of phenomenological $w_0w_a$CDM model. We show that this microscopic interaction leaves distinctive, testable signatures in structure growth by forming a two-fluid system in which only cold dark matter feels the scalar-mediated fifth force (and an associated drag term), while baryons remain uncoupled. When combined with neutrino-free streaming, this setup generates a characteristic coupling, neutrino-mass degeneracy, whose leading scaling $\sum m_ν\propto f_c^2 α^2$ follows analytically from the two-fluid growth equations. Using DESI DR2 BAO, CMB distance information, ACT DR6 lensing, redshift-space distortions, and three supernova compilations, we find that this scaling is recovered for both exponential and inverse-power-law potentials. We further show that the dominant contribution to the growth response arises from the coupling-induced modification of the background evolution, with the fifth force enhancing growth and the drag partially counteracting it. Finally, the interaction induces a running dark-matter mass, changing it at recombination by $4.3\%$--$5.5\%$, so CMB distance calculations must account for the evolving dark-matter density. These correlated growth and recombination effects provide a route to distinguish interaction-driven phantom crossing from purely kinematic dark-energy parametrizations.

</details>

### [3] [Schwarzschild solution from a Raychaudhuri-Huygens relation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19171)
*Maurice H. P. M. van Putten*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文从零测地线汇（null-congruences）的因果结构与拉乔杜里方程（Raychaudhuri equation）出发，在球对称下构造编码面积 $A_E=λR_g r$，并在 UV-IR 一致耦合下由 $p=A_E/A$ 的几何嵌入恢复施瓦西度规（Schwarzschild metric），从而给出引力相空间标度维数为二的非热力学起源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝肯斯坦（Bekenstein, 1973）曾基于热力学第二定律导出引力相空间两维标度维数，其热力学起源的解释是否唯一、是否存在非热力学路径，构成该文的问题动机。文中亦希望在不使用爱因斯坦方程（Einstein equations）的前提下，由等价原理的非局域延展导出相同的编码关系。

Method: 以零测地线汇描述辐射通道相空间，其膨胀标量 $θ$ 满足拉乔杜里方程；球对称下面积 $A=4πr^2$ 的波前满足 $θ=A^\prime/A=2/r$。围绕质量 $M$ 引入编码面积 $A_E=λ\varphi \ell_p^2=λR_g r$，与康普顿相位（Compton phase）$\varphi=(mc/\hbar)r$ 通过 $\ell_p^2=G\hbar/c^3$ 作 UV-IR 一致耦合，参数 $λ=8π$ 由大距离牛顿极限与视界处饱和极限 $A_E=A$ 共同固定，依据是林德勒时空（Rindler spacetime）中等价原理的非局域延展。

Result: 在球对称条件下，由 $p=A_E/A$ 的几何嵌入恢复了施瓦西度规，且该比值在引力透镜（gravitational lensing）中直接可观测；$λ=8π$ 同时满足牛顿极限与视界饱和条件，无需借助爱因斯坦方程。

Conclusion: 结果表明引力相空间中两个标度维数具有非热力学起源，此前该维数由贝肯斯坦基于第二定律推出，二者结论一致但机制不同；该框架可能对宇宙学时空中产生影响。

Abstract: Null-congruences encode the causal structure of spacetime, describing a phase space of radiation channels, whose expansion scalar $θ$ satisfies the Raychaudhuri equation. In spherical symmetry, the expansion scalar of wave fronts of area $A=4πr^2$ satisfies $θ= A^\prime/A = 2/r$. About a mass $M$, this carries an encoding area $A_E = λ\varphi \ell_p^2 = λR_g r \le A$ in a UV-IR consistent coupling $\ell_p^2= G\hbar/c^3$ to its Compton phase $\varphi = (mc/\hbar)r \propto 1/\hbar$. Here, $λ= 8π$ is fixed by the Newtonian limit at large distances and the saturation limit $A_E=A$ at horizon about $M$ based on the non-local extension of the equivalence principle in Rindler spacetime, without using the Einstein equations. In spherical symmetry, this recovers the Schwarzschild metric from a geometric embedding of $p=A_E/A$, which is directly observable in gravitational lensing. The result shows a non-thermal origin of the scaling dimension of two of gravitational phase space, previously derived based on the second law of thermodynamics by Bekenstein (1973), with possible implications for cosmological spacetime.

</details>


### [4] [An Operative Viability Boundary for Relaxed Single-Particle Penrose Extraction in Kerr-Vaidya Spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19175)
*Fabio Buffoli*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文修正了先前 Kerr-Vaidya 度规中 Penrose 过程研究的两处错误（g_rφ 度规分量与 Wald/Christodoulou 面积定理的遗漏），在单粒子近似下计算最优能量增益随自旋与质量损失率的变化，并给出临界质量损失率 alpha_crit(a)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期版本存在度规分量 g_rφ 错误（经 Boyer-Lindquist 坐标变换验证）以及分裂优化中遗漏 Wald/Christodoulou 面积定理（area theorem）两处问题，需给出修正后的数值研究。同时，动态黑洞（质量随时间变化）中有效自旋的漂移使静态度规与动态情形的直接比较具有误导性，需专门澄清。

Method: 采用标准单粒子处理（single-particle treatment）：分裂过程（split）中能量与角动量守恒，但不要求逃逸碎片的径向动量守恒。据此计算最优轨迹，并给出有界能量增益 ΔE = E3 − E1 随自旋 a/M 与质量损失率 alpha 的关系；另以完整四动量守恒（four-momentum conservation）作为对照检验。

Result: 静态 Kerr 黑洞（Kerr hole）的 ΔE 随自旋上升，从 a/M = 0.8 时的约 0.106 M 增至 a/M = 0.99 时的 0.245 M。动态情形下，固定 a 而 m(v) 减小使有效自旋 a/m(v) 强烈趋向极端（extremality），由 0.81 漂移至 0.9999。临界质量损失率随自旋下降：alpha_crit(0.90) ≈ 0.036、alpha_crit(0.95) ≈ 0.019、alpha_crit(0.99) ≈ 0.0038。完整四动量守恒检验在视界附近重现 Bardeen-Press-Teukolsky 界限，但在远离视界处逃逸解稀少。

Conclusion: 动态情形下有效自旋漂移使等名义 a/M 的静态-动态比较不可靠；超过 alpha_crit(a) 后不存在可行提取（extraction）解（基于 m ≥ 0）。由于完整四动量守恒下逃逸解在视界外罕见，本文主要结论基于文献中常用的松弛单粒子表述（relaxed single-particle formulation）。

Abstract: We present a corrected numerical investigation of the Penrose process in the Kerr-Vaidya metric for a rotating black hole losing mass at constant rate. We fix two errors from an earlier version: an incorrect metric component g_r phi (verified by transformation from Boyer-Lindquist coordinates) and the omission of the Wald/Christodoulou area theorem in the split optimization. Using the standard single-particle treatment (energy and angular momentum conserved at the split, but not the escaping fragment's radial momentum), we compute optimal trajectories and the bounded energy gain Delta E = E3 - E1 versus spin a/M and mass-loss rate alpha. For static Kerr holes, Delta E rises with spin from about 0.106 M at a/M = 0.8 to 0.245 M at a/M = 0.99. In the dynamic case, holding a fixed while m(v) decreases causes the effective spin a/m(v) to drift strongly toward extremality (from 0.81 to 0.9999), a geometric effect that makes static-dynamic comparisons at equal nominal a/M misleading. We map the critical mass-loss rate alpha_crit(a) above which no viable extraction is found (using m >= 0), obtaining alpha_crit(0.90) ~ 0.036, alpha_crit(0.95) ~ 0.019 and alpha_crit(0.99) ~ 0.0038, decreasing with spin. A check with full four-momentum conservation recovers the Bardeen-Press-Teukolsky bound near the horizon, but escaping solutions are rare away from it; thus our main results refer to the relaxed single-particle formulation common in the literature.

</details>


### [5] [Bosonic stars with dark electroweak fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19273)
*Etevaldo dos Santos Costa Filho,Romain Gervalle*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在爱因斯坦-温伯格-萨拉姆理论（Einstein-Weinberg-Salam theory）中构造了由 W、Z 玻色子静凝聚形成的玻色星（bosonic star），其处处正则、球对称且渐近平坦。因物理电弱能标下引力效应可忽略，作者将该模型解释为具有不同能标的暗电弱理论。取超轻矢量玻色子质量 m_W c²=8.7×10⁻¹³ eV 时，所得玻色星质量可达中等质量黑洞范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究的动机在于探索电弱标准模型玻色子部分与引力最小耦合后能否形成自引力束缚的天体。由于真实电弱能标下引力效应可忽略、构型仅为微观尺度，作者转而将其视为能标迥异的暗电弱理论，以寻找宏观可观测的天体物理对象。

Method: 方法为在爱因斯坦-温伯格-萨拉姆理论中构造玻色星解，即电弱标准模型玻色子部分与爱因斯坦引力理论最小耦合。所构造的构型处处正则、球对称、渐近平坦，由大质量 W、Z 玻色子的静凝聚体（static condensate）组成，其质量来源于希格斯机制（Higgs mechanism）。作者将牛顿常数与电弱质量比保持物理值，仅改变能标。

Result: 结果表明，当选取超轻矢量玻色子质量 m_W c²=8.7×10⁻¹³ eV（由 GW190521 的 Proca 星解释所建议）时，所构造的玻色星可达到中等质量黑洞（intermediate-mass black hole）的质量范围。

Conclusion: 结论是：电弱玻色子凝聚体在自引力下可形成正则、球对称的玻色星，但只有在将其解释为能标极为不同的暗电弱理论时才能在宏观质量尺度上产生可观测效应，并有望对应中等质量黑洞候选体。

Abstract: We construct bosonic stars in the Einstein-Weinberg-Salam theory, namely the bosonic sector of the electroweak Standard Model minimally coupled to Einstein's theory of gravity. These configurations are everywhere regular, spherically symmetric and asymptotically flat, consisting of a static condensate of the massive $W$ and $Z$ bosons, whose masses arise from the Higgs mechanism. Although the corresponding fields exist in nature, gravitational effects are negligible at the physical electroweak scale and the configurations would be microscopic. Keeping Newton's constant and the electroweak mass ratios at their physical values, we therefore interpret the model as the bosonic sector of a \textit{dark} electroweak theory with a radically different energy scale. Choosing the ultralight vector boson mass $m_{\mathrm{W}}c^{2}=8.7\times10^{-13}\,$eV, as suggested by the Proca star interpretation of GW190521, our bosonic stars can attain masses in the intermediate-mass black hole range.

</details>


### [6] [Deep Learning Detection of Beyond-General-Relativity Deviations in Gravitational-Wave Signals: A Detection-Threshold Study with Real LIGO Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19416)
*Muhammad Adnan Shahzad*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该研究利用机器学习（machine learning）在引力波信号中检测超越广义相对论（beyond-General-Relativity, beyond-GR）的受控偏离，结合一维卷积神经网络（1D convolutional neural network）与十个手工波形统计量进行分类。以真实 GW150914 应变和 H1 探测器噪声为模板，得到检测阈值约为 β≈0.25，并指出该阈值仅对应所采用的二次时间调制形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探索机器学习方法能否在真实探测器噪声条件下检测引力波信号中超越广义相对论的偏离，并量化其可检测性边界。该工作特别关注在缺乏匹配滤波（matched-filter）信号提取的情况下，纯机器学习搜索的性能极限。

Method: 在广义相对论旋进-并合-铃宕（inspiral-merger-ringdown）波形上施加三类偏离：振幅调制（amplitude modulation）、相位调制（phase modulation）与频率调制（frequency modulation），各由无量纲强度系数 β 参数化。采用合成 aLIGO-PSD 噪声与真实 LIGO H1 探测器应变，训练一个结合一维卷积神经网络与十个手工波形统计量的混合分类器（hybrid classifier），并在训练中排除某一偏离类型的条件下进行测试。

Result: 核心结果为随 β 变化的定量可检测性曲线（detectability curve）。使用真实 GW150914 应变作为模板并配合真实 H1 探测器噪声，检测阈值出现在 β≈0.25，分类准确率从 β≤0.2 时的随机水平（chance）平滑上升至 β≥0.5 时的完全正确分类。该阈值特定于本文采用的二次时间调制（quadratic-in-time modulation）形式。

Conclusion: 该阈值不应被解释为对 beyond-GR 参数的通用约束，而仅适用于所采用的特定调制形式。作者认为小 β 处的负结果具有信息价值：在无匹配滤波信号提取的情况下，它为真实探测器噪声中纯机器学习的 beyond-GR 搜索确立了定量极限。

Abstract: We study machine-learning detection of controlled beyond-General-Relativity (beyond-GR) deviations in gravitational-wave signals, using both synthetic aLIGO-PSD noise and real LIGO H1 detector strain. Three deviation families are applied to General-Relativistic inspiral-merger-ringdown waveforms: amplitude modulation, phase modulation, and frequency modulation, each parameterized by a dimensionless strength coefficient $β$. A hybrid classifier combining a one-dimensional convolutional neural network with ten hand-crafted waveform statistics is trained on GR and modified waveforms and tested on a deviation type excluded from training. The central result is a quantitative detectability curve as a function of $β$. Using the real GW150914 strain as a template and real H1 detector noise, we find a detection threshold at $β\approx 0.25$, with accuracy rising smoothly from chance at $β\leq 0.2$ to perfect classification at $β\geq 0.5$. The threshold value is specific to the quadratic-in-time modulation form adopted here and should not be interpreted as a generic constraint on beyond-GR parameters. We nevertheless argue that the negative result at small $β$ is informative: it establishes a quantitative limit on machine-learning-only beyond-GR searches in real detector noise, in the absence of matched-filter signal extraction.

</details>


### [7] [On Stochastic Memory Backgrounds In LISA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19462)
*James Buda,Andrew Laeuger,Yanbei Chen*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 基于两类超大质量黑洞种群模型与数值相对论波形，评估引力波记忆（gravitational-wave memory）累积形成的随机引力波记忆背景（SGWMB）。预测谱显著低于以阶跃函数（Heaviside function）建模的结果，信噪比约为 O(0.1–10)，且谱呈高度非高斯的"爆米花"（popcorn）特征，表明该背景更可能表现为间歇性脉冲集合而非连续重叠信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Gravitational-wave (GW) memory is a permanent change in spacetime geometry induced by a burst of gravitational waves. We further find that our models favor a highly non-Gaussian ("popcorn") spectrum, demonstrating that the background may not appear as the union of continuous, overlapping signals, but rather as a collection of intermittent bursts. We then study the implications of this mode-dependent transition for the stochastic description of perturbations. Using the Green's function method, we show that the stochastic spectrum reproduces the power spectrum of the quantum state initialized in the Bunch-Davies vacuum, independently of the mode-dependent transition when the gradient term is retained in the Langevin equation, for both white and colored noise coarse-graining. Homogeneous matching procedure can be used to set mode-dependent $σ(k)$ that reproduces the power spectrum of the quantum state at the end of inflation. The resulting spin-extended relation reduces the median absolute percentage error relative to the slow-rotation relation by 66\% across this range. Parameter estimation using the spin-extended relation reduces biases in recovered intrinsic parameters in some cases, though biases from mass-spin degeneracies dominate largely when a population is considered. </details>

In the extremal sector, the logarithmic phase interval doubles, halving the exponent which denotes the geometric spacing of successive node scales.

Abstract: We investigate traversable wormholes supported by Casimir vacuum energy with second-order generalized uncertainty principle (GUP) corrections.   <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: It has been shown that the thermodynamics of frame-invariant scalar-tensor gravity admits an Einstein-frame-like invariant representation, in which the scalar sector is minimally coupled and behaves as a perfect fluid with vanishing temperature. In this representation the metric field equations retain the Hessian sector characteristic of Jordan-frame scalar-tensor gravity. A $1+3$ decomposition of the corresponding effective stress tensor yields nonvanishing heat flux and anisotropic stress in a generic congruence. In the $Ψ$-comoving frame, the usual first-order thermodynamic identifications are recovered in invariant form, including $K_{\mathrm J}T_{\mathrm J}=-\dotΨ/(κ^2Ψ)$ and $η_{\mathrm J}=\dotΨ/(2κ^2Ψ)$, with the bulk channel obtained in homogeneous and isotropic sectors. Thus, frame-invariant scalar-tensor thermodynamics is not intrinsically restricted to an Einstein-frame-like perfect-fluid description: it also admits a Jordan-frame-like invariant formulation in which the effective imperfect-fluid interpretation is manifest.

Motivation: 共形界面在一维量子临界系统中对能量与信息透射的普适描述已在封闭系统中建立，但共形界面在耗散动力学中的作用尚不清楚；同时既有工作由 Liouvillian gap 定义的弛豫系数在某些边界条件下可能因决定最小衰减率的模式改变而变得非普适。

Abstract: Conformal interfaces provide an important setting for studying universal transmission phenomena in one-dimensional quantum critical systems. While energy and information transmission across such interfaces are characterized by universal quantities in closed systems, the corresponding role of conformal interfaces in dissipative dynamics is less understood. In this work, we study relaxation in locally dissipative quantum critical chains with a conformal interface and show that a universal characterization emerges at the level of individual relaxation modes. We first show that the relaxation coefficient defined in the recent work [1] from the Liouvillian gap can become non-universal for certain boundary conditions because the mode determining the smallest decay rate can change as the interface transmission is varied. To resolve this ambiguity, we introduce a mode-resolved relaxation coefficient $c_{\rm relax}$ by continuously tracking the same Liouvillian rapidity mode as a function of the interface transmission. Using analytical and numerical calculations for a critical harmonic chain and a critical free-fermion chain, we find that, in the weak-dissipation regime, $c_{\rm relax}$ follows the same universal dependence on the interface transmission in all cases considered, independent of microscopic details such as the boundary conditions, dissipation strength, and location of the local dissipation. For boundary dissipation, this universal behavior persists even at finite dissipation strength. Our results establish a universal mode-resolved characterization of relaxation across conformal interfaces in open quantum critical systems.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 在自发对称破缺相深处，各向同性序参量（isotropic order parameter）的零点比值解析地趋于无参数 Bessel 序列（parameter-free Bessel sequence），其形式由集体分量数固定。接近量子临界性（quantum criticality）时，涨落使该分布变形，由此产生的比值偏离及其有限尺寸耦合依赖（finite-size coupling dependence）携带额外临界信息。结果在具有常规与扩大的连续对称性（continuous symmetries）的多体系统的大规模量子蒙特卡洛模拟中得到验证。

Conclusion: 该方法无需测量所有竞争序，即可定量获取集体对称性与临界性（criticality），为从单一可及分量提取隐藏对称信息提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Symmetry plays a fundamental role in modern physics by guiding the classification of phases and the construction of effective theories of critical systems. Identifying collective symmetry remains challenging because tests based on preselected order parameters can overlook hidden components. A central goal is therefore to extract symmetry and critical information from a single accessible component. We pursue this goal using Lee--Yang zero ratios generated by a single ordering field. Unmeasured components shape the projected fluctuation distribution and hence the relative zero positions. Deep in a phase with spontaneous symmetry breaking, we show analytically that the zero ratios for an isotropic order parameter approach a parameter-free Bessel sequence fixed by the number of collective components. Near quantum criticality, fluctuations deform this distribution. The resulting ratio deviations and their finite-size coupling dependence carry additional critical information. We demonstrate these results with large-scale quantum Monte Carlo simulations of many-body systems with conventional and enlarged continuous symmetries. This approach provides quantitative access to collective symmetry and criticality without measuring every competing order.

</details>


### [26] [Encounter Propagator for Multiple Targets: A Dirichlet-to-Neumann Spectral Formalism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19995)

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目标情形的局域时间（boundary local time）方法已有成熟表述，但多目标问题中目标投影算子与 DtN 算子一般不交换，导致本征基无法对角化局域时间依赖，因此需要新的分解框架来处理多目标受限扩散。

Method: 将 DtN 算子分解为目标受限块（target-restricted blocks），对多维 Laplace 反演（Laplace inversion）得到切换展开，并导出等价的算子值更新方程（operator-valued renewal equation）；对双目标情形，通过对目标受限块对角化，得到由两个 DtN 块谱与目标间耦合矩阵构成的显式谱表示，并可对保谱模态的耦合进行精确重求和。

Result: 展开的逐项对应目标间的交替扩散转移；双目标情形下，当耦合保持谱模态时，切换级数可用修正贝塞尔函数（modified Bessel functions）精确重求和；一般情况下，分离目标对应的非对角 DtN 块具有光滑化性质，可采用低秩有限维近似；针对小且良好分离的目标，两模态约化（two-mode reduction）揭示出重复目标间转移被逐步抑制的参数区域。

Abstract: We develop an encounter-based formulation of restricted diffusion in a bounded domain with multiple targets, resolving separately the boundary local time accumulated on each target. Unlike the single-target problem, the target projection operators do not generally commute with the governing Dirichlet-to-Neumann (DtN) operator, so that its eigenbasis does not diagonalize the local-time dependence. We overcome this difficulty by decomposing the DtN operator into target-restricted blocks. Multi-dimensional Laplace inversion then yields a convergent switching expansion, whose successive terms describe alternating diffusive transfers between the targets. We also derive an equivalent operator-valued renewal equation. For two targets, diagonalizing the target-restricted blocks provides an explicit spectral representation in terms of two DtN block spectra and an inter-target coupling matrix. When the coupling preserves spectral modes, the switching series can be resummed exactly in terms of modified Bessel functions. In general, as the off-diagonal DtN block is smoothing for separated targets, one can resort to low-rank finite-dimensional approximations. We examine an effective two-mode reduction for small, well-separated targets and identify a regime in which repeated inter-target transfers are progressively suppressed. Probabilistic interpretations and implications for diffusion-controlled reactions are discussed.

</details>

Conclusion: 所报道构型可视为稳健的全局极小候选（global-minimum candidates），并为经典 Thomson 问题在圆盘几何下的能量基准与对称性图景提供了更新依据。

Abstract: We report improved global-minimum configurations for the classical Thomson problem of $N=60$, $61$, $92$, and $99$ repulsive Coulomb charges confined to a disk. By combining the quenched molecular dynamics (QMD) method with the fixed-border heuristic introduced by Amore and Zarate, we systematically obtain configurations with energies $E_{\mathrm{QMD}}(60)=2159.3584240930$, $E_{\mathrm{QMD}}(61)=2237.19264190$, $E_{\mathrm{QMD}}(92)=5358.35353314$, and $E_{\mathrm{QMD}}(99)=6254.83029083$, which improve upon the previously best-known values. For $N=60$, the Voronoi diagram of our configuration differs from the one reported earlier. The symmetries for $N=61$ $(C_{2})$ and $N=99$ $(D_{1})$ are confirmed and are consistent with the known symmetry patterns for these configurations. For $N=92$, whereas the previously reported Voronoi diagram has lower symmetry, our solution exhibits a clear $D_{1}$ (axial) symmetry of defects. The results are highly reproducible across multiple independent runs, providing strong evidence that these configurations are robust global-minimum candidates.

</details>


### [28] [Higher-Order Interactions in Complex Systems: Mechanisms, Behaviour, Representation and Reducibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20142)
*Francisco J. Pérez-Reche*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本综述区分"机制（mechanism）"、"行为（behaviour）"与"表示（representation）"三个层次的问题，指出高阶相互作用（higher-order interactions）产生的集体行为并非高阶机制独有的证据，并将可约性（reducibility）形式化为源模型与可容许低阶模型类在给定目标与容差下的比较问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阶相互作用能产生爆炸同步（explosive synchronization）、非连续相变等显著集体行为，但许多体（many-body）描述本身并不构成多体机制，因为机制与行为均依赖于系统的表示方式。综述旨在澄清机制、行为与表示之间的混淆，并提供判断高阶描述是否必要的形式框架。

Method: 以概念辨析与文献综述为主：区分直接多体机制、可模仿高阶行为的非线性成对（pairwise）机制、经由共享环境或消去变量的间接路径，以及属于观测假象（artefact）的时间聚合（temporal aggregation）；同时梳理信息论（information-theoretic）与推断性（inferential）方法，用于检测高阶依赖、检验低阶替代模型并重构相互作用结构。

Result: 同一行为可由不同机制产生，且某一高阶机制并无普适的行为后果；可约性被表述为源模型与可容许低阶模型类之间、相对于特定目标（微观规则、可观测量或定性特征）与容差的比较。信息论与推断方法可作为互补工具，但仅凭观测数据通常无法确立机制。

Conclusion: 尚待解决的问题包括：机制与可测量特征（signatures）的关联、粗粒化（coarse-graining）、可辨识性（identifiability）与因果性（causality），以及将约化方法推广至平均场（mean field）之外的情形。

Abstract: Higher-order interactions couple three or more elements in ways that cannot be decomposed into pairwise contributions. In diverse complex systems they produce striking collective behaviours such as explosive synchronization, discontinuous transitions and altered stability. Neither such behaviour nor a description written in many-body terms establishes a many-body mechanism, since both depend on how the system is represented. This review distinguishes three questions: what mechanism governs the system, what behaviour does it produce, and how is either represented? Representation covers both which variables are included and how relations among them are encoded. We examine direct many-body mechanisms, nonlinear but pairwise mechanisms that mimic them, and indirect routes through shared environments or eliminated variables, together with temporal aggregation, an artefact of observation rather than a mechanism. The same behaviour can arise from different mechanisms, and a given higher-order mechanism has no universal behavioural consequence. We then formulate reducibility, the question of whether a higher-order model can be replaced by a lower-order one, as a comparison between a source model and an admissible class of lower-order models, relative to a target and a tolerance. The target may be a microscopic rule, an observable or a qualitative feature. Information-theoretic and inferential methods are reviewed as complementary tools for detecting higher-order dependence, testing lower-order alternatives and reconstructing interaction structure. However, observational data alone generally cannot establish the mechanism. We close by identifying open problems in linking mechanisms to measurable signatures, coarse-graining, identifiability and causality, and in extending reductions beyond mean field. (Abridged)

</details>


### [29] [Landau diamagnetism and the de Haas-van Alphen effect from a single geometric construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20146)

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统朗道抗磁性由巨正则势（grand canonical potential）导出，虽得到正确磁化率（susceptibility）却缺乏物理直观；而"极值截面（extremal cross sections）决定 dHvA 振荡"这一规则多被直接断言而非展示。本文旨在提供一个更具物理洞察力的初等推导。

Conclusion: 该零温构造以态转移与质心算术直观重现朗道磁化率，并自然揭示极值截面主导 dHvA 振荡的机制，替代了传统上仅被断言的经验规则。

Abstract: Landau diamagnetism is usually derived from the grand canonical potential, a calculation that yields the correct susceptibility but little physical insight, while the rule that extremal cross sections govern the de Haas-van Alphen (dHvA) oscillations is commonly asserted rather than exhibited. We present an elementary zero-temperature construction in which the occupied states of a free-electron gas are grouped, interval by interval along the field direction, into the Landau levels onto which they condense. Within each interval the field-induced energy cost reduces to a transfer of states between two congruent triangles, and center-of-mass arithmetic yields the Landau susceptibility. The construction fails only in a narrow neighborhood of an extremal cross section of the Fermi surface; there the contribution oscillates with the dHvA period, one oscillation for each Landau level that passes through the extremal cross section. Direct zero-temperature state counting locates the oscillation: the residual is concentrated in the few intervals nearest the extremal cross section, the summed contribution of the distant ones being negligible on the scale of the peak oscillation amplitude.

</details>

Abstract: We investigate energy fluctuations of solvated DNA in three conformations: G-quadruplex, Hairpin, and Duplex. Our comprehensive molecular dynamics (MD) simulations demonstrate that the time-series of interaction energies for DNA self, DNA-water and DNA-ion show 1/f noise with a universal decay exponent $β\simeq 0.90$. We show that DNA topology and structural rigidity modulate its dynamic coupling with the surrounding hydration shell and mobile counter-ions. This coupling critically shapes the 1/f noise profile: DNA self energies in flexible structures (G-quadruplex, Hairpin) display pronounced low-frequency modes absent in the rigid Duplex. These findings reveal how the interplay between DNA topology and environmental coupling governs different stochastic behavior in biologically relevant forms of DNA.

### [31] [Khinchin's ergodicity and typicality in statistical mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20433)
*Dario Lucente,Marco Baldovin,Giacomo Gradenigo,Angelo Vulpiani*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文重新审视 Khinchin 的观点，论证统计力学（statistical mechanics）有效性的关键要素是系统的大量自由度与广延可观测量（extensive observables）的选择，而非微观动力学细节或混沌的存在；通过对谐振子链（harmonic chain）与 Toda 模型这两个经典可积系统（integrable systems）的解析与数值分析，表明即使在无混沌情形下也能再现麦克斯韦-玻尔兹曼分布（Maxwell-Boltzmann distribution）等平衡统计力学预言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gibbs 提出的系综（ensembles）表述在描述多粒子系统平衡态方面极为成功，但其成功原因仍存争议，动力学与概率之间的联系尚不清晰。通常推导平衡统计力学结果需对动力学的遍历性（ergodicity）作强假设，而经验观察却显示这些假设不成立时理论预言依然有效，这一矛盾构成研究动机。

Method: 采用 Khinchin 的视角，即统计力学成立仅需大量自由度与适当选取的广延可观测量，与微观动力学细节无关，尤其不要求动力学混沌（dynamical chaos）。具体通过讨论两个经典可积系统——谐振子链与 Toda 模型——的解析与数值结果来展开论证。

Result: 对谐振子链与 Toda 模型的分析表明，即使不存在混沌，也能发现平衡统计力学所预言的许多重要特征，例如麦克斯韦-玻尔兹曼分布。

Conclusion: 统计力学的有效性并不依赖于遍历性或动力学混沌等强假设，而主要由系统的大量自由度与广延可观测量的选择所保证，这支持并强化了 Khinchin 的观点。

Abstract: The formulation of statistical mechanics in terms of ensembles, originally proposed by Gibbs, has proved to be extremely effective in describing the equilibrium state of many-particle systems, in the most diverse contexts (critical phenomena, quantum mechanics, biophysics, just to mention a few). The reasons of this success are still debated, and the connection between dynamics and probability in large systems remains somehow elusive. Indeed, in order to derive the main results of equilibrium statistical mechanics, strong assumptions on the ergodicity of the dynamics are usually required. Nonetheless, empirical observations seem to suggest that the predictions of the theory hold true even when such assumptions are not verified. In this paper we reconsider the point of view put forward by Khinchin, stating that the only relevant ingredients for the validity of statistical mechanics are the large number of degrees of freedom in the system and the choice of extensive observables, irrespectively of the details of the microscopic dynamics. In particular, the presence of dynamical chaos is not required. To this aim, we discuss some analytical and numerical results on a couple of classical integrable systems, the harmonic chain and the Toda model, showing that many important features predicted by equilibrium statistical mechanics, as for instance Maxwell-Boltzmann distribution, are found even in absence of chaos.

</details>

Result: 对于每个小于 4 的耦合常数，证明磁芯（magnetic core）半径达到物理文献所预言的尺度。通过 Weber 方程建立标量场高斯尾部与界面的直接联系，并将界面识别为经典正常-超导转变层以获得其存在性、唯一性与单调性。匹配映射满秩的证明为整体渐近构造提供了基础。

Conclusion: 本文为大通量轴对称涡旋的形式渐近展开提供了严格数学框架，将多个经典方程与定理（Weber 方程、Ginzburg-Landau 转变层、Chapman-Howison-McLeod-Ockendon 定理、修正贝塞尔函数）纳入统一分析，并确认了物理文献对磁芯半径的标度预言。

Abstract: This paper studies axially symmetric Abrikosov-Nielsen-Olesen vortices in the quartic Abelian Higgs model as the magnetic flux becomes arbitrarily large. The emphasis is on developing the rigorous asymptotic analysis behind the formal large-flux expansions of Hakim, Lemaitre, and Mallick '01 and Dumitrescu, Gaikwad '25. For every coupling constant less than 4 we prove that the radius of the magnetic core is of the size predicted by the physics literature. We identify the interface between then normal and super-conducting regimes with the classical normal-to-superconducting transition layer in Ginzburg-Landau theory. The theorem of Chapman-Howison-McLeod-Ockendon '91 proves its existence, uniqueness and monotonicity. The connection of the Gaussian tail of the scalar field on the left with the interface is made directly with the Weber equation. The core Higgs equation is treated as a semiclassical Sturm-Liouville problem in the left overlap region, where the Higgs field decays like a Gaussian. The exterior region is described perturbatively around modified Bessel functions. A key step is to show that the matching map between the admissible Cauchy data at the two ends of the linearized interface equations has maximal rank.

Conclusion: 奇异强子的内部组态可能依赖于能量尺度：低能下呈奇异结构，高能下则趋于普通夸克组态。该新观点需通过未来实验检验，尤其是考察高能强子反应中的能量或动量依赖性（energy or momentum dependencies）。

Abstract: High-energy hadron reactions could be appropriate to find exotic evidences in exotic hadron candidates instead of global observables such as masses, spins, parities, and decay widths, because quarks and gluons are explicit degrees of freedom. Looking at these FFs, we should be able to find the exotic nature as the valence-quark distributions reflect the nature of valence constituents as shown in the pion and the proton. Recent accurate measurements of the $f_0$(980) FFs by the Belle collaboration made it possible to find its internal configuration by looking at their second moments and functional forms. Global analysis results of the $f_0$(980) FFs indicate that its internal configuration looks like $s\bar s$, which is different from a tetraquark or $K\bar K$-molecule like configuration suggested from low-energy studies. This fact indicates that the internal configuration looks different depending on the energy. At low energies, it looks like a tetraquark ($K\bar K$ molecule) hadron, but it looks like a $q\bar q$ hadron at high energies. In the similar way, some exotic hadron candidates could become ordinary $q\bar q$ or $qqq$ hadrons at high energies, although they are interpreted as exotic at low energies. This kind of new idea should be tested by future experiments especially by looking at energy or momentum dependencies in high energy hadron reactions.

Moreover, the diphoton invariant-mass distribution exhibits rich and intriguing structures, including a dip-bump structure in the Higgs-threshold region and a mild enhancement near the top-quark pair threshold. These features make the electroweak corrections indispensable for future precision studies of diphoton production at the LHC.

The $22$ TeV $7_M6_D$ benchmark remains in strong tension with IceCube even under our conservative response-loss extrapolation, while the $48$ TeV $9_M8_D$ benchmark also remains above the extrapolated response, although much closer to the sensitivity boundary. Abstract: We investigate the electromagnetic form factors of the $ρ$ meson family within a holographic QCD framework based on Einstein-dilaton gravity with confining backgrounds. Furthermore, we establish a holographic dictionary of the current matrix element. The elastic form factors are in agreement with available lattice QCD results, whereas the transition form factors, not yet determined within lattice QCD, are predictions of the model. For the elastic case, the electric form factor for the $ρ$ meson family exhibits a zero at $q^{2} = 6\, m_{ρ^{n}}^{2}$ and in particular for the ground state $q^{2} = 3.61\,\text{GeV}^{2}$, consistent with light-front and Dyson-Schwinger calculations. We also compute strong couplings between vector meson states and find some approximate selection rules. In the elastic case and low-$q^{2}$ regime, we calculate dimensionless electric radius, magnetic and quadrupole moments and compare with other approaches. In the high-$q^{2}$ regime, the form factors follow the expected scaling behavior, and the model predicts their asymptotic normalization. The results in the high-$q^{2}$ regime lead to superconvergence relations and establish a new sum rule involving masses, decay constants and couplings.

</details>

### [54] [Collective Ion Dynamics from Finite-Volume Fluctuations in Model Explicit-Solvent Electrolytes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20503)
*Jeongmin Kim*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 该文在有限体积（finite-volume）计数框架下，结合精确窗口投影（exact window projection）与惯性独立粒子参考（inertial independent-particle reference），从平衡涨落中推断集体离子扩散（collective ion diffusion），并应用于对称1:1溶剂原初模型（primitive model）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解集体离子输运（collective ion transport）如何从平衡涨落中涌现，是电解质统计力学（electrolyte statistical mechanics）的核心问题。有限体积涨落虽提供了获取该信息的可行途径，但其解释因波数与集体场的混合而变得复杂。

Method: 扩展用于推断集体离子扩散的有限体积计数框架，将精确窗口投影与惯性独立粒子参考相结合，并应用于对称1:1溶剂原初模型。

Result: 弹道（ballistic）与平台（plateau）区间之间离子数涨落呈近乎理想增长，但该表观理想性源于负的结构过量贡献与正的动力学过量贡献之间的相互抵消。耦合的离子数—溶剂弛豫揭示出溶剂关联投影与溶剂正交投影之间存在带符号重分布，且在总响应中被大幅掩盖。推断的集体扩散依赖观测长度与动力学闭合（dynamical closure）：仅测量结构并不能系统改善估计，而耦合场描述可减小高浓度下的偏差。

Conclusion: 上述结果为分析具有离子数、电荷与溶剂涨落相互耦合的更真实电解质提供了基础。

Abstract: Understanding how collective ion transport emerges from equilibrium fluctuations is central to electrolyte statistical mechanics. Finite-volume fluctuations provide an accessible route to this information, but their interpretation is complicated because they mix wave numbers and collective fields. Here, we extend the finite-volume counting framework for inferring collective ion diffusion by combining the exact window projection with an inertial independent-particle reference, and apply it to a symmetric 1:1 solvent primitive model. The growth of ion-number fluctuations between the ballistic and plateau regimes appears nearly ideal, but this apparent ideality results from compensation between negative structural and positive dynamical excess contributions. The coupled ion-number--solvent relaxation further reveals a signed redistribution between solvent-associated and solvent-orthogonal projections that is largely hidden in the total response. The inferred collective diffusion depends on observation length and dynamical closure: measured structure alone does not systematically improve the estimate, whereas the coupled-field description reduces the high-concentration discrepancy. These results provide a basis for analyzing more realistic electrolytes with mutually coupled ion-number, charge, and solvent fluctuations.

</details>


### [55] [Truncated automatic sparse differentiation for machine learning interatomic potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20510)
*Marcel F. Langer,Adrian Hill,Michele Ceriotti*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出利用机器学习原子间势（MLIPs）中相互作用的局域性（locality）导致的稀疏结构，通过自动稀疏微分（ASD）精确计算大体系的完整海森矩阵（Hessian），并进一步用截断 ASD 在几乎不影响观测量预测的前提下获得数量级加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海森矩阵等高阶导数可描述集体运动并直接预测实验可观测量，但对大体系而言通常被认为计算上不可行，因此需要新的计算策略来突破这一瓶颈。

Method: 利用物理体系中相互作用随距离衰减、且多数 MLIPs 通过有限感受野的消息传递（message passing）引入局域性的特点，推导 MLIP 导数的稀疏模式（sparsity pattern），并采用自动稀疏微分（automatic sparse differentiation, ASD）计算导数，同时提出丢弃远距离原子间微小非零海森矩阵元素的截断 ASD。

Result: 对多种基础 MLIPs，ASD 可精确计算大多孔材料（porous materials）的完整海森矩阵，但加速幅度有限；而截断 ASD 通过舍弃远处原子间的微小但非零海森矩阵元，实现数量级加速，且对预测可观测量的影响可忽略。

Conclusion: MLIPs 的局域性所导致的稀疏性与距离衰减结构可被 ASD 有效利用；在海森矩阵计算中，精确 ASD 收益有限，而截断 ASD 是兼顾精度与效率的可行方案。

Abstract: Machine learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) learn the mapping from atomic positions to potential energy. The forces, the negative gradient of this energy, drive molecular dynamics and are readily obtained using automatic differentiation. Higher-order derivatives, most notably the Hessian, describe collective motion and allow the direct prediction of experimental observables, but are considered computationally inaccessible for large systems. We suggest a solution: in physical systems, interactions decay with distance, and most MLIPs build on this locality through message passing up to a finite receptive field. This implies both sparsity of higher-order derivatives and their decay with distance. This structure can be exploited using automatic sparse differentiation (ASD). We explain how to compute the sparsity pattern for MLIP derivatives and demonstrate that, for multiple foundation MLIPs, ASD computes full Hessians of large porous materials exactly, but with modest speedups at best. The larger gains come from truncated ASD: discarding small, but nonzero, Hessian entries between distant atoms yields order-of-magnitude speedups with negligible impact on predicted observables.

</details>

### [56] [Recent benchmarks in the Analysis Grand Challenge and integration with Combine (and HS3)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19753)
*Peter Fackeldey,Massimiliano Galli,Hannah Havel,Alexander Held,Iason Krommydas,Oksana Shadura,Nick Smith*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文介绍分析大挑战（AGC）与 Combine 工具的整合工作，涵盖性能基准测试、在专用基础设施上的运行示例，以及高能物理统计序列化标准（HS3）的集成规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AGC 参考实现依赖现代 Python 包完成从数据访问到统计建模的流程，而 Combine 虽非 PyHEP 生态成员，却支撑了 CMS 近年逾 90% 的发表分析，因此有必要将 Combine 纳入 PyHEP 生态并以 AGC 为范例。长期目标还包括支持 HS3，以实现与语言无关的似然表示及跨框架互换。

Method: 以 AGC 为示例场景，采用 coffea、uproot、awkward-array 等经过性能优化的数据处理包，并尝试将 Combine 集成到专用基础设施 coffea-casa 中运行，同时探索 HS3 在 Combine 中的集成方式。

Result: 展示了数据处理包近期改进带来的性能收益（通过性能基准测试），给出了 Combine 在 coffea-casa 中集成与运行的示例，并提供了 HS3 集成于 Combine 的示例与计划。

Conclusion: 该工作推进了 Combine 与 PyHEP 生态的融合，并通过 HS3 支持为语言的独立似然表示与框架互换奠定基础。

Abstract: The Analysis Grand Challenge (AGC) showcases an example of HEP analysis. Its reference implementation uses modern Python packages to realize the main steps, from data access to statistical model building and fitting. The packages used for data handling and processing (coffea, uproot, awkward-array) have recently undergone a series of performance optimizations. While not being part of the HEP Python (PyHEP) ecosystem, the Combine tool is a pillar of CMS analyses, covering more than 90% of the analyses published in the last few years. As such, it is necessary to have Combine integrated in the PyHEP ecosystem, using the AGC as example. This project also includes, in the long-term, providing support and integration for the High Energy Physics Statistics Serialization Standard (HS3), as a way to have a language-independent way of representing the likelihood and use different frameworks interchangeably. In these proceedings we cover part of the recent work performed on the AGC and Combine, including: performance benchmarks, covering benefits introduced by the recent improvements in the data processing packages; examples of how Combine can be integrated and run in a dedicated infrastructure (coffea-casa); and examples and plans to integrate HS3 in Combine.

</details>


### [57] [Status and Prospects of the HEP Statistical Inference Ecosystem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19761)
*Massimiliano Galli*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 该文总结LHC主要实验（CMS、ATLAS）统计工具（statistical tools）的现状与未来规划，重点介绍ROOT生态下的改进：RooFit自动微分（automatic differentiation）、与JAX等现代库的互操作性（interoperability）以及通过HS3实现的跨框架通信。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统计推断（statistical inference）是高能物理（HEP）分析的关键环节，传统上基于RooFit与RooStats的工具正面临统计模型复杂度剧增（涉及数百个感兴趣参数（parameters of interest）与数千个 nuisance parameters）、大规模似然最小化（likelihood minimization）的可扩展性能需求，以及与机器学习生态等多元工具、硬件和框架互操作的需求。

Method: 该贡献以综述与规划的形式，梳理LHC主要实验（CMS、ATLAS）所用统计工具的状态与未来计划，围绕三条主线展开：ROOT世界的改进（RooFit自动微分）、与现代库（JAX）的互操作性、以及跨框架通信（HS3）。

Result: 摘要未给出定量结果，仅说明所涵盖的改进方向包括RooFit自动微分、与JAX的互操作性以及基于HS3的跨框架通信，作为统计工具演进的组成部分。

Conclusion: 统计工具需在可扩展性、互操作性与跨框架通信方面持续演进，以应对高能物理统计模型日益增长的复杂度；RooFit自动微分、JAX互操作与HS3通信是CMS、ATLAS等实验统计工具未来发展的重要方向。

Abstract: Statistical inference is a crucial part of HEP analyses. Historically based on RooFit and RooStats, the statistical tools used by the experiments are now facing unprecedented challenges, such as the rapidly growing complexity of statistical models (involving hundreds of parameters of interest and thousands of nuisance parameters), the need for scalable performance in large likelihood minimizations, and the demand for interoperability across an increasingly diverse ecosystem of tools, computational hardware, and frameworks and libraries, especially the ones developed and used within the machine learning world. This contribution summarizes status and future plans for the statistical tools used by some of the main LHC experiments (CMS, ATLAS), with a focus on improvements coming from the ROOT world (RooFit automatic differentiation), interoperability with modern libraries (JAX) and communication across frameworks (HS3).

</details>


### [58] [Constraints on the Higgs boson total width from on-shell signal-background interference in the $H\toγγ$ decay channel with $pp$ collisions at $\sqrt{s}=13$ TeV with the ATLAS detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19948)
*ATLAS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 利用 ATLAS 探测器 140 fb⁻¹ 的 13 TeV 质子-质子碰撞数据，通过共振 H→γγ 振幅与连续双光子本底的干涉效应，对希格斯玻色子总衰变宽度 Γ_H 进行直接的在壳（on-shell）测量，得到 95% 置信水平下观测（预期）上限 Γ_H < 307（258）MeV。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 希格斯玻色子总衰变宽度（total decay width）编码其全部衰变模式，任何超出标准模型（Standard Model, SM）的衰变都会使 Γ_H 高于 SM 预言的 4.1 MeV。与依赖耦合假设的间接离壳（off-shell）约束不同，本文旨在提供不依赖希格斯耦合假设的直接测量，以探测可能的新粒子衰变。

Method: 基于共振 H→γγ 振幅与连续双光子本底之间的干涉（interference），该干涉会以依赖 Γ_H 的方式畸变双光子不变质量分布（diphoton invariant mass distribution）；由于此方法不需对希格斯玻色子耦合作假设，可对 Γ_H 进行直接的在壳测量。分析使用 LHC 运行 2 期间 ATLAS 探测器记录的 140 fb⁻¹、√s = 13 TeV 质子-质子碰撞数据。

Result: 在 95% 置信水平（confidence level）下，给出观测（预期）上限 Γ_H < 307（258）MeV，约为 SM 预言的 75（63）倍。

Conclusion: 该测量不依赖关于希格斯玻色子耦合的假设，不同于对 Γ_H 的间接离壳约束，提供了一种对希格斯玻色子潜在衰变为新粒子敏感的、新颖且互补的探测手段。

Abstract: The total decay width of the Higgs boson, $Γ_H$, encodes all of its decay modes; any decay into particles beyond the Standard Model (SM) would increase $Γ_H$ above the SM prediction of 4.1 MeV. In this Letter, a direct, on-shell measurement of $Γ_H$ is reported, exploiting the interference between the resonant $H \to γγ$ amplitude and the continuum diphoton background, which distorts the diphoton invariant mass distribution in a way that depends on $Γ_H$ without requiring assumptions on the Higgs boson couplings. Using 140 fb$^{-1}$ of proton-proton collision data at $\sqrt{s}=13$ TeV recorded by the ATLAS detector during Run 2 of the LHC, an observed (expected) upper limit of $Γ_H < 307$ (258) MeV is set at 95% confidence level, approximately 75 (63) times the SM prediction. Unlike indirect off-shell constraints on $Γ_H$, this measurement does not rely on assumptions about the Higgs boson couplings, providing a novel complementary probe sensitive to potential Higgs boson decays into new particles.

</details>

Abstract: This white paper presents the current status of the ecosystem of statistical tools used in High Energy Physics (HEP), and attempts to summarize the views on the future R&D directions. These views have been collected during the latest IRIS-HEP statistical ecosystem blueprint, which took place in February 2026.

### [60] [Coverage Is Not Ordering: Ancillary Leakage and Representation Dependence in Covariance-Based Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20007)
*Tommaso Dorigo*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 协方差矩阵（covariance matrix）作为统计模型的紧凑替代会改变似然比（likelihood-ratio）排序。作者以可解的相关测量模型量化该效应，发现小不确定度下排序差异为一阶，而覆盖率缺陷为二阶，并在10%相对不确定度下约7.6%实验的置信决策发生改变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协方差矩阵常被用作测量统计模型的紧凑替代（compact surrogate），但该替代可能不仅改变拟合值或其不确定度，还可能改变实验本身的似然比排序。作者试图量化这一被忽视的推断后果，并将Peelle's Pertinent Puzzle与辅助量（ancillarity）及相关子集（relevant subsets）文献联系起来。

Method: 在可解的相关测量模型（correlated-measurement model）中，将数据分解为信息成分与辅助残差分歧（ancillary residual disagreement）。通过对等校准接受域（equally calibrated acceptance regions）对称差的概率质量来量化排序差异，并分析小不确定度区域下的一阶与二阶行为，比较数据依赖协方差矩阵与非线性变换后的高斯重构。

Result: 精确似然推断不使用辅助分歧，而数据依赖协方差矩阵与高斯重构可将其重新引入参数推断。小不确定度下排序差异一般为一阶，而常规与辅助条件化覆盖率缺陷从二阶开始；在覆盖率盲点（coverage-blind points）处覆盖率差异主导项消失但排序差异仍非零。代表性盲点情形（10%总相对不确定度）中约7.6%实验改变置信决策，且两过程均精确校准。

Conclusion: 协方差近似可为在真实模型中本属辅助（ancillary）的拟合优度统计量制造推断相关性（inferential relevance），从而改变同一信息内容所报告的置信区间。分析可推广至非等统计不确定度及多于两个测量的情形。

Abstract: A covariance matrix is often used as a compact surrogate for the statistical model of a measurement. We show that this replacement can change not only a fitted value or its uncertainty, but also the likelihood-ratio ordering of the experiment itself. In a tractable correlated-measurement model, the data separate into an informative component and an ancillary residual disagreement. Exact likelihood inference therefore does not use that disagreement, whereas data-dependent covariance matrices and Gaussian reconstructions after nonlinear transformations can reintroduce it into parameter inference.
  We quantify the resulting change by the probability mass of the symmetric difference of equally calibrated acceptance regions - the fraction of repeated experiments for which the confidence decision about a tested parameter value changes. In the small-uncertainty regime the ordering discrepancy is generically first order in the total relative uncertainty, whereas conventional and ancillary-conditioned coverage defects begin at second order. At 'coverage-blind' points the leading coverage difference vanishes while the ordering discrepancy remains nonzero. In a representative blind case with 10% total relative uncertainty, about 7.6% of experiments change their confidence decision despite exact calibration of both procedures.
  The same mechanism connects Peelle's Pertinent Puzzle to the classical literature on ancillarity and relevant subsets: a covariance approximation can manufacture inferential relevance for a goodness-of-fit statistic that is ancillary in the true model. We show directly how this changes the confidence interval reported for the same informative content, and extend the analysis beyond equal statistical uncertainties and beyond the two-measurement case.

</details>


### [61] [A heterogeneous and vectorized sequence for the HL-LHC full tracking reconstruction of the CMS experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20135)
*Emmanouil Vourliotis*

Main category: hep-ex

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高亮度大型强子对撞机（HL-LHC）预期每个束团交叉（bunch crossing）中同时碰撞数量空前庞大，带来前所未有的计算挑战，需要新的径迹重建策略。

Method: 该径迹重建序列（tracking sequence）结合了多种前沿算法：针对 GPU 并行执行优化的 Patatrack 与 LST，以及面向高效 CPU 性能向量化的 mkFit。

Result: 该组合方案降低了计算资源需求，并通过引入移位径迹（displaced tracking）与更多利用机器学习技术提升了物理覆盖范围（physics reach）。

Conclusion: 所提出的 GPU 与 CPU 优化算法组合，为应对 HL-LHC 的计算挑战提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: This contribution presents the new baseline strategy for the Phase-2 tracking of the CMS experiment for online event reconstruction, and for the main iteration of offline tracking. This tracking sequence takes advantage of the combination of cutting-edge tracking algorithms that are either optimized for parallel execution on GPUs (Patatrack and LST), or vectorized for efficient CPU performance (mkFit). Such a combined approach offers an effective solution to deal with the unprecedented computational challenges caused by the large number of simultaneous collisions per bunch crossing expected at the High-Luminosity Large Hadron Collider (HL-LHC). The proposed combination not only reduces the computational resource requirements but also enhances the physics reach by incorporating displaced tracking and increasingly leveraging machine learning techniques.

</details>

Zhou,S. Zhou,X. Zhou,X. K. Zhou,X. R. Zhou,X. Y. Zhou,Y. X. Zhou,Y. Z. Zhou,A. N. Zhu,J. Zhu,K. Zhu,K. J. Zhu,K. S. Zhu,L. X. Zhu,Lin Zhu,S. H. Zhu,T. J. Zhu,W. D. Zhu,W. J. Zhu,W. Z. Zhu,Y. C. Zhu,Z. A. Zhu,X. Y. Zhuang,M. Zhuge,J. H. Zou,J. Zu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 基于 BESIII 实验在 \(\sqrt{s}=3.08~\textrm{GeV}\) 的 \(e^+e^-\) 数据，通过测量与奇异夸克（\(s\) quark）或 \(s\bar{s}\) 对伴随产生的瞬发 \(\phi\) 介子，观测到显著的双 \(s\bar{s}\) 产生，其与单 \(\phi\) 产生的截面比约为 40%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双夸克对产生（double quark-pair production）的理论计算预测速率偏低，而已有 Belle 与 BABAR 合作组报告的双 \(c\bar{c}\) 产生水平高于理论预期，因此需要在 \(s\bar{s}\) 道进行实验检验以理解夸克强子化（quark hadronization）与量子色动力学（QCD）。

Method: 利用 BESIII 实验采集的 \(e^+e^-\) 对撞数据，测量与含 \(s\) 夸克或 \(s\bar{s}\) 对的强子伴随产生的瞬发 \(\phi\) 介子，覆盖末态 \(X(s\bar{s})\) 包括 \(\eta\)、\(\eta^{\prime}\) 介子以及 \(K^+K^-\)、\(K^+K^{*-}\)、\(K^-K^{*+}\)、\(K^0\bar{K}^{0}\)、\(K^0\bar{K}^{*0}+\textrm{c.c.}\) 等奇异介子对。

Result: 测得截面比 \(\sigma(e^+e^- \to \phi s\bar{s}+\textrm{anything}) / \sigma(e^+e^-\rightarrow\phi+\textrm{anything}) = (40.4\pm1.7_{\rm stat.}\pm1.5_{\rm syst.})\%\)，其双 \(s\bar{s}\) 产生水平与 Belle 及 BABAR 报告的双 \(c\bar{c}\) 产生水平相当。

Conclusion: BESIII 对双 \(s\bar{s}\) 产生的实验测量可为理解夸克强子化与 QCD 提供参考，并提示该过程的发生率与双 \(c\bar{c}\) 情况类似、均高于部分理论计算预期。

Abstract: We report the observation of significant double-$s\bar{s}$ production in the $e^+e^-$ continuum, based on the measurement of prompt $φ$ mesons produced in association with hadrons containing an $s$ quark or an $s\bar{s}$ pair. In an analysis of $e^+e^-$ collision data collected by the BESIII experiment at $\sqrt{s}=3.08~\textrm{GeV}$, the ratio $σ(e^+e^- \to φ s\bar{s}+\textrm{anything}) / σ(e^+e^-\rightarrowφ+\textrm{anything})$ is determined to be $(40.4\pm1.7_{\rm stat.}\pm1.5_{\rm syst.})\%$ by detecting and measuring $e^+e^-\toφ+ X(s\bar{s})$, where $X(s\bar{s})$ denotes an $η$ meson, an $η^{\prime}$ meson, or one of the strange-meson pairs $K^+K^-$, $K^+K^{*-}$, $K^-K^{*+}$, $K^0\bar{K}^{0}$, and $K^0\bar{K}^{*0}+\textrm{c.c.}$. The level of double-$s\bar{s}$ production is in line with the double-$c\bar{c}$ production reported by the Belle and \babar\ collaborations, for which theoretical calculations predict lower rates. The experimental measurement of double $s\bar{s}$ production at BESIII can shed light on the understanding of quark hadronization and QCD.

</details>

# cond-mat.soft [[Back]](#toc)

### [63] [Viscoelastocapillary extensional De-Oh or VE-DeOh stringiness of unentangled polymer solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19624)
*Louis B. Edano,Vivek Sharma*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究针对聚合物溶液"拉丝性（stringiness）"缺乏定量描述的问题，通过液滴落于基底（DoS）与液滴落入空气（DiA）流变测量，建立以拉伸松弛时间和稳态拉伸黏度为基础的无量纲图谱框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拉丝性（stringiness）的表征、控制与标定对聚合物溶液在滴加、喷射、喷涂、涂布等工艺以及纤维、膜、喷雾干燥产品的设计至关重要，但现有研究缺乏定量图谱、公式或标度。牛顿流体中剪切黏度与拉丝性关系直观，而剪切黏度相近的聚合物溶液在拉伸流变（extensional rheology）与表观拉丝性上差异显著，这构成了本研究的动机。

Method: 研究对比了一系列水相聚合物溶液的拉丝性，采用滴落于基底（dripping-onto-substrate, DoS）流变仪测定丝线寿命（filament lifespan），并通过液滴落入空气（dripping-into-air, DiA）测定丝线长度与寿命。同时考察聚合物化学结构与分子量对拉丝性的影响，并借助 DoS 流变仪表征夹断动力学（pinching dynamics）与拉伸流变响应。

Result: 拉丝性的增加分别与稳态终端拉伸黏度（steady, terminal extensional viscosity）及拉伸松弛时间（extensional relaxation time）呈正相关。

Conclusion: 研究提出一个通过拉伸 Ohnesorge-Deborah 图（$Oh-De$ plot），即 $Oh_E-De_E$ 图谱，比较不同聚合物溶液拉丝性的框架；其中 $Oh_E$ 由稳态终端拉伸黏度计算，$De_E$ 由拉伸松弛时间计算。

Abstract: Characterization, control, and calibration of stringiness, the propensity to form long, thin, persistent threads, are key to the design and application of polymer solutions and formulations transferred to substrates by dropwise dispensing, jetting, spraying, or coating, and are used in manufacturing fibers, membranes, or spray-dried products. For Newtonian fluids, enhancing shear viscosity translates into increasing stringiness as can be easily perceived during dripping of water vs. sugar syrups or glycerol-water mixtures. In contrast, even polymer solutions with comparable shear viscosity can display a significant contrast in extensional rheology and apparent stringiness. However, there are no quantitative maps, formulas, or scales for stringiness, which motivates this study. In this contribution, we contrast the stringiness for a series of aqueous polymer solutions by determining the filament lifespan using dripping-onto-substrate (DoS) rheometry and filament length and lifespan in using dripping-into-air (DiA). We investigate the influence of polymer chemistry and molecular weight on stringiness and rely on DoS rheometry to characterize pinching dynamics and extensional rheology response. We show that an increase in stringiness correlates with steady, terminal extensional viscosity, and extensional relaxation time, respectively. Lastly, we provide a framework, which compares the stringiness of different polymer solutions through an extensional Ohnesorge-Deborah ($Oh-De$) plot or $Oh_E-De_E$ map, by computing the two dimensionless groups $Oh_E$ using steady, terminal extensional viscosity and $De_E$ using extensional relaxation time, respectively

</details>


### [64] [Sensitivity of Nucleation Thermodynamics and Kinetics to the Treatment of Long-Range Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19646)
*Fernanda Sulantay Vargas,Kimia Sinaeian,Amir Haji-Akbari*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该工作以 Lennard-Jones（LJ）体系为基准，结合分子动力学（MD）、jumpy forward-flux sampling（jFFS）与自由能计算，量化势能截断对熔化热力学、均相晶体成核动力学及计算成本的影响，发现截断半径在 2.5σ–6σ 内成核速率变化约十个数量级，并提出基于经典成核理论（CNT）的外推与截断选择框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成核速率对热力学驱动力呈指数敏感，因而可能强烈依赖长程分子间相互作用的处理方式。现有研究缺少对截断方案如何影响成核动力学与热力学的系统性量化，且截断方案常未被作为力场开发的必要环节加以验证。

Method: 以 Lennard-Jones（LJ）体系为基准，耦合分子动力学（MD）模拟、jumpy forward-flux sampling（jFFS）与自由能计算；在 2.5σ ≤ r_c ≤ 6σ 的截断半径范围内，考察零压熔化温度、均相晶体成核速率及计算成本；借助经典成核理论（CNT）分析速率敏感性的来源，构建有限截断速率向全势极限（full-potential limit）外推的框架。

Result: 在 2.5σ ≤ r_c ≤ 6σ 范围内，零压熔化温度变化约 11%，而成核速率变化约十个数量级；CNT 分析表明显著的动力学敏感性主要源于截断引起的液–晶相化学势差变化。传统均相尾部校正（tail corrections）无法可靠替代，因其无法一致地描述成核过程中液相、晶相与界面环境。按“在可接受速率偏差内最小化计算成本”的标准，在 kT/ε = 0.5 时 r_c = 4σ 为合理折中。

Conclusion: 研究强调在成核及非均匀环境中间界面相变的模拟中，截断方案应作为力场开发的组成部分被明确指定并验证；所提 CNT 框架可用于有限截断速率的外推及在其他截断半径与温度下速率偏差的估计，并为截断半径选择提供系统依据。

Abstract: Nucleation rates are exponentially sensitive to the thermodynamic driving force and can therefore depend strongly on the treatment of long-range intermolecular interactions. Here, using the Lennard--Jones (LJ) system as a benchmark, we combine molecular dynamics (MD) simulations, jumpy forward-flux sampling (jFFS), and free-energy calculations to quantify the effect of potential truncation on melting thermodynamics, homogeneous crystal nucleation kinetics, and computational cost. Within the cutoff-radius range $2.5σ\le r_c\le 6σ$, the melting temperature at zero pressure varies by approximately 11%, while the nucleation rate changes by approximately ten orders of magnitude. By invoking classical nucleation theory (CNT), we show that this pronounced kinetic sensitivity originates primarily from cutoff-induced changes in the chemical potential difference between the liquid and crystalline phases. Building on this observation, we develop a CNT-based framework for extrapolating finite-cutoff rates to the full-potential limit and for estimating the expected rate deviations at other cutoff radii and temperatures. These findings also provide a systematic basis for cutoff selection: the optimal cutoff should minimize computational cost while keeping the deviation from the full-potential rate within acceptable bounds. At $kT/ε=0.5$, $r_c=4σ$ provides a reasonable compromise according to these criteria. We further demonstrate that conventional homogeneous tail corrections do not offer a reliable alternative, as they cannot consistently account for the liquid, crystalline, and interfacial environments present during nucleation. Our findings highlight the need to specify and validate the truncation scheme as an integral component of force-field development in simulations of nucleation and other interfacial phase transitions within inhomogeneous environments.

</details>


### [65] [Spontaneous filament formation and network self-assembly via active phase separation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19800)
*Elena Lucas,Varun Venkatesh,Amin Doostmohammadi*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 提出 Active Model H$^{-}$，一个描述过阻尼流体力学极限下非平衡相分离的标量相场模型，可自发形成具增强连接与停滞粗化的丝状网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有标量活性场论（scalar active field theories）未观察到此类丝状网络形态，论文旨在引入新模型以刻画这种非平衡相分离形态。

Method: 在过阻尼流体力学极限（overdamped hydrodynamic limit）下构建标量相场模型 Active Model H$^{-}$，并分析孤立丝的自发弯曲、曲率驱动的自推进（self-propulsion）与网络组装，以及在噪声下的动力学。

Result: 模型产生具增强连接与停滞粗化（arrested coarsening）的丝状网络；孤立丝不稳定于自发弯曲，曲率驱动其朝凸侧自推进，导致碰撞并组装成渗流网络（percolating network）；网络局部非平衡，具有异常弯曲的边、无序顶点角与异质拓扑荷（topological charge），宏观上仍恢复 Lewis 定律；噪声下呈现平衡的断裂—重组动力学。

Conclusion: Active Model H$^{-}$ 给出了一种既有标量活性场论未见的丝状网络形态，其特征为局部非平衡但宏观遵循 Lewis 定律，并在噪声下维持断裂与重组的平衡动力学。

Abstract: We introduce Active Model H$^{-}$, a scalar phase-field model of non-equilibrium phase separation in the overdamped hydrodynamic limit, and show that it produces filamentous networks with enhanced connections and arrested coarsening, a morphology not observed in existing scalar active field theories. Isolated filaments are unstable to spontaneous bending, and the resulting curvature drives self-propulsion towards the convex side, causing them to collide and assemble into a percolating network. The networks are locally non-equilibrium, with anomalously curved edges, disordered vertex angles and heterogeneous topological charge, yet recover Lewis's law macroscopically. Under noise they show balanced break and reform dynamics.

</details>


### [66] [The hypoexponential form for the fiber-length distribution in thermoplastic composites revisited: finite initial length, cascade fracture, and its mechanistic status](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20312)
*Yuichi Masubuchi*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文在前期提出的亚指数（hypoexponential/Erlang型）纤维长度分布函数基础上，引入有限初始长度 L₀ 并分析屈曲诱导断裂的力学机制；结果表明该闭式解可与玻纤数据比对、回溯性地验证无限长理想化的适用性，而 Mellin 变换分析指出特征长度源于破缺标度的因素，蒙特卡洛模拟则显示该分布宜作两参数拟合模型而非精确生成分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前期工作（Masubuchi et al., 2016）基于断裂与相邻断裂阻塞两个独立泊松过程，提出亚指数型纤维长度分布函数。本文旨在进一步评估该形式的有效性：既考察有限初始长度 L₀ 的实际影响，也厘清屈曲诱导纤维断裂过程背后的力学机制基础。

Method: 推导引入有限初始长度 L₀ 的闭式解（closed-form solution），并将其与玻璃纤维（glass fiber）数据集比对。对碎裂方程（fragmentation equation）进行 Mellin 变换（Mellin transform）分析以考察标度行为；并对唯象的有限机会级联（finite-opportunity cascade）进行蒙特卡洛（Monte Carlo）模拟。

Result: 有限 L₀ 闭式解与玻纤数据比对，为早期工作在此类体系中采用无限长理想化提供了回溯性依据，同时给出碎片尺寸与 L₀ 可比时的完整形式。Mellin 分析表明，标度不变的级联（scale-invariant cascades）产生幂律（power laws），无法生成特征长度；特征长度只能来自破缺标度的成分，尤其是截断（cutoff）区域与中止的加工历史（arrested processing history）。蒙特卡洛模拟显示，亚指数形式未能再现生成分布的峰值，但能捕捉其类指数尾部。

Conclusion: 亚指数形式是一种有用的两参数拟合模型（two-parameter fitting model），但并非精确的生成分布（generative distribution）；纤维碎片特征长度的出现需归因于破缺标度的机制成分。

Abstract: In a previous work [Masubuchi et al., Compos. Sci. Technol. 134, 43 (2016)], a fiber-length distribution function was proposed as the hypoexponential, or Erlang-type, convolution of two exponential waiting lengths, motivated by two independent Poisson processes for breakage and for blocking of adjacent breaks. The present paper further evaluates this hypoexponential form by introducing a finite initial length L_{0}, and by analyzing the mechanistic basis of the buckling-induced fiber breakage process. The obtained finite-L_{0} closed-form solution was compared to a glass fiber dataset to retroactively justify the infinite-length idealization in the earlier work for such systems while providing the complete form for processes in which fragments remain comparable to L_{0}. Concerning the mechanics of fiber fragmentation, a Mellin transform analysis of the fragmentation equation showed that scale-invariant cascades yield power laws and therefore cannot generate characteristic lengths; those lengths must instead arise from scale-breaking ingredients, particularly the cutoff region and the arrested processing history. Monte Carlo simulations of a phenomenological finite-opportunity cascade showed that the hypoexponential form does not reproduce the peak of the generated distribution but captures its exponential-like tail, implying that it serves as a useful two-parameter fitting model, although not an exact generative distribution.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化活性Janus颗粒之间的相互作用，是描述其集体行为（collective behaviour）必须解决但尚未解决的难题；同时，理解活性颗粒间随距离变化的相互作用（distance-dependent interaction），对于将其作为具有动态性质先进材料的构建单元至关重要。

Method: 受两颗活性Janus颗粒分别被独立光镊约束的光子力显微镜装置启发，推导每个颗粒的稳态概率分布（steady-state probability distribution），该分布携带配对趋泳相互作用的特征，并可推广至更广泛的相互作用类型；由此从位置构型（positional configurations）推断相互作用力与力矩，而无需任何颗粒取向（particle orientations）信息。

Result: 所得稳态概率分布包含配对趋泳相互作用的特征信息，且该框架可推广至更广泛的相互作用；据此可从位置构型中推断相互作用力与力矩，无需预先知道颗粒取向。

Conclusion: 该工作提出了一种无需取向信息的活性颗粒相互作用推断方法，并强调揭示活性颗粒间随距离变化的相互作用，是将其用作具有动态特性先进材料构建单元的关键。

Abstract: Quantifying the interactions between active Janus particles remains a challenge that needs to be addressed to describe their collective behaviour. Inspired by a photonic force microscopy setup where two active Janus particles are confined in separate optical tweezers, we derive the steady-state probability distribution of each particle bearing signatures of the pair phoretic interaction, which can be generalised to a broader range of interactions. This approach allows us to infer interaction forces and torques from positional configurations without any knowledge of the particle orientations. Unraveling the distance-dependent interaction between active particles is essential to harnessing them as building blocks for advanced materials with dynamic properties.

</details>


### [68] [Active Hydrodynamics Couples Polymer Organization, Shape Fluctuations, and Motility in Deformable Droplets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20730)
*Ritu Raj,P. B. Sunil Kumar*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究采用耗散粒子动力学（DPD）模拟，考察软液滴内受限半柔性活性聚合物（semiflexible active polymers）的集体行为。拉伸型与收缩型活性产生不同的流场与界面耦合方式，进而决定内部有序结构、界面涨落模式及液滴自驱动运动（motility）的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探究活性水力学（active hydrodynamics）如何在受限活性物质系统中同时支配内部结构、可变形界面涨落与涌现运动之间的耦合关系。软液滴提供了可变形边界，使活性流场与界面模式之间产生选择性相互作用，这一耦合此前缺乏系统刻画。

Method: 方法为耗散粒子动力学（dissipative particle dynamics, DPD）模拟：将半柔性活性聚合物约束于悬浮在流体中的软可变形液滴内，沿聚合物骨架施加拉伸型（extensile）与收缩型（contractile）力偶极子，比较其产生的流场、有效相互作用、界面模式激发与液滴动力学。

Result: 拉伸型活性促进聚合物平行排列与侧向吸引，收缩型活性则倾向垂直组织，导致定性不同的集体行为。活性流场与可变形界面强耦合，选择性增强与长波液滴形变相关的低阶球谐（spherical-harmonic）模式，并驱动界面弛豫偏离被动毛细行为，且两类液滴呈模式依赖的动力学。拉伸型液滴随聚合物数量增加运动愈发持续，收缩型液滴则可维持长寿命近弹道运动，其时程敏感依赖于聚合物数量与活性强度。

Conclusion: 结论指出，活性水力学决定了受限活性物质系统中内部结构、界面涨落与涌现运动之间的相互作用方式，并揭示了拉伸型与收缩型活性在液滴内部组织与迁移行为上的本质差异。

Abstract: We study semiflexible active polymers confined within a soft, deformable droplet suspended in a fluid using dissipative particle dynamics (DPD) simulations. Extensile and contractile force dipoles along the polymer backbone generate distinct hydrodynamic flow fields that mediate effective interactions between polymer segments. Extensile activity promotes parallel alignment and lateral attraction, whereas contractile activity favors predominantly perpendicular organization, leading to qualitatively different collective behavior. The activity-generated flows couple strongly to the deformable interface, selectively enhancing low-order spherical-harmonic modes associated with long-wavelength droplet deformations. Activity also drives the interfacial relaxation away from passive capillary behavior, with extensile and contractile droplets exhibiting distinct mode-dependent dynamics. These differences in internal organization and interfacial fluctuations strongly influence droplet motility. Extensile activity produces increasingly persistent droplet motion with increasing polymer number, whereas contractile activity can sustain long-lived near-ballistic motion whose duration depends sensitively on polymer number and activity strength. Our results demonstrate how active hydrodynamics governs the interplay between internal structure, interfacial fluctuations, and emergent motility in confined active matter systems.

</details>


### [69] [Scaling and Condensation of Dry Active Matter Around Circular Obstacles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20743)

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究通过数值模拟考察干活性布朗粒子（dry active Brownian particles）在固定圆形障碍物周围形成的自持涡旋（self-sustained vortex）的有限尺度标度行为，识别出低密度与高密度两种截然不同的标度区域（scaling regime）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 圆形障碍物周围的自持涡旋可作为粒子聚集的局部成核位点（nucleation site），其动力学性质已被部分刻画，但热力学极限（thermodynamic limit）下的行为尚不明确，因此需考察涡旋质量与空间尺度如何随系统尺寸变化。

Method: 采用排斥性活性布朗粒子与固定圆形障碍物相互作用的数值模拟（numerical simulation），测量涡旋质量、平均半径与最大半径，并将其表示为全局面积分数（global area fraction）、障碍物尺寸与系统尺寸的函数。

Result: 发现两个不同的标度区域：低密度下涡旋保持局域化，其特征量随系统尺寸增大而饱和；超过临界密度后，涡旋质量随总粒子数广延增长（grows extensively），空间尺度则与系统尺寸成线性关系。

Conclusion: 高密度下涡旋的标度行为表明出现了一种由障碍物稳定化的凝聚态（obstacle-stabilized condensed state），为障碍物诱导的活性物质聚集在热力学极限下的行为提供了证据。

Abstract: Active Brownian particles confined to rigid substrates are known to accumulate near rigid boundaries and, under suitable conditions, undergo motility-induced phase separation (MIPS). A particularly intriguing manifestation of this behavior is the formation of self-sustained vortices around circular obstacles, which act as localized nucleation sites for particle aggregation. While several dynamical properties of such vortices have been previously characterized, their behavior in the thermodynamic limit remains largely unexplored. Here, we investigate how the mass and spatial extent of a dry active-matter vortex scale with system size. Using numerical simulations of repulsive active Brownian Particles interacting with a fixed circular obstacle, we measure the vortex mass, mean radius, and maximum radius as functions of the global area fraction, obstacle size, and system size. We find two distinct scaling regimes. At low densities, the vortex remains localized and its characteristic properties saturate as the system size increases. Above a critical density, however, the vortex mass grows extensively with the total number of particles, while its spatial dimensions scale linearly with the system size, indicating the emergence of an obstacle-stabilized condensed state.

</details>


### [70] [Competing routes to spontaneous flow in confined active nematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20786)
*Rahil N. Valani,Vedad Dzanic,Sumesh P. Thampi,Julia M. Yeomans*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该文通过解析分析表明，受限几何中活性向列相（active nematics）自发流动的起始由两种竞争性不稳定性（instability）主导：长波长模式导致单向流动，有限波长不稳定性产生横向卷筒（transverse rolls）；并确定有限波长不稳定性临界活性可低于长波长模式，从而在单向流动之前出现涡旋（vortex）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 活性向列相在受限几何中超过临界活性（critical activity）后会自发产生流动，但其流动起始的模式选择机制尚需澄清。该研究旨在揭示主导流动起始的竞争性不稳定性及其控制参数。

Method: 采用解析方法，识别出两种竞争性不稳定性：长波长模式（long-wavelength mode）与有限波长不稳定性（finite-wavelength instability）。通过约化描述（reduced description）分析活性、流动取向（flow alignment）与向列弹性（nematic elasticity）对二者竞争的控制作用。

Result: 解析结果显示，长波长模式导致单向流动（unidirectional flow），有限波长不稳定性产生横向卷筒（transverse rolls）。在特定参数区间内，有限波长不稳定性的临界活性低于长波长不稳定性，使涡旋（vortices）先于单向流动出现。

Conclusion: 该研究确立波长选择（wavelength selection）为活性向列相自发流动起始的内在特征，并表明流动起始模式可由活性、流动取向与向列弹性的竞争关系决定。

Abstract: Active nematics can spontaneously develop flow beyond a critical activity in confined geometries. We show analytically that the onset of flow is governed by two competing instabilities: a long-wavelength mode leading to unidirectional flow and a finite-wavelength instability producing transverse rolls. A reduced description reveals how activity, flow alignment, and nematic elasticity control the competition between these modes. We identify regimes in which the finite-wavelength instability has a lower critical activity than the long-wavelength instability, causing vortices to emerge before unidirectional flow. This establishes wavelength selection as an intrinsic feature of the onset of spontaneous flow in active nematics.

</details>

### [71] [Proposing, never assigning: fair allocation of control-room shifts in experimental particle physics collaborations from ranked wishes under institutional quotas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19658)
*Kamal Benslama*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于实验粒子物理（experimental particle physics）合作组排班（shift staffing）的席位分配方法：以机构配额（institutional quota）与积分制（credit system）为基础，将席位按排名意愿（ranked wishes）分配并作为"要约（offers）"交由个人接受或拒绝，同时由独立校验器（independent checker）在发布前重算全部规则。合成合作组（48 机构、256 名合格成员）测试显示每周期 72 席中可分配 70–72 席，平均排名满意度 84–95%，且无规则被违反。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有做法是自报名（self-sign-up）的积分制：机构欠一定积分额度，席位开放时由个人自行抢订。该模式在公平性可见度、不可分割时段（indivisible blocks）对小型机构的公平性以及平台可信度方面存在不足，论文因此寻求一种"平台只提议、不指派"且可验证的替代方案。

Method: 席位从排名意愿出发、在机构配额下分配，结果以个人可接受或拒绝的要约形式发布，发布前由独立检查器（independent checker）重算每条规则。核心之外新增一条配额规则，使不可分割时段对份额不足一个时段的机构保持公平；并增加四条规则提升公平性的可见度：猫头鹰班（owl）、周末与假日时段的份额，同时以实验室时钟与机构自身时钟显示席位，为无法出行的机构提供可及池（reachable pools），以及按余额决定预订顺序（booking order by balance）。

Result: 在 48 个机构、256 名合格成员的合成合作组（synthetic collaboration）上，方法在两轮内每周期分配 72 席中的 70 至 72 席，平均排名满意度为 84% 至 95%，无任何规则被违反。运行一年后，四名成员及以上的机构均处于其累计份额（cumulative share）的 13% 以内；最小的机构（一个时段即因算术原因超出其份额）通过两两合并（pooling in pairs）被纳入同一区间。

Conclusion: 该方法表述完整到足以被重新实现，已有参考实现（reference implementation）并可在许可下供合作组使用。其适用对象为任何以时段块（blocks of shifts）在机构配额下为设施配备人员的科学合作组。

Abstract: Experimental particle physics collaborations staff their detectors around the clock with members drawn from dozens of institutions. The established practice is a credit system with self-sign-up: institutions owe a quota of credit, and people take the seats they want when booking opens. This paper describes a method in which seats are instead allocated from ranked wishes under institutional quotas, the result is issued as offers that each person accepts or declines, and an independent checker recomputes every rule before anything is published: the platform proposes, never assigns, and proves that it kept the rules. The credit and quota model is inherited from current practice. Around that core the paper adds a quota rule that keeps indivisible blocks fair to institutions whose share of a period is smaller than one block, and four rules that make fairness visible beyond the count: shares of owl, weekend and holiday blocks, seats shown in the laboratory's clock and the institution's own, reachable pools for institutions that cannot travel, and booking order by balance. On a synthetic collaboration of 48 institutions and 256 eligible members, the method allocates 70 to 72 of 72 seats per period in two rounds, with a mean rank satisfaction of 84 to 95% and no rule broken; after a year of running every institution of four members or more is within 13% of its cumulative share, and the smallest institutions, which one block overshoots by arithmetic, are brought within the same range by pooling in pairs. The method is stated completely enough to be reimplemented; a reference implementation exists and is available to collaborations under license. It applies as it stands to any scientific collaboration that staffs a facility in blocks of shifts under institutional quotas.

</details>


### [72] [Design and characterization of large-size monolithic layers of 3D-segmented plastic scintillator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19684)
*Jon Berisha,Xingyu Zhao,Andrey Boyarintsev,Sebastien Cap,Diogo Di Calafiori,Maksim Kiktev,Umut Kose,Anzhelika Kuzmina,Natalia Leskina,André Rubbia,Tetiana Sibilieva,Tim Weber,Davide Sgalaberna*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: 该工作验证了一种可将数千个塑料闪烁体立方体粘接为单一光学隔离整层的新技术，并成功扩展到 48×48 立方体规模，满足未来吨级探测器需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维细粒度塑料闪烁体探测器（plastic scintillator detector）广泛用于高能物理实验，并因成本低而具备扩展至多吨级（multi-tonne）探测器的潜力；但数百万独立微小闪烁体体素（voxel）的生产周期长、装配困难，构成规模化障碍。

Method: 采用将数千个 9×9×9 mm³ 塑料闪烁体立方体，以机械加工精度（machining precision）粘接成单一大型整体层（monolithic layer），同时保证高效光学隔离（optical separation）的技术路线。

Result: 该技术被证明可可靠扩展至由 48×48 个光学隔离闪烁立方体构成的整体层，即未来吨级探测器所需的尺寸。

Conclusion: 该技术方案满足未来中微子探测器（neutrino detector）与采样量能器（sampling calorimeter）的要求。

Abstract: Three-dimensional fine-grained plastic scintillator detectors are extensively used in high-energy physics experiments where particle tracking, identification, calorimetry and sub-nanosecond timing information are required. Moreover, the low cost of plastic scintillator makes, in principle, the scalability to multi-tonne detectors possible. In order to circumvent the difficulties related to the long production time and the challenging assembly of millions of tiny independent scintillating voxels, a new technology consisting of gluing a few thousand $9 \times 9 \times 9$ mm$^3$ plastic scintillator cubes in a single large monolithic layer with machining precision, while ensuring an efficient optical separation, was developed and demonstrated. In this work we demonstrate that such technology can be reliably extended to a monolithic layer of $48 \times 48$ optically-isolated scintillating cubes, the size required for future ton-scale detectors, fulfilling the requirements of future neutrino detectors and sampling calorimeters.

</details>


### [73] [Commissioning and Performance of the Time-of-Flight Detector for the T2K Neutrino Oscillation Experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20117)
*C. Alt,L. Amziane,N. Baudis,A. Blanchet,S. Bordoni,T. H. Bui,F. Cadoux,P. Collard,M. El Baz,Y. Favre,L. Giannessi,G. Ha,C. Jesús-Valls,V. S. Kasturi,A. Klustová,A. Korzenev,T. A. Le,T. Lux,A. D. Nguyen,D. T. Nguyen,H. Nguyen,S. Samani,F. Sánchez,M. Ta,T. Thaiduc,E. Villa*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: T2K ND280升级项目中的飞行时间（Time-of-Flight, ToF）探测器已完成设计、建造、集成、调试并稳定运行，用于降低中微子振荡参数测量的系统不确定度并提升对CP破坏相位δ_CP的灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: T2K ND280升级旨在降低中微子振荡参数（neutrino oscillation parameters）测量的系统不确定度（systematic uncertainties），并提高对电荷-宇称破坏相位（charge-parity (CP)-violating phase）δ_CP的灵敏度。ToF探测器是实现上述目标的关键组件。

Method: ToF探测器由六个面板、118根EJ-200塑料闪烁体条（plastic-scintillator bars）组成，环绕超级精细粒度探测器（Super Fine-Grained Detector, SuperFGD）与两个高角度时间投影室（High-Angle Time Projection Chambers, HA-TPCs）。每根条两端由硅光电倍增管阵列（silicon photomultiplier arrays）读出，并采用SAMPIC波形电子学（waveform electronics）进行数字化。

Result: 单条专用测量达到约130 ps的时间分辨率（time resolution）与2.6 cm的纵向位置分辨率（longitudinal position resolution）。安装于ND280后，宇宙射线刻度（cosmic-ray calibration）给出上下穿越事件的原位单条时间分辨率为169 ± 1 ps；束流数据（beam data）清晰分辨出T2K spill的八束团结构，证实与ND280触发和数据采集系统同步。

Conclusion: ToF探测器提供精确的定时与粒子方向信息，用于粒子鉴别（particle identification）及排除从外部进入径迹探测器的本底（backgrounds）。该探测器已成功完成调试，并在升级后的ND280探测器内稳定运行。

Abstract: The T2K ND280 Upgrade aims to reduce systematic uncertainties in measurements of neutrino oscillation parameters and improve sensitivity to the charge-parity (CP)-violating phase, $δ_{\mathrm{CP}}$. A key component is the Time-of-Flight (ToF) detector, comprising six panels with 118 EJ-200 plastic-scintillator bars surrounding the Super Fine-Grained Detector (SuperFGD) and two High-Angle Time Projection Chambers (HA-TPCs). Each bar is read out at both ends by silicon photomultiplier arrays and digitised using SAMPIC waveform electronics. The ToF provides precise timing and particle-direction information for particle identification and rejection of backgrounds entering the tracker from outside. This article presents the detector design, construction, signal reconstruction, integration, commissioning, and performance. Dedicated single-bar measurements achieve a time resolution of approximately 130 ps and a longitudinal position resolution of 2.6 cm. After installation in ND280, cosmic-ray calibration yields an in situ single-bar time resolution of $169 \pm 1$ ps for Top-Bottom crossing events. Beam data clearly resolve the eight-bunch T2K spill structure, confirming synchronisation with the ND280 trigger and data-acquisition systems. The ToF has been successfully commissioned and operates stably within the upgraded ND280 detector.

</details>
