Theodosopoulos,Yoichi Usui,Pieter Vermeesch,Aaron Vincent,Ronald Walsworth,Jin-Wei Wang,David Waters,Samuel S. Y. Wong,Audrey Wu,Gregory Wurtz,Wen Yin,Xiuyuan Zhang,Zhexian Zhang*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: 本文是 MDvDM'26（第四届"矿物探测中微子与暗物质"会议）的会议论文集摘要，概述了矿物探测器（mineral detectors）这一新兴粒子探测概念在理论、计算与实验方面的进展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矿物探测器概念近年因现代计算与高分辨率显微技术的出现而受到关注；此次会议旨在汇集相关贡献，展示推动该概念实现的最新进展，并开启完整的实验计划。

Method: 利用天然与人造晶体保留核反冲（nuclear recoils）所诱导的微观损伤特征，并通过多种微米与纳米分辨率的显微技术进行读出，包括在实验室时间尺度与地质时间尺度上的应用。

Result: 矿物探测器在实验室尺度可用于反应堆中微子监测与暗物质探测，并有望测量核反冲的方向与能量；古老天然晶体（paleo-detectors）可研究天体物理中微子、宇宙射线、暗物质及重奇异粒子及其在银河系生命周期内的通量变化。

Conclusion: 国际 MDvDM 社群近年来成功应对了实现矿物探测器概念的相关挑战，为完整的实验计划及未来潜在发现开辟了道路。

Abstract: The fourth "Mineral Detection of Neutrinos and Dark Matter" (MDvDM'26) meeting was held April 14-17, 2026 in Karlsruhe, Germany, hosted by the Institute for Astroparticle Physics (IAP) at Karlsruhe Institute of Technology (KIT). These proceedings detail the contributions that were presented during MDvDM'26, illustrating the unprecedented progress in theoretical, computational and experimental studies towards the realization of the concept of mineral detectors. Mineral detectors represent an emerging particle detection concept that has risen in prominence in recent years due to the advent of modern computational and high-resolution microscopy techniques. Natural and synthetic crystals are capable of retaining microscopic damage features induced by nuclear recoils, which could be then read out with a variety of micrometer and nanometer resolution microscopy techniques. On laboratory time scales mineral detectors could be employed for reactor neutrino monitoring and dark matter detection, with the potential to measure the directions as well as the energies of the induced nuclear recoils. Uniquely, ancient natural crystals (so-called paleo-detectors) that have been recording nuclear recoils over geological timescales could be used for studying astrophysical neutrinos, cosmic rays, dark matter and heavy exotic particles, as well as the variation of their fluxes over our Galaxy's lifetime. In recent years the international MDvDM community has been successfully tackling the challenges associated with realizing the concept of mineral detectors, opening the pathway towards a fully fledged experimental program and potential future discoveries.

</details>

### [75] [Classification of commutation relation for multi-radial SLE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19739)
*Chongzhi Huang,Hao Wu*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该文研究局部对易的多重径向 Schramm-Loewner 演化（SLE_κ）所对应的配分函数，证明径向 BPZ 方程解空间维数为 2^n，并分类共形 Ward 恒等式筛选出的子空间维数，进而构造正解基与几何实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局部对易的多重径向 SLE 由满足径向 Belavin-Polyakov-Zamolodchikov（BPZ）方程与含谱参数共形 Ward 恒等式的配分函数编码，但该方程组的解空间结构、可容许谱参数范围及被恒等式选中的子空间维数此前缺乏系统刻画，正解的存在性与全局几何实现亦有待建立。

Method: 在 κ>0、λ∈R 条件下分析径向 BPZ 方程组解空间维数；确定所有可容许的 Ward 参数 ν 及共形 Ward 恒等式所选子空间的精确维数（含一般与非退化情形）；在 0<κ≤4 且特定参数范围内，利用多重 SLE 构造正解基；在 κ=4 时以高斯自由场（Gaussian free field）水平线配分函数给出显式解。

Result: 解空间维数为 2^n（n 为变量个数）；完整给出可容许 ν 与子空间维数分类，并揭示宇称差异：n 为偶数时对任意 λ 均存在非零旋转不变解，n 为奇数时仅有限个例外 λ 值存在；在 0<κ≤4、奇数 n 需 λ>0、偶数 n 需 λ>-3/2 时构造出正解基，实现局部对易 SLE 的整体实现。

Conclusion: 径向 BPZ 系统与共形 Ward 恒等式共同给出局部对易多重径向 SLE 的代数-几何分类框架；正解基将配分函数与多重 SLE 的全局实现相联系，κ=4 情形进一步与高斯自由场水平线及边界数据、内部奇点对应。

Abstract: Locally commuting multiple radial Schramm-Loewner evolutions ($\mathrm{SLE}_κ$) are encoded by partition functions satisfying the radial Belavin-Polyakov-Zamolodchikov (BPZ) equations and a conformal Ward identity with spectral parameters $λ,ν\in\mathbb{R}$. For $κ>0$ and $λ\in\mathbb{R}$, we show that the solution space of the radial BPZ system has dimension $2^n$, where $n$ is the number of variables. We then determine all admissible Ward parameters $ν$ and the exact dimensions of the subspaces selected by the conformal Ward identity, covering both generic and degenerate cases. The classification reveals a parity difference: nonzero rotation-invariant solutions exist for every $λ$ when $n$ is even, but only at finitely many exceptional values when $n$ is odd.
  When $0<κ\leq4$, $λ>0$ for odd $n$ or $λ>-3/2$ for even $n$, we construct a basis of positive solutions using multiple SLE, providing global realizations of the locally commuting SLEs. At $κ=4$, we identify a family of explicit solutions as partition functions for level lines of a Gaussian free field with suitable boundary data and interior singularities.

</details>

<details>

Motivation: 摘要未明确阐述研究动机，仅以“announce”形式说明将报告相关极限形状定理，未提供背景或问题来源。

Method: 摘要未描述具体技术手段，仅指出研究对象为六顶点模型的高度函数，并在畴壁边界条件下考察反铁电与无序两个参数区域。

Result: 在反铁电区（arbitrary Δ<-1）与无序区（restricted range -1<Δ≤1/2）分别给出了高度函数的极限形状定理。

Conclusion: 作者宣告了畴壁边界条件下六顶点模型高度函数的极限形状结果，覆盖反铁电与（受限参数范围内的）无序两类区域。

Abstract: In this note we announce limit shape theorems for the height function of the six-vertex model in the antiferroelectric (for arbitrary $Δ<-1$) and disordered (in a restricted range $-1 < Δ\le 1/2$) regimes under domain-wall boundary conditions.

</details>


### [77] [The Airy line ensemble at the edge of uniform alternating sign matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20603)
*Yuchen Liao*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该论文证明均匀交替符号矩阵（alternating sign matrix）的高度层路径系综在西北极弧内部任意点附近收敛到 Airy 线系综（Airy line ensemble），等价于冰点处域壁六顶点模型（domain-wall six-vertex model）的密切路径（osculating paths）的极限定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交替符号矩阵与六顶点模型在冰点的路径结构是统计力学与组合学的核心对象，其涨落普适性此前多在特定边界或单点层面已知，而在北极弧内部刻画完整线系综的收敛仍属空白。论文旨在建立该情形下的 Airy 线系综极限定理。

Method: 证明有两条主线。可积输入为矩形冻角概率（frozen-corner probabilities）的新 Pfaffian 公式，由此得到顶路径单点收敛到 GUE Tracy–Widom 分布；概率输入为严格有序中点编码（midpoint encoding）的精确重采样律（resampling law）及配套桥估计，用以获得紧性与每个子列极限的布朗–吉布斯性质（Brownian Gibbs property）。

Result: 得到顶路径一点分布收敛至 GUE Tracy–Widom 分布；通过对子列极限证明紧性与布朗–吉布斯性质，结合 Aggarwal 与 Huang 的强刻画，识别每个子列极限即为 Airy 线系综，从而完成内部点附近的线系综收敛。

Conclusion: 均匀交替符号矩阵的高度层路径系综（等价地，冰点域壁六顶点模型的密切路径）在西北极弧内部点附近普遍收敛到 Airy 线系综，确立了该模型涨落极限的普适性刻画。

Abstract: We prove that the height-level path ensemble of a uniform alternating sign matrix converges to the Airy line ensemble near every interior point of the north-west arctic arc. Under the standard bijection, this is equivalently a limit theorem for the osculating paths of the domain-wall six-vertex model at the ice point. The proof has two main inputs. The integrable input is a new Pfaffian formula for rectangular frozen-corner probabilities, from which we obtain one-point convergence of the top path to the GUE Tracy--Widom distribution. The probabilistic input is an exact resampling law and accompanying bridge estimates for a strictly ordered midpoint encoding of the paths; these yield tightness and the Brownian Gibbs property for every subsequential limit. Aggarwal and Huang's strong characterization then identifies each such limit as the Airy line ensemble.

</details>


### [78] [Markovian renormalisation for percolation in high-dimension: Semi-decidability of mean field behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20764)
*Arthur Blanc-Renaudie*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文提出在维度 d>6 研究伯努利渗流（Bernoulli percolation）的新方法，通过用带标记的 p-开簇（pointed p-open clusters）构成的马尔可夫链（Markov chain）逼近 p'>p 时的开路径，将两点函数（two point function）的精确信息从 p 传递到 p'，并给出临界行为的可判定性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在研究高维（d>6）伯努利渗流中两点函数在 p↑p_c 时的渐近行为，并探讨"临界平均场行为（critical meanfield behavior）"这一问题在计算上的性质。

Method: 核心思路是用带标记的 p-开簇构成的马尔可夫链，在概率 p'>p 下逼近开路径，从而把 p 处两点函数的精确信息传递至 p'；通过归纳法获得 p↑p_c 时两点函数的良好渐近估计。

Result: 作为主要应用，证明"具有临界平均场行为"是一个半可判定（semi-decidable）问题；同时还证明了磁化率（susceptibility）与平均回转半径（average radius of gyration）的精确渐近，以及略微亚临界两点函数的局部中心极限定理（local central limit theorem）。

Conclusion: 该马尔可夫链逼近框架为高维伯努利渗流提供了从 p 到 p' 的信息传递工具，并由此导出关于临界平均场行为可判定性及若干精确渐近性质的结论。

Abstract: We develop a new approach to study Bernoulli percolation in dimensions $d>6$. The key idea is to approximate open paths at probability $p'>p$ by a Markov chain of pointed $p$-open clusters. This allows us to transfer sharp information on the two point function from $p$ to $p'$. This inductively gives good asymptotic estimates on the two point function as $p\uparrow p_c$. As a main application, we show that having critical meanfield behavior is a semi-decidable problem. Along the way, we also prove sharp asymptotics for the susceptibility and the average radius of gyration, and prove a local central limit theorem for the slightly subcritical two-point function.

</details>

### [79] [Reconfigurable Current Distributions in Percolating Au Films and CrO2 Powder Compacts - Beyond Geometrical Connectivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20285)
*E. Yu. Beliayev,I. G. Mirzoiev,V. A. Horielyi*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 半连续Au薄膜（semicontinuous Au films）与压实的CrO₂粉末（compacted CrO₂ powders）虽在形貌、维度与微观输运上存在根本差异，却在网络层面收敛于同一原则：电学相关网络是物理接触网络中随条件变化的子集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于厘清强非均匀导体（strongly inhomogeneous conductors）中几何连通性（geometrical connectivity）不足以解释输运的难题，并寻找不同材料体系在网络层面的共性规律。

Method: 通过对比两类体系展开分析：在接近几何渗流阈值（geometrical percolation threshold）的Au薄膜中调控偏压以观察低温响应转变；在CrO₂粉末压实体中施加磁场，考察其颗粒间结（intergranular junctions）自旋相关电阻的变化，并以Cohn定理（Cohn's theorem）作为灵敏度原理进行统一描述。

Result: Au薄膜中，增大偏压可逆地将低温响应从激活型最近邻跃迁（activated nearest-neighbour hopping）伴随负磁阻，转变为类弱局域化（weak-localization-like）伴随正磁阻，对应固定岛状网络中更广泛的电子参与。CrO₂压实体中，磁场主要改变既有结的自旋相关电阻，从而重排竞争性电流路径；温度与测量电流亦可扩展或耗竭活性网络。

Conclusion: 该对比识别出两种极限但可相互转换的重构模式——链路启用（link enabling）与链路重加权（link reweighting），并说明仅凭几何连通性无法解释强非均匀导体的输运行为；Cohn定理为两类情形提供了统一的灵敏度判据。

Abstract: Despite fundamental differences in morphology, dimensionality, and microscopic transport, semicontinuous Au films and compacted CrO2 powders converge on a common network-level principle: the electrically relevant network is a condition-dependent subset of the physical contact network. In Au films near the geometrical percolation threshold, increasing bias reversibly shifts the low-temperature response from activated nearest-neighbour hopping with negative magnetoresistance to a weak-localization-like regime with positive magnetoresistance, consistent with broader electronic participation of the fixed island network. In CrO2 powder compacts, magnetic field mainly changes the spin-dependent resistances of existing intergranular junctions and thereby reorders competing current paths; temperature and measuring current can additionally expand or deplete the active network. Cohn's theorem provides a compact sensitivity principle for both cases: local resistance changes influence the measured response in proportion to the current carried by the affected links. The comparison identifies two limiting but interconvertible modes of reconfiguration - link enabling and link reweighting - and shows why geometrical connectivity alone is insufficient for interpreting transport in strongly inhomogeneous conductors.

</details>

### [80] [Displacement field stabilizes even-denominator and partonic fractional quantum Hall states in the $\mathcal{N}{=}2$ Landau levels of Bernal-stacked bilayer graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19379)
*Rakesh K. Dora,Udit Khanna,Ajit C. Balram*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究计算了Bernal堆叠双层石墨烯（BLG）在$\mathcal{N}{=}2$朗道能级半填充下的位移场相图，发现随位移场增大，短程库仑排斥被软化，使Moore-Read态相对于复合费米子费米液体（CFFL）更稳定；同时$3/7$、$4/9$、$6/13$等分数量子霍尔态会转变为由部分子波函数（parton wave functions）描述的具有不同拓扑序的态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验在垂直磁场与有限层间位移场下于BLG第一激发朗道能级（$\mathcal{N}{=}2$）半填充处观测到偶分母分数量子霍尔（FQH）态，而零位移场下该能级半填充表现为无能隙的复合费米子费米液体（CFFL），二者对比促使作者探究位移场对相图的调控机制。

Method: 通过计算CFFL与Moore-Read态（候选偶分母FQH态）的热力学能量，研究二者竞争并给出随位移场变化的相图；同时考察BLG的$\mathcal{N}{=}2$朗道能级中$2/5$、$3/7$、$4/9$、$6/13$填充处的FQH态性质。

Result: 随位移场增大，单粒子态改变所诱导的修正有效库仑相互作用（effective Coulomb interaction）软化了短程电子间排斥，从而在BLG的$\mathcal{N}{=}2$朗道能级中使Moore-Read态相对CFFL更稳定；此外，随位移场增大，$3/7$、$4/9$、$6/13$处的Jain复合费米子态转变为可用部分子波函数良好描述的、具有不同拓扑序的态。

Conclusion: 位移场通过调节有效库仑相互作用，可选择性稳定Moore-Read等偶分母FQH态并驱动填充因子$3/7$、$4/9$、$6/13$处的拓扑序转变，表明BLG的$\mathcal{N}{=}2$朗道能级为探索与调控分数量子霍尔拓扑相提供了可调平台。

Abstract: Bernal-stacked bilayer graphene (BLG), in which a graphene layer is stacked atop another and laterally shifted by a lattice constant, offers remarkable tunability in its single-particle states under applied magnetic and displacement fields. Owing to this tunability, recent transport and scanning tunneling microscopy experiments in the presence of a perpendicular magnetic field and finite interlayer displacement fields have observed even-denominator fractional quantum Hall (FQH) states at half-filling in the first excited, namely, $\mathcal{N}{=}2$, Landau level (LL) of BLG. In contrast, at zero displacement field, a gapless composite fermion Fermi liquid (CFFL) is realized at half-filling of the $\mathcal{N}{=}2$ LL. Motivated by these experiments, we compute the phase diagram as a function of the displacement field in the half-filled $\mathcal{N}{=}2$ LL of BLG by studying the competition between the CFFL and the Moore-Read state---a candidate even-denominator FQH state---by calculating their thermodynamic energies in this setting. We find that the modified effective Coulomb interaction, induced by changes in the single-particle states with increasing displacement field, softens the inter-electronic repulsion at short distances, thereby stabilizing the Moore-Read state over the CFFL in the $\mathcal{N}{=}2$ LL of BLG. We also study the nature of FQH states at fillings $2/5$, $3/7$, $4/9$, and $6/13$ in the $\mathcal{N}{=}2$ LL of BLG. Our results suggest that, with increasing displacement field, the Jain composite-fermion states at $3/7$, $4/9$, and $6/13$ transition into states with distinct topological order that are well-captured by parton wave functions.

</details>


### [81] [Multiferroic Quantum Dot in an Artificial van der Waals Heterostructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19408)
*Antti Karjasilta,Mohammad Amini,Liwei Jing,Robert Drost,Jose Lado,Shawulienu Kezilebieke,Peter Liljeroth,Adolfo O. Fumega*

TL;DR: 通过在铁电SnTe纳米岛/层状磁体CrBr₂/高定向热解石墨的人工范德华异质结中构筑量子点，实现了量子限域、磁交换与铁电序在原子尺度上的耦合，获得铁电畴构型可调控自旋极化离散能级的多铁性量子点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子点（quantum dots, QDs）已是研究自旋、谷、拓扑与铁电自由度的通用平台，但量子限域同时与磁性、铁电性相互交织的多铁性量子点（multiferroic QD）尚未实现。该工作旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 以分子束外延（molecular beam epitaxy）在超高真空条件下生长人工范德华异质结：高定向热解石墨（highly oriented pyrolytic graphite）衬底上的层状磁体CrBr₂，其上沉积铁电SnTe纳米岛。结合扫描隧道显微镜与谱学（scanning tunneling microscopy and spectroscopy）、从头算（ab initio）计算及低能紧束缚（tight-binding）模型开展表征与理论分析。

Result: 观测到受限在SnTe岛内的自旋极化离散电子态（spin-polarized discretized electronic states）。该量子点的谱学响应强烈依赖SnTe纳米岛的铁电畴（ferroelectric domain）构型，表明量子限域、磁交换（magnetic exchange）与铁电序在原子尺度上存在相互作用。

Conclusion: 工程化范德华异质结可作为实现多铁性量子限域（multiferroic quantum confinement）的平台，并为电可调量子自旋电子学（quantum spintronic）器件开辟新路径。

Abstract: Quantum dots (QDs) provide a versatile platform for engineering quantum-confined electronic states with functionalities relevant for optoelectronics, spintronics, and quantum technologies. While substantial progress has been achieved in coupling confined states to spin, valley, topological, or ferroelectric degrees of freedom, the realization of a multiferroic QD in which quantum confinement simultaneously intertwines with magnetism and ferroelectricity remains elusive. Here, we engineer a multiferroic QD in an artificial van der Waals heterostructure grown by molecular beam epitaxy under ultra-high-vacuum conditions. The heterostructure consists of ferroelectric SnTe nanoislands deposited on the layered magnet CrBr$_2$ supported on highly oriented pyrolytic graphite. Combining scanning tunneling microscopy and spectroscopy with ab initio calculations and low-energy tight-binding models, we demonstrate the emergence of spin-polarized discretized electronic states confined within the SnTe islands. Remarkably, the spectroscopic response of the QD strongly depends on the ferroelectric domain configuration of the SnTe nanoislands, demonstrating an interplay between quantum confinement, magnetic exchange, and ferroelectric order at the atomic scale. Our results establish engineered van der Waals heterostructures as a platform for multiferroic quantum confinement and open new routes toward electrically tunable quantum spintronic devices.

</details>


### [82] [Quantum transport across normal-superlattice-normal graphene junctions: Fabry-Pérot interference, Hofstadter butterfly, and supersnake states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19627)
*Che-Pin Hsu,Alina Mreńca-Kolasińska,Aitor Garcia-Ruiz,Szu-Chao Chen,Denis Kochan,Klaus Richter,Ming-Hao Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作通过量子输运模拟研究栅极定义的 NGr-SGr-NGr 石墨烯超晶格结（gate-defined graphene superlattice junction），揭示其可在单一器件架构中承载多种输运机制，包括 Fabry-Pérot 干涉（Fabry-Pérot interference）、Hofstadter 蝴蝶谱（Hofstadter butterfly spectrum）以及新型 supersnake 态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静电调制石墨烯（graphene）为调控微带结构（miniband structures）提供了可调途径，因此有必要探究受限超晶格区域与常规石墨烯区域组成的结在量子输运层面可产生哪些新现象与机制。

Method: 对由二维超晶格石墨烯（two-dimensional superlattice graphene, SGr）区域夹在两片常规石墨烯（normal graphene, NGr）区域之间的栅极定义石墨烯超晶格结进行量子输运模拟（quantum transport simulations），包括有限宽度（finite-width）输运计算，并借助半经典回旋轨道（semiclassical cyclotron orbits）推导几何条件。

Result: 在低载流子密度（low carrier densities）的低场区，即便在单极区（unipolar regime），因 SGr 区域中的费米速度重整化（Fermi-velocity renormalization）仍出现稳健的 Fabry-Pérot 干涉条纹；在较强磁场（仅至 3 T）下，电导图清晰呈现 Hofstadter 蝴蝶谱；在中间磁场下，有限宽度模拟揭示新型 snake 态，即 supersnake 态，由 SGr 一侧的反常回旋弧（anomalous cyclotron arcs）与 NGr 一侧的常规半圆弧交替构成并沿结形成编织轨迹，其电导振荡与半经典几何条件吻合。

Conclusion: 栅极定义的 NGr-SGr-NGr 结可作为通用平台，在单一器件架构内承载多种输运区制（transport regimes），且该思路可推广至不局限于石墨烯的其他类型超晶格。

Abstract: Electrostatic modulation of graphene provides a tunable route to engineering miniband structures. We perform quantum transport simulations on a gate-defined graphene superlattice junction, formed by confining a two-dimensional superlattice graphene (SGr) region between two normal graphene (NGr) regions. In the low-field regime at low carrier densities, robust Fabry-Pérot interference fringes emerge even in the unipolar regime due to Fermi-velocity renormalization in the SGr region. At stronger magnetic fields but only up to 3 T, the conductance map clearly reveals the Hofstadter butterfly spectrum. At intermediate fields, our finite-width transport simulations reveal a new type of snake state, the supersnake state, composed of alternating anomalous cyclotron arcs on the SGr side and conventional semicircular arcs on the NGr side, forming a weaving trajectory along the junction. The resulting conductance oscillations agree well with geometrical conditions derived from semiclassical cyclotron orbits. Our results demonstrate that gate-defined NGr-SGr-NGr junctions provide a versatile platform hosting multiple transport regimes within a single device architecture and can be generalized to other types of superlattices not restricted to graphene.

</details>


### [83] [Transport Relaxation Mechanisms in Bilayer Graphene: Effects of Pauli Blocking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19643)
*Amit Varshney,SSZ Ashraf*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文在低能两带近似下，基于费米黄金规则与半经典玻尔兹曼输运方程，统一解析分析了双层石墨烯（bilayer graphene, BLG）中多种散射机制引起的载流子输运弛豫，并重点考察泡利阻塞（Pauli blocking）的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双层石墨烯中多种散射机制并存，其相对重要性及泡利阻塞的作用缺乏统一解析认识；同时需明确 BLG 与单层石墨烯及传统二维电子气的差异来源。

Method: 在低能两带近似（two-band approximation）框架内，运用费米黄金规则（Fermi's golden rule）与半经典玻尔兹曼输运方程（semiclassical Boltzmann transport equation），推导输运弛豫率的闭式表达式，并通过数值求解相应散射积分加以验证。涵盖表面粗糙度、带电与中性杂质、声学与光学声子以及衬底极性声子散射。

Result: 泡利阻塞会以依赖散射机制与能量的方式修正弛豫率，在简并载流子条件下尤其重要。在所考察参数下，中性杂质散射在较宽能量范围内占主导，声学声子与带电杂质散射为主要竞争机制；光学声子散射在其发射阈值以下被抑制，而衬底极性声子在支撑型 BLG 中提供额外弛豫通道。

Conclusion: 结果给出了各主要动量弛豫机制相对重要性的解析认识，阐明了泡利阻塞在 BLG 输运中的作用，并通过与单层石墨烯及传统二维电子气的对比，凸显能带色散、态密度、屏蔽与手性（chirality）对 BLG 独特散射行为的影响。

Abstract: We present a unified analytical treatment of carrier transport relaxation in bilayer graphene (BLG), including scattering by surface roughness, charged and neutral impurities, acoustic and optical phonons, and substrate polar phonons. The calculation is formulated within the low-energy two-band approximation using Fermi's golden rule and the semiclassical Boltzmann transport equation. Closed-form expressions for the transport relaxation rates are derived and verified by numerical evaluation of the corresponding scattering integrals. Particular attention is given to Pauli blocking, whose influence becomes important under degenerate carrier conditions owing to the approximately parabolic low-energy dispersion and nearly constant density of states of BLG. We find that Pauli blocking modifies the relaxation rates in a mechanism- and energy-dependent manner for both suspended and substrate-supported BLG. For the parameters considered, neutral-impurity scattering provides the dominant contribution over a substantial energy range, while acoustic-phonon and charged-impurity scattering remain important competing mechanisms. Optical-phonon scattering is suppressed below its emission threshold, whereas substrate polar phonons provide an additional relaxation channel in supported BLG. Comparison with monolayer graphene and a conventional two-dimensional electron gas highlights the role of band dispersion, density of states, screening, and chirality in determining the distinct scattering behavior of BLG. These results provide analytical insight into the relative importance of the principal momentum-relaxation mechanisms and the role of Pauli blocking in BLG transport.

</details>


### [84] [Observation of Dyakonov-Perel-type magnon spin relaxation in uniaxial antiferromagnetic insulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19700)
*Qinwu Gao,Andi Cong,Bokai Liang,Meng Yang,Jingjing Liu,Lang Chen,Shiwei Wu,Ka Shen,Junxue Li*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作揭示反铁磁绝缘体中沿易轴传输的磁振子自旋流由Dyakonov-Perel型磁振子自旋弛豫机制主导，并以Cr2O3与α-Fe2O3两种典型单轴反铁磁绝缘体加以验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反铁磁绝缘体中磁振子自旋流的长距离输运近期备受关注，但其磁振子自旋弛豫机制尚不明确，本文旨在厘清该机制。

Method: 在两种典型单轴反铁磁绝缘体Cr2O3与α-Fe2O3中，测量非局域信号的第一谐波、磁振子自旋扩散长度（magnon spin diffusion length）随磁场与温度的变化，并与包含Dyakonov-Perel型磁振子自旋弛豫的理论模型进行对比。

Result: 自旋翻转（spin-flop）转变前观察到磁场诱导的第一谐波非局域信号增强超过450%；两种晶体的磁振子自旋扩散长度随磁场增大而增加，并在0.8 T以上饱和；零场磁振子自旋扩散长度的温度依赖性亦与模型相符。

Conclusion: Dyakonov-Perel型磁振子自旋弛豫机制可解释沿易轴的自旋流输运，这些发现有助于低耗散反铁磁自旋电子学（antiferromagnetic spintronic）器件的发展。

Abstract: Long-distance transport of magnon spin currents in antiferromagnetic (AFM) insulators has attracted tremendous attention recently, however, the AFM magnon spin relaxation mechanisms remain elusive. Here, we report that the Dyakonov-Perel-type magnon spin relaxation mechanism governs the spin current transport along the easy axis in two prototypical uniaxial AFM insulators, Cr2O3 and alpha-Fe2O3. First, an over 450% enhancement of the first-harmonic nonlocal signal induced by a magnetic field is observed prior to the spin-flop transition, which can be well-interpreted by our model incorporating Dyakonov-Perel-type magnon spin relaxation. Secondly, we find that the magnon spin diffusion length in both crystals increases with magnetic field and saturates at fields above 0.8 T, consistent with our model. Finally, the temperature dependence of the zero-field magnon spin diffusion length in both AFM insulators can be qualitatively explained through our model. These findings are valuable for the development of low-dissipation antiferromagnetic spintronic devices.

</details>


### [85] [Highly uniform first-electron position in qubit arrays fabricated on dedicated QSOI(R) 300mm commercial platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20043)
*Johan Pelloux-Prayer,Elise Prin,Giselle A. Elbaz,Pierre-Louis Julliard,Amaryllis Comiti,Clément Nguyen,Sylvain Martin,Patrick Torresani,Renan Lethiecq,Carlos Augusto Suarez Segovia,Franck Arnaud,Etienne Nowak,Tristan Meunier,Bruna Cardoso Paz*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作展示了面向可扩展量子计算（scalable quantum computing）的量子绝缘体上硅（QSOI(R)）技术进展，该技术兼容300mm CMOS工艺并基于28nm全耗尽SOI平台。相较标准28nm FD-SOI器件，QSOI(R)在室温静电特性、晶圆级器件一致性与亚2K量子点可重复性方面均获得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标在于开发一种与300mm CMOS制造兼容、可扩展的量子硅平台，以支持量子器件的协同集成（co-integration）。为此需将现有28nm全耗尽SOI（FD-SOI）平台改造为适配量子计算的QSOI(R)技术，并验证其在器件性能与晶圆级一致性上的优势。

Method: 对比采用标准28nm FD-SOI与QSOI(R)技术制备的量子器件，评估其室温静电特性、器件变异性（variability）与晶圆级属性分散度；并用TCAD仿真（TCAD simulations）复现晶体管指标以验证静电行为。同时在亚2K温度下进行晶圆级测量，统计量子点（quantum dots）进入少电子区（few-electron regime）的成功率与一致性。

Result: QSOI(R)器件在室温静电特性上相较标准28nm FD-SOI有显著改善，并大幅降低器件变异性与晶圆级性能分散度。TCAD仿真复现了晶体管指标，表明QSOI(R)静电行为符合预期。亚2K晶圆级测量显示量子点可重复形成并达到少电子区，首个电子电荷探测成功率为69\%，377个量子点中首个电子位置分散度为±35 mV。

Conclusion: 结果表明QSOI(R)是一种有前景的平台，可用于CMOS兼容的量子器件协同集成，为可扩展量子计算提供了工艺与器件层面的一致性基础。

Abstract: We report progress toward the development of a quantum silicon-on-insulator (QSOI(R)) technology compatible with 300mm CMOS fabrication and adapted from the 28nm Fully-Depleted SOI (28nm FD-SOI) platform for scalable quantum computing. We compare quantum devices fabricated with standard 28nm FD-SOI and QSOI(R) technologies, and show striking improvements of room temperature electrostatic properties of the individual device. Moreover, the QSOI(R) technology has significantly reduced the device variability and the dispersion of device properties at the wafer level. Transistor metrics are reproduced by TCAD simulations showing that the electrostatics of the devices behave as expected for QSOI(R) technology. Wafer-scale measurements at sub-2K show reproducible quantum dots down to the few-electron regime with 69\% yield for successful charge detection of the first electron and a dispersion of the first electron position of $\mathrm{\pm 35 mV}$ over 377 quantum dots. These results establish QSOI(R) as a promising platform for CMOS-compatible quantum device co-integration.

</details>


### [86] [Plasmons in twisted bilayer graphene across dispersive and flat bands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20158)
*Antonio Palamara,Michele Pisarra,Antonello Sindona*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文以含时密度泛函理论（time-dependent density-functional theory）结合随机相位近似（random-phase approximation），研究转角双层石墨烯（twisted bilayer graphene）的介电响应，比较大角度色散能带与小角度准平带两种情形下的等离激元（plasmon）行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 转角双层石墨烯的电子关联与集体激发随转角显著变化，但大角度与小角度（含魔角）区间中动态介电响应（dynamical dielectric response）的系统描述仍不完整，尤其缺乏对准平带相关等离激元的识别。

Method: 采用含时密度泛函理论（time-dependent density-functional theory）并在随机相位近似（random-phase approximation）框架下计算介电响应；大角度区以参考层间距的色散能带处理，小角度区则以层间距缩减至角度依赖的“魔角距离”（magic distance）的可计算原胞代表第一魔角超胞，从而获得费米能级附近的四条准平带（quasi-flat bands）。

Result: 参考层间距下大角度结构保持狄拉克色散（Dirac dispersion）与本征 π 等离激元；电子掺杂激发二维狄拉克等离激元，其能量近似遵循转角几何标度，而类声学支仍嵌入单粒子连续谱中。小角度区中，准平带的部分占据产生色散的类等离激元低能激发，但在共振处无明显介电零点；同时识别出由准平带流形与其邻近高态密度区之间跃迁支撑的带间等离激元（interband plasmon）。

Conclusion: 经能带能量重标定（band-energy rescaling）后，该带间集体激发的特征能量落于魔角附近实验测得的中红外带间集体激发范围内，说明准平带相关跃迁可解释观测到的高能集体模式。

Abstract: The dynamical dielectric response of twisted bilayer graphene is explored in large-angle dispersive-band and small-angle quasi-flat-band regimes using time-dependent density-functional theory within the random-phase approximation. At the reference bilayer-graphene interlayer distance, weak coupling in the largest-angle structures preserves Dirac dispersions and the intrinsic $π$ plasmon. Electron doping activates a two-dimensional Dirac plasmon with energies obeying approximate geometric twist-angle scaling, while acoustic-like branches remain embedded in the single-particle continuum. The prohibitively large first-magic-angle supercell is represented by a tractable cell with its interlayer separation reduced to the angle-dependent magic distance, where four quasi-flat bands emerge around the Fermi level. Their partial occupation produces a dispersive low-energy plasmon-like excitation without a clear dielectric zero at resonance. A distinct interband plasmon is instead identified, supported by transitions involving the quasi-flat manifold and neighboring high-density-of-states regions. Band-energy rescaling places its characteristic energy in the mid-infrared range of interband collective excitations measured near the magic angle.

</details>


### [87] [Predictive Structure to Thermal Conductivity Modeling Framework for BEOL Interconnect Stacks in Advanced Technology Nodes Enabled by Extensive Layer Resolved Thermal Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20379)
*Zifeng Huang,Yiyang Sun,Tianyu Jia,Runsheng Wang,Zhe Cheng*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究基于时域热反射（time-domain thermoreflectance, TDTR）测量建立了一个实验导出的、结构感知（structure-aware）的热导率建模框架，用于先进互连堆叠与三维集成电路（3D IC）的预测性热分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先进集成电路中后段制程（BEOL）互连堆叠结构复杂度不断提升，亟需结构感知的热导率建模框架；然而，能够定量刻画热导率对互连结构依赖关系、且具备可泛化性的层分辨热测量模型仍然缺乏，限制了预测性热分析。

Method: 采用具有约100 nm深度分辨率的时域热反射（TDTR）测量，并对包含40余个实验测量的、覆盖多种BEOL层的层分辨热导率值数据集进行统计分析，从而归纳出可泛化的经验性“结构—热导率”关系；同时基于有效介质理论（effective medium theory）与实际版图建立层内三维热导率分布模型。

Result: 统计分析揭示出可泛化的经验性结构—热导率关系，支持基于互连结构的预测性建模；层内三维热导率分布模型进一步解析了实际互连层内部的空间热导率变化。

Conclusion: 上述模型共同构成一个实验导出、结构感知的热导率建模框架，可用于先进互连堆叠与3D IC的预测性、可泛化热分析。

Abstract: The increasing structural complexity of BEOL interconnect stacks in advanced integrated circuits demands a structure-aware thermal conductivity (\k{appa}) modeling framework. However, generalizable models derived from layer-resolved thermal measurements that quantitatively capture the dependence of \k{appa} on interconnect structures remain lacking, limiting predictive thermal analysis. Here, we establish an experimentally derived, structure-aware \k{appa} modeling framework enabled by time-domain thermoreflectance measurements with ~100 nm depth resolution. Statistical analysis of a compiled dataset comprising over 40 experimentally measured layer-resolved \k{appa} values across diverse BEOL layers reveals a generalizable empirical structure-to-\k{appa} relationship, enabling predictive modeling based on interconnect structure. An intra-layer three-dimensional \k{appa} distribution model based on effective medium theory and realistic layouts further resolves spatial \k{appa} variations within practical interconnect layers. Together, these models establish an experimentally derived, structure-aware \k{appa} modeling framework for predictive and generalizable thermal analysis of advanced interconnect stacks and 3D ICs.

</details>


### [88] [Coplanar Lateral Gating MoS2 on SrTiO3: A Unified Platform for Classical and Quantum Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20426)
*Prasad Muragesh,Manav Murali,Venkatesha Modur Ramachandra,Madhu Thalakulam*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种直接在单晶钛酸锶（SrTiO3）上共面侧向栅控（coplanar lateral-gating）的 MoS2 场效应晶体管架构，利用 SrTiO3 的高介电常数与量子顺电（quantum paraelectric）特性增强栅极耦合，实现无需独立栅绝缘层的低功耗二维电子学与低温量子器件平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原子级薄过渡金属二硫化物（transition-metal dichalcogenides, TMDs，如 MoS2）具备优异静电控制与抗短沟道效应能力，有望突破硅的缩放极限。但其潜力实现依赖栅堆叠工程（gate-stack engineering）：既需强栅极耦合，又需保持二维界面不受损伤。已有栅绝缘层会引入界面无序，因此需要新的栅控方案。

Method: 在单晶 SrTiO3 上直接制备 MoS2 场效应晶体管，采用共面侧向栅控架构。该设计利用 SrTiO3 的极高介电常数增强栅极-沟道耦合，并以共面几何省去独立栅绝缘层，从而降低界面无序；同时借助其量子顺电特性使介电常数随降温升高。

Result: 由于 SrTiO3 的量子顺电性质，降温时其介电常数增大，栅极耦合随之增强，导致阈值电压（threshold voltage）与亚阈值摆幅（subthreshold swing）下降。该降温下性能改善的趋势与传统 FET 架构相反。

Conclusion: 无介电层的 MoS2 表面结合增强的静电控制，使该架构成为低功耗二维电子学（two-dimensional electronics）与低温量子器件（cryogenic quantum devices）的有前景平台。

Abstract: Atomically thin transition-metal dichalcogenides, such as MoS2, offer a promising route to surpass the scaling limits of silicon owing to their excellent electrostatic control and resilience to short-channel effects. Realizing this potential depends critically on gate-stack engineering, where strong gate coupling must be achieved without compromising the pristine two-dimensional interface. Here, we demonstrate a coplanar lateral-gating architecture for MoS2 field-effect transistors fabricated directly on single-crystal SrTiO3. The exceptionally high permittivity of SrTiO3 enhances gate-channel coupling. The coplanar geometry eliminates the need for a separate gate insulator, reducing interface disorder. Owing to the quantum paraelectric nature, the SrTiO3 dielectric constant increases upon cooling, enhancing the gate coupling, leading to a decrease in threshold voltage and subthreshold swing an effect that contrasts with conventional FET architectures. The dielectric-free MoS2 surface, combined with enhanced electrostatic control, makes this architecture a promising platform for low-power two-dimensional electronics and cryogenic quantum devices.

</details>


### [89] [Disorder-induced modulation of the nonlinear Hall effect in Weyl semimetals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20471)
*Juan A. Cañas,Daniel A. Bonilla,A. Martín-Ruiz*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文在半经典玻尔兹曼（Boltzmann）框架下研究杂质散射对Weyl半金属非线性霍尔响应（nonlinear Hall response）的影响，推导了含动量依赖输运弛豫时间的一般整流与二次谐波电导张量，并对短程、高斯、屏蔽库仑及磁性杂质进行微观计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是厘清杂质散射的微观形式如何通过输运弛豫时间（transport relaxation time）进入Weyl半金属的非线性霍尔响应，尤其是区分标量杂质与极化磁性杂质带来的不同机制。

Method: 采用半经典玻尔兹曼方法，针对一般动量依赖的输运弛豫时间推导整流与二次谐波电导张量，并微观评估短程、高斯、屏蔽库仑和磁性杂质下的弛豫时间；对极化磁性杂质关注一阶与二阶Born振幅的干涉效应。

Result: 对标量杂质，弛豫时间各向同性且与手性（chirality）无关，非线性霍尔响应由Berry曲率（Berry curvature）关联的反常速度（anomalous velocity）决定，有限净响应需能量上不等价的Weyl节点以避免相反手性相互抵消。极化磁性杂质则通过一阶与二阶Born振幅干涉产生依赖螺旋度（helicity）的各向异性弛豫时间修正，改变非线性霍尔电导张量结构，并在电场相对杂质极化取特定方向时产生有限二次谐波电流。然而在代表性参数下，该各向异性贡献比主导的各向同性响应小若干数量级。

Conclusion: 研究确立了杂质散射的微观形式经由输运弛豫时间影响Weyl半金属非线性霍尔响应的机制，其中磁性杂质的各向异性效应虽在张量结构上定性不同，但数值上远弱于各向同性主项。

Abstract: We study the effects of impurity scattering on the nonlinear Hall response of Weyl semimetals within the semiclassical Boltzmann approach. We derive the rectified and second-harmonic conductivity tensors for a general momentum-dependent transport relaxation time and evaluate this quantity microscopically for short-range, Gaussian, screened Coulomb, and magnetic impurities. For scalar disorder, the relaxation time is isotropic and chirality independent. The nonlinear Hall response is then determined by the anomalous velocity associated with the Berry curvature, and a finite net response requires energetically inequivalent Weyl nodes to avoid cancellation between opposite chiralities. Polarized magnetic impurities lead to a qualitatively different behavior. We find that interference between the first- and second-order Born amplitudes generates a helicity-dependent anisotropic correction to the relaxation time. This anisotropy changes the tensor structure of the nonlinear Hall conductivity and gives rise to a finite second-harmonic current for suitable orientations of the electric field relative to the impurity polarization. For representative parameters, however, the anisotropic contribution is several orders of magnitude smaller than the dominant isotropic response. These results establish how the microscopic form of impurity scattering enters the nonlinear Hall response of Weyl semimetals through the transport relaxation time.

</details>


### [90] [Coherent and ultra-low-power EDSR with a flopping-mode spin qubit in germanium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20775)
*Alexei Orekhov,Wonjin Jang,Pan Zhang,Konstantinos Tsoukalas,Fabian Oppliger,Franco De Palma,Elena Acinapura,Younghun Ryu,Inga Seidler,Lisa Sommer,Leonardo Massai,Felix J. Schupp,Matthias Mergenthaler,Stefano Bosco,Patrick Harvey-Collard,Pasquale Scarlino*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作展示了基于平面Ge双量子点的flopping-mode（FM）空穴自旋量子比特，在5 mT近面内磁场下仅用-52 dBm驱动功率即实现高保真单比特操控，兼具对电荷噪声的一阶保护与高效电驱动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半导体量子点空穴自旋量子比特可实现全电控制，但传统电偶极自旋共振（EDSR）在有利于相干与读出的低磁场下需较大射频驱动功率（平面Ge中可达-27 dBm），带来发热与串扰，不利于可扩展架构。

Method: 采用flopping-mode（FM）比特方案，将单个自旋在双量子点中离域化，并结合FM-EDSR驱动；通过映射相干甜点（sweet-spots）随磁场方向的变化来优化工作点。

Result: 获得 T2*=1.4 μs、T2^Hahn=11.5 μs、T2^φ,CPMG32=130 μs、T1=226 μs，单比特门保真度最高达99.76%（门时间 t_Xπ=88 ns），且在5 mT近面内磁场与-52 dBm驱动功率下达成。

Conclusion: 该区间的弛豫与双光子Orbach过程一致，为进一步优化提供途径；结果表明FM-EDSR可支持超低功耗、高保真单比特操作，有望惠及可扩展空穴自旋架构与混合自旋-光子接口。

Abstract: Hole spin qubits in semiconductor quantum dots (QDs) enable high-fidelity all-electric control, but conventional electric dipole spin resonance (EDSR) can require substantial rf drive power at the low magnetic fields that are favorable for qubit coherence and readout. In planar Ge hole spin qubits, this can reach -27 dBm at the device, posing challenges for scalable architectures due to heating and crosstalk. Here, we demonstrate a flopping-mode (FM) qubit in Ge, where a single spin is delocalized in a double QD, combining first-order protection against charge noise with exceptionally efficient electric driving. By mapping out coherence sweet-spots as a function of magnetic field orientation we achieve $T_2^*= 1.4μ\mathrm{s}$, $T_2^{\mathrm{Hahn}}= 11.5 μ\mathrm{s}$, $T^{φ, \mathrm{CPMG32}}_2= 130 μ\mathrm{s}$, and $T_1= 226 μ\mathrm{s}$, and a single-qubit gate fidelity of up to 99.76$\%$ for a gate time $t_{Xπ} = 88$ ns. Importantly, these results are obtained at a nearly in-plane magnetic field of 5 mT using only -52 dBm drive power at the device. We further find that qubit relaxation in this regime is consistent with a two-photon Orbach process, providing a route for further optimization. Our results demonstrate that FM-EDSR supports ultra-low-power, high-fidelity single-qubit operations, improvements that could benefit scalable hole-spin-based architectures and hybrid spin-photon interfaces.

</details>

### [91] [Unified Transport and Susceptibility Analysis of a Thin BSCCO Film: From Local Pairing to Global Phase Coherence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19303)
*Santu Prasad Jana,Bismaya Ranjan Nayak,Sohini Guin,Akshay Naik,Dhavala Suri,Arindam Ghosh*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该研究通过电输运与交流磁化率测量，分析30 nm厚溅射Bi₂Sr₂CaCu₂O$_{8+\delta}$（BSCCO）薄膜的超导转变，揭示其由局域超导、超导分数扩展、磁屏蔽建立到全局相位相干的分阶段演化过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 薄膜高温超导（high-$T_c$）转变会因无序（disorder）、空间不均匀性（spatial inhomogeneity）和相位涨落（phase fluctuations）而显著展宽，需厘清电输运与磁响应之间的关系及其阶段特征。

Method: 对30 nm厚溅射生长的BSCCO薄膜开展电输运与交流磁化率（ac-susceptibility）测量；用局域$T_c$的高斯分布（Gaussian distribution）与Ambegaokar--Halperin模型分析电阻转变；将Choy--Stoneham磁化率模型扩展，引入由输运提取的超导分数和表征约瑟夫森耦合（Josephson coupling）发展的温度依赖连通性因子。

Result: 电阻转变可由局域$T_c$分布与热激活相位动力学（thermally activated phase dynamics）共同描述；扩展的磁化率模型可将输运与磁响应关联，并区分出局域超导起始、超导分数扩展、磁屏蔽发展与全局相位相干建立等不同温区。

Conclusion: 该超导转变是渐进过程，从局域超导区域演化为连通且相位相干（phase-coherent）的状态；该分析为关联非均匀超导薄膜中的空间不均匀性、耗散相位动力学（dissipative phase dynamics）与磁屏蔽提供统一框架。

Abstract: The superconducting transition in thin high-$T_c$ films can be significantly broadened by disorder, spatial inhomogeneity, and phase fluctuations. Here, we study the transition in a 30-nm-thick sputter-grown Bi$_2$Sr$_2$CaCu$_2$O$_{8+δ}$ (BSCCO) film through electrical-transport and ac-susceptibility measurements. The resistive transition is examined using a Gaussian distribution of local $T_c$ values and the Ambegaokar--Halperin model, describing spatial variations in superconductivity and thermally activated phase dynamics, respectively. To relate the transport and magnetic responses, we extend the Choy--Stoneham susceptibility model by introducing the superconducting fraction extracted from transport and a temperature-dependent connectivity factor representing the gradual development of Josephson coupling among superconducting regions. The results identify separate temperature regimes corresponding to the onset of local superconductivity, expansion of the superconducting fraction, development of magnetic screening, and establishment of global phase coherence. The transition therefore proceeds progressively, from locally superconducting regions to a connected, phase-coherent state. This analysis provides a common framework for relating spatial inhomogeneity, dissipative phase dynamics, and magnetic screening in inhomogeneous superconducting films.

</details>


### [92] [Encapsulation epitaxy of air-stable monolayer superconducting films for quantum circuits and qubits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19321)
*Xudong Zheng,Sameia Zaman,Kenan Zhang,Connor A Occhialini,Haowei Xu,Zhien Wang,Fangyuan Liu,Luiz Gustavo Pimenta Martins,Sejoon Lim,Tianyi Zhang,Tilo H. Yang,Jiangtao Wang,Yunyue Zhu,Zachariah Hennighausen,Sein Park,Steven Vitale,Kevin Tibbetts,Stephen Margiotta,Phillip Kim,Cong Su,Ju Li,Riccardo Comin,William D. Oliver,Joel Î-j. Wang,Jing Kong*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本工作提出"封装外延（encapsulation epitaxy）"机制，在3D衬底上预沉积2D封装层（石墨烯或hBN）作为模板与保护层，于其下外延生长大面积、空气稳定的单层NbSe2，并进一步集成至超导量子电路。所得1L-NbSe2表现出稳健超导性与增强的电荷密度波（charge density wave, CDW）序，且具有适合高动态电感量子电路器件的动能电感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维（2D）超导体因低维度、原子级平整界面与高结晶度，被视为强关联物理与量子信息科学的新兴平台，并有望用于超导电路中的紧凑集总元件器件。但大面积单层2D超导体因易氧化而难以合成，制约其应用。

Method: 提出封装外延机制：先在3D衬底（如SiO2或Si3N4）上预沉积2D封装层（石墨烯或六方氮化硼hBN），该层既作为其下单层NbSe2（1L-NbSe2）外延生长的模板，又作为保护性覆盖层。随后采用无氧化转移（oxidation-free transfer）与超导边缘接触（superconducting edge-contact）技术将1L-NbSe2集成进超导电路。

Result: 该策略可制备均匀、大面积（>1英寸）的1L-NbSe2，显著提升环境稳定性，并支持在空气中完成器件加工。所得1L-石墨烯/NbSe2异质结构表现出稳健超导性（Tc ~ 1 K）与增强的电荷密度波序（TCDW ~ 177 K），表明材料质量较高。1L-NbSe2测得动能电感（kinetic inductance）LK ~ 0.7 nH/square，适合需要高动能电感元件的量子电路。

Conclusion: 封装外延为制备空气稳定的2D超导体与范德华（van der Waals）异质结构提供了路径，并具备晶圆级、单片式（monolithic）制备超导量子电路的潜力。

Abstract: Two-dimensional (2D) superconductors are an emerging platform for strongly correlated physics and quantum information science. Their reduced dimensionality, atomically flat interfaces, and high crystallinity are attractive for realizing compact lumped-element devices in superconducting circuits. However, synthesizing large-area, monolayer 2D superconductors remains challenging because of their susceptibility to oxidation. Here, we report an "encapsulation epitaxy" mechanism that enables the growth of large-area, air-stable, monolayer superconducting NbSe2 films and explore their use in superconducting quantum circuits. A 2D encapsulation layer, such as graphene or hexagonal boron nitride (hBN), pre-deposited on a 3D substrate (e.g., SiO2 or Si3N4), serves both as a template for epitaxial growth of monolayer NbSe2 (1L-NbSe2) underneath it and as a protective cover. This approach produces uniform, large-area (>1-inch) 1L-NbSe2 with greatly enhanced ambient stability, enabling device fabrication in air. The resulting 1L-graphene/NbSe2 heterostructures exhibit robust superconductivity (Tc ~ 1 K) and enhanced charge density wave order (TCDW ~ 177 K), indicative of high material quality. We further integrate 1L-NbSe2 into superconducting circuits using oxidation-free transfer and superconducting edge-contact techniques. The 1L-NbSe2 exhibits a measured kinetic inductance LK ~ 0.7 nH/square, making it suitable for quantum circuits requiring high-kinetic-inductance elements. Encapsulation epitaxy thus provides a route to air-stable 2D superconductors and van der Waals heterostructures, with potential for wafer-scale, monolithic fabrication of superconducting quantum circuitry.

</details>


### [93] [Multiple Andreev Reflection Spectroscopy of Spin-Resolved Proximity States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19380)
*A. A. Golubov,M. Yu. Kupriyanov*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该论文预测了在电极中具有平行邻近诱导交换场的超导纳米收缩（superconducting nanoconstrictions）中出现的相干多重安德烈耶夫反射（multiple Andreev reflection）区间，其亚谐隙结构（subharmonic gap structure）由自旋分辨电极谱的重构而非BCS能隙边界的刚性位移所支配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于理解自旋分辨的邻近状态如何调控超导纳米结中的亚谐隙输运结构，并为探测自旋分辨邻近态提供直接的输运手段。

Method: 基于理论预测，考虑电极中平行的邻近诱导交换场（proximity-induced exchange fields），分析超导纳米收缩中的相干多重安德烈耶夫反射过程及其对自旋分辨电极谱重构的影响。

Result: 亚谐隙结构由自旋分辨电极谱的重构控制，而非BCS能隙边界的刚性位移；产生了分裂并重新分布的异常，其由外能隙边缘与偏移的交换诱导态密度奇异性（density of states singularity）之间的间隔所决定。该效应在通道透明度平均（channel transparency averaging）后依然存在。

Conclusion: 该效应可作为自旋分辨邻近态的直接输运探针（transport probe），表明多重安德烈耶夫反射区间对自旋分辨谱重构具有敏感性。

Abstract: We predict a coherent multiple Andreev reflection regime in superconducting nanoconstrictions with parallel proximity-induced exchange fields in the electrodes. The subharmonic gap structure is controlled by reconstruction of the spin-resolved electrode spectra, rather than by a rigid displacement of BCS gap edges. This produces split and redistributed anomalies governed by the interval between the outer gap edge and a shifted exchange induced density of states singularity. The effect survives channel transparency averaging and provides a direct transport probe of spin-resolved proximity states.

</details>


### [94] [Surface-condition-mediated supercooling of the A-B transition in confined superfluid helium-3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19471)
*Daksh Malhotra,Aymar Muhikira,Alexander J. Shook,John P. Davis*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 通过在1.8 μm腔体内表面预镀约4原子层$^4$He，四声氦亥姆霍兹谐振器（fourth-sound Helmholtz resonator）测量显示受限$^3$He的A–B相变出现明显的冷却/升温转变温度分离及随机涨落，即滞后（hysteresis）现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手性p波超导体与超流体（chiral p-wave superconductors and superfluids）是拓扑量子物质（topological quantum matter）研究的核心模型，但在低维条件下其性质与边界条件密不可分；$^3$He作为洁净的自旋三重态p波凝聚体，可用于直接调控边界条件并检验其对相变行为的影响。

Method: 采用四声亥姆霍兹谐振器（fourth-sound Helmholtz resonator），研究内表面预镀约4原子层$^4$He后、受限在1.8 μm腔体中的$^3$He的A–B相变，并与此前未表现出可分辨滞后（hysteresis）的四声测量结果进行对比。

Result: 预镀器件在冷却与升温转变温度之间呈现清晰分离，并伴随逐次运行的随机变化（stochastic run-to-run variations）；滞后随所研究压力升高而减小，与先前关于$^4$He介导准粒子边界散射（quasiparticle boundary scattering）压力依赖性的观测一致。

Conclusion: 表面制备可定性改变受限$^3$He中一级相变（first-order transition）动力学，使边界条件成为调控亚稳性（metastability）的实验可及控制参数，并为低维拓扑超流态（reduced-dimension topological superfluid states）的后续研究提供途径。

Abstract: Chiral $p$-wave superconductors and superfluids are central model systems in the search for topological quantum matter, but in reduced dimensions their properties are inseparable from the boundary conditions imposed by surrounding surfaces. Superfluid $^3$He provides a uniquely clean, well-established spin-triplet $p$-wave condensate in which these boundary conditions can be engineered directly. Here, we use a fourth-sound Helmholtz resonator to study the A--B phase transition of $^3$He confined to a $1.8\,μ$m cavity after preplating the internal surfaces with $\sim4$ atomic layers of $^4$He. In contrast to our previous fourth-sound measurements, which showed no resolvable hysteresis in the transition temperature, the preplated device exhibits a clear separation between cooling and warming transition temperatures together with stochastic run-to-run variations. The hysteresis decreases toward the highest pressures studied, consistent with previous observations of the pressure dependence of $^4$He-mediated quasiparticle boundary scattering. These results demonstrate that surface preparation can qualitatively modify first-order transition kinetics in confined $^3$He, making boundary conditions an experimentally accessible control parameter for metastability and for future studies of reduced-dimension topological superfluid states.

</details>


### [95] [Pairing symmetry and superconductivity from long-range Coulomb interactions in the extended t-t'-t''-Jz model for cuprates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19490)
*U. A. Diaz-Reynoso,F. Mireles*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 在扩展二维 $t$-$t'$-$t''$-$J_z$ 模型中，基态配对对称性（pairing symmetry）敏感依赖于跃迁参数 $t'$、$t''$ 的符号与大小，可呈纯 $p$ 波、纯 $d$ 波或二者共存；排斥相互作用驱动欠掺杂区（$\\delta\\approx0.07$–$0.15$）的重入超导（reentrant superconductivity）行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于将第一、第二、第三近邻电子跃迁（$t,t',t''$）与各向异性 Ising 型反铁磁相互作用（anisotropic Ising-like antiferromagnetic interaction）$J_z$ 纳入同一框架，考察跃迁项对基态配对对称性的决定性作用，并针对空穴掺杂铜氧化物（hole-doped cuprates）的具体情形进行分析。

Method: 采用扩展二维 $t$-$t'$-$t''$-$J_z$ 模型，在同一框架内处理 $t,t',t''$ 与 $J_z$；通过基态相图考察配对对称性，并分析长程与短程排斥相互作用（long-range and short-range repulsive interactions）对配对形成的影响，结合配对关联（pairing correlation）计算。

Result: 主导配对对称性敏感依赖于 $t'$ 与 $t''$ 的符号和大小，表现为纯 $p$ 波、纯 $d$ 波或共存配对通道；排斥相互作用有利于空穴对脱离条纹畴（stripe domains）；配对关联计算强烈提示排斥库仑相互作用（repulsive Coulomb interactions）在实验观测的欠掺杂区间（$\\delta\\approx0.07$–$0.15$）驱动重入超导行为，与钕基铜氧化物（Neodymium-based cuprates）情形一致。

Conclusion: 跃迁参数 $t'$、$t''$ 对配对对称性起决定性作用；排斥相互作用在空穴掺杂铜氧化物中既可促使空穴对逸出条纹畴，又可在欠掺杂区驱动重入超导，为理解钕基铜氧化物的实验现象提供理论线索。

Abstract: We investigate the pairing symmetry of the ground-state phase diagram of an extended two-dimensional $t$-$t'$-$t''$-$J_z$ model, in which the first-, second-, and third-nearest neighbor electron hopping terms ($t,t'$ and $t''$) and an anisotropic Ising-like antiferromagnetic interaction $J_z$ are treated on the same framework. We find that the dominant pairing symmetry depends sensitively on the sign and magnitude of the hopping parameters $t'$ and $t''$, showing pure $p$-wave, pure $d$-wave, or coexisting pairing channels, highlighting the decisive role of these terms. We further explore the role of the long-range and short-range repulsive interactions in the formation of pairs and analyze the specific case of hole-doped cuprates. Our results indicate that repulsive interactions favors hole pair escaping of the stripe domains. Furthermore, pairing correlation calculations strongly suggest that repulsive Coulomb interactions drive reentrant superconducting behavior at experimentally observed underdoped regime ($δ\approx0.07$ to $0.15$), as in Neodymium-based cuprates.

</details>


### [96] [Crystallographic imperfections and exotic superconductivity of UBe$_{13}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19678)
*Andreas Leithe-Jasper,Markus König,Yusei Shimizu,Konstantin Semeniuk,Primož Koželj,Mitja Krnel,Paul Simon,Wilder Carrillo-Cabrera,Nazar Zaremba,Marcel Naumann,Ulrich Burkhardt,Thomas Doert,Yurii Prots,Alfred Amon,Elena Gati,Matthias Vojta,Yuri Grin,Elena Hassinger,Eteri Svanidze*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该文通过微结构加工（microstructuring）研究无铝（aluminium-free）UBe$_{13}$ 晶体，发现极微小的晶格缺陷即可显著改变临界温度（critical temperature）与临界场（critical field），支持其非常规超导（unconventional superconductivity）性质，并推测其根源与铀的氧化态（oxidation state）对晶格环境的敏感性有关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导能隙（superconducting gap）的对称性与晶体结构对称性相关，在非常规超导体中，晶格的微小扰动即可导致临界温度、临界场乃至能隙结构的巨大变化。此前研究的 UBe$_{13}$ 单晶由铝助熔剂生长，可能偏离"完美"材料，因此需要更接近理想状态的样品来检验上述敏感性。

Method: 采用微结构加工（microstructuring）手段，制备并研究无铝的 UBe$_{13}$ 晶体，并与以往含铝生长的单晶进行对比；通过比较极微小缺陷对临界温度与临界场数值的影响来评估其超导性质。

Result: 极微小的缺陷（imperfections）对 UBe$_{13}$ 的临界温度和临界场产生剧烈影响，这一现象支持该材料的非常规超导本质；作者推测该效应很可能源于铀的氧化态，而氧化态对晶体环境高度敏感。

Conclusion: 铀基材料不仅是发现新非常规超导现象的重要来源，也为识别非常规超导体的能隙对称性提供了一条途径。

Abstract: The symmetry of the superconducting gap is related to the symmetry of the crystal structure. In unconventional superconductors, big changes of the critical temperature, critical field or even the gap structure can happen even for small perturbations of the lattice. In this letter, we use microstructuring to study aluminium-free crystals of UBe$_{13}$ which are expected to be closer to "perfect" material than previously studied aluminium-grown single crystals. We compare the effect of minuscule imperfections on the value of the critical temperature and critical field, which, in the case of UBe$_{13}$, has drastic effects, supporting its unconventional nature. We conjecture that this likely arises from the oxidation state of uranium, which is sensitive to its crystal environment. Our findings suggests that uranium-based materials provide not only an excellent reservoir of new unconventional phenomena, but also a way to identify the gap symmetry of unconventional superconductors.

</details>


### [97] [Superconductivity at the metal-insulator phase boundary in a bulk nickelate at ambient pressure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19686)
*Hyo-Bin Ahn,Xinglong Chen,Hong Zheng,Yu Zhang,Ramakanta Chapai,Yu Li,Arashdeep S. Thind,Robert F. Klie,Michael R. Norman,Ulrich Welp,J. F. Mitchell,Daniel Phelan*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该研究报告了块体镍酸盐（nickelate）单晶 (La1-xPrx)4Ni3O8 与 (La1-xYx)4Ni3O8 在常压下的超导迹象，超导出现在金属-绝缘体相边界附近金属相与电荷条纹相共存的窄窗口内，且表现为非渗流的低体积分数超导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 镍酸盐超导此前仅在薄膜或极端压力下的块体中实现，块体常压超导的探索是该领域的核心挑战。

Method: 通过用较小镧系离子（Pr、Y）取代 La，在具有方平面镍氧三层与类萤石（fluorite-like）间隔层交替结构的块体单晶中调控基态，并结合 TEM 观察共生缺陷。

Result: 母体化合物 La4Ni3O8 为绝缘基态，电子形成交织的绝缘电荷/自旋条纹；取代破坏该有序并最终产生金属基态，超导在金属相与电荷条纹相共存的相边界附近窄窗口出现，呈低体积分数、非渗流特征，TEM 中观察到近似已知无限层镍酸盐的共生缺陷。

Conclusion: Y 与 Pr 两个系列的电子相图在以类萤石间隔层体积为参数时重合，表明该空间位阻参数显著调制镍氧三层的电子结构；理解金属相与电荷有序相共存区是扩展块体常压镍酸盐超导的关键，间隔层工程（spacer-layer engineering）可作为探索该相竞争区间的设计工具。

Abstract: The discovery of superconductivity in nickelates has seeded a new field for exploring unconventional superconductivity in transition metal oxides. However, to date superconductivity has only been realized in thin films or under extreme pressure in the bulk. Here we report signatures of superconductivity at ambient pressure in bulk nickelate single crystals of (La1-xPrx)4Ni3O8 and (La1-xYx)4Ni3O8, whose crystal structure comprises interleaving trilayers of square-planar nickel oxide and fluorite-like spacer layers. The parent compound La4Ni3O8 exhibits an insulating ground state, where electrons order into intertwined, insulating charge/spin stripes. Substitution of smaller lanthanide ions disrupts this order, eventually leading to a metallic ground-state. We find that superconductivity emerges in a narrow window proximate to the insulator-metal phase boundary, where both metallic and charge-stripe phases co-exist. The observed low volume-fraction superconductivity is non-percolative, suggesting the prospect of filamentary superconductivity nucleated at the boundary between these phases. However, intergrowth defects that approximate the known infinite layer nickelates are observed in TEM, leaving open the possibility that superconductivity resides here rather than in the trilayer matrix. Remarkably, the electronic phase diagrams of both the Y and Pr series coincide when parameterized by the volume of the fluorite like spacer layers, revealing that this steric parameter profoundly modifies the nickel oxide trilayer electronic structure. Our results identify better understanding of the co-existence region between metallic and charge-ordered phases as a priority for expanding the range of bulk, ambient-pressure nickelate superconductivity and establish spacer-layer engineering as a design tool for exploring this regime of phase competition.

</details>


### [98] [Vortex-mediated spin current injection into two-dimensional superconductor NbSe2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19727)
*Meng Yang,Xiaolong Yin,Jingjing Liu,Yifeng Chen,Qinwu Gao,Hongxing Zhu,Danni Huang,Lang Chen,Shuo-Ying Yang,Junxue Li*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该工作在 NbSe2/LAFO 异质结中通过温度梯度与面内磁场下的热电测量，观测到涡旋介导的自旋流注入（vortex-mediated spin current injection），为涡旋作为自旋角动量载体提供了直接实验证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 向超导体注入纯自旋流（pure spin current）是超导自旋电子学（superconducting spintronics）的核心难题；既有研究多聚焦自旋极化准粒子与自旋三重态超电流，涡旋（vortex）虽被理论预言可携带自旋角动量，但长期缺乏直接实验证据。

Method: 采用 Ising 超导体/亚铁磁绝缘体（ferrimagnetic insulator）异质结构 NbSe2/LiAl2Fe3O8(LAFO)，在面外温度梯度与面内磁场下进行热电测量，并绘制磁场-温度相图以定位信号出现的相区。

Result: 在 NbSe2/LAFO 双层中于上临界磁场附近出现显著热电峰，其符号与单层 NbSe2 中常规涡旋能斯特信号（vortex Nernst signal）相反；该信号仅存在于 NbSe2 的涡旋液态（vortex liquid phase），且在超导转变温度以上消失。符号反转与自旋-涡度转换（spin-vorticity transmutation）及逆涡旋自旋霍尔效应（inverse vortex spin Hall effect）等理论机制一致。

Conclusion: 结果表明涡旋可作为超导体中高效的自旋信息载体，为涡旋介导的超导自旋电子器件开辟了新路径；同时通过相图定位与高温区信号缺失排除了准粒子贡献。

Abstract: Injection of pure spin current into superconductors remains a major challenge in superconducting spintronics. Previous studies have primarily focused on spin-polarized quasiparticles and spin-triplet supercurrents, while vortices, ubiquitous topological defects in type-2 superconductors, have been theoretically proposed as alternative carriers of spin angular momentum, yet direct experimental evidence is still lacking. Here, we report the vortex-mediated spin current injection in NbSe2/LiAl2Fe3O8(LAFO) bilayer, an Ising superconductor/ferrimagnetic insulator heterostructure. Under an out-of-plane temperature gradient and an in-plane magnetic field, the NbSe2/LAFO bilayer shows a pronounced thermoelectric peak near the upper critical magnetic field, which has the opposite sign to the conventional vortex Nernst signal observed in a single-layer NbSe2. The sign reversal suggests that the vortex flow induced by spin current injection is opposite to the flow driven by the temperature gradient, which is consistent with theoretical mechanisms including spin-vorticity transmutation and the inverse vortex spin Hall effect. By mapping the field temperature phase diagram, we reveal that the spin current injection occurs exclusively in the vortex liquid phase of NbSe2. Absence of the thermoelectric signal above the superconducting transition temperature further rules out the quasiparticle contribution. Our results establish vortices as efficient carriers of spin information in superconductors, opening a new route towards vortex-mediated superconducting spintronic devices.

</details>


### [99] [Generic thermodynamics of condensates with extremely small superfluid density ---Application to UTe$_2$ and hidden second phase transition---](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19834)
*Kazushige Machida*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 基于 UTe$_2$ 的核磁共振（NMR）与比热实验，理论刻画小超流密度凝聚体的热力学特征，构建贯穿 H-T-P 相空间的非幺正自旋三重态配对相图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期 UTe$_2$ 的核磁共振（NMR）与比热实验提示存在超流密度较小的凝聚态，其热力学特征与物理属性尚缺乏系统理论刻画，故本文围绕该问题展开探索。

Method: 本文采用理论分析手段，研究小超流密度凝聚体的热力学信号，并基于非幺正自旋三重态配对对称性（non-unitary spin-triplet pairing symmetry）$^3B_{3u}$ 构建相图描述。

Result: 识别出隐藏超导相 $A_2$（由自旋向上库珀对构成），其在常压下出现于较低温度 $T$ 与磁场 $H$；在 $a$、$b$、$c$ 三个主轴方向的高 $H$ 区 $A_2$ 均重现。$A_1$（自旋向下配对）到 $A_2$ 的相变线在 $H$-$T$ 平面均为水平，暗示源于自旋向上配对态随磁场增强而获得的磁能增益这一普适机制。

Conclusion: 该多相图可推广至压力（$P$）条件，得到以非幺正自旋三重态配对对称性 $^3B_{3u}$ 为基础的简洁图像，从而在完整的 $H$-$T$-$P$ 相空间内一致地描述超导相的演化。

Abstract: Motivated by recent experimental findings on UTe$_2$ via NMR and specific heat experiments, we theoretically explore the thermodynamic signatures of condensates with small superfluid density and characterize their physical properties. We identify the hidden superconducting phase $A_2$ composed of spin-up Cooper pairs
  at lower temperature ($T$) and field ($H$) under ambient pressure of UTe$_2$ and show that $A_2$ reappears at higher $H$ for all three principal field directions, $a$, $b$, and $c$-axis. The phase transition lines from the $A_1$ phase with spin down pairs to the $A_2$ phase in the $H$-$T$ plane are all horizontal in common, implying a universal physical origin due to the originating from the magnetic-energy gain of the spin-up pairing state with increasing magnetic field. This multiple-phase diagram is extended to these under pressure ($P$), resulting in a simple picture based on non-unitary spin-triplet pairing symmetry $^3B_{3u}$, which enables us to consistently capture the evolution of the superconducting phases throughout the entire $H$-$T$-$P$ phase space.

</details>


### [100] [Type-I superconductivity in a quasi-2D topologically nontrivial YbBi$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20093)
*Karolina Górnicka,Sudip Malick,Joanna Bławat,Michał Modrzejewski,Hanna Świątek,Michał J. Winiarski,Federico Mazzola,Ivana Vobornik,Chiara Bigi,Jacob Cook,Brenden R. Ortiz,Andrew F. May,Andrzej P. Kądzielawa,John Singleton,Bartlomiej Wiendlocha,Tomasz Klimczuk*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该文报道了层状稀土化合物 YbBi$_2$ 中低于 $T_c \sim$ 0.9 K 的体相第一类超导电性（type-I superconductivity），并结合量子振荡（quantum oscillations）与 ARPES 揭示其轻载流子、准二维费米面（quasi-two-dimensional Fermi surface）及非平凡拓扑特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在化学计量比（stoichiometric）材料中，本征超导与非平凡电子拓扑（nontrivial electronic topology）共存的情形仍属罕见，限制了对二者在同一电子体系中如何共存的实验研究。

Method: 采用热力学与输运测量确立超导基态，通过量子振荡提取回旋质量（cyclotron mass）与 Berry 相位（Berry phase），并与对称性保护的能带简并（band degeneracy）处的 Wilson 相位计算值对比，同时以 ARPES 对计算能带结构进行实验约束。

Result: 热力学与输运测量确认体相超导基态；量子振荡显示极轻载流子，回旋质量最低约 0.07 $m_e$，Berry 相位约 0.82 $\pi$，与对应轨道 Wilson 相位计算值 0.99 $\pi$ 接近；ARPES 结果与计算电子结构的关键特征吻合。

Conclusion: 本征第一类超导、轻的准二维载流子与非平凡电子拓扑特征的组合，使 YbBi$_2$ 成为一个独特的化学计量比平台，可用于研究拓扑非平凡电子环境中的超导电性。

Abstract: Intrinsic superconductivity in stoichiometric materials with nontrivial electronic topology remains uncommon, limiting opportunities to investigate how these two phenomena coexist within a single electronic system. Here, we report bulk type-I superconductivity below $T_c$ $\sim$ 0.9~K in YbBi$_2$, a layered rare-earth compound with a nonsymmorphic crystal structure and a quasi-two-dimensional Fermi surface. Thermodynamic and transport measurements establish the superconducting ground state, while quantum oscillations reveal exceptionally light carriers, with a cyclotron mass as low as 0.07 $m_e$, and a nonzero Berry phase of approximately 0.82 $π$. The latter closely matches the calculated value of the corresponding Wilson phase 0.99 $π$ for the corresponding orbit near a symmetry-protected band degeneracy. ARPES measurements show good agreement with key features of the calculated electronic structure, providing complementary experimental constraints on the normal-state band structure. The combination of intrinsic type-I superconductivity, light quasi-two-dimensional carriers, and signatures of nontrivial electronic topology identifies YbBi$_2$ as a distinct stoichiometric platform for investigating superconductivity in a topologically nontrivial electronic environment.

</details>


### [101] [Superconductivity in Pb2-xBixPd Single Crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20364)
*S. Srivastava,R. P. Singh*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 通过化学取代（chemical substitution）合成 Pb₂₋ₓBiₓPd 单晶，研究 Pb/Bi 混合对超导电性的调控，发现随 Bi 含量增加发生结构相变并伴随晶格单调膨胀，掺杂区间内所有超导样品均为弱耦合第二类超导体（weakly-coupled type-II superconductor）且具各向同性 s 波超导能隙（isotropic s-wave superconducting gap）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 化学取代被视为调控结构与超导性质、并探究相关材料中超导电性演化的有效途径。本文关注潜在拓扑超导体（topological superconductor）Pb₂Pd 与 β-Bi₂Pd 中 Pb/Bi 混合对超导电性的影响。

Method: 合成并表征 Pb₂₋ₓBiₓPd（0.2 ≤ x ≤ 1.8）单晶，通过改变 Bi 含量考察其结构与超导行为。

Result: Bi 含量增加触发结构相变：由非对称型（non-symmorphic，I4/mcm，x < 1）转变为层状（layered，I4/mmm，x ≥ 1），并引起晶格参数单调膨胀。整个掺杂范围内所有超导样品均表现为弱耦合第二类超导体，具有各向同性 s 波超导能隙。

Conclusion: Pb/Bi 混合可有效调控 Pb₂Pd 与 β-Bi₂Pd 体系的结构与超导电性，所观测到的弱耦合第二类超导与各向同性 s 波能隙特征在该掺杂区间内保持一致。

Abstract: Chemical substitution provides an effective route to tune the structural and superconducting properties and to explore the evolution of superconductivity across related materials. In this work, we investigate the effect of Pb/Bi mixing on superconductivity in possible topological superconductors \ch{Pb2Pd} and $β$-\ch{Bi2Pd}, by synthesizing and characterizing single crystals of \ch{Pb_{2-x}Bi_{x}Pd} ($0.2\le x\le1.8$). Increasing Bi content triggers a structural phase transition from non-symmorphic ($I4/mcm$, $x<1$) to layered ($I4/mmm$, $x\ge1$) and induces a monotonic expansion of the lattice parameters. Across the doped range, all superconducting samples behave as weakly-coupled type-II superconductors exhibiting isotropic s-wave superconducting gaps.

</details>
