### [132] [Bad metallicity in the semi-quantum regime of the Hubbard model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19264)
*Evyatar Tulipman,Vadim Oganesyan,Thomas P. Devereaux,Steven A. Kivelson*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用无限 $U$ Hubbard 模型（infinite-$U$ Hubbard model）与小空穴密度下的精确对角化（exact diagonalization），检验坏金属（bad metal）$T$-线性电阻率是否源于强局域关联（strong local correlations）的普适表现。结果发现一个类似液氦"半量子区"（semi-quantum regime）的中间温区：压缩率近似 $T$ 无关，但电阻率呈 $T$-线性且超越 Ioffe–Regel 极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 坏金属中超越 Ioffe–Regel 极限（Ioffe–Regel limit）的近似 $T$-线性直流电阻率出现在基态迥异的多种体系中，这一普适性提示该行为可能是强局域关联的共同体现。作者据此假设出发，试图在最小模型框架内验证该猜想。

Method: 采用无限 $U$ Hubbard 模型，在小空穴密度条件下，通过精确对角化（exact diagonalization）计算有限团簇的热力学与输运性质，并追踪降温过程中的行为演化。

Result: 降温过程中出现一个中间温区，其电子学类比为液氦的"半量子区"（semi-quantum regime）：量子效应导致压缩率（compressibility）近似与温度无关，而电阻率呈 $T$-线性并超过 Ioffe–Regel 极限。进入该温区伴随掺杂空穴周围形成准局域铁磁"自旋笼"（spin cages），为在涨落自旋背景中实现局域量子运动提供条件，类似液氦半量子区中控制非相干输运的瞬态晶笼。

Conclusion: 尽管模型极为简单，其呈现的坏金属行为与多种材料平台中的观测相似，支持坏金属输运是强局域关联普适表现的假设。

Abstract: Bad metals exhibit approximately $T$-linear dc resistivity beyond the Ioffe--Regel limit. That this behavior occurs in systems with radically different ground states suggests it is a generic manifestation of strong local correlations. We test this hypothesis in the infinite-$U$ Hubbard model with small hole densities using exact diagonalization to compute thermodynamic and transport properties of finite clusters. Upon cooling, we find an intermediate temperature range, an electronic analogue of the ``semi-quantum regime'' of liquid helium, in which quantum effects produce a roughly $T$-independent compressibility, yet the resistivity is $T$-linear and exceeds the Ioffe--Regel limit. Entry into this regime is accompanied by the formation of quasi-local ferromagnetic ``spin cages'' around doped holes, regions that facilitate local quantum motion embedded in a fluctuating spin background, analogous to the transient crystalline cages thought to control incoherent transport in semi-quantum liquid helium. Remarkably, despite the simplicity of the model, the bad metal behavior found here resembles that seen in various material platforms.

</details>


### [133] [Non-Abelian Anyon Condensation: a Path-Integral Monte Carlo Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19282)
*Rafael Flores-Calderón,Frank Pollmann,Michael Knap*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作为 Kitaev 量子双模型 $\mathcal D(G)$ 构建了无符号问题（sign-free）的路径积分框架，借助非可逆（non-invertible）1-形式对称性刻画任意子凝聚，并给出 $G=S_3$ 的量子蒙特卡洛结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非阿贝尔拓扑序（non-Abelian topological order）的相变在微观量子模型中难以描述，原因在于缺乏可在大尺度保持数值可解性的方法。

Method: 将单键微扰按非可逆的电、磁 1-形式对称性（1-form symmetries）组织；对任意有限群 $G$ 构造精确的"物质化"等距映射（matterization isometry），引入顶点自由度，把仅含键变量的模型映射为 $G$ 规范—希格斯理论（gauge–Higgs theory）。基于不动点的 1-形式对称性定义广义 Fredenhagen–Marcu 序参量，并以量子蒙特卡洛（quantum Monte Carlo）进行模拟。

Result: 对 $G=S_3$，增殖非阿贝尔电任意子导致一级相变，其中所有非平凡电任意子发生凝聚；在纯磁极限下模型退化为 $(2+1)$ 维纯 $G$ 规范理论，$G=S_3$ 时表现为一级禁闭相变，由 Wilson 环面积律（Wilson-loop area law）的出现和涌现磁 1-形式对称性的恢复所诊断。

Conclusion: 该框架统一描述了非阿贝尔任意子凝聚、禁闭（confinement）与广义对称性破缺的数值行为，为相关拓扑相变提供了可扩展的计算途径。

Abstract: Transitions out of non-Abelian topological order are difficult to describe in microscopic quantum models with numerical methods that remain tractable at large scales. We develop a sign-free path-integral framework for Kitaev quantum doubles $\mathcal D(G)$ by organizing single-link perturbations in terms of non-invertible electric and magnetic 1-form symmetries that proliferate distinct anyon species. For any finite group $G$, an exact $\textit{matterization}$ isometry introduces vertex degrees of freedom and maps the link-only model onto a $G$ gauge--Higgs theory. Furthermore, the 1-form symmetries of the fixed point allow us to define generalized Fredenhagen--Marcu order parameters that become finite when the corresponding anyons condense. For $G = S_3$, quantum Monte Carlo simulations show that proliferating a non-Abelian electric anyon drives a first-order transition in which all nontrivial electric anyons condense. In the purely magnetic limit, the model reduces to a $(2+1)$D pure $G$ gauge theory; for $G=S_3$, it exhibits a first-order confinement transition, diagnosed by the onset of a Wilson-loop area law and the restoration of an emergent magnetic 1-form symmetry. These results provide a unified numerical framework for non-Abelian anyon condensation, confinement, and generalized symmetry breaking.

</details>


### [134] [Valley-Helical Superconductivity Driven by Repulsion and Quantum Geometry: Applications to Time-Reversal Symmetric Rhombohedral Graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19283)
*Julian May-Mann,Trithep Devakul,Gal Shavit*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在无谷极化（valley polarization）与时间反演对称破缺（time-reversal symmetry breaking）条件下，过屏蔽排斥的 Kohn-Luttinger 机制可在两谷中诱导手性相反的手性配对（chiral pairing），形成谷螺旋态（valley-helical state），并在中等耦合下胜过常规谷间配对态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期在谷极化石墨烯与过渡金属二硫族化合物体系中观测到手性超导（chiral superconductivity）的实验迹象，促使本文考察手性配对是否必须依赖谷极化或时间反演对称破缺。

Method: 针对两谷具有相反 Berry 曲率（Berry curvature）的体系，采用过屏蔽排斥（overscreened repulsion，即 Kohn-Luttinger 机制）分析配对；并使用时间反演对称的菱面体石墨烯（rhombohedral graphene）现实模型进行计算，层数取 $4\leq N\leq 8$。

Result: 过屏蔽排斥可在每个谷中诱导手性相反的配对，形成谷螺旋态；该态在中等耦合下可超越更常规的谷间配对态，尽管仅后者具有弱耦合不稳定性。在现实模型中，谷螺旋超导在较宽的填充（filling）与位移场（displacement field）范围内占主导，且适用于所考察的全部层数。

Conclusion: 谷螺旋态可表现出拓扑投影的边缘模式（topologically projected edge modes）、异常磁响应以及交织的 Kekulé 型键序（Kekulé-like bond-order），这些特征可用于将其与更常规的超导体区分。

Abstract: Motivated by the recent experimental signatures of chiral superconductivity in valley-polarized graphene- and transition-metal-dichalcogenide-based systems, we investigate the possibility of chiral pairing in the absence of valley polarization or time-reversal symmetry breaking. For systems with opposite Berry curvature in the two valleys, we find that overscreened repulsion (i.e., the Kohn-Luttinger mechanism) can indeed induce pairing of opposite chiralities in each valley, producing a valley-helical state. Moreover, the valley-helical state can outcompete more conventional intervalley paired states at intermediate coupling, despite only the latter having a weak-coupling instability. Using a realistic model of time-reversal-symmetric rhombohedral graphene, we find extended ranges of fillings and displacement fields with dominant valley-helical superconductivity. This occurs for all layer numbers considered here, $4\leq N\leq 8$. We show that the valley-helical states can display topologically projected edge modes, unusual magnetic responses, and intertwined Kekulé-like bond-order; features that can be used to distinguish them from more conventional superconductors.

</details>


### [135] [Energy partitioning of wave packets in one-dimensional systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19450)
*Flavia B. Ramos,Imke Schneider,Sebastian Eggert,Rodrigo G. Pereira*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用含时密度矩阵重整化群（time-dependent density-matrix renormalization group, tDMRG）研究一维临界费米子系统（critical fermionic system）中高斯波包的实时演化，观察到相互作用导致的波包分数化（fractionalization）为左右反向传播模式，并结合 Luttinger 液体理论（Luttinger liquid theory）建立能量分配的场论预测与数值模拟的定量比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探究一维临界费米子体系中非平衡波包的能量如何在涌现激发（emergent excitations）之间分配，并厘清其与已被广泛研究的电荷分数化（charge fractionalization）之间的差异。

Method: 方法上，在多体基态（many-body ground state）之上注入高斯波包，采用含时密度矩阵重整化群追踪其实时演化；同时运用 Luttinger 液体理论推导涌现模式所携带能量的解析预测。

Result: 结果显示，相互作用使初始激发分数化为反向传播的左行与右行模式，其能量可在实空间分辨；在低能区（low-energy regime），场论预测与数值模拟吻合良好。与完全由 Luttinger 液体参数（Luttinger liquid parameter）决定的电荷分数化不同，能量分配是非普适的（non-universal），取决于注入波包的宽度等细节。

Conclusion: 结论是本文提供了一维系统中能量分配的实空间刻画，并建立了非平衡数值模拟与有效场论描述之间的定量比较。

Abstract: We investigate the non-equilibrium dynamics of wave packets in a one-dimensional critical fermionic system and analyze the resulting partitioning of energy between emergent excitations. Starting from a Gaussian wave packet injected on top of the many-body ground state, we follow its real-time evolution using the time-dependent density-matrix renormalization group. We observe that interactions lead to fractionalization of the initial excitation into counter-propagating left- and right-moving modes, whose energies can be resolved in real space. To interpret these results, we employ Luttinger liquid theory, which allows us to derive analytical predictions for the energy carried by the emergent modes. We find good agreement between field-theoretical predictions and numerical simulations in the low-energy regime. In contrast to charge fractionalization, which is completely determined by the Luttinger liquid parameter, we show that energy partitioning is non-universal and depends on details of the injected wave packet, such as its width. Our results provide a real-space characterization of energy partitioning in one-dimensional systems and establish a quantitative comparison between non-equilibrium numerical simulations and the effective field-theory description.

</details>


### [136] [Universality of the $1/9$ Magnetization Plateau and Quantum-Disordered States in the Kagome Family $\mathrm{Cs_8AB_3Ti_{12}F_{48}}$ ($A=\mathrm{Rb},\mathrm{Li}$; $B=\mathrm{K},\mathrm{Na}$)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19572)
*Prena Chaudhary,Asiri Thennakoon,Tommy Park,Hanru Wang,Leshan Zhao,Laurel Winter,Neil Herrison,Christina Hoffmann,Junghong H. He,Harald O. Jeschke,Hiroyuki Nojiri,Akira Matsuo,Koichi Kindo,Miwako Takahashi,Yukio Noda,Taku J. Sato,Shiyan Li,Hiroaki Ueda,Gia-Wei Chern,Seung-Hun Lee*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该摘要通过化学压力（chemical pressure）调控钛基笼目（kagome）家族 Cs₈AB₃Ti₁₂F₄₈，揭示 1/9 磁化平台（magnetization plateau）与 1/3 平台不必共享同一微观起源，且 1/9 平台可能是阻挫自旋-1/2 笼目磁性的更普适特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋-1/2 笼目反铁磁体（spin-1/2 kagome antiferromagnet）低场 1/9 磁化平台的微观起源长期未解。作者意在通过改变晶格尺度来辨析 1/9 与 1/3 平台之间的关系及其与量子无序基态（quantum-disordered ground state）的关联。

Method: 采用最高 60 T 的高场磁化测量，结合比热（specific-heat）测量与第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations），对 Cs₈AB₃Ti₁₂F₄₈（A=Rb, Li；B=K, Na）系列进行化学压力对比研究。

Result: 晶格膨胀的 Cs₈RbK₃Ti₁₂F₄₈ 与 Cs₈LiK₃Ti₁₂F₄₈ 出现稳健的 1/9 平台样相，却缺乏通常更稳健的 1/3 平台；被压缩的 Cs₈LiNa₃Ti₁₂F₄₈ 则既不呈现 1/9 平台样相，也无量子无序基态。晶格膨胀保持阻挫且全连通的笼目交换网络（exchange network）与无能隙量子无序基态，压缩则使交换网络重组为弱耦合准一维子系统（quasi-one-dimensional subsystems）并诱发连续磁相变（magnetic transitions）。

Conclusion: 1/9 与 1/3 平台无需共享共同微观起源；1/9 平台可能代表阻挫自旋-1/2 笼目磁性的更普适特征。

Abstract: The microscopic origin of the low-field $1/9$ magnetization plateau in spin-$1/2$ kagome antiferromagnets remains unresolved. Here, we show that chemical pressure reshapes the hierarchy of fractional magnetization plateaus in the titanium-based kagome family $\mathrm{Cs_8AB_3Ti_{12}F_{48}}$ ($A=\mathrm{Rb},\mathrm{Li}$; $B=\mathrm{K},\mathrm{Na}$). High-field magnetization measurements up to 60 T reveal a robust $1/9$ plateau-like phase in the expanded $\mathrm{Cs_8RbK_3Ti_{12}F_{48}}$ and $\mathrm{Cs_8LiK_3Ti_{12}F_{48}}$ compounds, despite the absence of the conventionally more robust $1/3$ plateau. In contrast, compressed $\mathrm{Cs_8LiNa_3Ti_{12}F_{48}}$ exhibits neither the $1/9$ plateau-like phase nor a quantum-disordered ground state. Specific-heat measurements and first-principles calculations show that lattice expansion preserves a frustrated, fully connected kagome exchange network and gapless quantum-disordered ground states, whereas compression reorganizes the exchange network into weakly coupled quasi-one-dimensional subsystems and induces successive magnetic transitions. These results demonstrate that the $1/9$ and $1/3$ plateaus need not share a common microscopic origin and suggest that the $1/9$ plateau may represent a more universal feature of frustrated spin-$1/2$ kagome magnetism.

</details>


### [137] [Vestigial chirality from fluctuating loop currents on the kagome lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19618)
*Yin Shi,Xuande Bu,Sheng Meng*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究 kagome 格子上公度三-$Q$ 环流序（loop-current order）的熔化行为，提出复合序可通过分阶段熔化留下残余序的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多分量序（multicomponent order）可能分阶段熔化，在主要成分变为短程后残留复合序。作者希望在 kagome 格子的公度三-$Q$ 环流序中检验这一可能性，并为 kagome 金属中报道的临界温度以上的时间反演奇响应提供热力学框架。

Method: 采用固定幅度符号模型（fixed-amplitude sign model）进行大规模团簇蒙特卡洛与并行回火蒙特卡洛（parallel-tempering Monte Carlo）模拟，并结合幅度分辨的 Ginzburg–Landau 理论做有限尺寸标度分析。

Result: 模拟揭示了直接熔化与两阶段熔化两种区域。两阶段区域中，平移扇区畴壁（translation-sector domain walls）先于手性改变畴壁（chirality-changing walls）增殖，从而恢复晶格平移对称性而保留长程时间反演奇序；中间相中主要 $M$ 点关联为短程。两阶段区域出现在最低能手性改变畴壁仅比同手性平移畴壁贵约 12–13% 时。有限尺寸标度表明该相在幅度弛豫下依然稳定。

Conclusion: 结果确立了一个定量的畴壁判据（domain-wall criterion）用于残余环流序，并给出无需长程环流布拉格序即可破缺时间反演对称性的涨落路径，为 kagome 金属中常规电荷密度波（charge-density-wave）有序临界温度以上的时间反演奇响应提供了可能的热力学框架。

Abstract: Multicomponent order can melt in stages, leaving a composite order after its primary constituents become short ranged. We study this possibility for commensurate three-$Q$ loop-current order on the kagome lattice. Large-scale cluster and parallel-tempering Monte Carlo simulations reveal direct and two-stage melting regimes in an effective fixed-amplitude sign model. In the latter regime, translation-sector domain walls proliferate before chirality-changing walls, restoring lattice translational symmetry while preserving long-range time-reversal-odd order. The primary $M$-point correlations are short ranged in this intermediate phase. The two-stage regime begins when the lowest-energy chirality-changing wall is only about $12$--$13\%$ more costly than a same-chirality translation wall. Finite-size scaling of a stable amplitude-resolved Ginzburg--Landau theory shows that the phase survives amplitude relaxation. Our results establish a quantitative domain-wall criterion for vestigial loop-current order and a fluctuation route to time-reversal symmetry breaking without long-range loop-current Bragg order. This separation provides a possible thermodynamic framework for time-reversal-odd responses recently reported above the critical temperature for conventional charge-density-wave ordering in kagome metals.

</details>


### [138] [Fractional quantum spin Hall crystals from hidden density-wave order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19638)
*Matthew Shammami,Sudip Chakravarty*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出破缺对称性（broken symmetry）与拓扑（topology）可互为因果，并以混合单-三重态 d 密度波为例，展示其隐藏键序如何生成时间反演配对的陈能带，并在分数填充下转变为分数量子自旋霍尔液体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 破缺对称性与拓扑通常被视为描述物质自组织的两种独立方式，本文旨在探讨二者之间的生成关系，并寻找兼具对称性破缺与拓扑特征的物态。

Method: 以粒子-空穴凝聚体（particle-hole condensate）形式的混合单-三重态 d 密度波（mixed singlet-triplet d-density wave）为出发点，结合有限尺寸精确对角化（exact diagonalization）研究分数填充下的相互作用效应。

Result: 该 d 密度波具有破坏平移对称性的隐藏键序（hidden bond order），在不产生常规电荷或自旋密度序的情况下生成时间反演配对的陈能带（Chern bands）；在奇分母分数填充下，排斥相互作用可将其转化为分数量子自旋霍尔液体（fractional quantum spin Hall liquid），其相反手性导致电荷流传输自旋、自旋流传输电荷的交叉响应。

Conclusion: 该框架同时揭示了该拓扑相的边界：强自旋间耦合与更稀的填充倾向电荷序并凸显剩余能带色散的作用；半填充下自旋解耦极限给出均匀关联液体，其征兆与复合费米液体（composite Fermi liquid）物理一致。

Abstract: Broken symmetry and topology are distinct descriptions for how matter organizes itself. Here, we propose one gives rise to the other. A mixed singlet-triplet $d$-density wave, which is a particle-hole condensate, has a hidden bond order that breaks translation symmetry and generates a time-reversed pair of Chern bands, without producing conventional charge or spin density order. At fractional fillings with odd denominators, finite size exact diagonalization studies show how repulsive interactions can transform this symmetry-broken topological state into a fractional quantum spin Hall liquid. Its opposite chiralities produce a crossed response in which charge flux transports spin, and spin flux transports charge. The same setting also reveals what borders such a phase. Strong interspin coupling and increasingly dilute fillings favor charge order and make residual band dispersion decisive, while at half filling, the spin-decoupled limit gives a homogeneous correlated liquid with signatures consistent with the physics of a composite Fermi liquid.

</details>


### [139] [When Is Kramers-Wannier Duality Invertible?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20090)
*Akash Sinha,Pramod Padmanabhan,Vladimir Korepin*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文研究量子伊辛链（quantum Ising chain）的 Kramers-Wannier 对偶（Kramers-Wannier duality）在有限希尔伯特空间（finite Hilbert space）上何时可实现为可逆（invertible）变换，并以伊辛键代数（Ising bond algebra）的表示为判定依据给出充要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子伊辛链的 Kramers-Wannier 对偶在有限周期链上自然表现为不可逆变换（noninvertible transformation），但引入对称扭曲扇区（symmetry-twisted sectors）后似可提升为可逆酉变换（invertible unitary transformation）。本文旨在厘清这一提升何时成立，即对偶可逆实现的条件及其代数根源。

Method: 作者在有限希尔伯特空间框架下，借助伊辛键代数的表示（representation）进行讨论，并以其两个中心元（central elements）为判据，建立对偶具备可逆实现的充要条件（necessary and sufficient condition）。该表示论判据将传统的不可逆实现与含对称扭曲扇区的可逆构造统一处理，并可推广至具有相同底层键代数的其他模型。

Result: 文章证明 Kramers-Wannier 对偶能否在有限希尔伯特空间上可逆实现，由伊辛键代数的表示决定，并给出基于两个中心元的充要条件。作为具体例子，作者在伊辛键代数的一个非平凡实现中找到了一个有序-无序对偶（order-disorder duality），该对偶允许可逆的 Kramers-Wannier 对偶。

Conclusion: Kramers-Wannier 对偶的可逆性并非普适，而是取决于底层键代数表示的性质；该表示论判据统一了不可逆与可逆两类实现，并可扩展至共享同一键代数的其他模型，同时揭示了非平凡实现中新型有序-无序对偶的存在。

Abstract: {\it Kramers-Wannier} duality of the quantum Ising chain is naturally realized as a noninvertible transformation on a finite periodic chain. Upon incorporating appropriate symmetry-twisted sectors, however, the duality can be promoted to an invertible unitary transformation. In this Letter, we classify when the Kramers-Wannier duality admits an invertible realization on a finite Hilbert space. We show that this is determined by the representation of the Ising bond algebra and establish a necessary and sufficient condition in terms of its two central elements. This representation-theoretic criterion unifies the conventional noninvertible realization with invertible constructions involving symmetry-twisted sectors and applies equally to other models with the same underlying bond algebra. As an explicit example, we find an order-disorder duality in a non-trivial realization of the Ising bond algebra that admits an invertible Kramers-Wannier duality.

</details>


### [140] [Pressure induced structural phase transition and magneto-elastic coupling in Eu doped LaCrO$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19795)
*Asish Kumar Mishra,Mrinmay Sahu,Bhagyashri Giri,Bidisha Mukherjee,Suvashree Mukherjee,Harekrishna Bhunia,Tamalkanti Mukherjee,Sujoy Ghosh,Partha Mitra,Peter Liermann,Goutam Dev Mukherjee*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过同步辐射X射线衍射、微区拉曼光谱与低温磁化测量，研究了5% Eu掺杂LaCrO₃（ELCO）的高压结构-磁性关联，发现约10.6 GPa发生正交到菱方结构相变，约4.5 GPa出现拉曼异常，并预测存在压力诱导的反铁磁基态变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在通过高压手段关联Eu掺杂LaCrO₃的结构与磁性行为，并与未掺杂LaCrO₃（LCO）的已有结果对比，以理解压力对磁基态的影响。

Method: 采用同步辐射X射线衍射（synchrotron X-ray diffraction, XRD）、微区拉曼光谱（micro Raman spectroscopy）以及低温磁化测量（low-temperature magnetization measurements）进行高压研究。

Result: 高压XRD显示约10.6 GPa处发生正交（orthorhombic）到菱方（rhombohedral）结构相变，高压拉曼数据予以佐证并表明低频软模（soft modes）相关的晶格不稳定性在驱动该相变中起关键作用；此外在约4.5 GPa处多个拉曼模式的频移与积分强度出现显著异常。

Conclusion: 结合低温磁化测量、XRD与拉曼异常，并与LaCrO₃的类似结果比较，预测存在压力诱导的磁基态向反铁磁（antiferromagnetic）有序的转变。

Abstract: In this study, we have carried out a detailed high pressure investigation on 5$\%$ Eu doped LaCrO$_3$ (ELCO) using synchrotron X-ray diffraction (XRD), micro Raman spectroscopy, and low-temperature magnetization measurements to correlate the structural and magnetic properties under pressure. The high pressure XRD reveals the orthorhombic to rhombohedral structural phase transition around 10.6 GPa. The high pressure Raman data corroborate this result and indicate that the lattice instabilities associated with the low-frequency soft modes play a crucial role in driving this transition. In addition, a pronounced anomaly in the Raman shift and integrated intensity of several Raman modes is observed around 4.5 GPa. By combining the low-temperature magnetization measurements with the anomalies observed in the XRD and Raman data and by comparing with the similar results in LaCrO$_3$ (LCO), a pressure-induced change in the magnetic ground state to antiferromagnetic ordering is predicted.

</details>


### [141] [Lyapunov-controlled thermalization: an exact real-time example](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20597)
*Jonas Loy,Jan C. Louw*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文证明：驱动停止后满足KMS关系并不足以判定系统达到平衡态。在大N、大q的SYK淬火协议中，尽管淬火后费米子关联函数完全热化，第二次淬火回溯却能揭示初始态的隐藏记忆，其衰减率即Lyapunov指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验“满足Kubo-Martin-Schwinger（KMS）关系即可证明平衡”这一常见判据是否充分。作者希望说明在相互作用多体系统中，热化（thermalization）与真正平衡之间存在可观测差异，需要一个非平衡判据。

Method: 采用大N、大q极限下的Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev（SYK）模型淬火协议（quench protocol）。首先对系统进行驱动后使其停止，检验KMS关系；随后实施第二次淬火回到初始哈密顿量，并考察连接第一次淬火之前时刻的关联函数。该极限下可获得完全精确的非平衡（nonequilibrium, NEQ）实时演化。

Result: 所有淬火后费米子关联函数在精确意义上呈现热性，但第二次淬火揭示了初始态的隐藏记忆，该记忆编码于跨越第一次淬火时刻的关联函数中。此记忆构成一个广延的非平衡见证量（NEQ witness），其衰减率为Lyapunov指数λ_L，即状态变得与Gibbs态有效不可区分所需的速率。

Conclusion: KMS关系的满足并不能证明系统已真正达到平衡；热化状态仍可保留初始态的隐藏记忆。Lyapunov指数λ_L决定了系统有效不可区分于Gibbs态的速率。由于大N、大q极限下非平衡实时演化可精确求解，该设置为解析研究热化提供了理想平台。

Abstract: A verified Kubo-Martin-Schwinger (KMS) relation, after a drive has ceased, is not sufficient evidence to prove equilibrium. We demonstrate this in a large-$N$ large-$q$ Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev (SYK) quench protocol. Although all post-quench fermion correlators are exactly thermal, a second quench back to the initial Hamiltonian reveals hidden memory of the initial state. It is encoded in correlators connecting to times before the first quench. This memory is an extensive nonequilibrium (NEQ) witness with its decay rate being the Lyapunov exponent $λ_L$. Thus $λ_L$ sets the rate at which the state becomes effectively indistinguishable from a Gibbs state. Despite being a unitary interacting many-body system, the complete NEQ real-time evolution is obtained exactly in the large-$N$, large-$q$ limit, making the setup ideal for analytically studying thermalization.

</details>


### [142] [Tunable flat band on the surface of a rhombohedral kagome ferromagnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19857)
*Xinglu Que,Qingyu He,Lihui Zhou,Lilian Prodan,Vladimir Tsurkan,István Kézsmárki,Hidenori Takagi,Dennis Huang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过扫描隧道显微镜（STM）研究层状kagome铁磁体Fe$_3$Sn$_2$，发现其费米能级附近存在平带，但该平带并非源于层内跳跃的破坏性干涉，而是由菱面体堆垛kagome面间的层间跳跃导致，且局限于表面层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索kagome基材料的一个核心目标是实现带宽为meV量级且靠近费米能级的平带；主流假设认为平带源于kagome晶格上跳跃过程的破坏性干涉（destructive hopping）。

Method: 对层状kagome铁磁体Fe$_3$Sn$_2$进行扫描隧道显微镜（scanning tunneling microscopy, STM）测量，并结合第一性原理平板计算（first-principles slab calculations）分析其能带来源与磁场响应。

Result: Fe$_3$Sn$_2$费米能级附近确实存在平带，表现为微分隧道电导中的尖锐峰；计算表明该平带由菱面体堆垛（rhombohedral-stacked）kagome面间的层间跳跃产生，局限于表面层，位于布里渊区角点$\bar{K}$与$\bar{K'}$附近，并呈现丰富的磁场依赖，包括旋转Fe磁矩导致的谷对称性破缺精细结构，以及与轨道磁矩相关的持续抗磁位移（diamagnetic shift），均被计算复现。

Conclusion: 层堆垛方式对kagome磁体能带结构具有关键作用，实验上展示了在拓扑磁体原子级薄层体积中生成磁可调平带的另一条机制。

Abstract: A central goal in the exploration of kagome-based materials is the realization of a flat band that has meV bandwidth and lies close to the Fermi energy. The prevailing assumption is that band flattening originates from destructive hopping processes on the kagome lattice. We perform scanning tunneling microscopy (STM) on the layered kagome ferromagnet Fe$_3$Sn$_2$ and show that it indeed hosts a flat band near the Fermi energy, which is manifested as a sharp peak in the differential tunneling conductance. First-principles slab calculations reveal, however, that this band is flattened not by the destructive interference of intralayer hopping, but by interlayer hopping between rhombohedral-stacked kagome planes in Fe$_3$Sn$_2$, and is confined to the surface layer. This surface band, forming in the vicinity of the Brillouin zone corners $\bar{K}$ and $\bar{K'}$, exhibits rich magnetic-field dependence, including fine structure due to valley-symmetry breaking by rotated Fe moments, as well as a persistent diamagnetic shift associated with orbital magnetic moments, all reproduced by our calculations. Our results, highlighting the crucial role of layer stacking on the band structure of kagome magnets, demonstrate experimentally an alternative mechanism of generating magnetically tunable flat bands in atomically thin volumes of topological magnets.

</details>


### [143] [Statistics, 't Hooft Anomaly, and the Else-Nayak Index: a careful comparison of concepts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20813)
*Hanyu Xue*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文指出现有将广义对称性、拓扑激发与对称性反常（symmetry anomaly）关联的做法存在隐患：格点截断所得的对称性斑块算符（symmetry patch operator）虽在几何上类似产生拓扑激发的跃迁算符（hopping operator），却无法给出良定义的统计（statistics）或保证与反常一致。作者提出更稳健的判据——拓扑激发的跃迁算符应对称，即与对称变换对易，并在适当假设下给出统计与反常的一一对应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学界普遍认为广义对称性（generalized symmetries）、拓扑激发、对称性反常与拓扑激发的统计之间存在关联，但该关联的建立方式存在陷阱。摘要指出，仅凭几何类比不足以确定统计性质，也不保证与对称性反常相符，因此需要一个更自然、更可靠的关联框架。

Method: 提出以“拓扑激发的跃迁算符是对称的”（即与对称变换对易）作为核心条件，并在适当假设下建立统计与反常的一一对应。此外，将边界物质（boundary matter）与高一维的 DW 规范场（DW gauge field）耦合，从规范化（gauging）视角解释该关系：跃迁算符本质上是规范化后作用于物理自由度的规范不变算符；固定规范场背景后，全局对称性来自保持该背景的规范变换，而跃迁的对称性条件正是剩余的规范不变性。

Result: 在适当假设下，跃迁算符的对称性条件给出了拓扑激发统计与对称性反常之间的一一对应（one-to-one correspondence）。通过高一维 DW 规范场的规范化视角，该对应得到解释：跃迁算符对应规范化后的规范不变算符，其对称性条件等价于剩余的规范不变性。

Conclusion: 该框架澄清了文献中关于对称性、拓扑激发、反常与统计之间关系的潜在误解，为比较对称性与拓扑激发提供了更可靠的框架。

Abstract: Generalized symmetries and topological excitations, as well as symmetry anomalies and the statistics of topological excitations, are widely believed to be related. There are, however, pitfalls in how this relation is established. A lattice truncation of a symmetry transformation to a finite patch gives a symmetry patch operator that creates symmetry defects at its boundary. This geometric picture resembles a hopping operator creating topological excitations at the boundary of its support, but does not provide well-defined statistics, let alone guarantee agreement with the symmetry anomaly. A more natural and robust relation is that the hopping operators of topological excitations are symmetric: they commute with symmetry transformations. Under suitable assumptions, this condition yields a one-to-one correspondence between statistics and anomalies. We further couple boundary matter to a DW gauge field in one higher dimension to explain this relation from the perspective of gauging. A hopping operator is, in essence, a gauge-invariant operator acting on the physical degrees of freedom after gauging. Once the gauge-field background is fixed, global symmetry comes from gauge transformations that preserve that background, while the symmetric condition on hopping is precisely the remaining gauge invariance. This distinction clarifies potential misconceptions in the literature and provides a more reliable framework for comparing symmetries and topological excitations.

</details>


### [144] [Corner entanglement scaling with projected entangled pair states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20020)
*Chloé Van Bastelaere,Rui-Zhen Huang,Laurens Vanderstraeten*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出一种可直接在热力学极限下从投影纠缠对态（projected entangled pair states, PEPS）中提取角纠缠熵（corner entanglement entropy）的方法，并用于二维量子临界点诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠缠标度（entanglement scaling）是探测量子临界点普适性质的有力手段；二维中角状二分（corner-shaped bipartition）产生的贡献具有由共形场论（conformal field theory）决定的普适标度行为，但需有效提取手段。

Method: 发展了一种直接在热力学极限（thermodynamic limit）下从投影纠缠对态中提取角纠缠熵的方法。

Result: 在量子临界点模型上，角贡献随有效关联长度（effective correlation length）呈现标度行为，与有限纠缠标度（finite-entanglement scaling）假设一致；角系数结果与其他方法相符。

Conclusion: 该方法可高效诊断二维强关联量子临界点（strongly-correlated quantum critical points）。

Abstract: Entanglement scaling provides a powerful probe of universal properties at quantum critical points. In two dimensions, contributions originating from a corner-shaped bipartition exhibit a universal scaling, which is determined by the underlying conformal field theory. We develop a method to extract this corner entanglement entropy from projected entangled pair states directly in the thermodynamic limit. When applied to models at a quantum critical point, we show that the corner contribution exhibits scaling with the effective correlation length, in agreement with the hypothesis of finite-entanglement scaling. Our results for the corner coefficients are consistent with other methods, demonstrating the efficiency of our method for diagnosing strongly-correlated quantum critical points in two dimensions.

</details>


### [145] [Pressure-induced electronic and structural evolution of EuIrGe3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20376)
*N. S. Dhami,V. Baledent,I. Batistic,C. M. N. Kumar,D. Kaczorowski,L. Nataf,J. M. Ablett,J. -P. Rueff,J. P. Itie,P. Fertey,S. R. Shieh,P. Popcevic,Y. Utsumi Boucher*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过 X 射线吸收谱、同步辐射 X 射线衍射（同步辐射 X-ray diffraction）、密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）计算与电阻率测量，研究了非中心对称（noncentrosymmetric）BaNiSn3 型反铁磁体 EuIrGe3 的压力诱导电子与晶体结构演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在揭示 EuTGe3 家族中过渡金属 d 电子态（d-electron states）在压力诱导电子与结构演化中的作用，并厘清 EuIrGe3 与 EuCoGe3、EuRhGe3 压力响应的差异。

Method: 采用 X 射线吸收谱（x-ray absorption spectroscopy）测量 Eu L3 边，结合同步辐射 X 射线衍射与 DFT 计算，并辅以电阻率（electrical resistivity）测量。

Result: Eu L3 边谱显示 Eu 平均价态随压缩连续增加，并伴随 Ge 与 Ir 电子态改变；高压衍射表明四方（tetragonal, I4mm）相存在各向异性晶格压缩，并在 38 GPa 以上出现结构相变（structural phase transition）；实验晶格与状态方程（equation-of-state）参数与 DFT 计算吻合；电阻率显示反铁磁有序温度（antiferromagnetic ordering temperatures）在 18 GPa 内单调升高。

Conclusion: 尽管非磁性 Eu3+ 组态对中间价态（intermediate valence state）贡献增大，反铁磁基态仍保持稳健；EuIrGe3 的压力响应不同于 EuCoGe3 与 EuRhGe3，凸显过渡金属 d 电子态在 EuTGe3 家族压力诱导演化中的重要作用。

Abstract: We investigated the pressure-induced evolution of the electronic and crystal structure of the noncentrosymmetric BaNiSn3-type antiferromagnet EuIrGe3 using x-ray absorption spectroscopy, synchrotron x-ray diffraction complemented by density functional theory calculations, and electrical resistivity measurements. The Eu L3-edge spectra reveal a continuous increase in the mean Eu valence under compression, accompanied by modifications of the Ge and Ir electronic states. High- pressure x-ray diffraction shows anisotropic lattice compression in the tetragonal (I4mm) phase and provides evidence for a structural phase transition above 38 GPa. The experimentally deter- mined lattice and equation-of-state parameters are in good agreement with the DFT calculations. Electrical resistivity measurements reveal a monotonic increase in the antiferromagnetic ordering temperatures up to 18 GPa, indicating that the antiferromagnetic ground state remains robust despite the increasing contribution of the nonmagnetic Eu3+ configuration to the intermediate va- lence state. These results demonstrate that EuIrGe3 exhibits a pressure response distinct from EuCoGe3 and EuRhGe3, highlighting the important role of the transition metal d-electron states in the pressure-induced electronic and structural evolution of the EuT Ge3 family

</details>


### [146] [Emergent Surface Kondo Flat Band Driven by Competing Interactions in a Topological Ferromagnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20432)
*Nazar Zaremba,Susmita Changdar,Haojie Guo,Iñigo Robredo,Daniel Lozano-Gomez,Andreas Leithe-Jasper,Rui Lou,Yurii Prots,Mitja Krnel,Markus König,Konstantin Semeniuk,Berit H. Goodge,Yuri Grin,Andrii Kuibarov,Oleksandr Suvorov,Alexander Fedorov,Adam Pikul,Ivan Soldatov,Rudolf Schäfer,Bernd Büchner,Oksana Kvitnitskaya,Yurii Naidyuk,Louis Lamm,Meike Pfeiffer,Elena Hassinger,Matthias Vojta,Sergey Borisenko,Miguel M. Ugeda,Jeroen van den Brink,Eteri Svanidze,Maia G. Vergniory*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作利用体敏感与表面敏感探针相结合的手段，在UAsS晶体中观察到关联量子相的自发空间分离：体态表现为具有拓扑能带结构的金属铁磁体，而表面则涌现出强关联的Kondo态特征。这一现象揭示了磁性可驱动不同强关联电子态在同一材料的不同区域共存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代凝聚态物理的核心目标之一是揭示强电子关联（strong electron correlations）、磁性（magnetism）与能带拓扑（band topology）交织作用下产生的新奇量子态。由Kondo相互作用（Kondo interactions）产生的重费米子相（heavy-fermion phases）是电子关联最显著的表现之一，拓扑重费米子态（topological heavy-fermion states）已在若干非磁性材料中被确认，但其与磁序竞争所产生的后果仍基本未被探索。

Method: 采用体敏感（bulk-sensitive）探针与第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）研究UAsS晶体的体态性质，并利用角分辨光电子能谱（ARPES）验证拓扑电子结构。同时采用表面敏感（surface-sensitive）ARPES与扫描隧道显微镜/谱（STM/STS）探测表面电子态。

Result: 体敏感测量显示UAsS晶体在体态为金属铁磁体，仅具有中等程度的关联驱动能带重整化（band renormalizations）；第一性原理计算揭示其具有包含节线（nodal lines）与Weyl点的拓扑电子结构，ARPES结果与预测一致，分辨出节线与Weyl交叉。与之形成鲜明对比的是，表面敏感的ARPES与STM/STS揭示出钉扎于费米能级（Fermi level）的显著平带（flat band），并伴随尖锐共振，这是第一性原理计算无法捕捉的涌现强关联Kondo态的标志。

Conclusion: 磁性可驱动不同的强关联电子态在同一材料的体态与表面区域共存，该研究确立了一种此前未知的组织量子物质的机制，为关联拓扑系统（correlated topological systems）的研究开辟了新方向。

Abstract: A central goal of modern condensed matter physics is to uncover new quantum states of matter arising from the intertwined effects of strong electron correlations, magnetism, and band topology. Heavy-fermion phases, generated by Kondo interactions, represent one of the most remarkable manifestations of electronic correlations, and topological heavy-fermion states have been identified in several non-magnetic materials. Yet, the consequences of their competition with magnetic order have remained largely unexplored. Here, we reveal a new phenomenon: the spontaneous spatial separation of correlated quantum phases. By showing that magnetism can drive distinct strongly correlated electronic states to coexist in different regions of a single material, our work establishes a previously unknown mechanism for organizing quantum matter and opens a new direction in the study of correlated topological systems. Using \emph{bulk-sensitive} probes, we show that UAsS crystals are, in the bulk, metallic ferromagnets with only moderate correlation-driven band renormalizations. First-principles calculations reveal a topological electronic structure hosting both nodal lines and Weyl points, pointing to a rich underlying topology. Angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (ARPES) measurements are consistent with these predictions, resolving the nodal lines and Weyl crossings. In striking contrast, \emph{surface-sensitive} ARPES and scanning tunneling microscopy/spectroscopy (STM/STS) measurements reveal a pronounced flat band pinned at the Fermi level, accompanied by a sharp resonance -- hallmarks of an emergent, strongly correlated Kondo state not captured by first-principles calculations.

</details>


### [147] [Non-Thermal Effects in Fermionic Atoms Coupled to Open Cavities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20434)
*Jules Sueiro,Marco Schiro*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了耦合到开放耗散单腔模的费米-哈伯德模型，利用Keldysh图技术导出并求解费米子的量子动理学方程，揭示无相互作用时费米子趋于一种可消除热流的非平衡稳态，并讨论了该非平衡涨落对超辐射相变平均场相图的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究开放腔模作为非平衡噪声与耗散源时，费米子体系如何达到非平衡稳态，以及该非平衡效应对相变行为的影响。这一动机源于腔量子电动力学与强关联电子体系交叉领域对非平衡多体物理的关注。

Method: 采用Keldysh图技术（Keldysh diagrammatics）导出并求解费米子的量子动理学方程（quantum kinetic equations），将玻色腔模处理为非平衡噪声与耗散源；在无哈伯相互作用时求解稳态分布，并在弱耦合区纳入哈伯相互作用（Hubbard interaction）。

Result: 无哈伯相互作用时，费米子普遍达到非平衡稳态（non-equilibrium steady-state），其分布函数为非热分布，且该分布恰好使费米子与腔模之间的热流为零。研究还发现低频有效温度（effective temperature）描述成立的参数区域，并表明涨落会改变超辐射相变（superradiance phase transition）的平均场相图。在弱耦合区引入哈伯相互作用后，体系趋于完全平衡分布（crossover）。

Conclusion: 腔模诱导的非平衡噪声与耗散可使费米子形成具有零热流的非热稳态，并在特定参数区可用有效温度描述；超辐射相变相图受涨落影响，而弱耦合哈伯相互作用则驱动体系向完全平衡分布过赹。

Abstract: We study a Fermi-Hubbard model coupled to a open dissipative single cavity mode. Using Keldysh diagrammatics we derive and solve the quantum kinetic equations for the fermions, taking the bosonic cavity mode as a source of non-equilibrium noise and dissipation. In absence of Hubbard interactions we show that the fermions reach generically a non-equilibrium steady-state, characterized by a non-thermal distribution function. Quite interestingly we demonstrate that the latter exactly nullifies the heat-current between fermions and cavity mode. We discuss the regimes of parameters where a low-frequency effective temperature description emerges and how the fluctuations affect the mean-field phase diagram for the superradiance phase transition. Finally we include Hubbard interaction in the weak-coupling regime and show that it leads to a crossover towards a full equilibrium distribution.

</details>


### [148] [Collective Charge-\(2e\) Bosonic Excitations in Charge-Ordered Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20472)
*Ping Tang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作在一有效电荷-赝自旋模型（charge-pseudospin model）中构建电荷序的集体激发理论，通过 Holstein-Primakoff 变换量子化有序态涨落，揭示携带相反量子化电荷 ±2e 的两支玻色型准粒子，并命名其为 "bichargon"。研究进一步给出其热电与光电输运响应，将磁振子（magnon）准粒子范式推广至电荷序体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电荷序通常以电子密度的静态调制描述，其集体激发在准粒子（quasiparticle）层面的理论发展远不如自旋有序体系中的磁振子（magnon）成熟。该文旨在为电荷序建立可与磁振子类比、以电荷（而非自旋角动量）为输运量的集体玻色准粒子图像。

Method: 采用有效电荷-赝自旋模型（charge-pseudospin model），将电荷有序基态表示为交错赝自旋序；对有序态周围的涨落施加 Holstein-Primakoff 变换（Holstein-Primakoff transformation）进行量子化。在此基础上分析热梯度、静态电场与交流电场下的输运行为。

Result: 得到两支简并、带隙（gapped）的玻色型准粒子分支，携带相反的量子化电荷 ±2e，被命名为 bichargon，与二分反铁磁体（bipartite antiferromagnet）中携带相反自旋角动量的两支磁振子分支相对应。化学势偏离电荷中性点（charge-neutrality point）时可解除两支简并，温度梯度驱动热激发的 bichargon 扩散并产生电荷塞贝克响应（charge Seebeck response）；尽管携带有限电荷，由于 bichargon 准粒子数不守恒，热 bichargon 气体在静态电场下仍为电绝缘。交流电场可参量式（parametrically）产生带相反电荷的 bichargon 相干对，在偏置电场下支持有限直流漂移电流，构成半导体中光激发电子-空穴对（electron-hole pairs）介导的光电导（photoconductivity）的玻色型类比。

Conclusion: 该文将 bichargon 确立为电荷序体系中一类新的集体玻色电荷载流子，并把自旋有序体系中磁振子的准粒子范式扩展到电荷扇区，其中以电荷取代自旋角动量作为输运量。

Abstract: Charge order is conventionally characterized by a static modulation of the electronic density, while its collective excitations remain less developed from a quasiparticle perspective than those of spin-ordered systems. Here, we formulate the collective excititations of charge order within an effective charge-pseudospin model, in which the charge-ordered ground state is represented by staggered pseudospin order. Quantizing fluctuations around this ordered state via a Holstein--Primakoff transformation, we reveal two branches of bosonic quasiparticles with degenerate, gapped dispersions that carry opposite quantized electric charges $\pm 2e$. We therefore term these charge-$2e$ bosons ``\textit{bichargons},'' closely paralleling the two magnon branches of a bipartite antiferromagnet that carry opposite spin angular momenta. We show that a temperature gradient drives the diffusion of thermally excited bichargons, generating a charge Seebeck response when the degeneracy between the oppositely charged branches is lifted by tuning the chemical potential away from the charge-neutrality point. In contrast, despite carrying finite electric charges, a thermal bichargon gas remains electrically insulating under a static electric field because bichargon quasiparticle number is not conserved. An ac electric field, however, can parametrically generate coherent pairs of oppositely charged bichargons that support a finite dc drift current in the presence of a bias electric field, offering a bosonic analogue of photoconductivity mediated by photoexcited electron--hole pairs in semiconductors. Our results establish bichargons as a new class of collective bosonic charge carriers in charge-ordered systems and extend the quasiparticle paradigm of magnons in spin-ordered systems to the charge sector, with electric charge replacing spin angular momentum as the transported quantity.

</details>


### [149] [Unconstrained compact lattice QED$_{2+1}$ coupled to phonons: Gauss sectors, orthogonal semimetal, and deconfined criticality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20502)
*João C. Inácio,Fakher F. Assaad*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作通过无约束紧致格子量子电动力学（compact lattice QED$_{2+1}$）耦合键声子（bond phonons）的精确辅助场量子蒙特卡洛（auxiliary-field quantum Monte Carlo）模拟，揭示了物理高斯扇区（Gauss sector）如何在能量上变为有利，并指出在投影到物理希尔伯特空间后，紧致格子 QED$_{2+1}$ 的去禁闭（deconfinement）总是不稳定，趋向反铁磁（AFM）或价键固体（VBS）序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维量子磁体的规范理论描述通常定义在由高斯定律约束的受限物理希尔伯特空间上，而该工作在无约束框架下考察高斯算符守恒但本征值未先验固定的情形，以厘清物理高斯扇区的能量选择及其与禁闭和对称破缺的联系。

Method: 采用 $N_f = 2$ 的精确辅助场量子蒙特卡洛模拟，研究耦合键声子的无约束紧致格子 QED$_{2+1}$，并考察当高斯定律被显式施加时该系统的行为变化。

Result: 识别出正交半金属（orthogonal semimetallic, OSM）相，其中费米子剥离规范荷形成狄拉克液体（Dirac liquid）；该相混合不同高斯扇区，并在显式强制高斯定律时变得不稳定。施加高斯定律后，紧致性允许携带反铁磁（AFM）与价键固体（VBS）量子数的单极子（monopole）激发；规范场涨落与自旋子-声子耦合（spinon-phonon coupling）调控单极子竞争，生成包含 OSM、AFM 与 VBS 序的相图。

Conclusion: OSM 相的转变与高斯定律的动力学生成同时发生，而 AFM 与 VBS 带电单极子之间的竞争产生的转变与去禁闭量子临界性（deconfined quantum criticality）一致；结果表明，在投影到物理希尔伯特空间后，紧致格子 QED$_{2+1}$ 中的去禁闭总是不稳定，趋向 AFM 或 VBS 序。

Abstract: Gauge theoretic descriptions of 2D quantum magnets are defined on a restricted physical Hilbert space by imposing Gauss's law. In this work, we study an unconstrained compact lattice QED$_{2+1}$ coupled to bond phonons, in which local Gauss operators are conserved, but their eigenvalues are not a priori fixed. Using exact auxiliary-field quantum Monte Carlo simulations at $N_f = 2$, we investigate how the physical Gauss sector becomes energetically favourable, and how this process is connected to confinement and symmetry breaking. We identify an orthogonal semimetallic (OSM) phase, in which fermions striped off their gauge charge and form a Dirac liquid. This phase mixes various Gauss sectors, and becomes unstable when Gauss's law is explicitly enforced. Upon the imposition of Gauss's law, compactness allows for monopole excitations carrying antiferromagnetic (AFM) and valence-bond-solid (VBS) quantum numbers. Gauge field fluctuations and spinon-phonon coupling tune between the competition of such monopoles and generate a phase diagram with OSM, AFM and VBS orders. The transitions out of the OSM phase coincide with the dynamical generation of Gauss's law, while the competition between AFM and VBS charged monopoles produces a transition consistent with deconfined quantum criticality. Thus our results establish that upon the projection to the physical Hilbert space, deconfinement in compact lattice QED$_{2+1}$ is always unstable towards either AFM or VBS order.

</details>


### [150] [Multiconfigurational Analysis of Local Electronic Structure of $\mathrm{RuO_2}$ Using Relativistic Embedded Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20526)
*Zhosan I. A.,Lomachuk Yu. V.,Maltsev D. A.,Moiseev I. A.,Andreev O. Yu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作以多组态相对论嵌入团簇方法研究RuO₂局部电子结构，发现其4d轨道谱更接近四方D₄h对称性而非形式上的正交D₂h对称性，轨道准简并抑制了交替磁性所需的xy四极序，从而解释块体RuO₂未见交替磁性而应变薄膜中却出现的原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RuO₂作为交替磁性（altermagnetism）候选材料，其块体实验未观测到预期磁性信号，而应变薄膜却报告了稳健的交替磁性特征，二者矛盾需要从局部电子结构层面给出解释。

Method: 构建逐步递进的嵌入团簇模型：从自由Ru离子，经Ru+Q₆静电模型、裸[RuO₆]⁸⁻配体模型，到高精度RuO₆@CTEP嵌入团簇以重现晶体环境；全部体系在SA-CASSCF与NEVPT2+SOC水平处理，以同时捕捉强电子关联（strong electron correlation）与自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）。

Result: 尽管RuO₂中Ru位点的形式局域对称性为正交D₂h，计算得到的4d轨道能谱及其劈裂模式更接近更高的四方D₄h对称性，相关4d轨道保持强准简并（quasi-degeneracy）。

Conclusion: 独立粒子模型中交替磁性自旋劈裂所依赖的局域xy四极序（quadrupolar order）需以对称性降低为前提，其被轨道准简并所抑制，为块体RuO₂实验未观测到交替磁性提供了自然解释，并与应变薄膜中交替磁性特征形成对照。

Abstract: We present a multiconfigurational, relativistic embedded-cluster study of the local electronic structure of ruthenium dioxide ($\mathrm{RuO_2}$), a candidate altermagnetic material. Starting from free $\mathrm{Ru}$ ions, we progressively build up the local environment through a $\mathrm{Ru+Q_6}$ electrostatic model, a bare $\mathrm{[RuO_6]^{8-}}$ ligand model, and finally a high-accuracy $\mathrm{RuO_6}$@CTEP embedded cluster that reproduces the crystalline surroundings. All systems are treated at the SA-CASSCF and NEVPT2+SOC levels of theory to capture strong electron correlation and spin-orbit coupling on an equal footing. While the formal local site symmetry of the $\mathrm{Ru}$ sites in $\mathrm{RuO_2}$ is orthorhombic ($D_{2h}$), we find that the calculated $4d$-orbital energy spectrum and its splitting pattern behave much closer to the higher tetragonal ($D_{4h}$) symmetry, preserving a strong quasi-degeneracy among the relevant $4d$ orbitals. Since the local $xy$ quadrupolar order responsible for altermagnetic spin splitting in independent-particle models relies on this symmetry reduction, its suppression by orbital quasi-degeneracy offers a natural explanation for why altermagnetism is not observed in bulk $\mathrm{RuO_2}$ experiments, in contrast to the robust altermagnetic signatures reported in strained thin films.

</details>


### [151] [Engineering Weak Universality with Quantum Dots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20755)
*Warre Missiaen,Michael Wimmer,Natalia Chepiga*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文提出基于量子点架构实现量子 Ashkin-Teller 模型与 XYZ/eight-vertex 模型，分别通过谐振器介导与直接库仑相互作用，以在实验中可控地探测连续变化的临界指数（critical exponents）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具有连续变化临界指数的量子临界理论（quantum critical theories）在实验上难以实现，因此需要可微观调控参数的平台来直接检验此类临界行为。

Method: 提出量子点（quantum-dot）架构：以谐振器介导相互作用实现量子 Ashkin-Teller 模型，以直接库仑相互作用（Coulomb interactions）实现 XYZ/eight-vertex 模型；两类临界线由连接局域序参量与拓扑弦算符（topological string operators）的非局域对偶（non-local duality）相联系。

Result: 短链即可分辨出连续变化的临界行为；在实验可行参数下，可及相互作用范围预期使 Ashkin-Teller 模型临界指数变化约 10%，eight-vertex 模型变化更大，且均远高于估计的测量不确定度。

Conclusion: 该类平台可对显式控制临界指数的参数提供微观调控，其中基于库仑的实现可达更强相互作用区域，从而在实验上可分辨连续变化的临界行为。

Abstract: Quantum critical theories with continuously varying critical exponents remain challenging to access experimentally. Here, we propose quantum-dot architectures for realizing the quantum Ashkin-Teller and XYZ/eight-vertex models using resonator-mediated and direct Coulomb interactions, respectively. We focus on the Ashkin-Teller and eight-vertex critical lines, which are connected by a non-local duality relating local order parameters to topological string operators. The resulting platforms provide microscopic control over the parameters explicitly controlling critical exponents, with the Coulomb-based implementation enabling stronger interaction regimes. We demonstrate that continuously varying critical behavior can already be resolved in short chains. For experimentally realistic parameters, the accessible interaction range is expected to produce changes in the critical exponents of order $10\%$ for the Ashkin-Teller model and substantially larger changes for the eight-vertex model. In both cases, the predicted variations lie well above the estimated measurement uncertainties.

</details>


### [152] [Spacetime Dynamics of Altermagnetic Magnons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20767)
*Ali Emami Kopaei,Karthik Subramaniam Eswaran,Krzysztof Wohlfeld*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文提出以局域自旋激发的实空间传播作为交错磁（altermagnetism）序的动力学指纹，基于棋盘格晶格（checkerboard lattice）的线性自旋波理论（linear spin-wave theory），揭示有限 J_2 导致方向依赖的磁振子群速度（magnon group velocity）并在波前内部形成十字状空间结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区分交错磁（altermagnetism）与传统铁磁（ferromagnetism）、反铁磁（antiferromagnetism）通常依赖动量空间（momentum-space）探测手段，缺乏实空间动力学层面的互补判据。

Method: 在二维棋盘格晶格（checkerboard lattice）上采用线性自旋波理论（linear spin-wave theory），考虑最近邻与次近邻耦合 J_1、J_2，分析局域自旋激发的时空传播行为。

Result: 有限 J_2 产生方向依赖的磁振子群速度（magnon group velocity）并使两支磁振子分支发生劈裂；传播整体更接近反铁磁体，保留近似圆形外波前，同时速度的方向依赖性在波前内部形成明显十字状结构。将 J_2 变号至无阻挫区（unfrustrated regime，J_2&lt;0）会提高特征传播速度并交换增强传播的对角方向，相当于各向异性空间图案旋转 pi/2。

Conclusion: 时空动力学（spacetime dynamics）可作为交错磁磁振子的互补探测手段，为在固态与合成量子系统中通过实空间传播图案识别非常规磁序提供了途径。

Abstract: Distinguishing altermagnetism from conventional ferromagnetism and antiferromagnetism typically relies on momentum-space probes. Here, we show that the real-space spreading of a localized spin excitation provides a distinctive dynamical fingerprint of altermagnetic order. Using linear spin-wave theory on a two-dimensional checkerboard lattice with nearest-neighbor and next-nearest-neighbor couplings J_1 and J_2, we demonstrate that finite J_2 produces direction-dependent magnon group velocities and splits the two magnon branches. The resulting propagation remains closer to that of an antiferromagnet than a ferromagnet, retaining an approximately circular outer wavefront, while the direction-dependent magnon velocities produce a pronounced cross-like spatial structure inside this front. We further show that changing the sign of J_2 to enter the unfrustrated regime (J_2<0) increases the characteristic propagation velocities and interchanges the diagonal directions of enhanced propagation, corresponding to a pi/2 rotation of the anisotropic spatial pattern. These results establish spacetime dynamics as a complementary probe of altermagnetic magnons, providing a route to identifying unconventional magnetic order through real-space propagation patterns in solid-state and synthetic quantum systems.

</details>


<div id='physics.comp-ph'></div>

# physics.comp-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [153] [Correlation-Free Transition Path Sampling through Shooting Point Generation Guided by Committor Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20461)
*Maximilian Negedly,Sebastian Falkner,Alessandro Coretti,Christoph Dellago*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: GenAIMMD 是一种结合 committor 学习与条件 Boltzmann 生成器的迭代算法，可在无需预设反应坐标的前提下，实现无相关且可完全并行化的过渡路径采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画系统在长寿命状态间的跃迁通常依赖增强采样技术，但跃迁事件罕见。过渡路径采样（Transition Path Sampling, TPS）虽实现简单且无需预设反应坐标（reaction coordinate），却受限于顺序采样带来的路径间相关性。既有结合条件 Boltzmann 生成器的方案虽能产生无相关路径，但依赖准确的反应坐标，而该坐标往往难以预先获知。

Method: 基于 committor 学习的最新进展，特别是分子机制发现人工智能方法（Artificial Intelligence for Molecular Mechanism Discovery, AIMMD），提出 GenAIMMD 迭代算法。该算法主动且自洽地学习理想反应坐标（即 committor），并训练条件 Boltzmann 生成器（conditioned Boltzmann Generator），以沿该坐标从任意偏置窗口（bias window）中采样。

Result: 在二维玩具模型（two-dimensional toy model）与更高维的聚合物体系（polymer system）上应用 GenAIMMD，两种情形均成功完成 Boltzmann 生成器的训练与 committor 的学习。基准测试（benchmark）显示，相较标准 TPS，性能有显著提升。

Conclusion: GenAIMMD 提供了一种无相关且可完全并行化的路径采样方案，无需事先了解系统的跃迁机制，并相较标准 TPS 在性能上取得显著改进。

Abstract: Studying the dynamical behavior of a system often depends on characterizing how it transitions between long-lived states. Because such transitions are rare, observing them usually requires specialized enhanced sampling techniques. Transition Path Sampling (TPS) is a well-established method for generating reactive trajectories, which is simple to implement and does not require the definition of a preconceived reaction coordinate. However, its efficiency is limited by its sequential nature and the resulting correlations between sampled paths. Previous work addressed this limitation by combining TPS with a sampling scheme based on conditioned Boltzmann Generators, a generative machine learning model capable of sampling a given target probability distribution. This approach produces uncorrelated transition paths but relies on an accurate reaction coordinate, which is rarely known in advance. Building on recent advances in committor learning, specifically on the Artificial Intelligence for Molecular Mechanism Discovery (AIMMD) method, in this work we introduce GenAIMMD, an iterative algorithm that actively and self-consistently learns the ideal reaction coordinate (the committor) and trains a conditioned Boltzmann Generator to sample from arbitrary bias windows along it. GenAIMMD thereby provides a correlation-free and fully parallelizable path sampling scheme that does not require prior knowledge of the system's transition mechanism. We apply GenAIMMD to a two-dimensional toy model and a higher-dimensional polymer system. In both cases, GenAIMMD succeeds in training the Boltzmann Generator and learning the committor. Benchmark results show a substantial increase in performance compared to standard TPS.

</details>


<div id='physics.soc-ph'></div>

# physics.soc-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [154] [When higher-order interactions matter: reducibility, parsimony, and microscopic organization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20039)
*Alex Arenas,Federico Battiston,Andrea Gabrielli*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 该文主张，仅凭形式上的可表示性（representability）不足以作为模型选择的标准；"更简单"与"等价"均是依赖于具体科学问题的概念，简约性（parsimony）须就完整的结构-动力学模型评估，充分性（adequacy）须相对于所需可观测量、尺度与区间界定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 围绕高阶相互作用（higher-order interactions）的争论提出一个根本问题：以群体相互作用组织的系统何时能被基于图（graph）的形式体系忠实表示。既有结果显示图描述可精确再现高阶动力学或保留部分宏观可观测量，但可约性（reducibility）未必意味着简化。

Method: 属于概念性/立场性论证（conceptual argument）。通过区分形式可表示性与模型选择标准，指出从相互作用结构中移除的信息可能以有效动力学复杂性形式重现，且嵌套性（nestedness）、异质性（heterogeneity）与跨阶相关性（cross-order correlations）等结构特征可能被约简遮蔽。

Result: 论证得出：简约性须针对完整的结构-动力学模型评估，充分性须相对于科学问题所要求的可观测量、尺度与区间来定义。约简模型或足以定位相变（phase transition）或识别普适类（universality class），但不足以再现微观轨迹、瞬态动力学或结构-功能关系。

Conclusion: 当群体结构携带相关的微观信息时，高阶表示可能仍是最直接、最可解释且最简约的描述，也是理解微观组织如何产生宏观行为与功能性的自然框架。

Abstract: The current debate on higher-order interactions raises a fundamental question: when can systems organized through group interactions be faithfully represented within a graph-based formalism? Recent results show that graph descriptions can reproduce higher-order dynamics exactly or preserve selected macroscopic observables. Yet reducibility does not necessarily imply simplification, as information removed from the interaction structure may reappear as complexity in the effective dynamics, while structural features such as nestedness, heterogeneity, and cross-order correlations may be obscured by the reduction. We argue that formal representability alone is insufficient as a criterion for model choice. Both "simpler" and "equivalent" are question-dependent notions: parsimony must be assessed for the complete structure-dynamics model, and adequacy must be defined relative to the observables, scales, and regimes required by the scientific question. A reduced model may therefore be fully adequate for locating a phase transition or identifying a universality class while being inadequate for reproducing microscopic trajectories, transient dynamics, or structure-function relations. When group structure carries relevant microscopic information, higher-order representations may remain the most direct, interpretable, and parsimonious description, as well as the natural framework for understanding how microscopic organization gives rise to macroscopic behavior and functionality.

</details>


### [155] [Is higher-order physics different?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20759)
*Pablo Villegas,Sandro Meloni*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 该文以统计物理（statistical mechanics）中的普适性（universality）为判据，重新审视高阶相互作用（higher-order interactions）模型所声称的“独特高阶物理”，主张集体行为的分类取决于长尺度下存活的红外要素（infrared ingredients），而非相互作用元数（arity）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阶相互作用（higher-order interactions）普遍存在，且常见模型的高阶扩展表现出成对基线（pairwise baselines）所无的集体现象，这些现象常被当作存在独特高阶物理的证据。作者意在检验这一主张能否通过统计物理中赋予“新”以精确含义的普适性（universality）检验。

Method: 重新审视若干经典高阶模型（canonical higher-order models），从普适性视角分析其集体行为，聚焦于长尺度下存活的红外要素（infrared ingredients），并据此评估元数（arity）能否作为普适类标签。

Result: 元数并非普适性标签（arity is not a universality label）；区分集体行为的是红外要素而非相互作用阶数。此外，论文还讨论了两个延伸问题：高阶结构是系统的客观事实还是描述方式的选择，以及数据能或不能揭示何种相互作用阶数（interaction order）信息。

Conclusion: 当高阶描述能揭示组织性结构、提供更好的机制性语言或改进预测时，它仍不可或缺。作者最后提出在声称新现象之前应测量什么，并指出高阶结构已获确证的适用位置。

Abstract: Group interactions are widespread, and higher-order extensions of familiar models display collective phenomena absent from their pairwise baselines, which are routinely offered as evidence of a distinct higher-order physics. We ask whether that claim survives the test that gives new a precise meaning in statistical physics: that of universality. Revisiting canonical higher-order models, we argue that what classifies collective behavior are the infrared ingredients that survive at long scales. Arity is not a universality label. Beyond universality, we examine two further questions: whether higher-order structure is a fact about the system or a choice of description, and what data can and cannot tell us about interaction order. Higher-order descriptions remain indispensable when they expose the organizing structure, provide a better mechanistic language, or improve prediction. We close with what should be measured before new phenomena can be claimed, and where higher-order structure already earns its place.

</details>

### [156] [Numerical Investigations of Phase Transitions in Lattice Field Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20536)
*Vamika Longia*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本论文围绕格点系统（lattice systems）中的相变（phase transitions），开展两项互补的高精度数值研究：其一以GPU加速的高阶张量重整化群（HOTRG）刻画二维广义XY模型的相图；其二在紧致U(1)格点规范理论中构建并基准测试构型温度估计量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相变研究对理解物理系统在临界（criticality）处的定性行为至关重要，尽管已有数十年进展，仍迫切需要能分辨精细临界现象的高精度数值工具。该需求构成本论文两项研究的直接动机。

Method: 方法包含两条互补路线：一是采用GPU加速的高阶张量重整化群（higher-order tensor renormalization group, HOTRG）技术处理二维广义XY模型（generalized XY model）；二是提出由欧几里得格点作用量（Euclidean lattice action）的梯度与Hessian构造的构型温度估计量（configurational temperature estimator），并在紧致U(1)格点规范理论（compact U(1) lattice gauge theory）中对其开发与基准测试。

Result: 第一项研究刻画了二维广义XY模型的铁磁（ferromagnetic）、向列（nematic）与顺磁（paramagnetic）相，并在热力学极限（thermodynamic limit）下利用热力学观测量（thermodynamic observables）映射其相边界。第二项研究建立了构型温度估计量的基准，显示其可跨不同算法与模型独立、低开销地验证热采样（thermal sampling），并可作为运行期诊断（runtime diagnostic）在大规模生产运行前识别采样病态。此外，当截断与有限键（finite-bond）效应得到充分控制时，张量网络方法能够捕捉丰富的相结构。

Conclusion: 两项研究具有互补性：张量网络方法在有效控制截断与有限键效应后能够解析丰富相结构，而构型温度估计量提供独立的低开销热采样验证手段，并可用作运行期诊断以在规模化计算前发现采样问题。二者共同回应了高精度数值工具的需求。

Abstract: The study of phase transitions plays an important role in understanding qualitative changes in the behaviour of physical systems at criticality. Despite decades of progress, there is still a strong demand for high-precision numerical tools capable of resolving subtle critical phenomena. Motivated by this need, in this thesis, we present two complementary numerical investigations of phase transitions in lattice systems. The first uses GPU-accelerated higher-order tensor renormalization group (HOTRG) techniques to study the two-dimensional generalized XY model, characterizing its ferromagnetic, nematic, and paramagnetic phases and mapping their phase boundaries using thermodynamic observables in the thermodynamic limit. The second develops and benchmarks a configurational temperature estimator, constructed from gradients and Hessians of the Euclidean lattice action, in compact U(1) lattice gauge theories. On one hand, tensor network methods capture rich phase structures when truncation and finite-bond effects are adequately controlled. On the other hand, the configurational temperature estimator provides an independent, low-overhead means of validating thermal sampling across different algorithms and models, and can also be used as a runtime diagnostic to identify sampling pathologies before large-scale production runs.

</details>


<div id='math.RT'></div>

# math.RT [[Back]](#toc)

### [157] [Unitary Branching for $\mathfrak{sl}(m\vert n), \mathfrak{osp}(m\vert 2n)$ and $F(4)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20332)
*Steffen Schmidt*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 该论文利用 Dirac 不等式与 Dirac 形式下的 Freudenthal 递推，确定了基本经典 Lie 超代数（Lie superalgebras）$\mathfrak{sl}(m|n)$、$\mathfrak{osp}(m|2n)$ 与 $F(4)$ 在限制到偶子代数时的不可约酉表示分支规律，并给出分支重数的封闭公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究目标是刻画基本经典 Lie 超代数的不可约酉表示（irreducible unitary representations）在限制到其偶子代数（even subalgebras）时的分支（branching）行为。已有 Dirac 方法通常依赖与相关实形式（real form）适配的正系统（positive systems），该限制促使作者寻求推广途径。

Method: 证明结合了 Huang-Pandžić-Dirac 算子 $\operatorname{D}_{\mathfrak{g},\mathfrak{g}_{\bar{0}}}$ 的 Dirac 不等式与由 Dirac 形式导出的 Freudenthal 递推（Freudenthal's recursion）的超类比。对于不与相关实形式适配的正系统，通过奇反射（odd reflections）传输 Dirac 不等式，并确定由此产生的修正项（correction terms）。

Result: 所得有限递推程序可确定出现的偶成分及其分支重数（branching multiplicities），并给出关于这些重数的封闭公式，且对非典型度（degree of atypicality）一致成立。对 $\mathfrak{sl}(m|n)$，修正后的 Dirac 不等式还给出所有与酉表示相关的正系统的酉性（unitarity）刻画。

Conclusion: Dirac 方法可推广至非适配正系统，从而统一处理基本经典 Lie 超代数在偶子代数限制下的酉表示分支问题，并附带提供酉性判据。

Abstract: We determine the branching of irreducible unitary representations of the basic classical Lie superalgebras $\mathfrak{sl}(m|n)$, $\mathfrak{osp}(m|2n)$, and $F(4)$ under restriction to their even subalgebras. The proof combines Dirac inequalities for the Huang-Pandžić-Dirac operator $\operatorname{D}_{\mathfrak{g},\mathfrak{g}_{\bar{0}}}$ with a super analogue of Freudenthal's recursion derived from the Dirac formalism. We extend the Dirac method to positive systems that are not adapted to the relevant real form by transporting the Dirac inequality through odd reflections and determining the resulting correction terms. This yields finite recursive procedures that determine the occurring even constituents and their branching multiplicities, together with closed formulas for the latter, uniformly in the degree of atypicality. For $\mathfrak{sl}(m|n)$, the corrected Dirac inequalities also give a characterization of unitarity for all positive systems relevant to unitary representations.

</details>

# nlin.PS [[Back]](#toc)

### [158] [The Origin of Imperfection Sensitivity in the Buckling of Cylindrical Shells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19436)
*Tian Yang,Tobias M. Schneider*

Main category: nlin.PS

TL;DR: 该研究通过数值延拓与分岔分析，揭示薄圆柱壳在轴向压缩下对缺陷几何的敏感性源自完整壳中已存在的局域平衡态，而非单纯的缺陷幅度效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 薄圆柱壳在轴向压缩下的屈曲（buckling）是亚临界失稳（subcritical instability）的经典案例，对微小几何缺陷高度敏感，但此种敏感性的物理起源尚未明确。

Method: 采用数值延拓（numerical continuation）与分岔分析（bifurcation analysis），系统改变单个局域高斯缺陷（localized Gaussian defect）的深度与尺寸进行参数扫描。

Result: 不完美壳的失稳源于完整壳中原本已存在的局域平衡态；缺陷通过破坏平移对称性（translation symmetry）钉扎这些平衡态，并改变其与不完美基态的连接方式，微小缺陷几何变化可切换触发屈曲的分岔，导致屈曲阈值非单调、不连续变化及屈曲模态突变。

Conclusion: 缺陷敏感性本质上并非对缺陷幅度的敏感，而是对底层分岔结构（bifurcation structure）相对于缺陷几何形状的敏感性。

Abstract: Buckling of thin cylindrical shells under axial compression, a classical example of a subcritical instability, is highly sensitive to small imperfections, with minute geometric variations causing large changes in buckling threshold. To uncover the origin of this sensitivity, we use numerical continuation and bifurcation analysis while systematically varying the depth and size of a single localized Gaussian defect. We show that the instabilities of the imperfect shell originate from localized equilibria already present in the perfect shell. By breaking translation symmetry, the defect pins these equilibria and changes how they connect to the imperfect base state. Small changes in defect geometry can thereby switch the bifurcation that triggers buckling, producing non-monotonic and discontinuous changes in buckling threshold and abrupt changes in buckling mode. Imperfection sensitivity is therefore not simply sensitivity to imperfection magnitude, but sensitivity of the underlying bifurcation structure to imperfection geometry.

</details>


<div id='math.SP'></div>

# math.SP [[Back]](#toc)

### [159] [Finiteness and exponential growth of full graph $p$-spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20291)
*Matthew J. Colbrook*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 该论文证明了有限图 $p$-拉普拉斯算子（$p$-Laplacian）的全谱（full spectrum）对任意 $p>1$ 均为有限，解决了 Amghibech（2003）提出的公开问题，并给出谱基数（spectral cardinality）与特征向量集拓扑复杂度的统一上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限图上 $p$-拉普拉斯算子全谱的有限性由 Amghibech（2003）提出，此前未获解决；同时谱基数上界与特征向量集的拓扑结构缺乏与 $p$ 及系数无关的统一刻画。

Method: 研究针对带符号加权图 $p$-薛定谔算子（$p$-Schrödinger operators）与任意实势；证明结合 o-极小（o-minimal）与普法夫几何（Pfaffian geometry）、射影 $L^p$-对偶（projective $L^p$-duality）、莫尔斯理论（Morse theory）、临界群（critical groups）及张量特征值界（tensor eigenvalue bounds）。

Result: 对 $n$ 个顶点、$m$ 条正权边的图，谱基数的对数与归一化特征向量集连通分支总数的对数均为 $O((n+m)^2)$，全谱上有理贝蒂数（Betti numbers）之和不超过 $\exp(C(n+m)^2)$；拓扑界推广至由有限线性形式族构建的齐次特征问题，含矩阵对的广义 $p$-特征值问题。对均匀加权 $K_n$ 且 $p\ne2$，非恒定特征线（eigenlines）对应 $\mathbb RP^{n-2}$ 坐标超平面胞腔分解的重心（barycentres），并给出 $p=2$ 两侧的局部莫尔斯数据（Morse data）；重整化对数极限（renormalised logarithmic limit）描述 $p=2$ 处的相变，正整数权重可分离临界值，从而给出至少 $(3^n-2^{n+1}+3)/2$ 个不同谱值。对比之下，$p=2$ 时最大谱基数为 $n$；$p=4$ 时无符号与一般类的最大谱基数增长率均恰为 $3$；$n\geq3$ 时上述例子解决 Amghibech 的极值问题。

Conclusion: 论文确立了有限图 $p$-拉普拉斯全谱的有限性，并给出关于顶点数与正权边数的一致界，同时通过具体图族刻画 $p=2$ 处的相变与谱基数增长行为，解决 Amghibech 的有限性与极值问题。

Abstract: We prove that the full spectrum of the graph $p$-Laplacian is finite for every finite graph and every real $p>1$. This resolves the problem of finiteness of the full graph spectrum posed by Amghibech (2003). More generally, for signed weighted graph $p$-Schrödinger operators with arbitrary real potentials, we obtain bounds in terms of the numbers of vertices and positive-weight edges, uniform in all coefficients and in $p$. On $n$ vertices with $m$ positive-weight edges, the logarithms of the spectral cardinality and of the total number of connected components of the normalised eigenvector sets are $O((n+m)^2)$; the sum over the full spectrum of their rational Betti numbers is at most $\exp(C(n+m)^2)$ for an absolute constant $C$. These topological bounds extend to homogeneous eigenproblems built from arbitrary finite families of linear forms, including generalised $p$-eigenvalue problems for matrix pairs. For the uniformly weighted $K_n$ and $p\ne2$, we identify the nonconstant eigenlines with the barycentres of the coordinate-hyperplane cell decomposition of $\mathbb RP^{n-2}$ and determine their local Morse data on either side of $p=2$. A renormalised logarithmic limit describes the transition at $p=2$. Positive integer weights then separate these critical values, giving at least $(3^n-2^{n+1}+3)/2$ distinct spectral values for every fixed $p\ne2$ and every $n\geq2$. By contrast, the maximal spectral cardinality at $p=2$ is $n$; at $p=4$, the maximal spectral cardinalities in the unsigned and general classes both have exponential growth rate exactly $3$. For $n\geq3$ the same examples resolve Amghibech's extremal question. The proof combines o-minimal and Pfaffian geometry with projective $L^p$-duality, Morse theory, critical groups, and tensor eigenvalue bounds.

</details>


<div id='physics.optics'></div>

# physics.optics [[Back]](#toc)

### [160] [Voltage-Current-Emission Framework of Time-Resolved Electroluminescence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19723)
*Hui Bao,Longjia Wu,Yanan Guo,Jianchang Yan,Ying Wang,Haizheng Zhong*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该工作提出电压-电流-发射（Voltage-Current-Emission）框架，将半导体器件的等效电路模型与电致发光过程的载流子动力学结合，实现对时间分辨电致发光（time-resolved electroluminescence）的定量理论描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间分辨电致发光是LED器件研发中的核心方法，但长期缺乏定量理论描述，这一空白限制了对其电流与载流子动力学的定量理解。

Method: 结合半导体器件的等效电路模型（equivalent circuit models）与电致发光过程的载流子动力学（carrier dynamics），构建以脉冲电压为输入、注入电流为中间量、电致发光为输出的电压-电流-发射框架。

Result: 该框架可定量解释不同LED器件的特征，验证了其在不同材料与器件结构LED中的普适性；并可模拟时间分辨电致发光曲线，从而定量描述LED工作时电流与载流子的动力学。

Conclusion: 电压-电流-发射框架为面向AI技术的高级LED器件实现提供理论指导。

Abstract: Time-resolved electroluminescence has been a central methodology in the development of LED devices. However, it still lacks quantitative theoretical descriptions of time-resolved electroluminescence. By combining the equivalent circuit models of semiconductor devices and the carrier dynamics of electroluminescence processes, we here developed a Voltage-Current-Emission framework to illustrate time-resolved electroluminescence from the pulse voltage as input, to injection current and finally generate electroluminescence as output. The features of different LED devices can be quantitatively interpreted, confirming the universal applicability across LEDs with different materials and device structures. Importantly, the curves of time-resolved electroluminescence can be simulated to quantitatively describe the dynamics of current and carriers of LED operation. In all, the Voltage-Current-Emission framework offers theoretical guidance for achieving advanced LED devices for AI technology.

</details>

### [161] [An integrable $\mathbb{Z}_2^2$-graded extension of Camassa-Holm equation and its bi-Hamiltonian structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19543)
*N. Aizawa,Ichi Fujii,Ren Ito,L. Vasconcellos*

Main category: nlin.SI

TL;DR: 通过在 $\mathfrak{osp}(1|2)$ 的李超代数 $\mathbb{Z}_2^2$-分次扩张的 loop 代数中构造 Lax 算子，导出了 Camassa-Holm 方程的一个 $\mathbb{Z}_2^2$-分次扩张，并证明其具有双哈密顿结构与无穷多守恒量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要未明确阐述研究动机，仅表明工作围绕 $\mathbb{Z}_2^2$-分次代数结构与可积方程之间的联系展开。

Method: 核心方法是在 $\mathbb{Z}_2^2$-分次扩张的李超代数 $\mathfrak{osp}(1|2)$ 的 loop 代数（loop algebra）中构造 Lax 算子（Lax operator），据此推导相应的 $\mathbb{Z}_2^2$-Camassa-Holm 方程。

Result: 所得方程是关于四个 $\mathbb{Z}_2^2$-分次交换函数（$\mathbb{Z}_2^2$-graded commutative functions）的可积非线性偏微分方程系统，每个函数对应一个不同的 $\mathbb{Z}_2^2$-次数。该方程具有双哈密顿结构（bi-Hamiltonian structure），并由此拥有无穷多守恒量，其中包括一个具有非平凡 $\mathbb{Z}_2^2$-次数的守恒量。

Conclusion: 这些守恒量在 $\mathbb{Z}_2^2$-分次泊松括号（$\mathbb{Z}_2^2$-graded Poisson brackets）下相互对合（in involution），表明所构造的 $\mathbb{Z}_2^2$-Camassa-Holm 方程是可积系统。

Abstract: By constructing Lax operators in the loop algebra of the $\mathbb{Z}_2^2$-graded extension of the Lie superalgebra $\mathfrak{osp}(1|2)$, we derive a $\mathbb{Z}_2^2$-graded extension of the Camassa-Holm equation. The resulting equation is an integrable nonlinear PDE for a system of four $\mathbb{Z}_2^2$-graded commutative functions, each associated with a distinct $\mathbb{Z}_2^2$-degree. We further show that the $\mathbb{Z}_2^2$-Camassa-Holm equation admits a bi-Hamiltonian structure. As a consequence, it possesses infinitely many conserved quantities, including one with non-trivial $\mathbb{Z}_2^2$-degree, which are mutually in involution with respect to the $\mathbb{Z}_2^2$-graded Poisson brackets.

</details>


### [162] [Integrability of the deformed Toda systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20679)
*Mikhail Vasilev*

Main category: nlin.SI

TL;DR: 该文证明了 Mucciconi-Petrov 所引入的变形 Toda 系统（deformed Toda system）的可积性，通过构造 Lax 算子给出对易的微分算子族，并说明该可积变形在经典与量子层面同时成立，且可推广至非相对论与相对论情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2020 年 M. Mucciconi 与 L. Petrov 提出了量子开链非相对论 Toda 系统（quantum open non-relativistic Toda system）的长程变形，但其可积性尚未得到证明，该文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 构造 $2 \times 2$ 的 Lax 算子（Lax operator），由此生成包含变形 Toda 系统哈密顿量（Hamiltonian）的对易微分算子族；对开链非相对论变形 Toda 系统另给出 $n \times n$ 的 Lax 矩阵（Lax matrix），并证明其产生相同的哈密顿量族；量子层面采用代数 Bethe ansatz（algebraic Bethe ansatz）技术。

Result: 证明该变形同时存在于经典与量子层面，并可应用于非相对论与相对论 Toda 系统；$2\times 2$ Lax 算子与 $n\times n$ Lax 矩阵生成同一哈密顿量族；进一步给出获得 van Diejen 型变形 Toda 系统的方法；并表明代数 Bethe ansatz 可用于该量子变形 Toda 系统。

Conclusion: 该长程变形 Toda 系统具有可积性，其 Lax 表述在经典与量子、非相对论与相对论层面均适用，且量子情形可由代数 Bethe ansatz 方法处理。

Abstract: In 2020 M. Mucciconi and L. Petrov introduced a long-range deformation of the quantum open non-relativistic Toda system. We prove the integrability of the deformed Toda system by constructing a $2 \times 2$ Lax operator, which produces the commutative family of differential operators containing the Hamiltonian of the deformed Toda system. Moreover, we show that the same integrable deformation exists on both classical and quantum levels and can be applied to both non-relativistic and relativistic Toda systems. For the open non-relativistic deformed Toda systems we also present an $n \times n$ Lax matrix and prove that it produces the same family of Hamiltonians. We also show how to obtain the van Diejen-type deformed Toda system. Lastly, we show that on the quantum level the algebraic Bethe ansatz technique can be applied to the deformed Toda system.

</details>

### [163] [How universal are warm turbulent cascades?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19432)
*Ben Y. Israeli,Gregory Falkovich*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该文质疑波动力学方程（wave kinetic equation）中“暖级联（warm cascades）”仅由守恒通量与温度参数化的假设，指出热阻尼（thermal damping）的非局域效应会引入对紫外截断（UV cutoff）的额外依赖，并通过数值方法验证这一非局域、非普适行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有理论通常假定波动力学方程的稳态解（warm cascades）仅由守恒通量与温度决定，而该假设依赖局域相互作用（local interactions）的微分模型。作者意在检验热阻尼的非局域效应对该假设有效性的影响。

Method: 通过时间尺度论证（time scale argument），分析热阻尼对级联的非局域效应，并针对三波与四波相互作用（three-wave and four-wave interactions）系统分别加以考察；随后对级联因该阻尼造成的损耗（depletion）进行数值演示。

Result: 热阻尼会使暖级联解依赖于额外参数——紫外截断（UV cutoff），从而动摇暖级联解的有效性；数值结果展示了这种非局域且非普适（nonlocal and nonuniversal）的行为以及级联的损耗。

Conclusion: 暖级联解所依赖的局域性假设在热阻尼存在时不成立，波动力学方程的稳态解可能并非仅由守恒通量与温度刻画，需考虑紫外截断等额外参数的影响。

Abstract: It is often assumed that general stationary solutions to the wave kinetic equation are described solely by conserved fluxes and temperature. The families of solutions parameterized by these quantities are referred to as warm cascades, and are derivable from differential models of wave kinetics. These models rely on the assumption of local interactions, and we demonstrate by a time scale argument that the nonlocal effect of thermal damping on the cascade may result in dependence on an additional parameter, the UV cutoff, calling into question the validity of warm cascade solutions. This is shown for systems with both three-wave and four-wave interactions. We then consider the depletion of the cascade by this damping, and present a numerical demonstration of this nonlocal and nonuniversal behavior.

</details>

### [164] [Machine-Learning Exploration of Defect Topologies and Thermodynamic Stability in Graphene with Atomic Vacancies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19191)
*Marcos V. N. da Costa,José R. da M. Lima Filho,Kádila R. de S. Oliveira,Raphael M. Tromer,Marcelo L. Pereira Junior*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究结合半经验原子热力学（semiempirical atomistic thermodynamics）、可解释机器学习（interpretable machine learning）与符号回归（symbolic regression），绘制并解析了石墨烯中空位（vacancy）浓度与间距所构成的稳定性图谱，最终将其凝练为紧凑的闭式解析表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原子空位及其团簇控制石墨烯的热力学稳定性与功能响应，但多空位在可变浓度与间距下的构型空间过大，难以用第一性原理方法（first-principles methods）穷举映射。因此需要一种可扩展且可解释的途径来刻画该稳定性图谱。

Method: 以 72 原子胞为基础构建数百个缺陷超胞，通过改变空位浓度与至第四近邻的间距，并在 MOPAC 中用 PM7 哈密顿量（Hamiltonian）弛豫，采用生成热（heat of formation）作为稳定性度量。结构经 Dynamic Collision Fingerprint 编码，该描述符具有平移与旋转不变性，将局部拓扑映射为虚拟探针粒子的类输运统计。随后用贝叶斯超参数搜索（Bayesian hyperparameter search）优化的梯度提升决策树（gradient boosted decision tree）建模，并以 SHAP 分析与符号回归解析。

Result: 梯度提升决策树在独立测试集上复现生成热，均方根误差（root mean squared error）约 23.5 kcal/mol，且无过拟合迹象；SHAP 分析表明缺陷浓度与空位间距为支配稳定性的两个变量。符号回归将所学映射压缩为紧凑闭式表达式，决定系数（coefficient of determination）R² = 0.9966，平均绝对误差 15.51 kcal/mol，均方根误差 24.18 kcal/mol。

Conclusion: 该工作流将高维结构—稳定性问题转化为可解释的解析规律，为二维材料（two-dimensional materials）中的理性缺陷工程（defect engineering）提供了可迁移的路径。

Abstract: Atomic vacancies and vacancy aggregates control the thermodynamic stability and the functional response of graphene, yet the configurational space spanned by many vacancies at variable concentration and separation is too large to be mapped exhaustively by first-principles methods. Here, we map and rationalize this stability landscape by combining semiempirical atomistic thermodynamics, interpretable machine learning, and symbolic regression. Several hundred defective supercells, built from a 72-atom cell by varying the vacancy concentration and the inter-vacancy distance up to the fourth neighbor, were relaxed with the PM7 Hamiltonian in MOPAC, and the heat of formation was adopted as the stability metric. Each structure was encoded with the Dynamic Collision Fingerprint, a translationally and rotationally invariant descriptor that maps the local topology onto transport-like statistics of virtual probe particles. A gradient boosted decision tree model, optimized by Bayesian hyperparameter search, reproduces the heat of formation of an independent test set with a root mean squared error of approximately 23.5~kcal/mol and no evidence of overfitting, and a SHAP analysis identifies the defect concentration and the inter-vacancy distance as the two variables that dominate the stability. Symbolic regression then condenses the learned mapping into a compact closed-form expression that reproduces the heat of formation with a coefficient of determination of $R^{2} = 0.9966$, a mean absolute error of 15.51~kcal/mol, and a root mean squared error of 24.18~kcal/mol. The workflow turns a high-dimensional structure--stability problem into an interpretable analytical law, providing a transferable route to rational defect engineering in two-dimensional materials.

</details>


### [165] [Comprehensive First-Principles Investigation of the Structural, Mechanical, Electronic, and Optical Properties of Homoelemental Phase T-GaN Monolayer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19192)
*Djardiel S. Gomes,Isaac M. Félix,Jorge O. A. L. Torres,Fabio L. L. de Mendonça,Sergio Azevedo,Marcelo L. Pereira Junior*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究采用密度泛函理论（DFT）系统研究了由四元环与八元环交替构成、同时含同元素键（Ga-Ga、N-N）与异极键（Ga-N）的二维四方氮化镓（T-GaN）单层的结构、力学、电子与光学性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非六方二维拓扑（non-hexagonal two-dimensional topologies）可突破传统蜂窝相（honeycomb phases）对III-V族单层性质的调控限制，为拓展其物理性质与应用提供新途径。

Method: 基于密度泛函理论，在广义梯度近似（GGA/PBE）与杂化HSE06泛函下计算性质，并通过声子色散（phonon dispersion）与从头算分子动力学（AIMD）模拟验证动力学稳定性。

Result: 声子色散在布里渊区（Brillouin zone）无虚频且AIMD支持其稳定性；力学上表现出显著面内各向异性，x与y方向临界应变约为16.5%与7.0%；电子结构为非磁性半导体，间接带隙为0.35 eV（PBE）与1.15 eV（HSE06），价带顶主要由N 2p轨道贡献、导带底由Ga 4s与4p态主导；沿三个晶向的光学响应在吸收系数、折射率与反射率上呈明显各向异性。

Conclusion: 研究结果深化了对四方III-V族单层物理性质的理解，表明T-GaN有望成为各向异性纳米电子（nanoelectronic）与光电子（optoelectronic）应用的有前景候选材料。

Abstract: The exploration of non-hexagonal two-dimensional topologies has opened new possibilities for tailoring the properties of group III-V monolayers beyond those accessible through conventional honeycomb phases. In this context, we have investigated the structural, mechanical, electronic, and optical properties of T-GaN, a two-dimensional tetragonal gallium nitride monolayer composed of alternating four- and eight-membered rings featuring coexisting homoelemental (Ga-Ga, N-N) and heteropolar (Ga-N) bonds, using density functional theory (DFT) within the generalized gradient approximation (GGA/PBE) and the hybrid HSE06 functional. The dynamical stability of T-GaN was confirmed by phonon dispersion calculations, which revealed the absence of imaginary frequencies throughout the Brillouin zone, and was further supported by \textit{ab initio} molecular dynamics (AIMD) simulations. The mechanical characterization reveals a pronounced in-plane anisotropy, with critical strains of approximately 16.5\% and 7.0\% along the $x$- and $y$-directions, respectively. The electronic band structure analysis indicates that T-GaN is a nonmagnetic semiconductor with an indirect band gap of 0.35~eV (PBE) and 1.15~eV (HSE06), with the valence band maximum dominated by nitrogen 2\textit{p} orbitals and the conduction band minimum governed by gallium 4\textit{s} and 4\textit{p} states. The optical response was evaluated along three crystallographic directions, exhibiting considerable anisotropy in the absorption coefficient, refractive index, and reflectivity. These findings provide new insights into the physical properties of tetragonal group III-V monolayers and suggest that T-GaN may serve as a promising candidate for anisotropic nanoelectronic and optoelectronic applications.

</details>


### [166] [Melting of corundum (α-Al2O3) under compression to 8 GPa: A decreasing melting slope](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19229)
*Vladimir L. Solozhenko*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究通过原位电阻率测量，在最高 8 GPa 压力下测定了刚玉（α-Al₂O₃）的熔化曲线，确认其熔化温度单调升高且熔化斜率不断下降，表明熔化曲线具有负曲率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刚玉（corundum）的高压熔化行为对氧化铝（alumina）富集深地系统的地球物理建模与从头算模拟（ab initio simulations）具有重要约束意义，但缺乏可靠的实验经验标准。

Method: 采用原位电阻率测量（in situ electrical resistivity measurements）在压缩条件下跟踪熔化，测量压力最高至 8 GPa，并将实验数据拟合至 Simon-Glatzel 方程。

Result: 熔化温度由常压下的 T0 = 2317 K 单调升至 7.7 GPa 时的 3090 K；拟合得到经验参数 a = 5.60 GPa、c = 0.334；熔化斜率 dTm/dp 由常压的 138 K/GPa 持续降至 7.7 GPa 的 78 K/GPa，确认 d²Tm/dp² < 0。

Conclusion: 熔化曲线的负曲率与液相优先致密化（preferential densification）及压力升高时熔化体积变化减小相一致；结果为刚玉高压熔化提供了稳健的经验标准，并为从头算模拟、热力学数据库（thermodynamic databases）及氧化铝富集深地系统的地球物理模型提供关键约束。

Abstract: Melting of corundum (α-Al2O3) has been studied under compression up to 8 GPa using in situ electrical resistivity measurements. The melting temperature increases monotonically from T0 = 2317 K at ambient pressure to 3090 K at 7.7 GPa. Fitting the experimental data to the Simon-Glatzel equation yields the empirical parameters a = 5.60 GPa and c = 0.334, providing a closed-form analytical description of the entire melting boundary. Most significantly, the melting slope, dTm/dp, decreases continuously from 138 K/GPa at ambient pressure down to 78 K/GPa at 7.7 GPa, confirming unequivocal negative curvature of the melting curve (d2Tm/dp2 < 0). This phenomenon is consistent with the preferential densification of the liquid phase and the associated reduction in the volume change upon melting as pressure rises.
