These findings provide a robust empirical standard for the high-pressure melting of corundum, establishing essential constraints for future ab initio simulations, thermodynamic databases, and geophysical models of alumina-rich deep-Earth systems.

</details>


### [167] [Electron $g$-value comparison of paramagnetic muonium and hydrogen centers in rutile TiO$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19338)
*T. U. Ito,W. Higemoto,A. Koda*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作利用双电子-缪子共振（double electron-muon resonance）测量金红石型 TiO₂ 中顺磁 Mu 中心的电子 g 值，并与 H 相关 Ti³⁺ 顺磁中心对比，验证了 Mu–H 对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缪子（muonium, Mu）常被用作凝聚态中孤立氢的模型，但其电子态是否真正等同于氢，尚需轨道敏感的探测手段加以检验。

Method: 采用双电子-缪子共振技术，测定金红石型 TiO₂（rutile TiO₂）中顺磁 Mu 中心在磁场沿 [100]、[110] 与 [001] 方向时的电子 g 值。

Result: 所得 g 值在数值大小与各向异性上均与 H 相关的 Ti³⁺ 顺磁中心高度一致。

Conclusion: 该一致性在 Ti 3d 电子态层面验证了 Mu–H 对应关系，并支持两个中心共享同一局域化的 Ti³⁺ 电子核心。

Abstract: Muonium (Mu) is widely used as a model for isolated hydrogen in condensed matter, but whether its electronic state is truly equivalent to that of hydrogen remains to be tested with an orbital-sensitive probe. Here we use double electron-muon resonance to determine the electron $g$ values of the paramagnetic Mu center in rutile TiO$_2$ for magnetic fields along [100], [110], and [001] directions. The obtained $g$ values closely follow those of the H-related Ti$^{3+}$ paramagnetic center in both magnitude and anisotropy. This agreement tests the Mu--H correspondence at the level of the Ti $3d$ electronic state and supports a common localized Ti$^{3+}$ electronic core for the two centers.

</details>


### [168] [Pressure-dependent melting and crystallization of B2-NiAl from neural-network molecular dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19355)
*A. S. Onegin,P. R. Levashov,N. M. Chtchelkatchev*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究采用深度势（Deep Potential）神经网络分子动力学方法，通过固-液两相共存模拟获得 B2-NiAl 的压强相关熔化曲线 T_m(P)，验证了热力学一致性，并证实其熔化在模型内保持同成分（congruent）特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有序 B2-NiAl 在高压下的熔化行为及熔化性质（是否同成分熔化）尚缺乏原子尺度的系统描述，且已有实验与分子动力学结果在高压缩区存在差异，需要更可靠的势模型与一致性检验。

Method: 使用基于 Deep Potential 原子间模型的神经网络分子动力学（neural-network molecular dynamics），在较宽压强范围内开展固-液两相共存（two-phase solid-liquid coexistence）模拟确定熔化温度，并计算熔化时的焓变与体积变化以比较 Clapeyron 斜率与拟合 T_m(P) 曲线的导数，另对共存构型做分层成分分析。

Result: 计算得到的 T_m(P) 在高压压缩下随压强上升的幅度强于已有实验和先前分子动力学结果；Clapeyron 斜率与拟合曲线导数在多数压强范围内吻合良好；共存液相在所有研究压强下基本保持等原子比，铝原子分数偏离化学计量值不超过 5×10⁻³。

Conclusion: 结果建立了 B2-NiAl 热力学自洽的压强相关熔化描述，并提供了其在压缩下保持同成分熔化的直接原子尺度证据。

Abstract: We investigate pressure-dependent melting of ordered B2-NiAl using neural-network molecular dynamics with a Deep Potential interatomic model. Melting temperatures are determined from two-phase solid-liquid coexistence simulations over a broad pressure range, yielding the melting curve $T_m(P)$. Relative to available experimental and previous molecular-dynamics results, the present calculations predict a stronger increase of the melting temperature with pressure at elevated compression. To assess the thermodynamic consistency of the calculated melting line, we evaluate the enthalpy and volume changes upon melting and compare the Clapeyron slope with the derivative of the fitted $T_m(P)$ curve. The two estimates are in good agreement over most of the investigated pressure range, supporting the internal consistency of the coexistence results. To probe the character of melting, we perform a layer-resolved composition analysis of the coexistence configurations and find that the coexisting liquid remains essentially equiatomic at all studied pressures, with deviations of the aluminum fraction from the stoichiometric value not exceeding $5\times10^{-3}$. This provides direct atomistic evidence that melting of B2-NiAl remains congruent within the present model. Together, these results establish a thermodynamically consistent pressure-dependent melting description of B2-NiAl and clarify the character of its melting under compression.

</details>


### [169] [The Origin of the Observed Raman Peaks in α-MnTe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19467)
*Nurul Azam,Syed Mohammad Shahed,Liam T. Schmidt,Sara Bey,Oksana Yastrubchak,Maria F. Munoz,Riccardo Torsi,Thi Hai Yen Pham,Dushyanthini Balasundaram,Resham Babu Regami,Wentao Liang,Imrankhan Mulani,Matthew Matzelle,Vineet Kumar Sharma,Sougata Mardanya,Sugata Chowdhury,Nirmal Ghimire,Patrick M. Vora,Angela R. Hight Walker,Xinyu Liu,Badih A. Assaf,Arun Bansil,Alberto De la Torre,Swastik Kar*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究论证室温交替磁体（altermagnet）α-MnTe 中争议性的 120 与 140 cm⁻¹ 拉曼峰实为表面暴露空气后生成的单质碲（elemental tellurium）所致，属外源性（extrinsic）信号，而非本征声子模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: α-MnTe 室温拉曼谱中 120(3) 与 140(3) cm⁻¹ 峰无法对应预测声子谱（phonon spectrum），在交替磁体（altermagnet）领域引发持续争论，亟需澄清其来源。

Method: 结合 MBE 生长薄膜（MBE-grown thin films）的拉曼光谱、X 射线光电子能谱（XPS）、以及截面扫描透射电镜与能量色散 X 射线谱（STEM-EDS）联用，对比空气暴露与 AlOₓ 覆盖（capping）样品。

Result: 这些峰与单质碲拉曼特征吻合，空气暴露后迅速出现，AlOₓ 覆盖样品中缺失；XPS 显示空气暴露一分钟内即破坏 Mn–Te 键并氧化 Mn；STEM-EDS 揭示氧化导致 Mn 外扩散（out-diffusion），形成数纳米厚氧化层与埋藏富 Te 区。此外所有样品均未出现常被归因于 MnTe₂ 的 175 cm⁻¹ 峰。

Conclusion: α-MnTe 的 120、140 与 90 cm⁻¹ 峰均为外源性的，源于表面氧化形成的单质碲；MnTe 及类似含 Te 材料的表面敏感与光学表征须警惕即使极短空气暴露带来的氧化影响。

Abstract: The Raman spectrum of the room-temperature altermagnet $α$-MnTe is reported to contain unassigned peaks at 120(3) cm$^{-1}$ and 140(3) cm$^{-1}$, absent from the predicted phonon spectrum of the material. This has generated considerable debate within the altermagnet community and necessitates urgent resolution. This work establishes that these peaks, together with the 90(5) cm$^{-1}$ peak that matches a theoretically predicted mode, are all extrinsic, originating from elemental tellurium formed during air exposure of the surface. Raman spectra of MBE-grown thin films show that these peaks closely match those of elemental tellurium, emerge soon after air exposure, and are absent in AlO$_x$-capped films. X-ray photoelectron spectroscopy shows that air exposure breaks Mn--Te bonds and oxidizes Mn within a minute. Cross-sectional scanning transmission electron microscopy with energy-dispersive X-ray spectroscopy reveals that the oxidation leads to Mn out-diffusion, forming a few-nanometer-thick oxide layer above a buried, Te-enriched region, likely responsible for the anomalous Raman peaks. Additionally, no sample exhibited the 175 cm$^{-1}$ Raman peak which is commonly attributed to MnTe$_2$. The aggressive surface oxidation and associated elemental Te formation in MnTe have important implications for any surface-sensitive and optical characterization of MnTe (and other similar Te-containing materials) involving even the briefest air exposure.

</details>


### [170] [ALIGNN 2.0: A Unified Line-Graph Neural Network Framework for Materials Screening, Force Fields, Inverse Design, Spectroscopy, and Microscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19487)
*Jaehyung Lee,Charles Rhys Campbell,Akshaya Ajith,Sergei V. Kalinin,Christopher Wolverton,Kamal Choudhary*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: ALIGNN 2.0 是 Atomistic Line Graph Neural Network 的无依赖纯 PyTorch 重实现，用单一图统一标量、谱、张量、逐原子与力场预测，在 JARVIS-Leaderboard 多数基准上超越原版，并支持多种光谱与显微成像模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图神经网络（graph neural network）依赖专用图库，损害可移植性与可复现性；且性质预测与力场（force-field）建模历来需要彼此独立的图处理流程，缺乏统一框架。

Method: 提供纯 PyTorch、无依赖的 ALIGNN 2.0 实现，从零构建线图（line graph）及其批处理（batching），运行于当代加速器；将标量、谱、张量、逐原子与力场预测统一到单一图中；并对比半径图与 k 近邻（k-nearest-neighbor, kNN）图。

Result: 在 JARVIS-Leaderboard 上，30 项单性质基准中 26 项领先原版 ALIGNN，压电与介电极大值、剥离能、模量及超导 Tc 提升显著；ALIGNN-FF 兼容 LAMMPS 与 OpenMM，在 Matbench-Discovery 与 CHIPS-FF 上以极少参数量匹配领先通用势，并可扩展至十万原子级胞；作为条件晶体扩散模型去噪器时，显式线图消息传递持续降低结构去噪损失。

Conclusion: 同一框架可从单一弛豫结构重建与实验和 DFT 一致的红外、拉曼、光学介电与中子谱，并驱动冻结声子（frozen-phonon）电子显微成像模拟；独立扩散的冗余键角状态可学习，但不能一致地改善重构，也无法与线图加性结合。

Abstract: Graph neural networks are central to materials property prediction and machine-learning interatomic potentials, yet their reliance on specialized graph libraries hampers portability and reproducibility, and property and force-field models have historically required separate graph pipelines. We present ALIGNN 2.0, a dependency-free, pure-PyTorch reimplementation of the Atomistic Line Graph Neural Network, with the line graph and its batching built from scratch, running on current-generation accelerators and unifying scalar, spectral, tensorial, per-atom, and force-field prediction behind a single graph, a combination that to our knowledge no existing framework provides. Comparing radius and k-nearest-neighbor (kNN) graphs, the wider kNN graph is more accurate for properties while the smoothly varying radius graph is required for energy-conserving molecular dynamics. On the JARVIS-Leaderboard, ALIGNN 2.0 leads on 26 of 30 single-property benchmarks against the original ALIGNN, with large gains for piezoelectric and dielectric maxima, exfoliation energy, moduli, and superconducting Tc. The LAMMPS- and OpenMM-compatible ALIGNN-FF matches leading universal potentials on the Matbench-Discovery and CHIPS-FF benchmarks at a small fraction of their parameters while scaling to hundred-thousand-atom cells. We further use ALIGNN 2.0 as the denoiser in a conditional crystal-diffusion model, where explicit line-graph message passing consistently lowers structural denoising loss. We also show, as work in progress, that an independently diffused, redundant bond-angle state is learnable but does not uniformly improve reconstruction or combine additively with the line graph. Finally, from a single relaxed structure the same framework reconstructs infrared, Raman, optical-dielectric, and neutron spectra in agreement with experiment and DFT, and drives frozen-phonon electron-microscopy image simulation.

</details>


### [171] [Portfolio-Based Constrained Multi-Objective Bayesian Optimization for Materials Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19550)
*Sushant Sinha,Christofer Hardcastle,Robert Robinson,Shakti Prasad Padhy,Brent Vela,Douglas Allaire,Raymundo Arroyave*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 将材料发现与设计中的采集函数（acquisition function）选择重构为自适应策略选择问题，并用 UCB-Bandit 与基于大语言模型（large language model, LLM）的 Agentic-Switch 两种控制器，在合成基准与两个材料设计案例中与固定策略基线比较；自适应策略在累积可行数与可行超体积提升两项指标上均具竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 材料发现与设计活动可表述为约束多目标贝叶斯优化（constrained multi-objective Bayesian optimization, CMOBO）问题，其中每次实验决策需在"发现可行候选"与"改进帕累托前沿（Pareto front）"两个相互耦合又竞争的目标之间权衡。单一采集函数往往难以同时兼顾二者，因此需要考察可自适应切换策略的方案。

Method: 把采集函数的选择重新表述为在传统采集函数与面向可行性的采集函数所构成组合（portfolio）上的自适应策略选择问题，并采用两种控制器实现：UCB-Bandit（改进的 UCB 多臂老虎机）与 Agentic-Switch（由 LLM 驱动的多智能体决策系统）。两者在五个合成基准函数与两个材料设计案例中与固定策略基线进行计算机模拟（in silico）对比评估。

Result: 自适应策略在累积可行数（cumulative feasibility count）与可行超体积提升（feasible hypervolume improvement）两项指标上表现具有竞争力；相比之下，各单一采集函数仅在其中一项指标上表现良好。评估覆盖五个合成基准函数与两个材料设计案例研究。

Conclusion: 结果表明自适应策略更适合约束条件下的材料科学问题，因其能够兼顾发现可行候选与改进帕累托前沿这两个竞争目标，而非依赖单一采集函数。

Abstract: Materials discovery and design campaigns can be formulated as constrained multi-objective Bayesian optimization (CMOBO) problems, within which each experimental decision negotiates between two coupled but competing goals: discovering feasible candidates and refining the underlying Pareto front. Here we recast acquisition-function choice as an adaptive policy-selection problem over a portfolio of conventional and feasibility-focused acquisition functions. This was done using two controllers: UCB-Bandit, a modified UCB multi-armed bandit, and Agentic-Switch, a multi-agent decision system driven by a large language model (LLM). Both were evaluated against fixed-policy baselines in silico across five synthetic benchmark functions and two materials design case studies. The adaptive policies performed competitively in terms of both cumulative feasibility count and feasible hypervolume improvement, while each individual acquisition function performed well for only one metric, suggesting that adaptive policies are better suited for constrained materials science problems.

</details>


### [172] [Hydrogen Reorganization in Hot Dense Ammonia](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19605)
*Yu Tao,Jingyi Liu,Li Lei*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过激光加热金刚石压砧实验，在150 GPa与1000 K以上观察到离子态氨转变为不透明新相，并经拉曼光谱与第一性原理计算确认为理论预言的NH₇离子相P4₁2₁2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 氢在高压下可与多种简单分子固体形成稳定化合物，但氨氢化物（ammonia hydride）此前一直未被实验观测到，这一空白构成本研究的出发点。

Method: 采用激光加热金刚石压砧（laser-heating diamond anvil cell, LHDAC）实验系列，结合拉曼光谱（Raman spectroscopy）探测结构变化，并与第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）结果相互印证以确定物相。

Result: 在150 GPa、1000 K以上，透明离子态氨转变为不透明相；拉曼光谱显示显著结构变化，并出现两个不同的不对称H₂振动模（ν₁与ν₂）；该相被鉴定为理论预言的NH₇离子相P4₁2₁2，由NH₄⁺、H⁻和H₂单元构成。

Conclusion: 氨氢化物的形成遵循不同于其他含H₂混合物的热力学路径，揭示了热稠密氨（hot dense ammonia）中由氢重组（hydrogen reorganization）驱动的新离子转变路径。

Abstract: Hydrogen is known to form stable compounds with a variety of simple molecular solids under high pressure. However, ammonia hydride has not been experimentally observed. Here, through a series of laser-heating diamond anvil cell (LHDAC) experiments, we observed the transparent ionic ammonia transformed into an opaque phase above 150 GPa and 1000 K. Raman spectroscopy reveals a pronounced structural change, accompanied by the emergence of two distinct asymmetric H$_2$ vibrons ($ν_1$ and $ν_2$). A new ionic phase produced following thermally induced dissociation and hydrogen reorganization in hot dense ammonia. The combination of experimental results and first-principles calculations identify this phase as the theoretically predicted ionic $P4_12_12$ phase of NH$_7$ , which consists of NH$_4^+$, H$^-$and H$_2$ units. Our results suggest that the formation of ammonia hydrides follows a thermodynamic pathway distinct from those of other H$_2$-containing mixtures, revealing a new ionic transformation pathway driven by hydrogen reorganization in hot dense ammonia.

</details>


### [173] [Grain Boundary Distortion Reorients the Local 4f Easy Axis and Splits Light from Heavy Lanthanides in Nd2Fe14B](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19614)
*Avik Mahataa,M. Zakotnik*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作利用嵌入多参考电子结构计算（embedded multireference electronic-structure calculations），系统解析了Nd2Fe14B中晶界（grain boundary）环境对4f晶体场（crystal field）的调制规律。晶界畸变沿镧系（lanthanide）序列产生反转：轻镧系晶体场减弱最多达36%，重镧系增强约25%，Nd为反转点，晶界位点成为磁化反转的有利成核中心。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀土永磁体的矫顽力（coercivity）源于作用于局域4f电子的晶体场，但晶界如何改变其底层单离子哈密顿量（single-ion Hamiltonian）尚不清楚。该研究旨在填补晶界环境下4f晶体场认识的空白。

Method: 采用嵌入多参考电子结构计算，对替代系列（substitutional series）Ce、Pr、Nd、Gd、Tb与Dy，分别确定其在本体（bulk）与晶界环境中的4f晶体场。并据单离子参数分析基态双重态（ground-state doublet）与易轴（easy axis）取向。

Result: 晶界畸变沿镧系序列引发晶体场强度反转：轻镧系减弱至多36%，重镧系增强约25%，Nd为反转点。Dy基态双重态更为孤立且更轴向，Ce则出现低能激发塌缩并抑制各向异性（anisotropy）。局域易轴由本体c轴转向基面（basal plane），表明晶界位点是磁化反转的有利成核中心（nucleation centers）。

Conclusion: 研究给出了重稀土晶界扩散（grain-boundary diffusion）的原子尺度依据，并提供可迁移的单离子参数以支持自旋-晶格模拟（spin-lattice simulations）。

Abstract: The coercivity of rare-earth permanent magnets originates from the crystal field acting on localized 4f electrons, yet how grain boundaries modify the underlying single-ion Hamiltonian remains largely unknown. Here we determine the 4f crystal field in bulk and grain-boundary environments of Nd2Fe14B using embedded multireference electronic-structure calculations for the substitutional series Ce, Pr, Nd, Gd, Tb, and Dy. Grain-boundary distortion produces a crossover across the lanthanide series: the crystal field weakens by up to 36% for light lanthanides but strengthens by about 25% for heavy lanthanides, with Nd marking the crossover. The ground-state doublet of Dy becomes more isolated and axial, whereas Ce exhibits a collapsed low-lying excitation that suppresses anisotropy. The local easy axis rotates from the bulk c axis toward the basal plane, identifying grain-boundary sites as favorable nucleation centers for magnetization reversal. These results provide an atomistic basis for heavy rare-earth grain-boundary diffusion and furnish transferable single-ion parameters for spin-lattice simulations.

</details>


### [174] [The Roadmap of Inorganic Computational Materials Databases: Capabilities, Credibility, Coverage, and the Open Frontier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19833)
*Miao Liu,Jianghao Jin,Tenglong Lu,Jianguo Si,Yin Shi,Sheng Meng,Weihua Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文以述评（perspective）形式整合第一性原理软件生态、十九类材料性质的计算成本与可信度、以及现有计算数据库覆盖情况，指出瓶颈已从方法能力转向“可信度的经济学（economics of trust）”，并据此提出三阶段路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算材料数据库（computational materials databases）已成为无机材料数据驱动发现的核心基础设施，但其在不同性质族（property families）间的增长明显不均衡，需要厘清领域现状与未来方向。

Method: 对主流密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）软件进行系统性调研，评估十九类材料性质族的计算成本与可信度，并将数据库覆盖情况映射到 Gartner 式成熟度周期（Gartner-style readiness cycle）上。

Result: 第一性原理代码生态在方法上已趋成熟，几乎所有技术相关性质都至少有一个生产级（production-grade）代码可计算；数据库覆盖呈显著分化：基态结构、能量学、弹性与拓扑已进入常规生产，而包括 NMR/EPR 参数、芯能级谱、电子-声子性质、热导率与量子输运在内的九类性质族尚无系统性计算数据库。

Conclusion: 这些“空白区（blank zones）”构成未来十年的科学机遇；作者提出三阶段路线图：近期整合覆盖与互操作性（interoperability），中期通过替代模型加速工作流（surrogate-accelerated workflows）实现中等成本性质的产业化，长期借助机器学习原子间势（machine-learned interatomic potentials）、自主计算基础设施与社区治理攻克高成本前沿。

Abstract: Computational materials databases have become central infrastructure for data-driven discovery of inorganic materials, yet their growth remains strikingly uneven across property families. This perspective synthesizes a systematic survey of mainstream density functional theory (DFT) software, the computational cost and credibility of nineteen material-property families, and the coverage of existing computational databases, into a coherent picture of where the field stands and where it should go. We show that the ecosystem of first-principles codes is methodologically mature: for nearly every property of technological interest, at least one production-grade code can compute it.The binding constraint is no longer methodological capability but the economics of trust - which properties can be computed cheaply enough, and accurately enough, to be harvested at database scale. Mapping database coverage onto a Gartner-style readiness cycle reveals a sharp divide: ground-state structure, energetics, elasticity, and topology have reached routine production, while nine property families - including NMR/EPR parameters, core-level spectra, electron-phonon properties, thermal conductivity, and quantum transport - remain without any systematic computational database. We argue that these blank zones define the scientific opportunity of the next decade, and we propose a three-horizon roadmap: consolidating coverage and interoperability in the near term, industrializing mid-cost properties through surrogate-accelerated workflows in the medium term, and conquering the high-cost frontier through machine-learned interatomic potentials, autonomous computing infrastructure, and community governance in the long term.

</details>


### [175] [Orbital fingerprinting of magnetism across the Mn-Ni-Ga Heusler ternary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19835)
*Felipe Hawthorne,Daniela A. Damasceno,Raphael M. Tromer,Ronaldo R. Pelá,Cristiano F. Woellner*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作针对 Mn–Ni–Ga Heusler 合金中近简并磁性基态难以判定、且偏离化学计量比时既有规则失效的问题，提出以投影态密度（projected density of states）中提取的"轨道指纹（orbital fingerprints）"作为机器学习输入，实现磁性有序、磁矩幅度、费米面自旋极化（spin polarization）的分类与相图插值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mn–Ni–Ga Heusler 合金中竞争磁态能量差仅为每原子数 meV 量级，基态无法由成分直接读出，必须借助电子结构判定。当体系偏离化学计量比时，既有的 Heusler 磁性规律不再适用，进一步加大了判定难度。

Method: 从投影态密度中提取轨道指纹作为特征，输入机器学习模型，分别用于磁性有序分类、磁矩幅度预测、费米能级自旋极化预测以及相图插值。模型训练数据为覆盖三元成分区的 370 个准随机结构（quasirandom structures）的自旋极化第一性原理（first-principles）计算结果。

Result: 所训练模型与文献中可获得的磁性基态及晶格参数吻合良好。在磁性有序任务中，排序最靠前的描述符与第一性原理计算中用于区分各相的物理量一致，表明轨道指纹捕捉到了其背后的物理机制。

Conclusion: 轨道指纹作为可解释的描述符，能够有效连接电子结构与磁性性质，为化学计量比偏离区域的 Heusler 合金磁性基态判定与相图构建提供可行途径。

Abstract: In Mn--Ni--Ga Heusler alloys, the competing magnetic states are separated by differences of a few meV per atom, so the ground state has to be resolved from the electronic structure and cannot be read off the composition. Moving away from the stoichiometric compounds, where the established rules for Heusler magnetism fall short, increases this difficulty. To overcome it, we introduce orbital fingerprints taken from the projected density of states, and use them as the input to machine-learning models for classification of the magnetic ordering, for the magnetic moment amplitude, for the spin polarization at the Fermi level and for interpolation of the phase diagram. The models are trained on a dataset of 370 spin-polarized first-principles calculations of quasirandom structures covering the ternary, which show good agreement with the magnetic ground states and lattice parameters available in the literature. For magnetic ordering, the top-ranked descriptor is the same quantity found by first-principles calculations to distinguish the phases, suggesting that the fingerprints capture the underlying physics.

</details>


### [176] [Machine Learning for High-Entropy Catalysts: Methods and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19837)
*Hao Chen,Zongrui Pei,Xianglin Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该综述系统梳理了机器学习（machine learning, ML）方法在高熵合金（high-entropy alloys, HEAs）催化领域的最新方法学进展与应用，指出ML可加速新型HEA催化剂的理性设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HEA催化剂因高构型熵（configurational entropy）、元素协同效应、可调电子结构与优异结构稳定性而展现优异催化性能，但其庞大的组分空间使传统实验与理论设计成本高、效率低，亟需数据驱动方法。

Method: 综述性方法：归纳ML用于HEA催化的最新方法学手段，包括预测模型与ML代理模型（ML surrogates）以解析组分—结构—性能关系，并利用大语言模型（large language models, LLMs）进行知识抽取、假设生成与验证，并作为构建集成设计工作流的基础。

Result: ML模型与代理模型能够破解HEA催化剂复杂的组分—结构—性能关系；LLMs支撑的知识抽取与假设验证及集成设计流程可显著加速新型HEA催化剂的开发。

Conclusion: 该综述总结了ML方法在HEA催化中的方法与应用的进展，讨论了现存挑战，并对未来研究方向提出展望，以支持催化剂的理性设计与高效开发。

Abstract: High-entropy alloys (HEAs) exhibit exceptional catalytic performance in various reactions due to their high configurational entropy, synergistic elemental effects, tunable electronic structures, and excellent structural stability. However, the vast compositional space of HEA catalysts makes traditional experimental and theoretical design costly and inefficient. In recent years, data-driven machine learning (ML) methods have emerged as powerful tools for studying HEAs in catalysis. Through predictive models and ML surrogates, researchers can decipher the intricate composition-structure-performance relationships of these materials. In addition, by leveraging large language models (LLMs) for knowledge extraction, hypothesis generation and validation, and as a foundation to build integrated design workflows, ML approaches can significantly accelerate the design of novel HEA catalysts. This review systematically summarizes the latest methodological advances and applications of ML methods for HEAs in catalysis, discusses the challenges, and offers insights into future research directions to support the rational design and efficient development of catalysts.

</details>


### [177] [Anharmonic Phonon Renormalization and Defect Tolerance of the Thermoelectric Power Factor in Monolayer SnSe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20019)
*Nguyen Tran Gia Bao,Thang Bach Phan,Vu Thi Hanh Thu,Nguyen Tuan Hung*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究结合密度泛函理论、随机自洽谐振近似（SSCHA）与包含电子-声子及电子-缺陷散射的玻尔兹曼输运计算，揭示单层 SnSe 中 α 相与 β 相声子非谐行为的差异及其对热电功率因子与点缺陷容忍度的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单层硒化锡（SnSe）具有相依赖的非谐晶格动力学，但其对热电功率因子（PF）与点缺陷容忍度的影响尚未明确，故需澄清不同相中非谐效应的后果。

Method: 联合使用密度泛函理论、随机自洽谐振近似（SSCHA）以及考虑电子-声子与电子-缺陷散射的玻尔兹曼输运计算，对比研究单层 α-SnSe（Pnma）与 β-SnSe（Cmcm）。

Result: 在动力学稳定的 α-SnSe 中，SSCHA 对有限温度声子进行重整但未改变 n 型输运的定性图景；在 β-SnSe 中，SSCHA 消除了 800-1000 K 下 Cmcm 相的谐波软模不稳定性，使高温输运计算成为可能，其中 LO/TO-2 为主要电子散射通道。在约 10^12 cm^-2 的低密度窗口，800-900 K 下 n 型 PF 达 15-19 μW/(K^2·cm)，并因更高电导率而超过 p 型；Se 空位（V_Se）的电子-缺陷散射弱于 Sn 空位（V_Sn），两相中 p 型输运的缺陷容忍度均低于 n 型。

Conclusion: 研究提出操作性的临界缺陷浓度 C_crit（PF 相对无缺陷值下降 15%），p 型 α-SnSe 含 V_Sn 时最低为 8.841×10^-5（约 88 ppm），而 n 型 β-SnSe 含 V_Se 时在 5×10^-3（5000 ppm）内仍未达到该阈值；结果区分了稳定 α-SnSe 的有限温度声子重整与 β-SnSe 的非谐稳定化，并为保持 PF 提供了缺陷浓度上限。

Abstract: Monolayer tin selenide (SnSe) exhibits phase-dependent anharmonic lattice dynamics, yet their consequences for the thermoelectric power factor (PF) and point-defect tolerance remain unresolved. We combine density functional theory, the stochastic self-consistent harmonic approximation (SSCHA), and Boltzmann transport calculations including electron-phonon and electron-defect scattering to investigate monolayer $α$-SnSe (Pnma) and $β$-SnSe (Cmcm). In dynamically stable $α$-SnSe, SSCHA renormalizes the finite-temperature phonons without changing the qualitative n-type transport picture. In $β$-SnSe, SSCHA removes the harmonic soft-mode instability of the Cmcm phase at 800-1000 K, and thereby enables high-temperature transport calculations; LO/TO-2 is the principal electron-scattering channel. In the lower-density window near $10^{12}$ cm$^{-2}$, the n-type PF reaches 15-19 $μ\mathrm{W}/(\mathrm{K}^{2}\cdot\mathrm{cm})$ at 800-900 K and exceeds the p-type PF primarily because of the higher electrical conductivity. Se vacancies ($V_{\mathrm{Se}}$) produce weaker electron-defect scattering than Sn vacancies ($V_{\mathrm{Sn}}$), and p-type transport is less defect tolerant than n-type transport in both phases. We define an operational critical defect concentration, $C_{\mathrm{crit}}$, at which the PF decreases by 15% relative to the corresponding defect-free value. The lowest $C_{\mathrm{crit}}$ is $8.841\times10^{-5}$ (approximately 88 ppm) for p-type $α$-SnSe with $V_{\mathrm{Sn}}$; for n-type $β$-SnSe with $V_{\mathrm{Se}}$, the 15% threshold is not reached up to $5\times10^{-3}$ (5000 ppm). These results distinguish finite-temperature phonon renormalization in stable $α$-SnSe from anharmonic stabilization in $β$-SnSe and provide defect-concentration limits for preserving the PF.

</details>


### [178] [Modeling Dynamic Magnetic Response of Spinel Soft Ferrites with the Steepest-Entropy-Ascent Quantum Thermodynamics Formalism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20062)
*Deepak Dhariwal,William T. Reynolds,Michael R. von Spakovsky*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究采用场驱动的陡峭熵升量子热力学（SEAQT）模型，在统一第一性原理框架下比较 Fe₃O₄、MnFe₂O₄ 与 (Mn₀.₅Zn₀.₅)Fe₂O₄ 的电子、声子与磁振子响应，发现无单一铁氧体在所有指标上占优，材料优选取决于具体性能、频率、场幅与阳离子构型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交变场（alternating-field）应用中筛选软磁铁氧体（soft ferrites）需在磁响应、耗散、非线性与发热之间取得平衡，因此需要一个可在同一框架下按频谱与动力学分辨比较不同铁氧体响应的工具。

Method: 使用场驱动的陡峭熵升量子热力学（steepest-entropy-ascent quantum thermodynamics, SEAQT）模型，处理空间均匀的纵向弛豫（longitudinal relaxation），忽略畴壁运动、横向转动、共振、涡流效应与散热；三种材料统一取 τ_e=0.05 ps、τ_p=3 ps，纵向磁振子弛豫时间分别取 500、200、85 ps，这些取自文献的值为模型输入而非对实测损耗的拟合。

Result: Mn–Zn 铁氧体给出最大的纵向磁化强度变化峰值、在高频下响应保持最好，并具有最大的每周期功与磁振子温度峰谷变化；MnFe₂O₄ 则给出最大的归一化 χ'' 峰值。

Conclusion: 没有任何单一铁氧体在所有指标上排名最高，优选材料取决于所关注的性能、频率、场幅与阳离子构型；该模型因而是面向工程比较的频谱与动力学分辨筛选工具，而非总铁芯损耗（total core loss）的预测方法。

Abstract: Selecting soft ferrites for alternating-field applications requires balancing magnetic response, dissipation, nonlinearity, and heating. We use a field-driven steepest-entropy-ascent quantum thermodynamic (SEAQT) model to compare the electron, phonon, and magnon responses of Fe$_3$O$_4$, MnFe$2$O$4$, and (Mn${0.5}$Zn${0.5}$)Fe$_2$O$_4$ within a common first-principles framework. The model treats spatially uniform longitudinal relaxation and neglects domain-wall motion, transverse rotation, resonance, eddy-current effects, and heat removal. We use $τ_e=0.05$ ps and $τ_p=3$ ps for all three materials, with effective longitudinal magnon relaxation times of 500, 200, and 85 ps, respectively; these literature-motivated values are model inputs, not fits to measured losses. Within this framework, the Mn--Zn ferrite gives the largest peak longitudinal magnetization change, retains its response best at high frequency, and shows the largest work per cycle and peak-to-peak magnon temperature change, whereas MnFe$_2$O$_4$ gives the largest normalized $χ''$ peak. Thus, no single ferrite ranks highest across all metrics: the preferred material depends on the property, frequency, field amplitude, and cation configuration of interest. The model therefore provides a spectrum- and kinetics-resolved screening tool for engineering comparison of ferrites rather than a prediction of total core loss.

</details>


### [179] [Coherent antiferromagnetic resonance in MnO driven by impulsive terahertz excitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20122)
*Yinchuan Lv,Martin J. Cross,Hari Paudyal,Christopher T. Parzyck,A. H. M. Reid,Durga Paudyal,Matthias C. Hoffmann*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 在单晶 MnO 中实现太赫兹脉冲激发与时间分辨双折射探测反铁磁共振，发现共振在趋近奈尔温度时软化并强阻尼，且其光学可见度显著弱于 NiO，可归因于 Mn²⁺ 弱自旋-轨道介导的磁光耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反铁磁体（antiferromagnets, AFMs）因本征快速自旋动力学与零净磁矩，被视为超快信息处理的候选平台。但实现该潜力需澄清太赫兹（terahertz, THz）场如何激发相干磁振子（coherent magnons），以及自旋运动如何转换为光学信号。

Method: 对单晶氧化锰（manganese(II) oxide, MnO）施加脉冲太赫兹激发，并采用时间分辨双折射（time-resolved birefringence）测量，在时域探测反铁磁共振（antiferromagnetic resonance, AFMR）。

Result: 在反铁磁相中观测到长寿命相干自旋振荡；随温度升高趋近奈尔温度（Néel temperature）时，共振发生软化并呈现强阻尼。尽管激发行为相似，MnO 中探测到的双折射明显弱于 NiO，该抑制被归因于轨道单态、高自旋 Mn²⁺ 的弱自旋-轨道介导磁光耦合。

Conclusion: 结果说明相干磁振子的激发与其光学探测受不同微观相互作用支配。

Abstract: Antiferromagnets (AFMs) offer a promising platform for ultrafast information processing owing to their intrinsically fast spin dynamics and vanishing net magnetization. Realizing this potential, however, requires understanding how terahertz fields excite coherent magnons and how the resulting spin motion is transduced into an optical signal. Here we report impulsive terahertz (THz) excitation and time-domain detection of the antiferromagnetic resonance (AFMR) in single-crystal manganese(II) oxide. Time-resolved birefringence measurements reveal long-lived coherent spin oscillations in the AFM phase. The resonance softens and becomes strongly damped upon warming toward the Néel temperature. Despite this similar excitation behavior, the detected birefringence in MnO is markedly weaker than in NiO. We associate this suppressed optical visibility with the weak spin--orbit-mediated magneto-optical coupling of orbital-singlet, high-spin Mn$^{2+}$. These results demonstrate that coherent magnon excitation and its optical detection are governed by distinct microscopic interactions.

</details>


### [180] [Divergent Solid-state Conversion Pathways in Evaporated All-perovskite Tandem Solar Cells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20326)
*Huagui Lai,Amber Wright,Nick Huber,Niels Uythoven,Federico De Giorgi,Jincheng Luo,Tristan Sachsenweger,Sunil B. Shivarudraiah,Chih-Jen Shih,Wolfgang Tress,Fan Fu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究通过时间切片非原位分析揭示顺序热蒸发（sTE）宽带隙（WBG）与锡铅窄带隙（NBG）前驱体叠层的固态转化机制差异，并据此制备带隙1.26-1.96 eV吸收层，实现首个蒸发型全钙钛矿叠层电池（TSC），效率19.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 顺序热蒸发被视为无溶剂制备中带隙钙钛矿的路径，但其向混合卤化物宽带隙与Sn-Pb窄带隙吸收层的拓展受限于对固态转化（solid-state conversion）机理认识不足。研究旨在厘清不同带隙前驱体叠层的转化差异，以指导全钙钛矿叠层电池（all-perovskite tandem solar cells）的蒸发制备。

Method: 采用时间切片非原位分析（time-sliced ex situ analysis）对比顺序蒸发宽带隙（WBG）与窄带隙（NBG）前驱体叠层的固态转化过程，考察FA物种渗透、Br/I再分布、PbBr₂厚度与Sn/Pb互扩散等行为，并据此设计带隙跨度1.26-1.96 eV的sTE吸收层、构筑蒸发型全钙钛矿叠层电池并开展封装稳定性测试（ISOS-D-2）。

Result: WBG叠层中，含甲脒（FA）物种穿透无机模板，Br/I再分布先于三维钙钛矿大量形成，光活性相从化学混合储库中结晶，决定最终带隙的是绝对PbBr₂厚度而非名义PbBr₂/PbI₂比。NBG叠层中，碘化甲脒沉积后早期形成富Pb钙钛矿相，限制FA向埋底SnI₂前驱体的进一步渗透；后续退火促使晶格重组快于Sn/Pb互扩散，留下垂直组分梯度。所制吸收层带隙覆盖1.26-1.96 eV，首个蒸发型全钙钛矿TSC效率达19.2%；封装器件在65 °C、1,200 h后平均保持初始效率的80%。

Conclusion: 结果表明固态转化（solid-state conversion）存在带隙特异性机制，对其进行针对性调控可作为顺序蒸发钙钛矿叠层光伏（perovskite tandem photovoltaics）的设计原则。

Abstract: Sequential thermal evaporation (sTE) is emerging as a solvent-free route to high-quality mid-bandgap perovskites, but its extension to mixed-halide wide-bandgap (WBG) and Sn-Pb narrow-bandgap (NBG) absorbers for all-perovskite tandem solar cells (TSCs) remains limited by an incomplete understanding of solid-state conversion. Here, using time-sliced ex situ analysis, we reveal divergent solid-state conversion mechanisms in sequentially evaporated WBG and NBG precursor stacks. In WBG stacks, formamidinium (FA)-containing species penetrate the inorganic template and Br/I redistribution precedes substantial three-dimensional perovskite formation. The photoactive phase then crystallizes from a chemically mixed reservoir, and absolute PbBr$_2$ thickness, rather than nominal PbBr$_2$/PbI$_2$ ratio, determines the final bandgap. In NBG stacks, by contrast, an early Pb-rich perovskite phase forms upon formamidinium iodide deposition, restricting further FA penetration into the buried SnI$_2$ precursor. Subsequent annealing promotes rapid lattice reorganization faster than Sn/Pb interdiffusion, leaving vertical compositional gradients. Guided by these insights, we develop sTE absorbers with bandgaps spanning 1.26-1.96 eV and demonstrate the first evaporated all-perovskite TSC, reaching a power conversion efficiency of 19.2%. Encapsulated tandems retain on average 80% of their initial efficiency after 1,200 h at 65 $^\circ$C (ISOS-D-2). These results establish bandgap-specific control of solid-state conversion as a design principle for sequentially evaporated perovskite tandem photovoltaics.

</details>


### [181] [A Computational Method to Simulate Electrostatic Actuation in Polycatenated Architected Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20373)
*Mateus Maciel Vivaldi,Alexandre F. Fonseca*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作提出并定性验证了一套用于模拟带静电的多链环架构材料（polycatenated architected materials, PAMs）的计算框架，可重现静电驱动并揭示可逆的流-固转变与静电刚度调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PAMs 已展现应力-应变滞后（stress-strain hysteresis）及几何驱动的类固-类流转变等特性，但现有有限元模型（finite element models）均未考虑静电效应，缺乏对带电 PAMs 的预测工具。

Method: 提出一个计算框架，用于模拟带静电 PAMs 的结构、动力学与力学响应，并与实验进行定性验证。

Result: 该框架成功重现静电驱动（electrostatic actuation），与实验吻合良好；静电电荷可实现可逆的流-固转变（fluid-to-solid transition），并提供静电刚度（electrostatic stiffness）的估算手段，充电后刚度提升最高达七倍。

Conclusion: 该研究拓展了 PAMs 的设计空间，并为可主动控制的力学超材料（mechanical metamaterials）提供了预测性工具。

Abstract: A novel class of polycatenated architected materials (PAMs) has recently emerged, exhibiting distinctive mechanical properties such as stress-strain hysteresis and a geometry-driven transition between solid-like and fluid-like behavior. Although finite element models exist for conventional PAMs, none currently account for electrostatic effects. Here, we propose and qualitatively validate a computational framework to simulate the structure, dynamics, and mechanical response of electrostatically charged PAMs. Our approach successfully reproduces electrostatic actuation in close agreement with experiments, and reveals that electrostatic charges enable a reversible fluid-to-solid transition while also providing a means to estimate electrostatic stiffness. Notably, stiffness enhancements of up to sevenfold are achieved upon charging. This work expands the design space of PAMs and offers a predictive tool for actively controllable mechanical metamaterials.

</details>


### [182] [Ultralow-Tensile Strain Enables Exciton Funneling and Energy Transfer to Boost MoSe2 Photoluminescence Quantum Yield](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20387)
*Gayatri,Mehdi Arfaoui,Debashish Das,Mateusz Raczyński,Marta Bilska,Piotr Tatarczak,Aleksandra Krystyna Dąbrowska,Tomasz Kazimierczuk,Takashi Taniguchi,Kenji Watanabe,Piotr Kossacki,Andrzej Wysmołek,Saroj Kumar Nayak,Adam Babiński,Johannes Binder,Maciej R. Molas,Arka Karmakar*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过在 hBN 气泡上构建 ReS2/MoSe2 范德华异质结并施加 0.1% 双轴拉伸应变，结合层间能量转移过程，实现光致发光量子产率 8 倍增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 应变工程（strain engineering）是调控范德华异质结（van der Waals heterostructures）中激子动力学的重要手段，而层间能量转移（energy transfer, ET）亦是控制光载流子弛豫路径的关键因素。本工作旨在将这两种机制结合，以提升范德华材料的光致发光量子产率（photoluminescence quantum yield, PL QY）。

Method: 采用由单层 ReS2 与 MoSe2 组成、中间以薄 hBN 间隔层分隔、并置于 hBN 气泡之上的异质结；仅施加 0.1% 双轴拉伸应变（biaxial tensile strain）。实验数据由第一性原理密度泛函理论（density-functional theory, DFT）与相干转移矩阵方法（coherent transfer-matrix method）计算加以支持。

Result: 相对 PL QY 获得 8 倍增强，归因于高效的激子漏斗效应（exciton funneling）与跃迁偶极矩（transition dipole moment）的增大。计算结果显示光学干涉（optical interference）并非该增强的主导来源。

Conclusion: 本工作提出了一条通过拉伸应变与能量转移过程相互作用来增强范德华材料 PL QY 的创新途径。

Abstract: Strain engineering is a powerful route for controlling the exciton dynamics in van der Waals (vdW) heterostructures (HSs). The interlayer energy transfer (ET) process is another key factor in controlling the photocarrier relaxation pathways in vdW HSs. In this work, we combine these two processes to achieve an 8-fold enhancement to the relative photoluminescence (PL) quantum yield (QY) in a HS formed from monolayers of ReS2 and MoSe2, separated by a thin hBN interlayer, placed onto an hBN bubble. We achieve this enhancement by applying only 0.1% biaxial tensile strain, which results in efficient exciton funneling and an increased transition dipole moment. Our experimental data are supported by first-principles density-functional theory and coherent transfer-matrix method calculations, ruling out optical interference as the dominant origin of the enhancement. This work provides an innovative route for enhancing the PL QY of vdW materials via interplay between the tensile strain and the ET process.

</details>


### [183] [Tuning the Coercive Field in Ferroelectric Hf0.5Zr0.5O2-Al2O3 Heterostructures via Interfacial Charge Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20482)
*Marshall B. Frye,Chanyoung Kim,Jeong-Woo Sun,John Wellington-Johnson,Lance Fernandes,Prasanna Venkatesan Ravindran,Bogdan Dryzhakov,TaeYoung Song,Mengkun Tian,Asif I. Khan,Lauren M. Garten*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究揭示，铁电 Hf0.5Zr0.5O2（HZO）薄膜中插入介电层所导致的记忆窗口（MW）提升，源于界面氧空位（oxygen vacancies）介导的界面电荷动力学，而非介电常数差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将介电层插入 HZO 薄膜可提升记忆窗口，但其幅度超出介电常数所能解释的范围，明确其物理机制对实现 HZO 铁电 NAND（FeNAND）的潜力至关重要。

Method: 结合 X 射线光电子能谱（XPS）刻蚀实验、极化依赖 XPS、一阶反转曲线（FORC）以及相场建模（PFM）进行分析，并利用模型模拟 36 个不同电荷密度与介电厚度的器件。

Result: 界面处非化学计量比增加，氧空位提升 2.3 倍；界面缺陷态间的隧穿导致 0.56 MV/cm 的双向内建偏置；PFM 仅在缺陷密度与隧穿势垒高度与实验一致时才能重现矫顽场、FORC 与内建偏置，定量给出 0.55 MV/cm 的内建电场。

Conclusion: 缺陷在铁电 HZO 中的角色由有害转变为可工程化调控；该工作为调控铁电器件架构以提升存储性能提供了预测性框架与关键见解。

Abstract: Interleaving dielectric layers into ferroelectric Hf0.5Zr0.5O2 (HZO) films increases the memory window (MW) beyond what is accounted for by the dielectric constants. Determining the physical mechanisms behind these MW improvements is critical to reaching the full potential of HZO FeNAND. Here, we show that MW improvements stem from the interfacial charge dynamics enabled by oxygen vacancies at the interlayer interface. X-ray photoelectron spectroscopy(XPS) etching experiments demonstrate increased off-stoichiometry at the interface, with a 2.3x increase in oxygen vacancies. Polarization-dependent XPS and first-order reversal curves(FORC) show that tunneling between interfacial defect states causes a bidirectional internal bias of 0.56MV/cm. The impact of defects is further corroborated through phase-field modeling(PFM), which only recreates the coercive fields, FORC, and internal bias for defect densities and tunneling barrier heights that are consistent with experiment, quantitatively capturing an internal electric field of 0.55 MV/cm. The phase field models are then used to simulate 36 devices with varied charge densities and dielectric thickness to provide a predictive framework for further improvements in the MW of interlayer HZO. These findings redefine the role of defects in ferroelectric HZO from deleterious to engineerable and provide critical insights into how to tune ferroelectric device architectures for improved memory.

</details>


### [184] [Strain-Induced Metal-to-Insulator Transition in Antiferromagnetic SrCrO$_3$ Thin Films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20486)
*S. Jöhr,A. Carta,J. Moreno,A. Suter,Z. Salman,A. Panda,J. Spring,G. De Luca,J. Herrero-Martin,B. Mundet,C. Piamonteze,M. Gabay,C. Ederer,M. Gibert*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过磁控溅射在宽范围拉伸/压缩应变衬底上生长高质量SrCrO₃薄膜，证实其低应变下同时具备反铁磁性与金属基态，并揭示应变可诱导金属-绝缘体转变与电阻异常上翘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反铁磁（antiferromagnetic, AF）金属稀少，但兼具抗杂散场与电学读出等自旋电子学（spintronics）器件优势；候选材料SrCrO₃因合成困难而长期未被充分研究。

Method: 采用磁控溅射（magnetron sputtering）在施加宽范围拉伸与压缩应变的衬底上生长SrCrO₃薄膜；结合μ子自旋弛豫（muon spin relaxation）、X射线磁二向色性（x-ray magnetic dichroism）、电阻率测量及密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）计算进行表征与分析。

Result: 低温下出现伴随稀磁无序的反铁磁相，低应变时电阻测量确认其金属基态；随压缩与拉伸应变增大，薄膜发生金属-绝缘体转变（metal-to-insulator transition），而磁转变温度起始点不变；电阻率异常上翘伴随主导载流子类型改变，其温度与应变相关。

Conclusion: 结果表明SrCrO₃存在复杂的应变依赖能带结构，应变诱导的Jahn-Teller畸变（Jahn-Teller distortions）或CrO₆八面体（CrO₆ octahedra）倾转依应变符号而出现，与DFT计算结果一致。

Abstract: Antiferromagnetic (AF) metals are rare, yet they combine properties attractive for spintronic devices like robustness against stray fields and electrical readout. Among AF metal oxide candidates, SrCrO$_3$ remains largely unexplored due to its notoriously difficult synthesis. In this paper, we demonstrate the growth of high-quality SrCrO$_3$ thin films by magnetron sputtering on substrates that impose a wide range of tensile and compressive strains. Muon spin relaxation experiments, supported by x-ray magnetic dichroism, unveil the emergence of an AF phase with dilute magnetic disorder at low temperatures, while resistivity measurements confirm the simultaneous metallic ground state of SrCrO$_3$ under low strain. As both compressive and tensile strain increase, a metal-to-insulator transition is induced in the films, while the onset of the magnetic transition temperature remains unchanged. Moreover, an intriguing resistivity upturn, accompanied by a change in the dominant charge-carrier type, occurs at a temperature that correlates with strain. These observations suggest a complex strain-dependent band structure, with strain-induced Jahn-Teller distortions or tilting of the CrO$_6$ octahedra that emerge depending on the sign of the strain, as inferred from density functional theory calculations.

</details>


### [185] [Magnon-Phonon Dynamics in Multidimensional Antiferromagnetic Oxides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20616)
*Yogendra Limbu,Michael E. Flatté,Durga Paudyal*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 结合第一性原理（ab initio）与自旋哈密顿量（spin Hamiltonian）建模，将 Cr2O3 从体相映射至单层极限，确认其单层与双层均保持反铁磁半导体（antiferromagnetic semiconductor）基态，并因磁振子（magnon）与声子（phonon）支交叉杂化而成为量子自旋电子学（quantum spintronics）的理想平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反铁磁氧化物（antiferromagnetic oxides）为磁子学（magnonics）提供了有吸引力的研究范式，但 Cr2O3 在低维极限下的磁性与电子基态尚待澄清。已有预测认为其单层为半金属铁磁相（half-metallic ferromagnetic），本文旨在检验并修正该结论。

Method: 采用第一性原理（ab initio）计算与自旋哈密顿量（spin Hamiltonian）建模相结合的方式，对 Cr2O3 从体相到单层进行系统性映射，并通过声子谱（phonon spectra）验证结构稳定性。

Result: 与先前预测的半金属铁磁单层相不同，单层与双层构型均被确证保持反铁磁半导体（antiferromagnetic semiconductor）基态，尽管带隙（gap）存在系统性收窄；磁振子（magnon）与声子（phonon）支的交叉驱动了强杂化（hybridization）。

Conclusion: 低维 Cr2O3 被确立为量子自旋电子学（quantum spintronics）的理想平台，其价值源于稳定的反铁磁半导体基态以及显著的磁振子-声子杂化。

Abstract: Antiferromagnetic oxides offer a compelling paradigm for magnonics. Combining ab initio and spin Hamiltonian modeling, we map Cr2O3 from bulk to monolayer limits, verifying stability via phonon spectra. Contrary to previously predicted half-metallic ferromagnetic monolayer phases, we conclusively demonstrate that monolayer and bilayer configurations preserve an antiferromagnetic semiconductor ground state despite systematic gap narrowing. Intersecting magnon and phonon branches drive strong hybridization, establishing low-dimensional Cr2O3 as an ideal platform for quantum spintronics.

</details>


### [186] [Spin-Lattice Dynamics and Interactions in Magnonic Spinels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20618)
*Hari Paudyal,Yuri Suzuki,Michael E. Flatté,Durga Paudyal*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究揭示尖晶石铁氧体中Al/Li位点选择性有序化诱导亚铁磁绝缘态，并阐明低频磁子与声学声子的强杂化机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁子学（magnonics）面临降低磁阻尼与理解自旋-晶格相互作用（spin-lattice interactions）的关键挑战，需要从微观层面阐明替代驱动自旋动力学的机理。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算（ab initio calculations），分析尖晶石铁氧体（spinel ferrites）中Al/Li的位点选择性有序化及其对电子态密度与声子/磁子模式的影响。

Result: Al/Li有序化驱动亚铁磁绝缘态（ferrimagnetic insulating state），淬灭费米能级态密度（Fermi-level density of states）；计算显示存在集体声学模式（acoustic modes）以及四面体与八面体网络上的亚晶格选择性光学模式（sub-lattice-selective optical modes）；低频磁子（magnon）与声学声子（acoustic phonon）模式相交并发生强杂化（strong hybridization）。

Conclusion: 位点选择性有序化可通过调控电子态与磁子-声子杂化实现低损耗磁子学技术，为替代驱动的自旋动力学提供微观理解。

Abstract: Minimizing magnetic damping while understanding spin-lattice interactions remains a key challenge for magnonics. We show site-specific Al/Li ordering in spinel ferrites drives a ferrimagnetic insulating state, quenching the Fermi-level density of states. \textit{Ab initio} calculations reveal collective acoustic modes alongside sub-lattice-selective optical modes on tetrahedral and octahedral networks, providing a microscopic understanding of substitution-driven spin dynamics. Crucially, the low-frequency magnon and acoustic phonon modes intersect, driving strong hybridization for low-loss technologies.

</details>


### [187] [First-principles theory of phonon renormalization from nonlinear electron-phonon interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20639)
*Florian Kluibenschedl,Matthew Houtput,Jacques Tempere,Cesare Franchini,Mikhail Lemeshko,Ragheed Alhyder*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作在线性电子-声子耦合之外，引入长程单电子-双声子非线性相互作用，通过图解框架推导化学势与温度依赖的自能图，并对LiF与KTaO₃进行第一性原理计算，揭示两种通道对声子谱的定性差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子-声子相互作用（electron-phonon interactions）重构声子频率与寿命，是固体动力学性质的核心；但既有研究通常在**线性**电子-声子耦合（linear electron-phonon coupling）框架下描述这些效应，**非线性**电子-声子相互作用对声子性质的贡献仍基本未获探索。

Method: 在**图解框架**（diagrammatic framework）内同时处理长程**线性**单电子-单声子（one-electron-one-phonon）与**非线性**单电子-双声子（one-electron-two-phonon）相互作用，推导对应的**自能图**（self-energy diagrams），其依赖于化学势（chemical potential）与温度；随后对两种极性半导体（polar semiconductors）LiF与KTaO₃开展**第一性原理**（first principles）计算与评估。

Result: 两种相互作用通道对声子谱的重整化方式存在定性差异：线性贡献尖锐地局域于**布里渊区中心**（Brillouin-zone center）附近，而非线性过程将入射声子与整个谱中其他声子支耦合，从而重整化全布里渊区的声子；后者呈现由相应声子支**热占据**（thermal occupation）主导的显著温度依赖性，为单电子-双声子耦合提供清晰的实验特征。非线性声子重整化在LiF中较小，在KTaO₃中相对更大。

Conclusion: KTaO₃中效应更强的原因归因于其在室温下具有更多热激发的声子支。该工作建立了评估非线性电子-声子效应对声子性质影响的通用框架，适用于晶格涨落更强的材料，包括铅卤钙钛矿（lead-halide perovskites）等软性半导体（soft semiconductors）。

Abstract: Electron-phonon interactions renormalize phonon frequencies and lifetimes and are central to the dynamical properties of solids. While these effects are usually described within linear electron-phonon coupling, the role of nonlinear electron-phonon interactions for phonon properties remains largely unexplored. In this work, we study phonon renormalization arising from the long-range linear one-electron-one-phonon and the nonlinear one-electron-two-phonon interactions within a diagrammatic framework. We derive the corresponding self-energy diagrams, which depend on the chemical potential and temperature, and evaluate them from first principles for the two polar semiconductors LiF and KTaO$_3$. In both materials, the two interaction channels renormalize the phonon spectrum in qualitatively distinct ways. The linear contribution is sharply localized near the Brillouin-zone center, whereas the nonlinear process couples an incoming phonon to other branches throughout the spectrum. As a result, it renormalizes phonons across the entire Brillouin-zone, with a pronounced temperature dependence governed by the thermal occupation of those branches. This behavior provides a clean experimental signature of the one-electron-two-phonon coupling. While the nonlinear phonon renormalization is small in LiF, it is somewhat larger in KTaO$_3$, which we attribute to its greater number of thermally populated phonon branches at room temperature. Our results establish a general framework to assess nonlinear electron-phonon effects on the phonon properties in materials with stronger lattice fluctuations, including soft semiconductors such as lead-halide perovskites.

</details>


### [188] [A Gaussian process coarse-grained potential for Na-montmorillonite](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20645)
*Yalda Pedram,Yaoting Zhang,Laurent Brochard,Chang Seok Kim,Laurent Karim Béland*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种表格化势函数（tabulated potential），将莫尔斯相互作用（Morse interactions）与高斯过程回归（Gaussian process regression, GPR）修正结合，用于再现钠基蒙脱石（Na-MMT）水化与微观结构控制的孔隙、输运及刚度特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 压实膨润土（compacted bentonite）中的水力输运受扩散控制，并由钠基蒙脱石的水化与微观结构决定；但实验难以解析片层相互作用如何支配孔隙结构、输运与刚度，而现有粗粒化模型（coarse-grained models）会平滑水化振荡或需人工修正。

Method: 构建表格化势函数，将片层中心与边缘位点间的莫尔斯相互作用与基于全原子平均力势（potentials of mean force）训练的高斯过程回归修正相结合，并应用于干密度 0.8–1.3 g cm^-3 的单分散与多分散 Na-MMT 组装体。

Result: 模型捕捉了平均力势剖面中水化诱导的复杂性（包括三水（3-W）水化极小值），并可迁移至不同几何、层电荷变体及未见构型；同时在组装体中再现了 3-W 至 1-W 转变、层间外孔隙损失、孔隙结构演化、随机游走迂曲度（tortuosity）、扩散与刚度，且预测扩散与压实钠基膨润土实测值一致。

Conclusion: 该表格化势函数结合高斯过程回归修正的方法可有效连接片层相互作用与 Na-MMT 水化及微观结构特性，实现对孔隙结构、输运与刚度的预测，并具备向多种几何与层电荷变体迁移的能力。

Abstract: Hydraulic transport in compacted bentonite is diffusion-controlled and governed by the hydration and microstructure of sodium montmorillonite (Na-MMT). Experiments cannot resolve how platelet interactions govern pore structure, transport and stiffness, while existing coarse-grained models smooth hydration oscillations or require manual corrections. We develop a tabulated potential combining Morse interactions between platelet centre and edge sites with a Gaussian process regression correction trained on all-atom potentials of mean force. It captures the hydration-induced complexity of the potential-of-mean-force profiles, including the three-water (3-W) hydration minimum and transfers across geometries, layer-charge variants and unseen configurations. Applied to monodisperse and polydisperse Na-MMT assemblies at dry densities of 0.8-1.3 g cm^-3, the model captures the 3-W to 1-W transition, loss of non-interlayer porosity and evolution of pore structure, random-walk tortuosity, diffusion and stiffness. Predicted diffusion agrees with compacted Na-bentonite measurements.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.other'></div>

# cond-mat.other [[Back]](#toc)

### [189] [Momentum-dependent precessional and nutational spin pumping in a honeycomb antiferromagnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19289)
*Suman Mukherjee,Subhadip Ghosh,Ritwik Mondal*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 本研究将自旋惯性（spin inertia）引入 Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert 方程，在二维蜂窝状反铁磁体中研究动量分辨的自旋泵浦（spin pumping），揭示进动与章动（nutation）两种通道在布里渊区内呈现互补的动量依赖关系，从而提供区分自旋章动与常规进动的判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 亚皮秒尺度上的超快磁惯性动力学会产生额外的高频太赫兹章动共振，但这一章动效应在动量空间中对自旋泵浦的贡献尚不清晰，需要加以区分与刻画。

Method: 在 Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert 方程中引入自旋惯性，采用含 $J_1$-$J_2$-$J_3$ 交换相互作用的微观双子晶格模型，计算进动与章动的磁振子（magnon）谱及其子晶格内与子晶格间的自旋泵浦贡献，参数取自 MnPS$_3$。

Result: 两种泵浦通道呈现显著的互补动量依赖：进动电流在 $Γ$ 点附近被强烈抑制（$0.01\%$），向布里渊区边界增强（$K$ 点 $100\%$、$M$ 点 $87\%$）；章动电流则在 $Γ$ 点最大（$100\%$），向边界衰减（$K$ 点 $68\%$、$M$ 点 $69\%$）。章动泵浦电流受惯性弛豫时间 $η$ 强烈调控，小 $η$ 区子晶格内章动泵浦电流呈 $1/η^3$ 的主导阶依赖；该响应还可通过交换作用强度与磁矩调节，且章动泵浦电流占比随磁场增强而上升，进动分量则下降。

Conclusion: 进动与章动自旋泵浦电流的互补动量依赖可作为一种区分自旋章动与常规进动运动的手段，并可通过惯性弛豫时间、交换相互作用、磁矩及外磁场对泵浦响应进行调控。

Abstract: The ultrafast magnetic inertial dynamics on subpicosecond timescales generate an additional high-frequency terahertz nutational resonance. Here, we investigate momentum-resolved spin pumping in a two-dimensional honeycomb antiferromagnet by incorporating spin inertia into the Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert equation. Using a microscopic two-sublattice model with $J_1$-$J_2$-$J_3$ exchange interactions, we calculate the precessional and nutational magnon spectra and their corresponding intra- and inter-sublattice spin pumping contributions throughout the Brillouin zone. For parameters representative of MnPS$_3$, we find a pronounced complementary momentum dependence of the two spin pumping channels: the precessional current is strongly suppressed around the $Γ$ point ($0.01\%$) and increases towards the Brillouin zone boundary ($100\%$ at $K$ and $87\%$ at $M$), whereas the nutational current is largest near $Γ$ ($100\%$) and decreases towards the boundary ($68\%$ at $K$ and $69\%$ at $M$). This contrasting momentum dependence provides a means of distinguishing spin nutation from conventional precessional motion. We further demonstrate that the nutational spin pumping current is strongly controlled by the inertial relaxation time $η$, in contrast to the comparatively weak $η$ dependence of the precessional current. In the small-$η$ regime, our analytical results show that the intra-sublattice nutational spin pumping current exhibits a leading-order dependence of $1/η^3$. The spin pumping response can also be tuned through the exchange interaction strengths and magnetic moment. Our further results on the magnetic field dependence of spin pumping reveal that the ratio of nutational spin pumping current increases with magnetic field strength, whereas the corresponding precessional counterpart decreases.

</details>


<div id='math.OA'></div>

# math.OA [[Back]](#toc)

### [190] [Superselection theory for 2D braided quantum spin systems via Connes fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20725)
*Gregory Faurot,Charlton Li,David Penneys,Emeline Sherman*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文在几何偏序集（geometric poset）上的冯·诺依曼代数网（von Neumann algebra net）框架中，改用康涅斯融合张量积（Connes fusion tensor product）构造与已有工作等价的张量积与编织结构，并据此证明某二维编织量子自旋系统（2D braided quantum spin system）的超选择扇区（superselection sectors）范畴等价于 $\mathsf{Hilb}(\mathcal{B})^{\rm op}$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前 arXiv:2410.21454 依赖局部化与可迁移自同态（localized and transportable endomorphisms）构造编织 $\mathrm{W^*}$-张量范畴（braided $\mathrm{W^*}$-tensor category）；本文旨在给出不依赖局部化、基于康涅斯融合的等价构造，并借助共形网（conformal nets）中的相关思想（arXiv:1812.04470）扩展适用范围。

Method: 在几何偏序集上的冯·诺依曼代数网中，采用康涅斯融合张量积（Connes fusion tensor product）直接定义张量积与编织（braiding），从而避免使用局部化（localization）手段；构造借鉴了 arXiv:1812.04470 中针对共形网（conformal nets）的方法。

Result: 所构造的张量积与编织与 arXiv:2410.21454 中基于局部化与可迁移自同态的构造等价；作为应用，证明了由幺正编织融合范畴（unitary braided fusion category）$\mathcal{B}$ 构造的二维编织量子自旋系统的超选择扇区范畴，作为编织 $\mathrm{W^*}$-张量范畴，等价于 $\mathsf{Hilb}(\mathcal{B})^{\rm op}$，即 $\mathcal{B}$ 的幺正 ind-完备化（unitary ind-completion）的对偶范畴。

Conclusion: 康涅斯融合可在不进行局部化的前提下复现编织张量范畴结构，表明两类构造在等价意义下一致；该等价性进一步支持用范畴化工具刻画二维编织量子自旋系统的超选择扇区，并将其与幺正 ind-完备化的对偶范畴对应起来。

Abstract: The article arXiv:2410.21454 constructed a braided $\mathrm{W^*}$-tensor category of superselection sectors associated to a net of von Neumann algebras on a suitably geometric poset using localized and transportable endomorphisms. In this article, we construct an equivalent tensor product and braiding using the Connes fusion tensor product without localizing, using ideas from arXiv:1812.04470 for conformal nets. As an application, we prove that the category of superselection sectors associated to the 2D braided quantum spin system constructed from a unitary braided fusion category $\mathcal{B}$ in arXiv:2506.19969 is equivalent to $\mathsf{Hilb}(\mathcal{B})^{\rm op}$, the opposite of the unitary ind-completion of $\mathcal{B}$, as a braided $\mathrm{W^*}$-tensor category.

</details>


<div id='math-ph'></div>

# math-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [191] [Three-Dimensional Coulomb Discrete Spectrum via Symplectic Geometry and Phase-Space Constraints: A Geometric Framework for Condensed-Matter Coulomb-Like Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19159)
*Gang Zheng,Peng Chen,Mengli Wang,Wenqi Xue,Benniu Zhang*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出一种几何相空间（phase-space）框架，通过经典辛几何（symplectic geometry）与全局边界约束，无需显式算符对角化即可精确恢复三维库仑束缚态的离散能级与壳层简并。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统获取库仑型束缚态离散能级的方法依赖薛定谔方程（Schrödinger equation）直接对角化，或半经典近似（semiclassical approximation），后者在低能区失效。因此需要一种既能规避对角化、又在低能下保持精确的替代路径。

Method: 借助 Kustaanheimo–Stiefel 正则化（regularization），将负能开普勒轨道（Kepler orbit）映射为四维各向同性谐振子（isotropic harmonic oscillator）。由辛流（symplectic flow）的群合成律导出基于经典两点作用量（two-point action）与 Van Vleck 振幅的积分核，其无穷小生成元满足精确线性演化方程，即“二次闭合（quadratic closure）”。

Result: 施加三类几何约束——无穷远处衰减、原点正则性、以及 Hopf 纤维化（Hopf fibration）下的纤维不变性——精确恢复 E_n/E_1=1/n^2 标度与 g_n=n^2 简并。相标度 α 仅设定作用量单位，谱结构特征均与其取值无关。

Conclusion: 对于二次型哈密顿量（quadratic Hamiltonian），经典核即使在地态能量处也零半经典误差，几何约束可直接识别对称性保护的简并而无需对角化。该方法应用于浅杂质态、莫尔超晶格（moiré superlattice）与半经典输运，展示几何筛选规则对凝聚态环境谱分析的简化作用。

Abstract: Discrete energy-level structures in Coulomb-like localized bound states are conventionally obtained by direct diagonalization of the Schrödinger equation or by semiclassical approximations that fail at low energies. Here we develop a geometric phase-space framework that recovers the exact discrete energy levels and shell degeneracy of three-dimensional Coulomb bound states from classical symplectic geometry and global boundary constraints, bypassing explicit operator diagonalization. Using Kustaanheimo--Stiefel regularization, we map negative-energy Kepler orbits to four-dimensional isotropic harmonic oscillators. The group composition law of the symplectic flow determines an integral kernel built from the classical two-point action and Van Vleck amplitude, whose infinitesimal generator satisfies an exact linear evolution equation---a property we term \textit{quadratic closure}. Imposing three geometric constraints---decay at infinity, regularity at the origin, and fiber invariance under the Hopf fibration---we recover the exact $E_n/E_1=1/n^2$ scaling and $g_n=n^2$ degeneracy. The phase scale $α$ sets the action unit; all spectral structural features are independent of its value. This framework offers a symmetry-transparent route to spectral data: for quadratic Hamiltonians, the classical kernel incurs zero semiclassical error even at ground-state energies, and the geometric constraints directly identify symmetry-protected degeneracies without diagonalization. We apply the method to shallow impurity states, moiré superlattices, and semiclassical transport, demonstrating how geometric screening rules simplify spectral analysis in condensed-matter environments.

</details>


### [192] [The spectral $ζ$-function of Sturm--Liouville operators with $N$ generalized point potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19322)
*Guglielmo Fucci,Jonathan Stanfill*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究带有 N 个广义点势（generalized point potentials）的正则与奇异 Sturm–Liouville 算子（Sturm–Liouville operators）所对应的谱 zeta 函数（spectral zeta function），将其表示为含特征函数（characteristic function）的围道积分（contour integral），并通过实例展示向原点邻域的解析延拓（analytic continuation）及 ζ-正则化泛函行列式（ζ-regularized functional determinant）的显式计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 点相互作用（point interactions）可由算子在相邻区间链上的自伴扩张（self-adjoint extensions）刻画，而带点势的 Sturm–Liouville 算子的谱 zeta 函数及其正则化行列式具有研究价值。摘要未明确给出更具体的研究缺口陈述。

Method: 将点相互作用视为定义在 N+1 个相邻区间链上 Sturm–Liouville 算子的特定自伴扩张，每个相互作用由 SL(2,R) 匹配条件（matching conditions）描述，关联相邻区间的广义边界值（generalized boundary values）；据此以含适当特征函数的围道积分构造谱 zeta 函数。

Result: 建立了以围道积分形式表示的谱 zeta 函数构造；通过具体实例说明其向原点邻域的解析延拓过程，并显式计算了相应的 ζ-正则化泛函行列式。

Conclusion: 该框架为带广义点势的 Sturm–Liouville 算子提供了谱 zeta 函数的围道积分表示与解析延拓方法，实例表明 ζ-正则化泛函行列式可显式求得。摘要未给出更一般性的结论陈述。

Abstract: This work analyzes the spectral zeta function associated with regular and singular Sturm--Liouville operators endowed with $N$ generalized point potentials. The point interactions are characterized as a particular class of self-adjoint extensions of Sturm--Liouville operators defined on a chain of $N+1$ adjacent intervals. Each interaction is described by $SL(2,\R)$ matching conditions relating generalized boundary values across neighboring intervals. We construct the spectral zeta function associated with Sturm--Liouville operators endowed with $N$ generalized point potentials in terms of a contour integral involving an appropriate characteristic function. The process of analytic continuation of the spectral zeta function to a neighborhood of the origin is illustrated by means of specific examples where the $ζ$-regularized functional determinant is also explicitly computed.

</details>


### [193] [Site-scale, Multi-resolution WRF Wind Modeling with Observational Nudging for Methane Emission Monitoring in the Permian Basin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19442)
*Ehsan Kharazmi,Erin Menezes,George Karniadakis,Anantha Sundaram,Arash Fathi*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该研究构建了面向二叠纪盆地（Permian Basin）油气设施的多尺度天气研究与预报（WRF）框架，通过四重嵌套（3 km→40 m）与大涡模拟（LES）模式结合分钟级观测同化（observational nudging），生成了可用于甲烷（methane）羽流建模的高分辨率风场，风速偏差最高下降约87%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确的甲烷源定位与排放速率估算需要能刻画场址尺度时空变化的风场，而单一风速计或粗分辨率业务气象产品无法满足这一需求。

Method: 采用多尺度WRF框架，将每小时3 km的高分辨率快速更新（HRRR）场经四重嵌套域（3 km、1 km、200 m、40 m）动力降尺度；外层两域使用行星边界层（planetary boundary layer）参数化，内层两域采用大涡模拟（LES）模式，并通过仅风场观测松弛（wind-only observational nudging）同化两台现场风速计的1分钟观测，同时考察嵌套反馈与湍流闭合方案的影响。

Result: 基准模拟可再现观测风事件的总体演变，但在弱风时段出现漂移；观测松弛结合单向与双向嵌套使风速绝对偏差分别降低约65%（一台传感器）与87%（另一台），均方根误差相应降低33%与44%，并增强了高频能量与可解析空间梯度，但湍流统计量的改善并不一致。

Conclusion: 结果展示了一条基于物理的实用路径，可为甲烷羽流建模生成高分辨率风场，同时凸显了在远离同化传感器处验证精度时对独立空间观测的需求。

Abstract: Accurate methane source localization and emission-rate estimation at oil and gas facilities require wind fields that resolve site-scale spatial and temporal variability, which cannot be characterized by a single anemometer or coarse operational weather products. We develop a multiscale Weather Research and Forecasting (WRF) framework for a Permian Basin facility that dynamically downscales hourly, 3 km High-Resolution Rapid Refresh (HRRR) fields through four nested domains with grid spacings of 3 km, 1 km, 200 m, and 40 m. The two outer domains use planetary-boundary-layer parameterization, whereas the two inner domains operate in large-eddy-simulation mode. One-minute wind observations from two on-site anemometers are assimilated through wind-only observational nudging in the innermost domain, and the effects of nesting feedback and turbulence-closure choices are examined. Simulations for winter and summer 2025 periods are evaluated using near-surface wind-speed time series, power spectra, spatial fields, and statistical metrics. The baseline simulation reproduces the broad evolution of observed wind events but drifts during weak-wind periods. Observational nudging with one- and two-way nesting reduces the absolute bias of wind-speed by %65 at one sensor and %87 at the other, while reducing the corresponding root-mean-square errors by %33 and %44. Nudging also increases high-frequency energy and resolved spatial gradients, although improvements in turbulence statistics are not uniform. These results demonstrate a practical physics-based pathway for generating high-resolution wind fields for methane-plume modeling while emphasizing the need for independent spatial observations to validate accuracy away from assimilated sensors.

</details>


### [194] [Boundary parity in the mirror model on the Manhattan lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19498)
*Jian Gu,Qin Hao*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究曼哈顿格（Manhattan lattice）镜像模型（mirror model）的有限域事件，提出有限尺度约束判据（finite-scale confinement criterion）：若某偶数两方格矩形内边界-边界轨迹穿越标记连接件（connector）偶数次的概率达到普适阈值，则平面上几乎所有轨迹为周期性的。作者构建精确归一化的三色张量表示（three-color tensor representation），并借助有向延续的符号部分置换结构（signed partial-permutation structure）证明近似收缩的确定性界。数值上证明在镜像密度 \(p=2/5\) 时该边界奇偶概率随矩形宽度非单调变化，但数值始终低于约束阈值，未能在该密度下解决平面局域化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要动机：研究曼哈顿格上镜像模型的平面局域化（planar localization）问题，需要一个可计算、可严格验证的有限尺度约束判据，把数值评估转化为严格的有限体积命题。

Method: 方法：定义边界-边界轨迹穿越标记连接件偶数次的有限域事件（内部环不受限制）；构造精确归一化的三色张量表示；利用有向延续（directed continuations）的符号部分置换结构，证明近似收缩（approximate contractions）的确定性界；并以该判据在 \(p=2/5\) 处进行数值计算与实验（密度扫描、匹配界与逐项误差界比较、局部镜像改动的端点敏感与合作响应）。

Result: 结果：主定理给出有限尺度约束判据——若该事件在某个偶数两方格矩形内概率达到普适阈值，则平面上几乎所有轨迹为周期性。在 \(p=2/5\) 时，边界奇偶概率随矩形宽度非单调，先下降后出现经认证的上升；但其值始终低于充分约束阈值，因此未解决该密度下的平面局域化。实验进一步刻画有限尺度最小值随密度的变化，并比较匹配型与逐项误差界，展示对局部镜像改动的端点敏感与合作响应。

Conclusion: 结论：三色张量表示与确定性收缩界可将数值评估提升为严格的有限体积陈述，并给出可验证的约束判据；在 \(p=2/5\) 下该判据尚未触发，平面局域化问题仍开放，后续需在更广密度范围内考察有限尺度最小值的走向。

Abstract: We study a finite-domain event for the mirror model on the Manhattan lattice: every boundary-to-boundary trajectory crosses a marked connector an even number of times, while internal cycles are unrestricted. Our main theorem gives a finite-scale confinement criterion: if this event's probability reaches a universal threshold in one even two-square rectangle, then almost surely every trajectory in the plane is periodic. We construct an exactly normalized three-color tensor representation and prove deterministic bounds for approximate contractions using the signed partial-permutation structure of directed continuations. These tools turn numerical evaluations into rigorous finite-volume statements. At mirror density \(p=2/5\), we prove that the boundary-parity probability is nonmonotone in the rectangle width, with a decrease followed by a certified increase. These values remain below the sufficient confinement threshold and do not resolve planar localization at this density. Further experiments track how the measured finite-size minimum varies with density, compare matching-based and entrywise error bounds, and exhibit endpoint-sensitive and cooperative responses to local mirror changes.

</details>


### [195] [Eberlein convolution as an inner product and quantitative Bombieri--Taylor results](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19501)
*Daniel Lenz,Nicolae Strungaru*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一个简洁的论证，给出非常一般的 Bombieri–Taylor 型（Bombieri–Taylor-type）结果，用于将衍射理论（diffraction theory）中的布拉格峰（Bragg peaks）作为极限来计算。该论证的抽象核心在于将 Eberlein 卷积（Eberlein convolution）视为一种内积（inner product）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于以更一般、更抽象的方式计算衍射理论中的布拉格峰，从而推广既有的 Bombieri–Taylor 型结论。摘要未进一步说明具体应用背景。

Method: 方法上，将 Eberlein 卷积解释为内积，并由此得到抽象的 Cauchy–Schwarz 型不等式（Cauchy–Schwarz type inequality）。该不等式可推出 Bombieri–Taylor 型结果及其若干推广。

Result: 所得结果为一个非常一般的 Bombieri–Taylor 型定理，可在衍射理论中将布拉格峰表示为极限，并涵盖若干推广情形。

Conclusion: 结论是：以 Eberlein 卷积作为内积的抽象框架，配合 Cauchy–Schwarz 型不等式，足以统一导出 Bombieri–Taylor 型结果及其推广，表明该论证具有较强的普适性。

Abstract: We present a simple argument providing a very general Bombieri--Taylor-type result to compute Bragg peaks in diffraction theory as limits. At its abstract core the argument relies on considering the Eberlein convolution as an inner product. This inner product gives an abstract version of a Cauchy--Schwarz type inequality which implies the Bombieri--Taylor-type result as well as generalizations.

</details>


### [196] [A c-transform optimal transport method for high-contrast freeform reflector design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19663)
*Gang Bao,Yixuan Zhang,Jiaqi Yu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于最优传输对偶（optimal transport duality）的快速 c-变换（c-transform）方法，用于远场（far field）高对比度自由曲面反射器（freeform reflector）设计，无需引入人工背景正则化（artificial background regularization）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高对比度自由曲面反射器设计困难，原因在于零强度区域（zero-intensity regions）会导致相关 Monge-Ampere 型方程（Monge-Ampere-type equation）退化。常见做法是向目标强度添加正的人工背景以改善方程正则性（regularity），但这会降低可实现的照明对比度，并在本应保持黑暗的区域引入非零强度。

Method: 提出一种基于最优传输对偶的快速 c-变换方法，将重复的离散 c-变换整合进基于测度（measure-based）的对偶优化方案中，以在迭代全过程保持反射器势函数（reflector potential）的 c-凹性（c-concavity）。为高效计算离散 c-变换，开发了一种利用其接触结构（contact structure）的局部搜索算法（localized search algorithm），并证明其精确性与线性复杂度。

Result: 通过保持迭代量的容许性（admissibility），方法对含零强度区域的目标实现了稳定收敛（stable convergence）；数值实验表明，所提方法能够在无背景条件下准确实现高对比度照明目标。

Conclusion: 所提方法无需人工背景正则化即可准确实现零背景的高对比度照明目标，兼具稳定性与线性复杂度的计算效率。

Abstract: Design of high-contrast freeform reflectors is challenging, as the presence of zero-intensity regions leads to degeneracy in the associated Monge-Ampere-type equation. A common remedy is to add a positive artificial background to the target intensity, which improves the regularity of the equation. However, this regularization inevitably reduces the achievable illumination contrast and introduces nonzero intensity into regions that are intended to remain dark. We propose a fast c-transform method based on optimal transport duality for high-contrast freeform reflector design in the far field without artificial background regularization. The method integrates repeated discrete c-transforms into a measure-based dual optimization scheme to maintain the c-concavity of the reflector potential throughout the iteration. This preserves the admissibility of the iterates and enables stable convergence for targets with zero-intensity regions. To efficiently compute the discrete c-transforms, we develop a localized search algorithm that exploits their contact structure and prove its exactness and linear complexity. Numerical experiments demonstrate that the proposed method accurately realizes high-contrast illumination targets with zero background.

</details>


### [197] [Contact Large Deviations of Stochastic Vector Bundles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19679)
*D. Y. Zhong*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在随机向量丛（stochastic vector bundles）上建立接触大偏差理论（contact large deviation theory），使速率函数、标度累积生成函数与 Gallavotti–Cohen 型涨落对偶均由接触 1-形式导出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大偏差理论（large deviation theory）的速率函数与涨落对称性缺乏几何基础，本文旨在以接触几何为其提供统一框架。

Method: 以约束函数作为广义拉格朗日量（generalized Lagrangian），由最小约束原理导出动力学；接触势（contact potential）由主方程逐阶构建，形成由不变密度、漂移与涨落张量支配的耦合 Hamilton–Jacobi–输运系统。

Result: 接触路径测度满足大偏差原理，其速率函数由约束作用量给出；标度累积生成函数服从含 Donsker–Varadhan 变分刻画的定态本征值方程，并由带反向漂移的时间反演对合得到 GC 型对偶 λ_fwd(q)=λ_rev(q+1)+λ_fwd(−1)。

Conclusion: 熵产生率与涨落–耗散组合 e=½g^T Ag−σ 为物理可观测量；在可逆情形 Ag=0 下可恢复经典一维 GC 对称性。

Abstract: Large deviation theory lacks a geometric foundation for its rate functions and fluctuation symmetries. This paper develops a contact large deviation theory on stochastic vector bundles, in which the rate function, the scaled cumulant generating function, and a Gallavotti--Cohen-type fluctuation duality all follow from the contact 1-form. The constraint function acts as a generalized Lagrangian; the least constraint principle yields the dynamics, and the contact potential is built order by order from the master equation, producing a coupled Hamilton--Jacobi--transport system governed by the invariant density, drift, and fluctuation tensor. The contact path measure satisfies a large deviation principle with rate function given by the constraint action; the scaled cumulant generating function obeys a stationary eigenvalue equation with a Donsker--Varadhan variational characterization. The entropy production rate, the fluctuation--dissipation combination $e=\tfrac12 g^T Ag-σ$, is the physical observable; the time-reversal involution $J:(t,y,φ)\mapsto(t,y,-φ-\nabla\lnρ)$ with reversed drift $v^{\mathrm{rev}}=-v+Ag$ yields a Gallavotti--Cohen-type duality $λ_{\mathrm{fwd}}(q)=λ_{\mathrm{rev}}(q+1)+λ_{\mathrm{fwd}}(-1)$. The classical one-dimensional GC symmetry is recovered in the reversible case $Ag=0$.

</details>


### [198] [Reconstructability of Inverse Problems under Symmetry: Separating Structural, Effective, and Physical Upper Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19697)
*Isshin Arai*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 论文提出重构维度（Reconstruction Dimension）的三级上界框架：结构性（SRD）、有效性（ERD）与物理性（PRD）重构维度，分别由中间空间的表示论结构、重构映射的具体设计以及物理过程 $P_o$ 下状态空间的退化程度决定，并满足 $\mathrm{SRD}\ge\mathrm{ERD}\ge\mathrm{PRD}$ 的层级关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有的重构维度仅由对称性决定，是可达自由度（degrees of freedom）的上界；但实际物理系统中状态量经由原因 $O$ 诱导的物理过程 $P_o$ 生成，状态空间常呈退化，使该上界无法达到。因此需要区分仅由对称性决定的上界与经物理过程实现的上界。

Method: 引入三级上界结构：结构性重构维度（SRD）由中间空间的表示论（representation-theoretic）结构决定，有效性重构维度（ERD）由重构映射（reconstruction map）的具体设计决定，物理性重构维度（PRD）由物理过程 $P_o$ 下状态空间的退化（degeneracy）决定；对每个不可约分量（irreducible component）建立层级关系。

Result: 对每个不可约分量，三者构成层级 $\mathrm{SRD}\ge\mathrm{ERD}\ge\mathrm{PRD}$，两个间隙（gap）可定位重构性在何种层级上损失。该框架通过两个对比系统加以说明：以速度梯度为原因的片状粒子取向动力学，以及原因本身由对称性定义的两体问题（two-body problem）。

Conclusion: 该工作为物理逆问题（physical inverse problems）中的重构提供了具体框架，实现了仅由对称性决定的上界与物理过程后实际实现的上界之间的区分。

Abstract: In our previous work we introduced the Reconstruction Dimension, an upper bound on the reconstructable degrees of freedom determined solely by the symmetry. In actual physical systems, however, the state quantities are generated through physical processes $P_o$ induced by the cause $O$. The state space is therefore often degenerate, and the upper bound determined by the representation-theoretic structure is not reached. This calls for a distinction between the upper bound determined solely by symmetry and the one realized after the physical process. We therefore introduce a three-level structure of upper bounds: the Structural, Effective, and Physical Reconstruction Dimensions (SRD, ERD, PRD), determined respectively by the representation-theoretic structure of the intermediate space, by the concrete design of the reconstruction map, and by the degeneracy of the state space under the physical process $P_o$. For each irreducible component they form the hierarchy $\mathrm{SRD}\ge\mathrm{ERD}\ge\mathrm{PRD}$, so that the two gaps identify at which level reconstructability is lost. The framework is illustrated through two contrasting systems: the orientation dynamics of flake-like particles, where the cause is a velocity gradinet, and the two-body problem, where the cause itself is defined through the symmetry. This paper provides a concrete framework for reconstruction in physical inverse problems.

</details>


### [199] [Classification of pairs of second-order Hamiltonian operators and hydrodynamic type systems in six components](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19785)
*Giorgio Gubbiotti,Lambertus Van Geemen,Pierandrea Vergallo*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在近期以射影空间上交错三形式刻画二阶哈密顿结构流体力学系统的基础上，完成六分量系统的完全分类，得到5个标准型，并对其中一类给出任意维度的普适结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二阶哈密顿结构（second-order Hamiltonian structure）的流体力学系统此前已被用射影空间（projective space）上的交错三形式（alternating three-forms）加以刻画，但缺乏对具体分量数的系统分类，故需填补这一空白。

Method: 采用非平凡群作用（non-trivial group action）技术，对6分量（6 components）系统进行等价类约化与分类。

Result: 在6分量情形下获得5个标准型（canonical forms）；并对其中一种情形给出适用于任意维度（every dimension）的一般性结果，其几何结构与辛标准型（symplectic canonical form）密切相关。

Conclusion: 该分类给出了6分量二阶哈密顿流体力学系统的完整标准型清单，并揭示了其中一类与辛结构（symplectic structure）之间的内在联系，可推广至任意维度。

Abstract: Following a recent characterisation of hydrodynamic systems with second-order Hamiltonian structure in terms of alternating three-forms on the projective space, we present a complete classification of systems in 6 components. We use non-trivial group action techniques to obtain 5 canonical forms. A general result for every dimension is also obtained for one of these cases, whose geometric structure is strictly related to the symplectic canonical form.

</details>


### [200] [A Cumulative Framework for Solid Deformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19852)
*Lev Steinberg*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 提出固体变形的累积变分框架（cumulative variational framework），将相容Cosserat分量、分布非相容分量与缺陷源分量递归耦合为单一材料状态，并受统一能量与场方程支配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统做法易将相容变形、非相容性与缺陷源视为相互独立的边值问题并按预定时间顺序求解；本文旨在以统一累积方式刻画三者的联合响应。

Method: 以递归组合三种结构分量构成组合材料状态，在共同物理边界与初始条件下通过单一累积能量及相应场方程耦合；并给出含缺陷与源梯度项的空间推广。

Result: 一维特例展示了结构分岔、残余变形与残余应力、对初始组合状态的依赖、反向加载下的方向性记忆、参数化累积滞回及与结构稳定化相关的可观测切线刚度变化；空间推广导出尺寸相关的结构稳定算子。

Conclusion: 对所选稳定四次能量，梯度项可正则化非均匀扰动，但本身不能在已稳定的三分量分支上产生有限波长局域化失稳，从而区分累积结构与能量的后果与需附加本构演化律的现象。

Abstract: A cumulative variational framework for solid deformation is proposed. Its central organizing principle is the recursive combination of three structural components of one material body: a compatible Cosserat component, a distributed-incompatibility component, and a defect-source component. At any prescribed external condition these components constitute a combined material state. They are governed under common physical boundary and initial conditions and are coupled through one cumulative energy and the associated field equations. They are not separate boundary-value problems and need not appear in a prescribed temporal sequence. The cumulative character of the framework concerns the response of the combined state to variation of externally prescribed conditions. For the special case of an initially perfect body, loss of stability may produce successive branches with defect and then source participation. Such a sequence is a bifurcation property of the selected initial state and constitutive energy, rather than a defining hierarchy of the framework. A one-dimensional specialization illustrates structural bifurcation, residual deformation and residual stress produced by retained structure, dependence on the initial combined state, directional memory under reverse loading, parametric cumulative hysteresis, and changes of observable tangent stiffness associated with structural stabilization. A spatial extension introduces defect and source gradient terms and yields a size-dependent structural stability operator. For the stable quartic energy considered, the gradient terms regularize nonuniform perturbations but do not by themselves generate a finite-wavelength localization instability on an already stable three-component branch. The examples thereby distinguish consequences of the cumulative structure and energy from phenomena requiring additional constitutive evolution laws.

</details>


### [201] [Asymmetric phase transitions in random noncommutative geometries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19898)
*Benedek Bukor,Masoud Khalkhali,Samuel Kováčik,Adam Kubiś,Katarína Magdolenová,Nathan Pagliaroli,Juraj Tekel*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过三种方法研究四阶（quartic）类型 (0, 1) 与 (1, 0) Dirac 系综（Dirac ensembles）的非对称相，重点关注 Schwinger-Dyson 与鞍点方程（saddle point equations）的非对称解；三种方法在大矩阵极限下结果高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究聚焦于 Dirac 系综中非对称解（asymmetric solutions）的求解空间，摘要指出其结构极为复杂（deeply intricate），因此需要多方法交叉验证以厘清其相结构。

Method: 采用三种途径：Riemann-Hilbert 方法、带正性约束的自举（bootstrapping with positivity），以及哈密顿蒙特卡洛（Hamiltonian Monte Carlo, HMC）模拟。

Result: Riemann-Hilbert 方法给出多种解的特征值分布（eigenvalue distributions）与自由能（free energy）的显式公式；HMC 模拟重建了相结构（phase structure）；带正性约束的自举方法由自举矩（bootstrapped moments）重建了特征值分布。

Conclusion: 三种方法在大矩阵尺寸下（large matrix size）表现出极佳的一致性（excellent agreement），相互印证了所研究的非对称相及其解结构。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the asymmetric phases of the quartic type (0, 1) and (1, 0) Dirac en- sembles via three approaches: the Riemann-Hilbert approach, bootstrapping with positivity, and Hamiltonian Monte Carlo (HMC) simulations. The focus of this work is on asymmetric solutions to the Schwinger-Dyson and saddle point equations of these models, whose solution spaces prove deeply intricate. Via the Riemann-Hilbert approach, we are able to give explicit formulae for the eigenvalue distributions and free energy of various solutions. Using Hamiltonian Monte Carlo simulations, we are able to reconstruct the phase structure. Lastly, using bootstrapping with positivity, we are able to reconstruct the eigenvalue distribution of these models from their bootstrapped moments. All three methods show excellent agreement for a large matrix size.

</details>


### [202] [Heterogeneous Anisotropic Kac Limits on Rectifiable Resolved Cut Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20215)
*Sai Peng*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究切割体（cut body）上非局域能量（nonlocal energy）的环境收敛问题，指出环境收敛不能决定该能量，因为切割虽体积为零，微观键（bond）却记录其路径穿过哪一标记侧。作者以测度论方法直接刻画这一信息，并证明相应的锐界面 Gamma 极限（sharp-interface Gamma-limit）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于：环境收敛（ambient convergence）无法确定切割体上的非局域能量，原因是切割体积为零但微观键保留其穿过的标记侧（labelled bank）信息，需要直接以测度论方式解析该信息并给出对应的锐界面极限。

Method: 方法上不作闭性、正可达性（positive reach）、有限图册、曲率界、角度条件或分支集正则性假设，允许切割为有限或可数族 Borel、可数可求长（countably rectifiable）超曲面，具有限总表面质量、可测近似法向与可测二元侧数据。对由一致张成（uniformly spanning）且可能非交换流（noncommuting flows）生成的异质 Kac 相互作用（heterogeneous Kac interactions）取极限。证明结合流的穿越精确时空面积公式、一维分裂延拓下界与有序流紧性，并去除紧径向支撑。

Result: 结果是极限由解析后的各向异性变差（anisotropic variation）、外边界接触项以及每侧一个系数为一的接触项构成；当裂纹接近边界时，Gamma 泛函不含强制性的低维项，常数恢复不生集中。有限一阶矩（first moment）给出相同的局部 Griffith 泛函；在幂律阈值处，极限分离为局部、对数重整化局部与真正分数（fractional）三种机制。光滑裂隙与结点探测器被还原为直接穿越规则的均匀等价实现。

Conclusion: 结论是：切割体的非局域能量须由直接穿越规则确定，而非由环境收敛决定；所得锐界面 Gamma 极限在极弱正则性假设下成立，并随矩条件呈现局部、对数重整化局部与分数机制的转变。

Abstract: Ambient convergence does not determine a nonlocal energy on a cut body: a cut has zero volume, but a microscopic bond retains which labelled bank its path crosses. We give a direct measure-theoretic resolution of this information and prove the corresponding sharp-interface Gamma-limit.
