The cut may be a finite or countable family of Borel, countably rectifiable hypersurfaces with finite total surface mass, measurable approximate normals, and measurable binary bank data. No closedness, positive reach, finite atlas, curvature bound, angle condition, or regularity of the branch set is assumed. For heterogeneous Kac interactions generated by a uniformly spanning family of possibly noncommuting flows, the limit is the resolved anisotropic variation plus the outer boundary contact and one coefficient-one contact on each bank. The Gamma-functional contains no compulsory lower-dimensional term where a crack approaches the boundary; constant recovery creates no concentration there. The proof combines an exact spacetime area formula for flow crossings, a one-dimensional split-extension lower bound, and ordered-flow compactness. We also remove compact radial support. A finite first moment yields the same local Griffith functional; at the power-law threshold the limits separate into local, logarithmically renormalized local, and genuinely fractional regimes. Smooth slit and junction detectors are recovered as uniformly equivalent realizations of the direct crossing rule.

</details>


### [203] [Tangential Surface Pressures and Boundary Localization in Disordered Edwards--Anderson Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20266)
*Hexiang Wang,Keheng Zhu,Mauris Chueng*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究无序 Edwards–Anderson Ising 模型（Edwards–Anderson Ising models）中的边界自由能（boundary free energies），证明了矩形条带中自由-固定边界修正除以切向长度在任意有限逆温及零温下收敛。结合一致近可加性估计与乘积测度集中，得到切向区间上的自平均性（self-averaging），并给出有限体积高斯插值恒等式与边界局域化结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文关注无序自旋系统中边界自由能的可定义性与可加性这一基础问题，即在随机耦合下期望的边界修正是否在热力学极限下收敛。该问题涉及无序系统边界效应与自平均性的严格刻画。

Method: 方法上组合了一致近可加性估计（uniform almost-additivity estimate）与乘积测度集中（product-measure concentration）；导出了精确的有限体积高斯插值恒等式（finite-volume Gaussian interpolation identity），并证明一致指数边界混合条件可推出边界局域化（boundary localization）及显式指数速率。切向压强定理（tangential pressure theorem）被推广至所有维度及有限一阶矩的对称耦合分布，并在乘积集中假设下沿切向立方体获得自平均性；此外给出亚临界开键判据（subcritical open-bond criterion）。

Result: 结果为：对固定宽度的矩形条带，期望自由-固定边界修正除以切向长度在任意有限逆温和零温下收敛；切向区间上具有自平均性；一致指数边界混合蕴含带显式指数速率的边界局域化；切向压强定理推广到任意维度与有限一阶矩的对称耦合分布；稀疏带符号耦合（sparse signed couplings）满足亚临界开键判据，从而在低温下实现局域化。

Conclusion: 结论表明无序 Edwards–Anderson Ising 模型边界自由能的性质可在较一般条件下严格建立，且低温局域化的获得无需假设无限体积 Gibbs 态（infinite-volume Gibbs-state）性质。

Abstract: We study boundary free energies in disordered Edwards--Anderson Ising models. For rectangular strips of fixed width, we prove that the expected free-to-fixed boundary correction, divided by the tangential length, converges for every finite inverse temperature and at zero temperature. The proof combines a uniform almost-additivity estimate with product-measure concentration, yielding self-averaging along tangential intervals. We derive an exact finite-volume Gaussian interpolation identity and prove that a uniform exponential boundary-mixing condition implies boundary localization with an explicit exponential rate. The tangential pressure theorem extends to all dimensions and symmetric coupling laws with finite first moment; under a product-concentration hypothesis, self-averaging is obtained along tangential cubes. Finally, a subcritical open-bond criterion, verified explicitly for sparse signed couplings, gives low-temperature localization without assuming an infinite-volume Gibbs-state property.

</details>


### [204] [An Electrodynamic Lifting of the Toda Monopoles in Curved 3+1 Spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20272)
*Hanwen Liu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究双曲 Toda 方程（hyperbolic Toda equation）的解如何决定 (2+1) 维 Einstein–Weyl 空间上的经典阿贝尔磁单极（Abelian monopole），并判定其联络何时同时构成具有类空 Killing 场（spacelike Killing field）的弯曲 Lorentzian 3+1 时空上的无源电磁势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于将 Toda 方程的解与磁单极联络建立联系，并考察该联络能否提升为弯曲 Lorentzian 3+1 时空中的无源电磁势（source-free electromagnetic potential），从而在更一般的背景几何下刻画可允许的几何结构。

Method: 方法上，通过分析该联络在含类空 Killing 场的弯曲 Lorentzian 3+1 时空中是否为无源电磁势，并利用 Maxwell 方程（Maxwell's equation）对可允许的 Killing 纤维尺度（Killing-fibre scale）与水平扭转（horizontal twist）进行分类；同时讨论分离的 Liouville 分支（separated Liouville branch）。

Result: 结果表明，在一般分支（generic branch）上，Maxwell 方程通过 Toda 场的一个特征量来分类可允许的 Killing 纤维尺度与水平扭转；分离的 Liouville 分支给出纯磁场（purely magnetic fields），其纤维几何自由度明显更大；球面解（round spherical solution）给出单位电荷磁单极，在收缩屏（contracting screen）上具有守恒通量。

Conclusion: 结论指出，所构造的单极解存在于既非 Einstein 也非自对偶（neither Einstein nor self-dual）的背景度规中，说明该框架适用于更广泛的弯曲时空背景，而无需施加这些常见限制。

Abstract: Every solution of the hyperbolic Toda equation determines a classical Abelian monopole on a (2+1)-dimensional Einstein--Weyl space. We determine when its connection is also a source-free electromagnetic potential on a curved Lorentzian 3+1 spacetime with a spacelike Killing field. On the generic branch, Maxwell's equation classifies the admissible Killing-fibre scale and horizontal twist through one characteristic of the Toda field. A separated Liouville branch gives purely magnetic fields with considerably freer fibre geometry. The round spherical solution yields a monopole of unit charge with conserved flux on a contracting screen. The background metric here is neither assumed to be Einstein nor self-dual.

</details>


### [205] [Factorization method for a clamped obstacle from near-field measurements via a far-field transformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20528)
*General Ozochiawaeze,Isaac Harris*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究二维双调和亥姆霍兹方程下不可穿透夹持障碍物的逆形状问题，利用近场点源与偶极子测量数据，引入与障碍物无关的远场变换，使近场算子获得对称分解，从而建立严格的因子分解法刻画并给出重建算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双调和亥姆霍兹方程（biharmonic Helmholtz equation）对应的近场算子（near-field operator）无法直接满足因子分解法（factorization method）所需的对称分解（symmetric factorization），因此无法直接对夹持障碍物（clamped obstacle）进行理论刻画与重建。

Method: 引入与障碍物无关的远场变换（far-field transformation），将近场算子增广为具备对称分解的远场算子（far-field operator）；据此利用因子分解法理论获得障碍物的完整刻画，并基于变换后算子的谱数据（spectral data）设计实用重建算法，数值实验中用基本解方法（method of fundamental solutions）生成合成近场数据。

Result: 数值实验验证了所提方法的有效性；此外，仅使用点源产生的散射场测量进行重建的实验表明，该方法具有减少测量数据量的潜力。

Conclusion: 通过远场变换桥接近场算子与因子分解法，可在双调和亥姆霍兹方程框架下严格重建夹持障碍物，并具备降低测量数据需求的可能性。

Abstract: This paper considers an inverse shape problem for recovering an unknown impenetrable clamped obstacle in two dimensions from near-field point source and dipole measurements for the biharmonic Helmholtz equation in the frequency domain. The measured data consist of the scattered field and its normal derivative on a closed measurement curve surrounding the obstacle. Since the associated near-field operator does not directly admit the symmetric factorization required by the factorization method, we introduce a far-field transformation. This transformation is defined independently of the obstacle and augments the near-field operator into the associated far-field operator which does admit a symmetric factorization. This yields a rigorous complete characterization of the obstacle by the factorization method theory and leads to a practical reconstruction algorithm based on the spectral data of the transformed operator. Numerical experiments are presented to demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method, with synthetic near-field data generated using the method of fundamental solutions. We also consider reconstructions using only scattered-field measurements generated by point sources, demonstrating the potential for reduced the amount of measured data.

</details>


### [206] [Phase transitions in non-Hermitian spherical integrals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20685)
*Pierre Bousseyroux,Marc Potters*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究非厄米随机矩阵（non-Hermitian random matrices）中一个带约束的球面积分（constrained spherical integral）的大 N 渐近行为，其中两个向量的范数与内积固定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该问题的动机源于厄米球面积分（Hermitian spherical-integral）机制与库仑气体（Coulomb-gas）图像，旨在将相关分析推广至非厄米情形，并以数学物理（mathematical physics）的精神展开研究。

Method: 在去局域化（delocalized）区域，渐近行为由非厄米变换（non-Hermitian transforms）R_1 与 R_2 主导；在鞍点（saddle-point）层面，约束积分表现出向局域化（localized）区域的转变，该转变由移位矩阵的最大奇异值（largest singular value）及其左右奇异向量的重叠（overlap）控制。

Result: 鞍点分析揭示了约束积分从去局域化向局域化区域的转变，该转变由移位矩阵的最大奇异值及相应左右奇异向量的重叠共同控制。

Conclusion: 受厄米球面积分机制与库仑气体图像启发，本文提出了关于单特征值大偏差（one-eigenvalue large deviations）与边界涨落（boundary fluctuations）的猜想。

Abstract: We study the large-$N$ asymptotics of a constrained spherical integral for non-Hermitian random matrices, in which the norms and mutual scalar product of two vectors are fixed. In the delocalized regime, the asymptotics are governed by the non-Hermitian transforms $\mathcal R_1$ and $\mathcal R_2$. At saddle-point level, the constrained integral exhibits a transition to a localized regime controlled by the largest singular value of a shifted matrix and by the overlap of its associated left and right singular vectors. Motivated by the Hermitian spherical-integral mechanism and by the Coulomb-gas picture, we formulate conjectures for one-eigenvalue large deviations and boundary fluctuations. Throughout, our analysis is carried out in the spirit of mathematical physics.

</details>


### [207] [Disorder on the hyperbolic square lattice I: Anderson delocalization and absolutely continuous spectrum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20798)
*Simon Becker,Izak Oltman*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究双曲晶格（hyperbolic lattices）上的安德森模型（Anderson model），证明在弱无序（weak disorder）条件下，于具有正测度的确定性集合上存在绝对连续谱（absolutely continuous spectrum）且无奇异谱（singular spectrum）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于考察安德森模型在双曲几何（hyperbolic geometry）背景下的谱性质，其中图结构为欧几里得晶格（Euclidean lattice）与正则双曲方格晶格（regular hyperbolic square lattice）的笛卡尔积（Cartesian product）。

Method: 方法上采用谱理论分析，针对每个顶点含五个方格的正则双曲方格晶格与欧几里得晶格的笛卡尔积图，并引入截断柯西分布（truncated Cauchy distributions）与受控扰动（controlled perturbations）进行讨论。

Result: 在弱无序条件下，于正测度的确定性集合上证明了绝对连续谱的存在与奇异谱的不存在；当单点密度（single-site density）在[-1,1]上有下界时，结果对0<λ≤2成立。截断柯西分布与受控扰动可在区间上给出纯绝对连续谱（purely absolutely continuous spectrum）。

Conclusion: 结论表明，双曲晶格上的安德森模型在弱无序及特定参数范围内具有绝对连续谱，并排除了奇异谱，拓展了该模型在非欧几何结构上的谱理论认识。

Abstract: We study the Anderson model on hyperbolic lattices. The graph is the Cartesian product of the Euclidean lattice with the regular hyperbolic square lattice having five squares at each vertex. We prove the existence of absolutely continuous spectrum and the absence of singular spectrum on a deterministic set of positive measure at weak disorder, and for $0<λ\le2$ when the single-site density is bounded below on $[-1,1]$. Truncated Cauchy distributions and controlled perturbations give purely absolutely continuous spectrum on intervals.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.quant-gas'></div>

# cond-mat.quant-gas [[Back]](#toc)

### [208] [Hysteresis in Atomic Josephson Junctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19260)
*Vijay Pal Singh,Ludwig Mathey,Luigi Amico*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该工作提出速度扫描（velocity-sweep）方案，在二维玻色超流体的原子约瑟夫森结（atomic Josephson junction）欠阻尼区中实现了磁滞（hysteresis）与再俘获（retrapping）现象，并证明其动力学可由RCSJ模型定量描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁滞与再俘获是欠阻尼约瑟夫森动力学（underdamped Josephson dynamics）的标志性特征，但在原子约瑟夫森结中一直难以观测。

Method: 采用二维玻色超流体的经典场模拟（classical-field simulations），引入速度扫描协议，测量速度—失衡（velocity-imbalance）特性，并确定再俘获电流。

Result: 在速度—失衡特性中观测到磁滞，并确定再俘获电流；动力学可由电阻电容并联结（RCSJ）模型定量刻画。磁滞源于临界电流以下的持续相位滑移（phase slips），相关耗散由弱链接处涡旋—反涡旋对（vortex-antivortex pair）的成核所中介；再俘获电流遵循欠阻尼结的Stewart-McCumber标度。

Conclusion: 该结果为原子约瑟夫森结与超导约瑟夫森结建立了一个共同的相位动力学（phase-dynamical）框架。

Abstract: Hysteresis and retrapping are hallmarks of underdamped Josephson dynamics, yet they have remained elusive in atomic Josephson junctions. Here, we introduce a velocity-sweep protocol to demonstrate these phenomena in the underdamped regime of an atomic Josephson junction. Specifically, using classical-field simulations of a two-dimensional bosonic superfluid, we show hysteresis in the velocity-imbalance characteristics and determine the retrapping current. The dynamics are quantitatively captured by the resistively and capacitively shunted junction (RCSJ) model. We show that hysteresis originates from persistent phase slips below the critical current, with the associated dissipation mediated by vortex-antivortex pair nucleation at the weak link. The retrapping current follows the Stewart-McCumber scaling of underdamped junctions, establishing a common phase-dynamical framework for atomic and superconducting Josephson junctions.

</details>


### [209] [Radial dam breaks in a two-dimensional droplet bearing environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19508)
*F. Bristy,S. Chandramouli,G. A. Bougas,G. C. Katsimiga,P. G. Kevrekidis,S. I. Mistakidis*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该工作研究了二维液滴环境中径向溃坝流（dam break flow, DBF）的可控动力学生成，识别出由密度决定的三类响应机制：亮环孤子波列、环状扭结-色散冲击波，以及环状扭结-稀疏波复合结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维克尔介质（Kerr media）中波坍缩（wave-collapse）会抑制此类非线性波型的形成，因此需要借助液滴环境中吸引与排斥相互作用的竞争来探索可行的替代机制，并实现可实验实现的径向溃坝流。

Method: 采用扩展Gross-Pitaevskii方程（extended Gross-Pitaevskii equation）对二维超冷混合液滴中盘状均匀密度初态进行建模，并辅以基于三次-五次非线性Schrödinger方程（cubic-quintic nonlinear Schrödinger equation）的简化模型验证所得非线性波型。

Result: 在小密度下出现明亮环孤子（bright ring soliton）的运动波列（称为环状溃坝流）；中等密度下形成环状扭结-色散冲击波；大密度下则演化为向中心传播的环状扭结-稀疏波复合结构。

Conclusion: 所识别波型的产生源于液滴环境固有的吸引-排斥竞争机制，其区别于二维克尔介质中的坍缩禁令；这些不同的溃坝流波型可通过当前最先进的超冷原子实验实现。

Abstract: The controlled dynamical generation of radial dam break flows (DBFs) is demonstrated in two-dimensional droplet environments. These ultracold mixtures feature competing mean-field and quantum fluctuation effects and are initialized on a disk-shaped uniform density. Depending on the value of the latter, reflecting also the dominance of attractive or repulsive interactions, three dynamical response regimes are identified. At small densities a moving wavetrain of bright ring solitons is observed (dubbed ring DBF), at intermediate densities a ring kink-dispersive shock wave, while at large densities the flows encompass composite ring kink-rarefaction waves moving towards the center. The emergence of these patterns, whose formation would be prohibited in two-dimensional Kerr media due to wave-collapse, is rooted in the competition between attraction and repulsion inherent to droplet environments, modeled by the extended Gross-Pitaevskii equation. The characterization of the ensuing nonlinear waveforms is further corroborated by reduced models based on the cubic-quintic nonlinear Schrödinger equation. The different wave patterns emanating from dam break flows can be experimentally realized using current state-of-the-art ultracold atom experiments.

</details>


### [210] [Odd/Even or Half ? Entanglement Anomaly in the Bose-Hubbard model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19884)
*Humberto E. López-Hernández,Santiago F. Caballero-Benitez*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该文考察一维 Bose-Hubbard 模型中不同空间二分方式对纠缠熵标度律的影响：Mott 绝缘体中连续切割服从面积律（area law），而交错切割出现体积律（volume law）$S\propto N_s$；超流态下两种切割均塌缩至二项饱和。作者用广义从玻色子（slave-boson）方法结合平均场与量子涨落给出解析极限，并以 ED 与 DMRG 验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面积律（area law）通常将基态纠缠熵与子系统边界大小相联系，但边界几何本身很少被讨论。论文旨在揭示同一格点数目、不同边界排布方式下纠缠熵标度律的差异，并检验其是否为真正的多体特征而非有限尺寸效应。

Method: 在固定密度的一维 Bose-Hubbard 模型（Bose-Hubbard model）中，对周期格点比较四种二分：前半、后半、偶数格点与奇数格点。采用基于平均场（mean-field）加量子涨落的广义从玻色子（slave-boson）方法推导解析极限，并由从玻色子高斯基态的约化关联矩阵计算任意二分的纠缠熵；小尺寸用精确对角化（Exact Diagonalization, ED）验证，大尺寸用密度矩阵重整化群（Density Matrix Renormalization Group, DMRG）验证。

Result: Mott 绝缘体中连续切割服从面积律，交错切割服从体积律 $S\propto N_s$，构成一种反常；超流态中因态离域化，两种切割均塌缩至二项饱和（binomial saturation）。从玻色子计算与 ED（$N_s\in[4,10]$）及更大格点的 DMRG 结果高度一致；计算成本由局域截断 $n_{\max}$ 决定而非希尔伯特空间维度，因而可达远超 ED 的格点尺寸。

Conclusion: 该工作将依赖于划分方式的标度律确立为多体特征，而非仅由有限尺寸效应导致，且与 ED 和 DMRG 结果吻合良好。所提从玻色子方法能够在超越 ED 的大格点尺度上计算任意二分的纠缠熵，为讨论边界几何对纠缠标度的影响提供了可行框架。

Abstract: The area law relates the bipartite entanglement entropy of a quantum many-body ground state to the size of the boundary between the subsystems, but the geometry of this boundary is rarely discussed. We inspect this in the 1D Bose-Hubbard model at fixed density by comparing four spatial bipartitions of the periodic lattice: first half, second half, even sites, and odd sites; sharing the same number of sites but differing in how the boundary is arranged. We find analytical limits with perturbation theory: in the Mott insulator the contiguous cut obeys the area lay while the alternating cut obeys a volume law $S\propto N_s$, in this sense an anomaly, and for the superfluid both cuts colapse to the binomial saturation due to delocalization of the state. We formulate these limits as a statement about the many-body problem using a generalized slave-boson approach based on mean-field with quantum fluctuations while verifying with Exact Diagonalization (ED) for small lattice sizes and Densitiy Matrix Renormalization Group (DMRG) simulations for $N_s\gg 1$. The slave-boson Gaussian ground state allows to compute the entanglement entropy from a reduced correlation matrix for any desired bipartition consistent with ED and DMRG results. Using slave bosons the computational cost is set by the local cutoff $n_{\max}$ rather than the Hilbert space dimension, so we can reach lattice sizes far beyond ED. Our method is capable of establishing the partition-dependent scaling laws as a many-body feature, not only a finite-size effect, in great agreement with the ED for $N_s\in[4,10]$ and DMRG for larger lattice sizes.

</details>


### [211] [Fragmentation of Quantum Fluid in dipolar Bose-Einstein condensate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20013)
*Shivam Singh,Ayan Khan*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该文研究偶极玻色量子流体（dipolar Bosonic quantum fluid）中平均场、超越平均场与三体相互作用同偶极相互作用的竞争，及其导致的碎裂（fragmentation）现象，并考察元激发（elementary excitations）与超流、凝聚体占比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文关注偶极玻色量子流体中多种相互作用（平均场、超越平均场、三体相互作用）与偶极相互作用之间的竞争，以及这种竞争所引发的碎裂（fragmentation）问题。摘要未进一步说明具体的物理背景或应用动机。

Method: 基于该量子流体的理论框架，研究上述相互作用之间的竞争关系，并分析其元激发（elementary excitations）。

Result: 研究中识别出两种不同的色散区域（dispersion regimes），即 roton-mode（roton 模）与调制不稳定性（modulational instability）。

Conclusion: 通过计算超流占比（superfluid fraction）与凝聚体占比（condensate fraction），对所观察到的现象提供了支持性验证。摘要未给出具体的数值结论或定量结果。

Abstract: In this article, we study the dipolar Bosonic quantum fluid. The fluid experiences mean-field, beyond mean-field, and three body interactions. We investigate their competition with dipolar interaction and fragmentation as a result of this competition. We further investigate the elementary excitations and note two distinct dispersion regimes, namely roton-mode and modulational instability. We support our observation by calculating the superfluid fraction and the condensate fraction.

</details>


### [212] [Statistical delocalization in the Anyonic Aubry-André model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20029)
*Andoni Agirre,André Eckardt,Tobias Grass*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该研究展示了一种由交换统计（exchange statistics）单独诱导的多体离域化（many-body delocalization）现象：在一维任意子（anyon）Aubry-André准周期系统中，增大任意子统计相位会系统性地将多体局域化（many-body localization）转变点推向更大的准周期势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多体局域化（many-body localization）的显著特征是即便存在动力学相互作用，局域化仍能持续存在。本文旨在探究其逆现象，即能否仅由交换统计（exchange statistics）引发增强的多体离域化（delocalization）。

Method: 研究一维无密度-密度相互作用（density-density interactions）的任意子（anyon）Aubry-André准周期系统；以任意子算符表示时系统由二次型哈密顿量（quadratic Hamiltonian）描述。通过分析其谱性质的有限尺寸标度（finite-size scaling）与多体本征态的逆参与比（inverse participation ratio），并借助淬火（quenches）后初始密度失衡（density imbalance）的持续性作为动力学探针进行验证。

Result: 增大任意子统计相位（anyonic statistical phase）会系统性地将多体局域化转变点（crossover）向更大的准周期势方向移动；这一结论通过谱性质、逆参与比以及密度失衡动力学探针均得到一致证实。

Conclusion: 结果确立交换统计（exchange statistics）为一种能够改变多体局域化的独立机制，揭示了一维量子系统中一条根本不同的离域化（delocalization）路径。

Abstract: Many-body localization is remarkable in that localization persists despite the presence of dynamical interactions. Here we demonstrate the converse phenomenon: enhanced many-body delocalization induced solely by exchange statistics. We study a Aubry-André quasiperiodic system of anyons in one dimension in the absence of density-density interactions. In terms of anyon operators, the system is described by a quadratic Hamiltonian. By analyzing the finite-size scaling of its spectral properties and the inverse participation ratio of many-body eigenstates, we find that increasing the anyonic statistical phase systematically shifts the many-body localization crossover toward larger quasiperdiodic potentials. We further confirm these findings using a dynamical probe, namely the persistence of an initial density imbalance following quenches. Our results establish exchange statistics as an independent mechanism capable of altering many-body localization, revealing a fundamentally distinct route to delocalization in one-dimensional quantum systems.

</details>


### [213] [Hubbard models with ultracold shielded molecules in optical lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20286)
*Kevin Pérez,Joseph W. Desroches,Kaden R. A. Hazzard,Tijs Karman*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该文指出，在适当参数区间内，光晶格中的超冷屏蔽分子（collisionally shielded molecules）可实现可控扩展哈伯德模型（extended Hubbard model），且晶格深度与微波诱导偶极相互作用（microwave-induced dipolar interactions）可独立调控格点内与格点间相互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超冷屏蔽分子具有大半径硬核排斥（large-radius hard-core repulsion），这通常不利于实现可控的格点模型；同时，掺杂莫特绝缘体（doped Mott insulators）中空穴间相互作用对超导与奇异金属性至关重要，而现有量子气体显微术（quantum gas microscopy）无法区分这类相互作用。

Method: 作者推导并计算了该体系的哈伯德参数（Hubbard parameters），分析晶格深度与微波诱导偶极相互作用对格点内（on-site）和格点间（off-site）相互作用的调控作用。

Result: 结果表明，晶格深度与微波诱导偶极相互作用可对格点内与格点间相互作用提供灵活且相互独立的调控；由此屏蔽分子成为研究扩展哈伯德模型的有力平台，并可用于实现可区分空穴间相互作用的相互作用显微镜（interaction microscope）。

Conclusion: 在适当参数区间，大半径硬核排斥并不妨碍屏蔽分子体系实现受控扩展哈伯德模型；该平台可为掺杂莫特绝缘体中量子气体显微术难以区分的空穴间相互作用提供新的探测途径，从而有助于超导与奇异金属性（strange metallicity）研究。

Abstract: We show that, in appropriate regimes, ultracold collisionally shielded molecules in an optical lattice realize a controlled extended Hubbard model, despite their large-radius hard-core repulsion. We compute the Hubbard parameters and show lattice depth and microwave-induced dipolar interactions provide flexible, independent control of on- and off-site interactions. This makes shielded molecules a powerful platform for extended Hubbard models and enables an interaction microscope that can distinguish inter-hole interactions in doped Mott insulators - crucial to superconductivity and strange metallicity - that are indistinguishable with quantum gas microscopy alone.

</details>


### [214] [Unified hydrodynamics for density and spin supersolids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20487)
*Ashwath N. Madhusudan,Au-Chen Lee,P. B. Blakie,R. N. Bisset*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该工作建立多组分超固体的普适流体动力学理论，并以二元偶极凝聚体微观计算加以验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双组分超固体（two-component supersolids）可呈现同相或反相的组分密度调制，分别对应密度超固体与自旋超固体，但缺乏统一描述其集体动力学的理论框架。

Method: 依据体系破缺的对称性（broken symmetries）与守恒律（conservation laws），构建多组分超固体（multicomponent supersolids）集体动力学的普适流体动力学（hydrodynamic theory）理论，并与受限于一维无界管（infinite tube）中二元偶极凝聚体（binary dipolar condensate）的微观计算进行基准比对。

Result: 在密度超固体与自旋超固体区域内，三个戈德斯通支（Goldstone branches）的声速（sound speeds）均与微观计算定量一致。

Conclusion: 所得关系式将集体模（collective-mode）测量与弹性系数（elastic coefficients）及超流组分（superfluid fractions）联系起来。

Abstract: Two-component supersolids can exhibit in-phase or out-of-phase component density modulations, corresponding to density and spin supersolids, respectively. We formulate a general hydrodynamic theory for the collective dynamics of multicomponent supersolids, guided by their broken symmetries and conservation laws. We benchmark it against microscopic calculations for a binary dipolar condensate confined to an infinite tube, finding quantitative agreement for the sound speeds of all three Goldstone branches throughout the density- and spin-supersolid regions. The resulting relations link collective-mode measurements to elastic coefficients and superfluid fractions.

</details>


<div id='quant-ph'></div>

# quant-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [215] [Femtoscopic Correlation Functions in Density Operator Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19161)
*Hao-Nan Liu,Duo-Lun Ge,Zhi-Wei Liu,Jun-Xu Lu,Li-Sheng Geng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文在算符层面重新表述飞秒关联函数（femtoscopic correlation functions, CFs），以回应其物理合理性受质疑的问题；在四项合理约化下可回到Koonin-Pratt形式，并强调源侧与相互作用侧表征必须一致匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 飞秒关联函数（femtoscopic CFs）日益被用于提取不稳定粒子对之间的强相互作用（strong interactions），但其物理合理性近期受到质疑，故需为该方法的理论基础提供更严格的表述。

Method: 在算符层面（operator level）构造关联函数：观测子系统由约化密度算符（reduced density operator）描述，随后的动力学演化被吸收进有效测量算符（effective measurement operator）；并在四项动机充分的约化条件下恢复Koonin-Pratt形式。

Result: 该表述明确指出源侧（source-side）与相互作用侧（interaction-side）的表征必须被一致地匹配，从而解释了此前物理合理性受质疑的根源。

Conclusion: 由此提出一种操作约定（operational convention）：以实验测得的参考关联（reference correlation）建立兼容的源—相互作用配对，并可将其推广至其他粒子对，用于关联函数对关联函数（CF-to-CF）的预测。

Abstract: Femtoscopic correlation functions (CFs) have been increasingly used to extract strong interactions between pairs of unstable particles, but their physical soundness has recently been questioned. To answer this, we formulate CFs at the operator level, with the observed subsystem described by a reduced density operator and subsequent dynamics absorbed into an effective measurement operator. The Koonin-Pratt form is recovered under four well-motivated reductions. The formulation makes explicit that the source-side and interaction-side representations must be consistently matched, and motivates an operational convention in which a measured reference correlation establishes a compatible source--interaction pairing that can be extended to other pairs for CF-to-CF predictions.

</details>


### [216] [Energy-Constrained Commutator Variance for Weyl Pairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19163)
*Hassan Nasreddine*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究 Weyl 关系表示下，交换子方差 $\operatorname{Var}_ρ(-i[A_ρ,B])=4$ 的可达性，并给出优化后的能量约束系数的精确值 $4$。进一步对固定三角 Weyl 见证与单模二次能量证明渐近展开，其领头项系数由 $M$、$π$ 与能量 $E$ 显式给出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文关注在平均能量约束（mean-energy constraint）下，对法向态（normal state）集合中交换子方差的最优化问题，需确定其可达上确界系数。核心动机在于刻画能量阈值与算术语义约束下该系数的精确取值，并给出固定见证（fixed witness）情形下的定量误差阶。

Method: 设 $W$ 为可分 Hilbert 空间上 Weyl 关系的表示，$\mathcal R_W(K)=\{W(f):f\in K\}''$。当 $K_1,K_2$ 的辛配对（symplectic pairing）非零时，构造 $B\in\mathcal R_W(K_2)$ 与依赖态 $ρ$ 的 $A_ρ\in\mathcal R_W(K_1)$。对固定三角见证 $h$，利用 $4-h^2$ 的恒等式将亏损化为对可交换平方 Weyl 平移（commuting squared Weyl translations）的相位固定问题。对 $G_M$ 与 $G=dΓ(H_1)$ 分别作渐近与情形分析。

Result: 辛配对非零时，存在自伴酉元 $B$，使得对每个法向态 $ρ$ 可取 $A_ρ$ 满足 $\operatorname{Var}_ρ(-i[A_ρ,B])=4$，故优化后的平均输入能量约束系数在每个容许能量阈值处等于 $4$。对 $G_M=\frac12R^TMR-\frac12\sqrt{\det M}$（$M>0$，$u^TΩv=π$），证明 $4-γ_{G_M,E}(h)=\frac{u^TMu+v^TMv+2π\sqrt{\det M}}{4E}+O(E^{-2})$，下界对所有满足平均能量约束的法向态成立，且局部化 Zak 构造达到相同系数。对 $G=dΓ(H_1)$，当见证方向 $u,v\in\operatorname{dom}H_1^{1/2}$ 中至少一个位于 $\ker H_1$ 之外时固定见证亏损为 $Θ(E^{-1})$；若两方向均为零模，则约束上确界在每个正阈值处等于端点值。

Conclusion: 结果为能量约束下交换子方差的最优化提供精确系数刻画：一般情形系数恒为 $4$，单模二次能量情形给出以 $E^{-1}$ 为主阶的渐近公式，并区分了零模与非零模见证方向上确界的定性差异。

Abstract: Let $W$ be a representation of the Weyl relations on a separable Hilbert space and write $\mathcal R_W(K)=\{W(f):f\in K\}''$. If the symplectic pairing of $K_1$ and $K_2$ is nonzero, there is a self-adjoint unitary $B\in\mathcal R_W(K_2)$ such that, for every normal state $ρ$, one can choose a self-adjoint unitary $A_ρ\in\mathcal R_W(K_1)$ with $\operatorname{Var}_ρ(-i[A_ρ,B])=4$. Hence the optimized mean-input-energy-constrained coefficient equals $4$ at every admissible energy threshold. For the fixed trigonometric Weyl witness $h$, an exact identity for $4-h^2$ reduces the deficit to a phase-fixed problem for commuting squared Weyl translations. For the one-mode quadratic energy $G_M=\frac12 R^TMR-\frac12\sqrt{\det M}$, with $M>0$ and $u^TΩv=π$, we prove $4-γ_{G_M,E}(h)=\frac{u^TMu+v^TMv+2π\sqrt{\det M}}{4E}+O(E^{-2})$. The lower bound holds over all normal states satisfying the mean-energy constraint, and a localized Zak construction attains the same coefficient. For $G=dΓ(H_1)$ and witness directions $u,v\in\operatorname{dom}H_1^{1/2}$, the fixed-witness deficit is $Θ(E^{-1})$ whenever at least one direction lies outside $\ker H_1$; if both directions are zero modes, the constrained supremum equals the endpoint at every positive threshold.

</details>


### [217] [Lazy training of quantum physics informed neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19239)
*Anderson Melchor Hernandez,Giacomo De Palma*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究量子物理信息神经网络（quantum physics-informed neural networks, QPINNs）求解带狄利克雷边界条件（Dirichlet boundary conditions）的二阶椭圆型偏微分方程（second-order elliptic PDEs）时的梯度流训练动力学，并在过参数化（overparameterized）区间给出非渐近的惰性训练（lazy-training）理论，证明大电路宽度下非线性梯度流可由线性化神经正切核（neural tangent kernel, NTK）模型定量近似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作多为渐近分析或经验观察，缺乏对QPINNs训练动力学在有限规模下的严格刻画。该研究旨在为参数化量子电路作为函数逼近器求解PDE时的训练行为提供非渐近理论保证。

Method: 将参数化量子电路（parameterized quantum circuits）用作函数逼近器，从NTK视角分析其过参数化区间，对QPINNs及其变分表述（variational formulation）建立理论推导，并给出显式误差界。

Result: 证明当电路宽度足够大时，非线性梯度流可被线性化NTK模型定量近似；所得界显式依赖于量子比特数（number of qubits）、电路深度（circuit depth）、电路光锥几何（circuit light-cone geometry）以及PDE解的定义域维数（dimension of the domain）。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了QPINNs的非渐近惰性训练理论，说明在足够大宽度下其训练动力学近似等价于线性NTK模型，为量子物理信息神经网络的过参数化训练提供了理论刻画。

Abstract: We study the gradient-flow training dynamics of quantum physics-informed neural networks (QPINNs) for the solution of second-order elliptic partial differential equations with Dirichlet boundary conditions. We consider parameterized quantum circuits as function approximators and analyze their overparameterized regime through the lens of the neural tangent kernel (NTK). Our contribution is a nonasymptotic lazy-training theory for QPINNs and their variational formulation: we prove that, for sufficiently large circuit width, the nonlinear gradient flow is quantitatively approximated by a linearized NTK model, with explicit bounds depending on the number of qubits, circuit depth, circuit light-cone geometry, and the dimension of the domain of the solution to the PDE.

</details>


### [218] [Enhanced Precision in Entangled Quantum Clocks with Phase Estimation Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06606)
*Won-Young Hwang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种增强型纠缠量子钟（entangled-quantum-clock）协议，借助量子相位估计（quantum phase estimation）算法将固有时差直接作为未知相位进行估计，在完全免疫动力学相位噪声的同时达到海森堡极限（Heisenberg limit）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于提升固有时差（proper-time difference）的估计精度，并克服动力学相位噪声（dynamical phase noise）对测量不确定度的限制，同时探索超高精度协议在引力波探测（gravitational wave detection）等相对论传感（relativistic sensing）与基础物理检验中的可行性。

Method: 方法上引入量子相位估计（quantum phase estimation）算法，将固有时差直接作为未知相位进行估计，并采用高度纠缠的多时钟态（highly entangled multi-clock states）作为探针资源。

Result: 结果显示，可达不确定度与量子钟总数成反比，即达到海森堡极限 $O(1/N)$，且对动力学相位噪声完全免疫。

Conclusion: 结论指出该协议可用于引力波探测，并展现出在量子增强相对论传感（quantum-enhanced relativistic sensing）与基础物理检验方面的潜力。

Abstract: We present an enhanced entangled-quantum-clock protocol that incorporates a quantum phase estimation algorithm to directly estimate proper-time differences as an unknown phase. By employing highly entangled multi-clock states, the achievable uncertainty scales inversely with the total number of quantum clocks, achieving the Heisenberg limit $O(1/N)$ while remaining completely immune to dynamical phase noise. Furthermore, we discuss the feasibility of utilizing this ultra-high precision protocol for gravitational wave detection, highlighting its potential for quantum-enhanced relativistic sensing and fundamental physics tests.

</details>


### [219] [Emergent classicality and wavefunction branching in an isolated quantum many-body system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19254)
*Saúl Pilatowsky-Cameo,Jordan Cotler,Daniel Ranard,C. Jess Riedel*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作证明孤立多体系统可在不引入外部环境（external environment）的前提下自发涌现经典性（classicality）：在一个弱无序的、$3$-局域（3-local）混沌踢陀螺（kicked top）中，集体自旋（collective spin）自由度充当宏观描述，置换（permutation）自由度充当内部浴，导致集体自旋约化动力学收敛为经典混沌 Fokker--Planck 方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统退相干（decoherence）模型将经典性归因于系统与外部环境的相互作用，该框架无法解释孤立多体系统（isolated many-body systems）在宏观尺度上同样呈现经典行为这一事实。因此需要说明孤立系统中退相干如何由宏观描述不可见的微观自由度内部涌现。

Method: 研究对象为 $N$ 个量子比特（qubits）构成的弱无序、$3$-局域混沌踢陀螺（chaotic kicked top），其中集体自旋扇区对应宏观描述，置换扇区充当内部浴（internal bath）。方法上从封闭幺正动力学（closed unitary dynamics）出发，推导集体自旋变量的有效 Lindblad 方程（effective Lindblad equation），并辅以数值验证。

Result: 推导出的有效 Lindblad 方程得到数值确认；在热力学极限（thermodynamic limit）下，约化动力学收敛为球面相空间上的经典混沌 Fokker--Planck 方程，其扩散系数极小。该结果给出了超越 Ehrenfest 时间（Ehrenfest time）的量子-经典对应（quantum-classical correspondence）。

Conclusion: 混沌动力学将纯多体波函数演化为与不同经典轨迹对应的连续分支（branching components），这些分支在置换扇区获得近乎正交的微观记录（microscopic records），从而抑制量子干涉（quantum interferences），保证相应历史（histories）保持一致性。经典性因此可在孤立系统内部生成。

Abstract: Decoherence in quantum systems is conventionally modeled as the effect of interactions with an external environment. However, such a prescription excludes isolated many-body systems, which are also expected to display classical behavior at macroscopic scales. In isolated systems, decoherence must emerge internally from microscopic degrees of freedom that are invisible to the macroscopic description. Here we explicitly show that classicality can emerge in such a fashion. We consider a weakly disordered, $3$-local chaotic kicked top of $N$ qubits, where the collective spin sector serves as the macroscopic description, while the microscopic permutation sector acts as an internal bath, decohering the collective spin sector. Starting from closed unitary dynamics, we derive and numerically confirm an effective Lindblad equation for the collective spin variables. In the thermodynamic limit these reduced dynamics converge to a classical chaotic Fokker--Planck equation with vanishingly small diffusion on the spherical phase space, producing a quantum-classical correspondence beyond the Ehrenfest time. The chaotic dynamics evolve the pure many-body wavefunction into continuously branching components associated with distinct classical trajectories. These branches acquire nearly orthogonal microscopic records in the permutation sector, preventing quantum interferences and ensuring the corresponding histories remain consistent.

</details>


### [220] [A Square-Root Barrier to Quantum Gate Speed under Linear Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19280)
*Pablo Restrepo Gaviria,Mischa P. Woods*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文将驱动脉冲视为量子玻色场而非经典波形，证明在给定保真度下门跃迁速率至多随脉冲能量按平方根增长，说明压缩等非经典态工程无法突破线性驱动的平方根能量规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 更快的量子门（quantum gate）可抑制计算过程中的退相干（decoherence），但在超导处理器（superconducting processor）中，更强的微波脉冲会加剧泄漏（leakage）、非共振激发、杂散场误差与串扰（crosstalk）。由此形成能量—速度—误差的核心权衡：能否通过量子态工程（quantum state engineering）使门在参数上更快，而不成比例地付出驱动能量代价。

Method: 将驱动脉冲处理为量子玻色场（quantum bosonic field）而非经典波形，考虑与场线性耦合的有限维系统；在关于耦合与所接受动力学的既定均匀性条件（uniformity conditions）下，证明固定保真度（fixed-fidelity）门跃迁速率的能量标度上界，并允许任意脉冲态（含压缩态与非高斯态）、驱动—系统纠缠（drive–system entanglement）与反作用（back action）。

Result: 固定保真度门跃迁速率至多按脉冲能量的平方根增长；该上界对任意脉冲态（包括压缩态与非高斯态）及驱动—系统纠缠与反作用均成立，而相干高斯脉冲（coherent Gaussian pulses）恰好达到该能量指数。

Conclusion: 仅靠压缩或其他态工程无法将常规线性驱动的平方根能量规律替换为一般量子速度极限（quantum speed limits）所允许的线性标度；实现该改进除需合适的非经典脉冲外，还须改变相互作用类别，从而表明相互作用非线性（interaction nonlinearity）是放松实际门速度误差权衡的关键资源。

Abstract: Faster quantum gates can suppress the decoherence accumulated during a computation, but in superconducting processors stronger microwave pulses can also increase leakage, off-resonant excitation, stray-field errors, and crosstalk. This creates a central energy--speed--error tradeoff: can quantum state engineering make a gate parametrically faster without paying proportionally more drive energy? We address this question by treating the driving pulse as a quantum bosonic field rather than a classical waveform. For a finite-dimensional system coupled linearly to that field, we prove that fixed-fidelity gate transition rates grow at most as the square root of the pulse energy, under stated uniformity conditions on the coupling and accepted dynamics. The bound permits arbitrary pulse states, including squeezed and non-Gaussian states, as well as drive--system entanglement and back action; coherent Gaussian pulses attain its energy exponent. Thus squeezing or other state engineering alone cannot replace the square-root energy law of a conventional linear drive by the linear scaling allowed by general quantum speed limits. Achieving that improvement requires changing the interaction class, in addition to using a suitable nonclassical pulse, thereby identifying interaction nonlinearity as an essential resource for relaxing the practical gate-speed error tradeoff.

</details>


### [221] [Quantum tree networks: complete semidefinite hierarchies and the source-transfer model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19310)
*Xiangling Xu,Igor Klep,Marc-Olivier Renou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过引入“源传输模型（source-transfer model）”刻画量子树网络（quantum tree networks）中的关联，并证明其等价于一个无维度限制的混合量子模型，进而建立两类收敛的半定规划（semidefinite program, SDP）外层层级，证明混合模型下量子树网络博弈值判定问题为 coRE-完全（coRE-complete）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解量子系统的能力与极限是量子信息处理的核心问题之一，其中包含由独立源（independent sources）所分配的量子网络中可产生何种关联。然而对一般量子网络而言，尚未有系统性且与维度无关（dimension-free）的方法能任意好地界定这些关联，该问题因此亟待解决。

Method: 针对所有量子树网络（quantum tree networks）提出新的源传输模型，用 C*-代数（C*-algebras）上的态与沿树递归复合的完全正映射（completely positive maps）描述关联；通过将局域测量重构为网络所规定空间张量积中的 Radon-Nikodym 导数来证明其与混合量子模型的等价性。基于该刻画，建立两个收敛的半定规划外层层级：新的源传输层级与 inflation-NPA 层级，并将非交换实代数几何（noncommutative real algebraic geometry）推广至带完全正映射的多态多项式。

Result: 证明了源传输模型与混合量子模型的等价性，且该混合模型在有限维下退化为标准张量积模型；给出了源传输层级的重构定理以确立其收敛性，并提供提取有限维实现的充分停止准则；对 inflation-NPA 层级，从源副本平均的弱算子极限中重构源传输实现。由此得出混合模型下量子树网络博弈值承诺问题（game-value promise problem）为 coRE-完全。

Conclusion: 该工作为量子树网络中的关联提供了系统性且与维度无关的界定框架，通过源传输模型与两类收敛 SDP 层级解决了此前缺乏通用方法的问题，并刻画了混合模型下相关判定问题的计算复杂度。

Abstract: Understanding the capabilities and limits of quantum systems is central to quantum information processing. This includes determining which correlations can arise in networks of quantum systems distributed by independent sources. Yet for general quantum networks, no systematic, dimension-free method is known to bound these correlations arbitrarily well. We resolve this problem for all quantum tree networks through a new source-transfer model, which we prove equivalent to a mixed quantum model. The mixed model places no restriction on dimension and reduces to the standard tensor-product model in finite dimensions; the source-transfer model instead describes these correlations through states on $C^*$-algebras and completely positive maps composed recursively along the tree. We prove the equivalence by reconstructing local measurements as Radon-Nikodym derivatives in the spatial tensor products prescribed by the network. Building on this characterization, we establish two convergent outer hierarchies of semidefinite programs (SDPs): a novel source-transfer hierarchy and the inflation-NPA hierarchy. For the source-transfer hierarchy, we extend noncommutative real algebraic geometry to multi-state polynomials with completely positive maps, prove a reconstruction theorem establishing its convergence, and give a sufficient stopping criterion for extracting finite-dimensional realizations. For the inflation-NPA hierarchy, we reconstruct source-transfer realizations from weak-operator limits of averages over source copies. Consequently, the game-value promise problem for quantum tree networks in the mixed model is $\mathsf{coRE}$-complete.

</details>


### [222] [Observing and evading quantum back-action on a kilogram-scale oscillator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19317)
*Begüm Kabagöz,Eric Oelker,Dhruva Ganapathy,Nergis Mavalvala,Vivishek Sudhir,Vladimir Bossilkov,Joseph Betzweiser,Valery V. Frolov,Anamaria Effler,Adam Mullavey,Lisa Barsotti,Evan D. Kasprzack,K. Kawabe,N. Kijbunchoo,P. J. King,J. S. Kissel,K. Komori,A. Kontos,R. Kumar,K. Kuns,M. Landry,B. Lantz,M. Laxen,K. Lee,M. Lesovsky,F. Llamas Villarreal,M. Lormand,H. A. Loughlin,R.
