Warner,B. Weaver,R. Weiss,C. Whittle,B. Willke,C. C. Wipf,J. L. Wright,V. A. Xu,H. Yamamoto,L. Zhang,M. E. Zucker*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在 LIGO 的 40 kg 反射镜差分运动中，通过将系统置于辐射压反作用主导频段并调控暗端口注入的量子态，使读出不准度与反作用产生相关性并部分抵消，观测到的共振附近运动降低约 47%，实现宏观光机械系统中的量子反作用规避（back-action evasion）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续量子位移测量（continuous quantum displacement measurement）本质上受读出不准度（readout imprecision）与测量反作用（measurement back-action）之间权衡的限制，该权衡由海森堡不确定性原理（Heisenberg uncertainty principle）约束。在激光干涉引力波天文台（LIGO）中，这两种量子噪声分量主导了大部分观测频段，使其成为研究该问题的理想实验平台。

Method: 将 40 kg 反射镜的差分运动捕获于辐射压反作用（radiation-pressure back-action）主导的频段，从而诱导出一个亚赫兹线宽（sub-Hz-linewidth）的光机械模式（optomechanical mode）。通过工程化进入暗端口（dark port）的量子态，在不准度与反作用之间建立相关性（correlations），并采用可分辨不准度、反作用与相关项的框架进行分析。

Result: 所建立的相关性使不准度与反作用的贡献部分抵消，共振附近观测到的运动降低约 47%。所提出的解析框架能够识别该抑制效应的来源。

Conclusion: 该结果在宏观光机械系统（macroscopic optomechanical system）中演示了量子反作用规避（quantum back-action evasion）与量子储层工程（quantum reservoir engineering）。

Abstract: Continuous quantum displacement measurements are fundamentally limited by a trade-off between readout imprecision and measurement back-action, constrained by the Heisenberg uncertainty principle. In the Laser Interferometric Gravitational-Wave Observatory (LIGO), these two quantum noise components dominate much of the observation band, making it an excellent testbed. We induce a sub-Hz-linewidth optomechanical mode by trapping the differential motion of the 40-kg mirrors in a band where radiation-pressure back-action dominates the motion. Engineering the quantum state entering the dark port creates correlations between imprecision and back-action that partially cancel their contributions, reducing observed motion near resonance by ~47%. A framework resolving the imprecision, back-action, and correlation terms identifies the origin of this suppression. These results demonstrate quantum back-action evasion and quantum reservoir engineering in a macroscopic optomechanical system.

</details>


### [223] [Exceptional points and Jordan-chain signatures in quantum first-passage statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20148)
*Lachlan Bridges*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文从精确变换域结构出发，论证一阶通过量（first-passage）可观测量中的例外点（exceptional point）特征由两个独立存活机制支配，并给出阈值分解定理、无通行定理与最小见证模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对例外点在通过量可观测量中的出现条件缺乏精确刻画；本文旨在厘清非线性谱亏损（spectral defectivity）如何传递到物理的一级阶梯描述，以及阶梯 Jordan 模（Jordan mode）与制备、末态观测之间需满足何种重叠条件。

Method: 针对有限重置形式（reset-form）监测量子系统，在计数不含向上跳跃（upward-skip-free counting）的条件下，证明阈值分解 $H_N(s)=R_s^N$，识别物理 Perron 分支，导出转移分解 $Q_s(r)=B_s(r)(R_s-rI)$；借助余子式可逆性分析 Smith 数据的局部相等，利用二阶 Keldysh 公式构造独立观测门，并证明固定重置的无通行定理。

Result: 证明阈值因子化与转移因子化；余子式可逆时 Smith 数据局部相等，余子式奇异时可产生阶梯中不存在的转移重数；固定重置下，即便全倾斜生成元亏损，若计数后阶梯为标量则无法生成累积计数 Jordan 多项式；构造三次重置监测 Lindblad 见证（在不可约非负阶梯中最小），含真实次主导 $N\lambda^N$ 阈值项，并给出相干调谐模型，在横向变换域例外点处保持精确多项式特征。

Conclusion: 例外点特征的出现受两个独立存活机制约束：谱亏损须传递至物理一级通过量阶梯，且对应 Jordan 模须与制备及末态观测均有非零重叠。该分析停留在精确变换域结构层面，时域渐近与微扰稳健性仍属独立问题。

Abstract: Exceptional-point signatures in first-passage observables are governed by two independent survival mechanisms: nonlinear spectral defectivity must pass from the one-record or transfer description to the physical one-level first-passage ladder, and the resulting ladder Jordan mode must have nonzero overlap with the chosen preparation and terminal observation. For finite reset-form monitored quantum systems with upward-skip-free counting, we prove the exact threshold factorization $H_N(s)=R_s^N$, identify the physical Perron branch, and derive the transfer factorization $Q_s(r)=B_s(r)(R_s-rI)$. Invertibility of the cofactor gives local equality of Smith data, whereas a singular cofactor can contribute transfer multiplicity absent from the ladder. An order-two Keldysh formula yields an independent observation gate. We then prove a fixed-reset no-go theorem: even a defective full tilted generator cannot generate a cumulative-count Jordan polynomial when the post-count ladder is scalar. Finally, we construct a three-reset monitored-Lindblad witness, minimal within irreducible nonnegative ladders, with a genuine subleading $Nλ^N$ threshold term and a coherence-tuned model in which a binary terminal effect retains the exact polynomial signature at a transversal transform-domain exceptional point. The analysis is formulated at the level of exact transform-domain structure; time-domain asymptotics and perturbative robustness remain separate questions.

</details>


### [224] [Entanglement Dynamics in Katz-Weighted Graph States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20390)
*Lucio De Simone,Lorenzo Capra,Roberto Franzosi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究图上非局域 Ising 相互作用的纠缠动力学，其相互作用由网络的 Katz 核刻画，物理上源于同一图上带隙费米子中介粒子的微扰消除。借助纠缠距离（Entanglement Distance）给出初始可分态纠缠生成的精确解析表达，并区分弱与强传播两种区域的行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于将纠缠动力学与复杂网络的游走结构及谱结构建立联系，并以带隙费米子中介粒子在图上传播的物理图像为相互作用模式提供依据，从而刻画非局域 Ising 相互作用下的纠缠产生规律。

Method: 采用 Katz 核定义的图上有非局域 Ising 相互作用，通过微扰消除同一图上的带隙费米子中介粒子得到有效 Katz 加权 Ising 哈密顿量；使用纠缠距离（Entanglement Distance）推导初始可分态所生成纠缠的精确解析表达式，并将其应用于若干代表性确定性图族。

Result: 在弱 Katz 区域，动力学可作系统的模体（motif）展开，三角形在一阶出现，四环、局部度结构及重叠三角形在二阶出现；在强传播区域，相互作用由主邻接模及其本征向量的局域化性质主导。对 Erdős–Rényi 图，弱传播展开可解析平均，稀疏区呈现局部树状贡献，稠密区纠缠距离密度饱和。

Conclusion: 研究结果将纠缠动力学与复杂网络基于游走的结构以及谱结构联系起来，表明纠缠生成可由 Katz 核相互作用的模体展开与主邻接模性质在不同传播区域中加以刻画。

Abstract: We investigate the entanglement dynamics of quantum states defined on graphs with non-local Ising interactions governed by the Katz kernel of the underlying network. The interaction pattern is physically motivated by a gapped fermionic mediator propagating on the same graph, whose perturbative elimination yields an effective Katz-weighted Ising Hamiltonian. Using the Entanglement Distance, we derive an exact analytical expression for the entanglement generated from an initially separable state and apply it to representative deterministic graph families.
  We then characterize the dynamics in different propagation regimes. In the weak-Katz regime, the dynamics admits a systematic motif expansion with triangles entering at first order order and four-cycles, local degree structure, and overlappin triangles appearing at second order. In the strong-propagation regime, the interaction is instead dominated by the principal adjacency mode and by the localization properties of its eigenvector. For Erdős--Rényi graphs, the weak-propagation expansion can be averaged analytically, revealing a locally tree-like contribution in the sparse regime and saturation of the Entanglement Distance density in the dense regime.
  Our results connect entanglement dynamics with both the walk-based and spectral structure of complex networks.

</details>


### [225] [Multiparameter sensing of axion dark matter with superconducting-qubit networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20254)
*Le Bin Ho*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于纠缠超导量子比特（entangled superconducting qubits）的量子传感器网络（quantum sensor network），将QCD轴子（axion）暗物质探测转化为两参数量子估计（two-parameter quantum estimation）问题，并结合贝叶斯推断（Bayesian inference）重建轴子-光子耦合（axion-photon coupling）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对量子色动力学（QCD）轴子暗物质的直接探测需求，利用超导量子比特的纠缠特性构建量子传感器网络；同时解决弱信号区域中估计框架出现的奇异性（singularity）问题。

Method: 在静磁场中，振荡的轴子场诱导相干的量子比特旋转，其编码角（encoding angle）与轴子-光子耦合和未知暗物质相位成正比。将相位视为附加未知参数，把探测转化为两参数量子估计问题；采用笛卡尔坐标（Cartesian coordinates）表述信号以规避奇异性，建立规则的多参数量子估计框架，并据此优化传感器网络，结合贝叶斯推断重建耦合。

Result: 优化后的协议在质量范围 m_a∈[0.1,10] μeV 内准确恢复 KSVZ 与 DFSZ 基准耦合（benchmark couplings），可泛化至未见过的质量，并在真实的超导量子比特退相干（decoherence）与门误差（gate errors）下保持稳健。

Conclusion: 将暗物质相位纳入估计参数并采用笛卡尔坐标的规则框架，可使基于纠缠超导量子比特的量子传感器网络实现对QCD轴子暗物质的直接探测，并具备对不同质量与实验噪声的泛化性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: We propose a quantum sensor network for direct detection of quantum chromodynamics (QCD) axion dark matter using entangled superconducting qubits. In the presence of a static magnetic field, the oscillating axion field induces a coherent qubit rotation described by an encoding angle proportional to the axion-photon coupling and an unknown dark-matter phase. Treating the phase as an additional unknown parameter turns axion detection into a two-parameter quantum estimation problem. We formulate these signals in Cartesian coordinates, thereby avoiding the singularity that arises in the weak-signal regime and establishing a regular framework for multiparameter quantum estimation. Using this framework, we optimize the quantum sensor network and combine it with Bayesian inference to reconstruct the axion-photon coupling. The optimized protocol accurately recovers the KSVZ and DFSZ benchmark couplings over the mass range $m_a\in[0.1,10]~μ{\rm eV}$, generalizes to previously unseen masses, and remains robust against realistic superconducting-qubit decoherence and gate errors.

</details>


### [226] [Suzuki-Trotter Decompositions and other Methods for Quantum Time Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19184)
*Johann Ostmeyer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该综述系统梳理了量子系统时间演化数值方法（如 Trotter 分解、分裂方法与 Lie 乘积公式）的最新进展，重点关注近年出现的高效算法、误差界（error bounds）估计的改进，以及其在含噪量子硬件上的应用与相关推广方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间演化数值方法在经典方程求解、蒙特卡洛模拟，以及经典与量子计算机上的实/虚时间演化中广泛使用，但方法名称繁多、进展分散，需要一份面向实践的最新综述来整合理论与应用。

Method: 综述以理论物理学家的视角展开，回顾该领域最新进展，重点介绍近年发现的高效时间演化算法及其最佳实践，并系统总结误差界估计的改进；同时涵盖推广与替代方法，如含时哈密顿量动力学、processed methods、多乘积公式（multi-product formulae）、辛积分器（symplectic integrators）、张量网络 TDVP、量子信号处理（quantum signal processing）与 Crouch-Grossman 方法等。为便于阅读，省略全部数学证明及部分技术细节。

Result: 综述指出过去十年误差界估计获得显著改进，并整理了新近发现的高效时间演化算法及其实际使用方式；同时讨论了时间演化方法在（尤其是含噪）量子计算中的相关性，并给出推广、相关方法与 Trotterization 替代方案的全面概览。

Conclusion: 该工作定位为一份实操指南（hands-on guide），同时作为获取进一步细节的文献起点，而非包含完整数学证明的理论专著。

Abstract: (Suzuki-)Trotter decompositions, splitting methods, (Lie) product formulae... The most common numerical methods for the time evolution of quantum systems come with many names. And they are used practically everywhere with applications ranging from the solution of classical equations of motion and various Monte Carlo simulations to the real and imaginary time evolution on classical as well as quantum computers. Here we review the state of the art of said methods, focussing especially on the progress made over the last few years. We highlight recently discovered efficient time evolution algorithms and explain how best to use them in practice. A central part of this work is the estimation of error bounds that has improved greatly within the past decade. The relevance of time evolution methods for quantum computing is discussed with a focus on noisy hardware. Finally, a comprehensive overview of generalisations, related methods and alternatives to Trotterization is provided. This includes time-dependent Hamiltonian dynamics, processed methods, multi-product formulae, symplectic integrators, TDVP for tensor networks, quantum signal processing, Crouch-Grossman methods and more. The overall perspective in this work is that of a theoretical physicist. All mathematical proofs as well as some technical details are omitted for easier readability. Instead, this review serves as a hands-on guide and, of course, as a starting point for references that provide further details.

</details>


### [227] [Theory of post-selected entanglement transitions in monitored bosons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19251)
*Ilia Komissarov,Emanuele G. Dalla Torre,Ahana Chakraborty*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作针对无界占据数玻色子系统（bosonic systems）中测量诱导的纠缠相变（measurement-induced entanglement transition）难以刻画的问题，提出无副本（replica-free）Keldysh 场论框架，将 Fock 态初始化的 Renyi 熵表示为 Green 函数矩阵的积和式（permanent）。在一维 cross-stitch 格子的连续监测无点击轨迹下，发现体积律到对数律纠缠标度的转变，其由非厄米谱（non-Hermitian spectrum）重组控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测量驱动的纠缠相变已成为开放量子多体物理的核心范式，但既有结论主要建立在有限局域 Hilbert 空间维度体系（如 qubit、fermion）上。无界局域占据数的玻色子体系实现此类相变仍不清楚，且即使无相互作用，玻色子数态本质上非高斯（non-Gaussian），使标准关联矩阵方法失效。

Method: 发展无副本（replica-free）的 Keldysh 场论框架，将初始化为格点 Fock 态的玻色系统 Renyi 熵表达为由单粒子 Green 函数（single-particle Green's function）构成的矩阵的积和式（permanent）。将该框架应用于连续监测的一维 cross-stitch 格子，并以无点击轨迹（no-click trajectory）为条件进行分析。

Result: 观察到从体积律（volume-law）到对数律（logarithmic）纠缠标度的转变。该转变由非厄米谱（non-Hermitian spectrum）的重组所控制，使长寿命模式数目从广延（extensive）变为有限。强监测区玻色子动态凝聚至少数最慢衰减模式，产生对数纠缠标度；弱监测下广延的长寿命模式流形则导致体积律纠缠。

Conclusion: 结果确立了自由玻色子体系中测量诱导纠缠相变的一种独特机制，并提供了一种计算高效的测量诱导玻色凝聚（measurement-induced bosonic condensation）诊断方法。

Abstract: Entanglement phase transitions driven by quantum measurements have emerged as a central paradigm in open quantum many-body physics. Such phase transitions are well established for systems with finite local Hilbert-space dimensions, such as qubits and fermions, while their realization in bosonic systems with unbounded local occupation numbers remains poorly understood. Even in the absence of interactions, number states of bosons are intrinsically non-Gaussian, preventing the use of standard correlation-matrix approaches. To address this problem, we develop a replica-free Keldysh field-theoretic framework that expresses the Renyi entropy of bosonic systems initialized in on-site Fock states in terms of permanents of matrices constructed from single-particle Green's functions. Applying this framework to a continuously monitored one-dimensional cross-stitch lattice conditioned on the no-click trajectory, we uncover a transition from volume-law to logarithmic entanglement scaling. We show that the transition is controlled by a restructuring of the non-Hermitian spectrum that changes the number of long-lived modes from extensive to finite. In the strongly monitored regime, bosons dynamically condense into a microscopic number of slowest-decaying modes, producing logarithmic entanglement scaling, whereas an extensive manifold of long-lived modes at weak monitoring gives rise to volume-law entanglement. Our results establish a distinct mechanism for measurement-induced entanglement transitions in free bosonic systems and provide a computationally efficient diagnostic of the measurement-induced bosonic condensation.

</details>


### [228] [Engineering and Probing a One-Dimensional Dipolar Spin Ensemble in Diamond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19496)
*Lingjie Chen,Shreyas Parthasarathy,Simon A. Meynell,Lillian B. Hughes Wyatt,Eveline Postelnicu,Haopu Yang,Zilin Wang,Weijie Wu,Winston V. Peloso,Casey K. Kim,Chris R. Laumann,Kunal Mukherjee,Norman Y. Yao,Ania C. Bleszynski Jayich*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究在金刚石中构建并表征了由光学暗态P1中心与可光学寻址NV中心组成的准一维无序自旋链，利用化学气相沉积中台阶束对氮的择优掺入实现横向与纵向限域，并结合材料表征与纳米尺度量子传感确认其低维特性，进而用关联光谱研究无穷温度偶极自旋输运。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 维度（dimensionality）在决定相互作用量子系统集体行为中起核心作用，但在约化维度下构建强相互作用固态自旋缺陷系综仍是应用与基础科学交叉处的重大挑战。

Method: 利用化学气相沉积（chemical vapor deposition）过程中台阶束（step bunches）对氮的择优掺入，在金刚石（diamond）中实现横向与纵向限域；结合空间分辨材料表征（materials characterization）与纳米尺度量子传感（nanoscale quantum sensing），并用关联光谱（correlation spectroscopy）探测局部自旋自关联与无穷温度偶极自旋输运（infinite-temperature dipolar spin transport）。

Result: 成功构建位置无序（positionally disordered）的准一维自旋链，由光学暗态替位氮缺陷（substitutional nitrogen defects，P1 centers）与可光学寻址探针氮-空位中心（nitrogen-vacancy，NV centers）组成，并确认了该光学暗态、未极化P1自旋系综的一维特性。

Conclusion: 该结果确立了基于材料的低维量子自旋系统（low-dimensional quantum spin systems）工程化路径。

Abstract: Dimensionality plays a central role in determining the collective behavior of interacting quantum systems. Engineering strongly interacting ensembles of solid-state spin defects in reduced dimensions remains a significant challenge at the interface between the applied and fundamental sciences. Here, we create and characterize a positionally disordered, quasi-one-dimensional spin chain in diamond, consisting of optically dark substitutional nitrogen defects (P1 centers) and optically addressable probe nitrogen-vacancy (NV) centers. Our approach exploits the preferential incorporation of nitrogen along step bunches formed during chemical vapor deposition to achieve both lateral and vertical confinement. Combining spatially resolved materials characterization with nanoscale quantum sensing, we establish the one-dimensional character of the optically dark, unpolarized P1 spin ensemble. We then use correlation spectroscopy to probe local spin autocorrelations and investigate infinite-temperature dipolar spin transport. Our results establish a materials-based route for engineering low-dimensional quantum spin systems.

</details>


### [229] [Semiclassical scaling of eigenstate thermalization in single-particle chaotic systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20052)
*Yaoqi Ye,Xiao Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究时间反演不变的单粒子混沌系统中实空间可观测量非对角矩阵元，基于 Berry 猜想推导的半经典方差表达式，揭示可观测量矩阵的带状结构，并给出其与单粒子本征态热化假设（ETH）及多体 ETH 的对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在阐明 Berry 猜想的动力学含义，并比较单粒子本征态热化（single-particle eigenstate thermalization）与多体本征态热化假设（many-body ETH）之间的异同，特别是陡壁束缚系统中的标度行为。

Method: 从 Berry 猜想出发推导非对角方差（off-diagonal variance）的半经典表达式（semiclassical expression），分析其带状结构；通过量子台球（quantum billiards）案例研究，并在广义四分之一 Sinai 台球（generalized quarter-Sinai billiard）中数值验证半经典标度。

Result: 非对角元呈现自然涌现的带状结构；局域可观测量具有与晚期时间尺度相关的特征带宽，该时间尺度与粒子速度成反比；对陡壁束缚系统，预测幅度遵循 ETH 的熵标度（entropy scaling），并额外乘以一个依赖动能的因子，该因子与空间维度无关且不被常规多体 ETH 涵盖。

Conclusion: 结果阐明了 Berry 猜想的动力学含义，并提供了单粒子本征态热化与多体本征态热化假设之间的对比。

Abstract: We study the off-diagonal matrix elements of real-space observables in time-reversal-invariant single-particle chaotic systems. By analyzing the semiclassical expression for the off-diagonal variance derived from Berry's conjecture, we show that the banded structure of the observable matrix emerges naturally. For local observables, we identify a characteristic bandwidth associated with a late-time timescale inversely proportional to the particle velocity. We further show that, for systems with steep-wall confinement, the predicted magnitude follows the entropy scaling of the eigenstate thermalization hypothesis (ETH), multiplied by an additional kinetic-energy-dependent factor that is independent of spatial dimension and is not captured by conventional many-body ETH. We illustrate these results through a case study of quantum billiards and verify the semiclassical scaling numerically in a generalized quarter-Sinai billiard. Our results elucidate the dynamical implications of Berry's conjecture and provide a comparison between single-particle eigenstate thermalization and many-body ETH.

</details>


### [230] [Divergence Geometry of Quantum Multi-Mpemba Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20320)
*Domingos S. P. Salazar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 量子姆潘巴效应（quantum Mpemba effect）是否出现，取决于对“远离平稳态”的度量方式。该工作表明，归一化算子凸 Petz 散度（operator-convex Petz divergences）之间的一致性由一个单参数 χ² 剖面决定；其符号确定共同次序，交替符号裕度保证反复穿越，有限维情形给出多项式正性检验。该剖面同时解释了简单实慢模的度量无关晚期次序，并把相干性（coherence）识别为度量依赖的局域来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子姆潘巴效应的存在性判定依赖于“非平稳性距离”的具体度量选择，因此需要厘清不同度量之间何时给出一致结论。该文旨在将这一度量依赖性从定性观察转化为可判定的数学结构与可控问题。

Method: 以归一化算子凸 Petz 散度族为对象，引入单参数 χ² 剖面作为统一分析工具：用其符号判定共同次序，用交替符号裕度刻画反复穿越，并在有限维下转化为多项式正性检验。结合简单实慢模与囚禁离子 qutrit 理想模型进行验证，并分析相干性在度量依赖性中的作用。

Result: χ² 剖面的符号固定各度量下的共同次序；交替出现的符号裕度保证次序发生多次穿越；有限维情形可由多项式正性检验判定。该剖面解释了简单实慢模下与诊断无关的晚期次序，并表明相干性是度量依赖性的局域来源。在囚禁离子 qutrit 理想模型中，所报告的制备态给出度量选择性的穿越，而邻近的制备态在数值上（floating-point）可作为两种族整体反转的候选。

Conclusion: 该框架将“对度量选择是否稳健”这一诊断问题转化为可处理的量子控制问题，为量子姆潘巴效应的判定与设计提供了统一判据。

Abstract: Whether a quantum Mpemba effect occurs can depend on how distance from stationarity is measured. We show that agreement across normalized operator-convex Petz divergences is decided by a one-parameter $χ^2$ profile. Its sign fixes the common order, alternating sign margins guarantee repeated crossings, and finite dimension yields a polynomial positivity test. The same profile explains diagnostic-independent late-time order for a simple real slow mode and isolates coherence as the local source of diagnostic dependence. In a trapped-ion qutrit ideal model, the reported preparation gives diagnostic-selective crossings, while a nearby preparation is a floating-point candidate for two family-wide reversals. The framework turns diagnostic robustness into a tractable control problem.

</details>


### [231] [State-Selective Floquet Memory in Degenerate Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20428)
*Ayan Sahoo,Argha Debnath,Debraj Rakshit*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究周期性驱动（periodic driving）下哪些初态能保留记忆，指出简并（degeneracy）本身并非决定性因素，关键在于扰动在投影多重态上的作用方式；并以交替 XXZ 链（alternating XXZ chain）为例给出长寿命态与边带共振等结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理想极限下会产生不变结构（invariant structures），无法说明实际初态的记忆保留问题，因此需要离开该极限、进入近简并流形（near-degenerate manifolds）中考察。核心问题是：在周期驱动下，究竟哪些初态能保持记忆。

Method: 对理想极限下解耦为局域团簇（local clusters）的系统，在简并微扰论（degenerate perturbation theory）框架内计算投影。稳定性由两条判据决定：耦合不能将目标态与流形内其他简并伙伴相连（借助动力学阻塞（kinetic blocking）或对投影耦合对角化）；驱动的局域选择定则（local selection rule）只允许转移能量偏离 Floquet 边带（Floquet sidebands），此时投影驱动生成元（projected drive generator）严格为零。

Result: 在周期性驱动的干净、短程交替 XXZ 链中形式化了上述理解，得到的结果包括：长寿命态（既含乘积形式，也含跨团簇纠缠形式）、一族周期性的边带共振（sideband resonances），以及一个明确示例——能量与荷（charges）完全相同却命运相反的态。

Conclusion: 简并本身不足以判定记忆保留，实际决定因素是结构性与驱动性不完美等扰动投影到多重态上的效果；满足两条判据时投影驱动生成元可严格消失，从而实现长寿命记忆的保持。

Abstract: In order to understand which initial states retain memory under periodic driving, it is necessary to depart from the ideal limit giving rise to invariant structures and work inside near-degenerate manifolds. We show that degeneracy itself is not conclusive and what actually matters is how perturbations, such as structural and driving imperfections, acts on the projected multiplet. For systems decoupling into the local clusters in the ideal limit, the projections can be computed within degenerate perturbation theory. There the stability is decided by two criteria: the coupling can not connect the state with other degenerate partners in the manifold, either by kinetic blocking, or more generally, by diagonalizing the projected coupling, and the drive's local selection rule must admit only transfer energies detuned from Floquet sidebands, in which case the projected drive generator exactly vanishes. We formalize these understandings in a periodically driven clean, short-ranged alternating XXZ chain and demonstrate a set of results that include, long-lived states, both of product form and entangled across the clusters, a periodic family of sideband resonances, and an explicit example showing opposite fates for states with identical energy and charges.

</details>


### [232] [TetrisCNN for interpretable detection of phases of matter from experimental quantum simulator data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20693)
*Kacper Cybiński,Björn van Zwol,James Enouen,Guillaume Bornet,Thierry Lahaye,Antoine Browaeys,Antoine Georges,Anna Dawid*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出 TetrisCNN，一种具有并行不同形状滤波器分支的卷积架构，可直接在自旋关联函数（spin correlators）层面学习稀疏可解释的潜在表示，并在二维 Ising 与 XY 量子模拟器的实验快照上同时实现相变检测与符号化表达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检测物质相（phases of matter）通常依赖正确的序参量（order parameter），但对未知相变而言，该任务长期依赖物理直觉与经验猜测，且十分困难。已有神经网络方法虽无需先验物理知识即可定位相变，但仍属黑箱，仅识别相而不阐明其性质，且面对真实含噪实验数据时往往表现不佳。

Method: 引入 TetrisCNN，一种卷积架构（convolutional architecture），其并行分支采用不同形状的滤波器，形似俄罗斯方块（Tetris blocks），从而直接以自旋关联函数（spin correlators）的形式学习稀疏、可解释的潜在表示（latent representations）。

Result: 将该网络应用于在多组基下测量的二维 Ising 与 XY 量子模拟器（quantum simulators）实验快照，网络不仅能检测相变与交越（crossovers），还能将其潜在表示与决策边界表达为由实验可测自旋关联函数构成的符号公式（symbolic formulas）。

Conclusion: 该框架为将可解释神经网络（interpretable neural networks）与量子模拟器相结合、以发现并理解新的物质相开辟了路径。

Abstract: Detecting phases of matter in general relies on identifying the correct order parameter - a task that remains notoriously difficult for unknown transitions and traditionally is guided by physical intuition and educated guess. Neural networks have recently offered an alternative route by locating phase transitions in known models without any a priori physical knowledge. Yet these approaches remain black boxes and only identify phases without elucidating their properties. Moreover, they often struggle when confronted with realistic, noisy experimental data, which constitute the ultimate testbed for automated methods in physics. Here, we bridge these perspectives by introducing TetrisCNN, a convolutional architecture with parallel branches of differently shaped filters, reminiscent of Tetris blocks, that learns sparse, interpretable latent representations directly in terms of spin correlators. Applied to experimental snapshots of two-dimensional Ising and XY quantum simulators measured in multiple bases, the network not only detects phase transitions and crossovers but also expresses its latent representation and decision boundaries as symbolic formulas built from experimentally measurable spin correlators. This framework opens the way to integrating interpretable neural networks with quantum simulators to uncover and understand new phases of matter.

</details>


### [233] [Locally optimized variational evolution for quantum many-body systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20802)
*Carolin Wille,Max Marvell,Lauren Stewart,Max Murphy,Vinul Wimalaweera,Lesley Gover,Andrew G. Green*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种变分时间演化原理（variational time-evolution principle），以局域约化密度矩阵（local reduced density matrices）上的代价函数替代全局态保真度，从而利用热化系统局域可观测量复杂度与系统总尺寸无关的特性。所提算法基于矩阵乘积态（matrix-product states）的局域优化，具有类似含时变分原理（time-dependent variational principle）的闭式运动方程，并给出对散粒噪声与硬件噪声鲁棒的量子-经典混合实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多体动力学中的量子优势（quantum advantage）依赖规避全局波函数指数复杂度带来的经典模拟代价；但局域可观测量并不继承该全局复杂度，而可能由与系统总尺寸无关的内禀局域复杂度支配。这一区分在热化系统（thermalising systems）中尤为关键：局域可观测量丢失微观细节记忆，弛豫到仅由少数参数决定的平衡值。论文旨在利用这一区分降低模拟代价。

Method: 提出变分时间演化原理：以定义在局域约化密度矩阵上的代价函数替代全局态保真度。具体算法基于矩阵乘积态的局域优化，并给出类似含时变分原理的闭式运动方程。同时构建该变分原理的量子-经典对应版本，将局域代价的量子评估与对散粒噪声（shot noise）和硬件噪声均鲁棒的优化策略相结合。

Result: 所提出的演化在短时保持相干动力学（coherent short-time dynamics），同时在较晚时间利用热化带来的简化。在 Quantinuum H2 与 IBM Heron 处理器上的原理验证（proof-of-principle）实现成功恢复了特征性的局域动力学。

Conclusion: 通过将变分目标从全局态保真度转向局域约化密度矩阵，可同时保留短时相干动力学与利用后期热化简化，且该原理存在噪声鲁棒的量子-经典混合实现形式，并在真实量子硬件上完成了初步验证。

Abstract: Conventional quantum advantage in many-body dynamics is based on avoiding the simulation cost on a classical computer that arises from the extensive exponential complexity of the global wavefunction. Local observables, however, do not inherit this extensive complexity and may instead be governed by an intrinsic local complexity that is independent of the total system size. This distinction is particularly relevant in thermalising systems, where local observables lose memory of microscopic details and relax towards equilibrium values determined by only a few parameters. Here we introduce a variational time-evolution principle that exploits this distinction by replacing global-state fidelity with a cost function defined on local reduced density matrices. The resulting evolution retains coherent short-time dynamics while exploiting the simplification produced by thermalisation at later times. The concrete algorithm we propose is based on locally optimising matrix-product states and admits closed-form equations of motion analogous to the time-dependent variational principle. We show that the same variational principle has a quantum-classical counterpart, combining quantum evaluation of the local cost with an optimisation strategy robust to both shot and hardware noise. Proof-of-principle implementations on Quantinuum H2 and IBM Heron processors recover the characteristic local dynamics.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [234] [Learning-Induced Dynamical Transition in Recurrent Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19288)
*Varun Vaidya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出非平衡动力学平均场理论（DMFT），刻画循环神经网络（recurrent neural networks）在学习过程中从混沌到稳定动力学的转变，并预测临界反馈强度与学习率相关的临界时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环神经网络的学习可从根本上重塑其潜在动力学，将初始混沌活动转变为稳定的任务依赖行为，但缺乏对这一学习转变过程的解析理论描述。

Method: 构建非平衡动力学平均场理论（dynamical mean-field theory, DMFT），推导学习全过程中的双时相关函数（two-time correlation function），并识别临界反馈强度与临界时间。

Result: 识别出一个临界反馈强度及依赖于学习率的临界时间，用以分隔混沌与稳定两种机制；该转变源于学习到的反馈结构逐步形变有效动力学景观（effective dynamical landscape）。

Conclusion: 该理论从初始未训练状态出发预测训练期间网络输出的时间演化，并与数值模拟（numerical simulations）定量吻合。

Abstract: Learning in recurrent neural networks can fundamentally reshape their underlying dynamics, transforming initially chaotic activity into stable task-dependent behavior. We develop a non-equilibrium dynamical mean-field theory(DMFT) to describe this transition during learning. We show that a slow feedback-driven learning process generates an evolving effective feedback strength that drives the network through a transition from chaotic to stable dynamics defined by a bifurcation of the DMFT solution. By deriving the two-time correlation function throughout learning, we identify a critical feedback strength and a corresponding learning rate dependent critical time separating these regimes. The transition arises from the progressive deformation of an effective dynamical landscape by the growing learned feedback structure. Starting from the untrained state, the theory predicts the time evolution of the network output during training and shows quantitative agreement with numerical simulations.

</details>


<div id='math.AP'></div>

# math.AP [[Back]](#toc)

### [235] [Long-time dynamics for the magnetic Schr{ö}dinger equation on the torus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19224)
*Julien Lechaux*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究平坦二维环面上磁场薛定谔流（magnetic Schrödinger flow）在长时间尺度下的半经典测度（semiclassical measures），并在有效磁力的几何非零假设下证明量子唯一遍历性（quantum unique ergodicity, QUE）的动力学形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究磁场薛定谔流长时间行为中的半经典测度，需刻画量子遍历性在磁场扰动下的表现形式与时间阈值。对有效磁力施加几何非零假设，以排除退化情形并提供可分析的动力系统结构。

Method: 在半经典（semiclassical）框架下考察与平坦二维环面（$\mathbb T^2$）上磁场薛定谔流相联系的含时半经典测度，识别两个时间阈值，并分别对位形边缘（configuration marginal）与动量边缘（momentum marginal）进行极限测度分析。

Result: 确定两个时间阈值：当时间尺度 $\tau_h \gg h^{-1/2}$ 时，位形边缘为 $\mathbb T^2$ 上的归一化勒贝格测度（normalized Lebesgue measure）；当 $\tau_h \gg h^{-1}$ 时，动量边缘在每个正则水平集（regular level set）上条件化为该曲线上的归一化不变测度（normalized invariant measure）。

Conclusion: 在有效磁力几何非零假设下，磁场薛定谔流在长时间极限中呈现出量化（quantized）且分层的遍历行为：位形空间层面趋于均匀分布，动量空间层面沿正则水平集趋于不变测度，构成量子唯一遍历性的动力学形式。

Abstract: We investigate time-dependent semiclassical measures associated with the magnetic Schrödinger flow on the flat two-dimensional torus in long-time regimes. Under a geometric nonvanishing assumption on an effective magnetic force, we identify two thresholds and prove a dynamical form of quantum unique ergodicity for such systems. If the time scale $τ_h \gg h^{-1/2}$, then the configuration marginal is the normalized Lebesgue measure on $\mathbb T^2$. In addition, if $τ_h \gg h^{-1}$, then the momentum marginal is, conditionally on each regular level set, the normalized invariant measure on that curve.

</details>


### [236] [Non-simple blow-up for the Chern--Simons--Higgs equation: A priori analysis and constructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20042)
*Youngae Lee,Lei Zhang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该论文首次证明了自对偶 Chern–Simons–Higgs 方程非简单爆破解的存在性，涵盖此前未解决的有限高度 Chern–Simons 区域，并刻画了非简单聚簇的质量、剖面与几何排布及质量间隙判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自对偶 Chern–Simons–Higgs 方程的爆破具有丰富的多尺度结构，但既有理论主要局限于每个集中点由单一剖面描述的简单情形。非简单爆破需同时解析多个相互作用的规则核心，分析上更为精细，其解的存在性长期悬而未决。

Method: 基于对一般有限高度聚簇的定量剖面估计与矩估计，构造近似解并厘清有限高度构造背后的核心相互作用；此外在单位圆盘（unit disk）上另作构造，沿着 Dirichlet 迹（Dirichlet trace）变化的序列生成两个平均场核心。

Result: 证明非简单聚簇具有相等的极限核心质量、共同的剖面类型以及正多边形（regular-polygon）排布；确定了其可能的总质量，并导出质量间隙（mass-gap）判据，对某些涡旋构型排除此类爆破。

Conclusion: 该工作给出该方程非简单爆破解的首个存在性结果，特别覆盖此前未解决的有限高度 Chern–Simons 区域，并为理解有限高度构造中的核心相互作用提供了定量刻画。

Abstract: Blow-up in the self-dual Chern--Simons--Higgs equation has a rich multiscale structure, but the detailed theory has largely focused on the simple regime, where one entire profile resolves each concentration point. By contrast, non-simple blow-up is analytically more delicate: several interacting regular cores must be resolved simultaneously as they collapse toward a single vortex while remaining separated on their own scales. Because of this difficulty, the existence of such solutions has remained a long-standing open problem. To the best of our knowledge, we prove the first existence result for non-simple blow-up solutions of this equation, covering in particular the previously unresolved finite-height Chern--Simons regime.
  We also show that non-simple clusters have equal limiting core masses, a common profile type, and regular-polygon arrangements. We determine their possible total masses and derive a mass-gap criterion that rules out such blow-up for certain vortex configurations. Our quantitative profile and moment estimates for general finite-height clusters guide the design of the approximate solution and clarify the core interactions underlying the finite-height construction. A separate construction on the unit disk produces two mean-field cores along a sequence with varying Dirichlet traces.

</details>


### [237] [The linear instability of Kasner spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20809)
*Oliver Petersen*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该论文在大爆炸（Big Bang）方向上证明了 Kasner 时空（Kasner spacetimes）的线性稳定性，并完整刻画了其线性层面的不稳定性，允许显式有限维非衰减自相似解（self-similar solutions）空间的存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静止型（quiescent）真空大爆炸时空在单一观测者视角下渐近趋于 Kasner 时空，但据 Belinski、Khalatnikov 与 Lifschitz 的振荡猜想，它们预期高度不稳定。本文旨在无对称性假设下，于线性层面完整描述这一不稳定性。

Method: 核心创新是引入 Kasner 时空上的准正规模（quasinormal modes）概念，类比于稳态黑洞时空（stationary black hole spacetimes）上的准正规模，据此分析 Kasner 时空朝向大爆炸方向的线性演化。

Result: 证明了 Kasner 时空沿大爆炸方向的线性稳定性，其模除显式有限维非衰减自相似解空间外均衰减。增长最快的解为 Taub 显式 Bianchi II 解（Taub's explicit Bianchi II solution）的线性化，描述一次 Kasner 跃迁（Kasner transition）；其余非衰减解为其非均匀类似物及线性化 Kasner 度规。

Conclusion: 四维所有静止型（与振荡型相对）真空大爆炸时空在大爆炸处单一观测者视角下渐近于 Kasner 时空，本文在不假设对称性的情形下给出了该不稳定性的线性层面完整描述。

Abstract: We prove linear stability of Kasner spacetimes in the direction of the Big Bang, up to an explicit finite dimensional space of non-decaying self-similar solutions. The fastest growing solution is the linearization of Taub's explicit Bianchi II solution, describing a Kasner transition. All other non-decaying solutions are inhomogeneous analogues of this, and the linearized Kasner metric. The key novelty is a notion of quasinormal modes on Kasner spacetimes, similar to quasinormal modes on stationary black holes spacetimes.
  All quiescent (as opposed to oscillatory) vacuum Big Bang spacetimes of dimension 4 are asymptotic to a Kasner spacetime from the perspective of a single observer at the Big Bang. They are expected to be highly unstable due to the oscillations conjectured by Belinski, Khalatnikov and Lifschitz. This paper provides a complete description of this instability on a linear level without symmetry assumptions.

</details>


<div id='nucl-th'></div>

# nucl-th [[Back]](#toc)

### [238] [A comprehensive theory framework for perturbative calculations of $δ_C$ in superallowed beta decays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20448)
*Chien-Yeah Seng*

Main category: nucl-th

TL;DR: 摘要指出超允许β衰变中同位旋破缺修正δ_C的"标准"计算违背核子基独立性（nucleon basis independence），存在不可控系统误差，并主张微扰形式是达到Cabibbo幺正性检验精度要求的唯一途径，进而发展完整的生成函数方法（generating function approach）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前由超允许β衰变（superallowed beta decays）确定|V_ud|的高精度结果主要依赖对费米矩阵元（Fermi matrix element）同位旋破缺修正（isospin breaking correction）δ_C的"标准"计算。该标准计算假设δ_C=δ_{C1}+δ_{C2}的可加性分裂，作者认为此形式违背核子基独立性（nucleon basis independence），类似量子场论中的规范不变性（gauge invariance）要求，从而使结果带有不可控系统误差。因此需要建立能在Cabibbo幺正性检验所需精度水平上计算δ_C的方案。

Method: 作者论证微扰形式（perturbative formalism）是唯一可在Cabibbo幺正性（Cabibbo unitarity）检验所需精度下计算δ_C的方法，并在既有文献基础上发展完整的"生成函数方法"（generating function approach）以微扰方式计算δ_C。该方法以核质量分裂（nuclear mass splittings）与核电荷半径中的同位旋破缺（isospin breaking in nuclear charge radii）作为理论基准（theory benchmarks）。

Result: 摘要声明标准计算因违背核子基独立性而必然引入不可控系统误差，而作者发展的生成函数方法提供了微扰计算δ_C的完整框架，并与核质量分裂及核电荷半径的同位旋破缺建立了联系作为基准。摘要未给出具体的数值结果。

Conclusion: δ_C的"标准"可加性计算形式在原理上存在缺陷，微扰形式是满足Cabibbo幺正性检验精度要求的唯一途径，生成函数方法为此类微扰计算提供了完整框架及可对照的核物理基准。

Abstract: The present high precision determination of $|V_{ud}|$ from superallowed beta decays of $J^P=0^+$, $T=1$ nuclei is largely built on top of the ``standard'' calculation of the isospin breaking correction $δ_C$ to the Fermi matrix element, which assumes the splitting $δ_C=δ_{C1}+δ_{C2}$, where $δ_{C1}$ and $δ_{C2}$ represent the ``isospin mixing'' correction and the ``radial mismatch'' correction, respectively. In this paper I show that this formalism violates the rule of nucleon basis independence, similar to the gauge invariance requirement in quantum field theory, therefore its outcome is inevitably subject to uncontrolled systematic errors. I further argue that the perturbative formalism is the only approach that allows the computation of $δ_C$ at the precision level required for the test of the Cabibbo unitarity. Advancing from existing literature, I develop the full ``generating function approach'' to compute $δ_C$ perturbatively, with connections to nuclear mass splittings and the isospin breaking in nuclear charge radii as theory benchmarks.

</details>


### [239] [Projective Origin of the Spin Hydrodynamic Attractor and Its Resurgent repeller](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19165)
*Qi Zhou,Enke Wang*

Main category: nucl-th

TL;DR: 该论文研究 Bjorken 膨胀（Bjorken expansion）中自旋流体力学吸引子（spin hydrodynamic attractor）的射影结构（projective structure）与 resurgence 结构，指出非线性自旋吸引子族由二维线性解空间的射影类决定，并证明 repeller 的信息编码于 attractor 展开的 Borel-Stokes 结构中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: resurgence 关系通常依赖高阶展开与数值 Borel 分析提取，缺乏显式解析实现；本文旨在为最小因果自旋流体力学中的吸引子、repeller 及其 Borel-Stokes 联系提供统一的解析描述。

Method: 采用射影 transseries 结构（projective transseries structure）分析 Bjorken 膨胀下的自旋流体力学吸引子，将非线性自旋吸引子族与二维线性解空间的射影类相联系，其中线性模态比（linear modes ratio）生成完整的单参数 transseries 塔（one-parameter transseries tower）。

Result: attractor 与 repeller 对应两个特定的射影方向，并构成与底层线性解空间这两个射影方向相关的完备全局解分支；解析地证明 repeller 的数据被编码于 attractor 展开的 Borel-Stokes 结构中。

Conclusion: 研究为通常经由高阶展开和数值 Borel 分析获得的 resurgence 关系提供了显式解析实现，并在最小因果自旋流体力学（minimal causal spin hydrodynamics）中给出 attractor、repeller 及其 Borel-Stokes 联系（Borel-Stokes connection）的统一描述。

Abstract: We investigate the projective and resurgent structure of a spin hydrodynamic attractor in Bjorken expansion. We show that the nonlinear spin attractor family is determined by the projective classes of the two dimensional linear solution space, with the attractor and repeller corresponding to two distinguished projective directions and the linear modes ratio generating the full one-parameter transseries tower. We identify the attractor and repeller as complete global solution branches associated with the two distinguished projective directions of the underlying linear solution space.
  Using the projective transseries structure, we show analytically that the data of repeller are encoded in the Borel-Stokes structure of the attractor expansion. These results provide an explicit analytic realization of resurgence relations that are often extracted through high order expansions and numerical Borel analysis, and yield a unified description of the attractor, the repeller, and their Borel-Stokes connection in minimal causal spin hydrodynamics.

</details>


<div id='math.DG'></div>

# math.DG [[Back]](#toc)

### [240] [Spacetime Positive Mass Theorem with Boundary for Multiple Time Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19711)
*Changwen Fang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该论文证明了一类带多个时间方向的单端渐近平坦自旋初值（spin initial data）的正质量定理（positive mass theorem），在标量曲率型能量条件与第二基本形式两两交换的条件下，边界上的固定辅助时间方向诱导局部手征性条件，并导出不等式 $E\geq\|\mathcal P\|_{\mathrm{tr}}$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典正质量定理多针对单一时间方向的初值问题，而多时间方向与带边界情形下的质量下界尚缺乏系统刻画。该工作旨在将正质量定理推广至具有多个时间方向且带光滑紧致边界的渐近平坦自旋初值，并厘清边界项对能量下界的影响。

Method: 在仿射能量空间（affine energy space）中构造 Dirac–Witten 调和旋量（harmonic spinor），通过逼近方法（approximation）计算质量恒等式（mass identity）。证明中假设迹范数主导能量条件（trace-norm dominant energy condition）与第二基本形式（second fundamental forms）的两两交换性，并识别在给定边界条件下残余的混合边界项（mixed boundary term）。

Result: 在边界条件 $H+\operatorname{tr}_\Sigma k^T+\|\mathcal Q_T\|_{\mathrm{tr}}\leq0$ 下，得到能量下界 $E\geq\|\mathcal P\|_{\mathrm{tr}}$。此外，论文建立了该边界条件对固定投影算子（projector）的逐点尖锐性（pointwise sharpness），并给出具有非零混合边界项的双时间数据（two-time data）实例。

Conclusion: 结果表明，多时间方向与边界手征性条件可相容地纳入正质量定理框架，边界项以混合形式进入质量不等式，且所给边界条件在固定投影下是最优的；同时说明非零混合边界项确实可能出现。

Abstract: We prove a positive mass theorem for one-ended asymptotically flat spin initial data with multiple time directions and a smooth compact boundary. Assuming the trace-norm dominant energy condition and pairwise commutativity of the second fundamental forms, a fixed auxiliary time direction on each boundary component determines a local chirality condition. We identify the surviving mixed boundary term and obtain the inequality $E\geq\|\mathcal P\|_{\mathrm{tr}}$ under the boundary condition $H+\operatorname{tr}_Σk^T+\|\mathcal Q_T\|_{\mathrm{tr}}\leq0$. The proof constructs a Dirac--Witten harmonic spinor in an affine energy space and evaluates the mass identity by approximation. We also establish the pointwise sharpness of this boundary condition for the fixed projector and give two-time data with a nonzero mixed boundary term.

</details>
